### [1] [DualClusterNet: Personalized Pattern-Space Composition for Next-POI Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03726)
*Ziyang Su,Guanjie Zheng,Jilin Hu,Linghe Kong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出DualClusterNet，一种因果序列推荐器，通过组合两套群体共享的原型字典（模式与空间）来建模跨用户复现但落在不同地点的移动模式，并在NYC和TKY上取得有竞争力的全候选排序结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一POI推荐需支持个性化地点发现。用户的移动规律可在不同用户间复现，但在不同地点实现并按个人偏好调整，因此需要同时捕捉群体共享模式与个体差异。

Method: 在每个已观测前缀上，软路由器从模式与空间两个原型字典中检索混合表示；因子化乘法交互耦合两者，逐特征仿射调制适配共享表示；另设用户条件分支，通过加性—投影门保留个体差异。预测头将该表示与学习到的一阶转移项结合，对完整训练POI词表排序。采用仅训练集词表及original-final-target协议评估（不桥接未知POI、不替换为更早目标）。

Result: NYC与TKY上单种子结果显示具有竞争力的全候选排序性能；受控分析支持组合分支与个性化分支的贡献。

Conclusion: 双原型字典组合加用户条件门控可在严格评估协议下有效实现因果序列POI推荐，但当前证据限于单种子实验。

Abstract: Next point-of-interest (POI) recommendation supports personalized place discovery on location-based Web platforms. Mobility routines can recur across users while being realized at different places and adapted to individual preferences. We present DualClusterNet, a causal sequential recommender that composes two population-shared prototype dictionaries. At each observed prefix, soft routers retrieve pattern and spatial mixtures; a factorized multiplicative interaction couples them, feature-wise affine modulation adapts the shared representation, and a separate user-conditioned branch with an additive--projective gate retains individual variation. The prediction head combines this representation with a learned first-order transition term and ranks the complete training POI vocabulary. We evaluate on NYC and TKY using a training-only vocabulary and an original-final-target protocol that neither bridges unknown POIs nor substitutes earlier targets. The available single-seed results show competitive full-candidate ranking, while controlled analyses support the contribution of the compositional and personalized branches. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DualClusterNet-F4EE/.

</details>


### [2] [EvoMemState: Evolving Memory State for Personal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03743)
*Zhishang Xiang,Yunbo Tang,Zhongqiao Liu,Junkai Chen,Zerui Chen,Qingguo Hu,Yuxuan Zhang,William Grant,Qinggang Zhang,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出以状态为中心的长期记忆框架 EvoMemState，在持久事实之外引入关系状态与推理状态，提升记忆检索完整性与基于记忆的回复准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体的长期记忆系统以独立事实为单位进行抽取、组织、更新与检索，忽视了新事实如何更新、约束或与旧事实冲突，也隐含地假设事实的适用性、角色与优先级对当前请求是自明的。

Method: EvoMemState 包含三部分：(i) 本体引导的记忆状态归纳，抽取持久事实并构建关系状态以维护其有效性、作用域与演化关系；(ii) 基于强化学习的状态演化，将记忆使用经验内化为策略，生成面向当前请求的推理状态；(iii) 状态感知推理，融合相关事实、其关系状态与生成的推理状态来引导个性化智能体。

Result: 在长期对话与记忆使用基准上的大量实验表明，该方法在检索完整性和基于记忆的回复准确性上相较现有记忆系统获得一致提升。

Conclusion: 记忆不应仅视为独立事实集合，而应建模为随交互演化的状态；显式刻画事实间关系与应用方式可有效提升智能体的个性化长期记忆能力。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables large language model (LLM) agents to provide continuous personalized assistance by retaining user information across interactions. Existing systems support this process through fact extraction, organization, updating, and retrieval, but largely maintain memories as independent facts. This fact-centric paradigm may overlook how new facts update, constrain, or conflict with earlier ones, while leaving their applicability, role, and priority for the current request implicit. In this paper, we introduce EvoMemState, a state-centric framework that augments persistent facts with two complementary states: Relational States that capture how facts remain valid and relate to one another as memory evolves, and a Reasoning State that determines how these facts should be applied to the current request. Specifically, EvoMemState consists of three key components: (i) Ontology-Guided Memory State Induction extracts Persistent Facts and constructs Relational States that maintain their validity, scope, and evolving relations. (ii) RL-Based State Evolution internalizes memory-use experience into a policy through reinforcement learning to generate a request-specific Reasoning State. (iii) State-Aware Reasoning then combines the relevant facts with their Relational States and the generated Reasoning State to guide the personal agent. Extensive experiments on long-term dialogue and memory-use benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over existing memory systems in retrieval completeness and memory-grounded response accuracy. Our code and implementation details are available at https://github.com/XMUDeepLIT/EvoMemState.git.

</details>


### [3] [Enhancing Stock Market Information Retrieval with Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03747)
*Huan Phong Thai,Thu Anh Ngoc Le,Nhu Huynh Thi Le,Nhan Huu Tran,Lenh Cong Phan Pham,Hong Nhung Nguyen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该文构建了一个面向股票市场信息问答的检索增强生成（RAG）系统，将检索式方法与生成能力结合，并在100条股市相关查询上以ROUGE-1/2/L及响应延迟进行评估，取得较高ROUGE分数且平均响应时间1.16秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于NLP的金融系统在动态市场环境中难以保持上下文准确性并生成相关回答，需引入检索增强生成以提升响应质量。

Method: 设计并实现面向股市信息的RAG系统，融合检索模块与生成模块以提升金融文本生成的准确性与相关性；采用ROUGE-1、ROUGE-2、ROUGE-L衡量生成文本与参考文本的词汇及结构相似度，并测量响应延迟。

Result: 在100条股市相关查询的数据集上ROUGE分数较高，表明生成内容与参考文档高度一致；平均响应时间为1.16秒，体现良好实时性。

Conclusion: 该RAG系统能够提供准确、及时的股市信息洞察，是金融信息检索的一种有前景的方案。

Abstract: Natural Language Processing (NLP) has been widely applied in financial domains, enabling automated analysis and retrieval of stock market information. However, traditional NLP-based systems often struggle with maintaining contextual accuracy and generating relevant responses in dynamic financial environments. To address these challenges, Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) models have emerged as a powerful solution, combining retrieval-based methods with generative capabilities to enhance response quality. In this study, we present a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system designed to generate responses related to stock market information. The system integrates retrieval-based and generative approaches to enhance the accuracy and relevance of financial text generation. To evaluate its effectiveness, we employ the ROUGE metric, specifically ROUGE-1, ROUGE-2, and ROUGE-L, to measure lexical and structural similarity between generated and reference texts. Experimental results on a dataset of 100 stock market-related queries demonstrate high ROUGE scores, indicating strong alignment with reference documents. Additionally, we assess latency to evaluate the system responsiveness, achieving an average response time of 1.16 seconds. These findings confirm that our RAG model is capable of providing accurate and timely stock market insights, making it a promising approach for financial information retrieval.

</details>


### [4] [Modular and Efficient Approximate Nearest Neighbors Search with Vectorium and kANNolo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03927)
*Silvio Martinico,Franco Maria Nardini,Cosimo Rulli,Rossano Venturini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出两个开源 Rust 库 Vectorium 与 kANNolo，将 ANN 检索的设计空间拆分为数据管理层与索引层，统一支持稠密、稀疏和多向量三种表示，并在编译期特化各组合以消除运行时开销，性能达到或超过 Faiss 等现有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ANN 库要么固定单一组合，要么对每种组合分别硬编码，要么只解决一侧问题，均无法覆盖稠密、稀疏、多向量三种表示；需要一种模块化且无运行时开销的设计来系统性地组合索引结构、相似度函数与量化方案。

Method: 设计两层架构：Vectorium 独立抽象向量、距离函数、量化器与数据集，提供从 32 位浮点到 sub-bit 编码的 11 种编码器；kANNolo 在其上实现泛型化的 HNSW 图索引与 IVF 索引（对表示、编码器和度量均泛型），以及可将任意一阶段索引与任意重打分数据集组合的两阶段索引；所有组合在编译期特化。

Result: PQ 在精度上与 Faiss 持平且速度有竞争力；RaBitQ 与 HNSW 达到或超过参考实现；IVF 优于 Faiss。在 7 个公开数据集上对比 6 个稠密与 5 个稀疏系统：稠密数据在 12 个"数据集-精度"对中有 9 个取得最佳内存-吞吐权衡，且分布外查询表现良好；稀疏数据在全部 3 个数据集上领先，其中 Seismic 与 SeismicWave 最快，kANNolo 索引最紧凑。

Conclusion: 通过分层抽象与编译期特化，可在不引入运行时开销的前提下统一支持三类向量表示的 ANN 检索，并取得 SOTA 级的精度、内存与吞吐表现，验证了模块化设计的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Approximate Nearest Neighbors (ANN) search over neural embeddings powers modern retrieval systems such as Retrieval-Augmented Generation. Embeddings come in three representations (dense, sparse and multi-vector), and efficient search combines an index structure, a similarity function and a quantization scheme. Existing libraries fix one combination, hard-code each one separately, or factor only one side of the problem, and none covers all three representations. We present Vectorium and kANNolo, two open-source Rust libraries that split this design space into a data-management layer and an indexing layer. Vectorium abstracts the vector, the distance function, the quantizer and the dataset independently, and provides 11 encoders, from 32-bit floats to sub-bit codes. On top of it, kANNolo implements Hierarchical Navigable Small-World (HNSW) graph and Inverted Vector File (IVF) indexes that are generic over representation, encoder and metric, and a two-stage index that composes any first-stage index with any rescoring dataset. Since every combination is specialized at compile time, this modularity adds no run-time overhead, and the resulting indexes reach state-of-the-art performance: our Product Quantization matches Faiss in accuracy at competitive speed, our RaBitQ and HNSW match or outperform their reference implementations, and our IVF outperforms Faiss. Against six dense and five sparse systems on seven public collections, our indexes give the best memory-throughput trade-off on dense data in 9 of 12 collection-accuracy pairs and perform well on out-of-distribution queries. On sparse data, the systems built on Vectorium lead on all three collections, with Seismic and SeismicWave the fastest and kANNolo's indexes the most compact.

</details>


### [5] [Characterizing Adversarial GEO via Implicit Brand Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04005)
*Shobhnik Kriplani,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出多智能体框架IBI，通过在文章中隐式插入单次品牌提及来提升品牌在生成式检索中的曝光与传播，并构建IBIBench基准验证其有效性与隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式搜索引擎基于检索到的网页内容生成答案，从而影响用户对品牌和商业实体的认知。通过修改检索系统可获取的内容来影响答案中出现的品牌，存在被对抗性利用的风险，需系统研究该问题并揭示防御需求。

Method: 定义对抗性生成引擎优化（GEO）场景：通过单次语境融合的品牌提及将其加入文章。提出Implicit Brand Injection（IBI）多智能体框架，包含定位插入位置、改写目标句、验证编辑、筛选最终候选四个环节；并构建IBIBench（1000篇网页文章、12个领域、60个合成品牌，含本土与非本土投放）。在等预算下与Direct Prompting、Best-of-N with ranking对比，评估插入质量、目标块检索与下游品牌传播。

Result: 相比基线，IBI的插入更好保留语义、困惑度扰动更小，且在全部截断、查询设定与投放设置下均提升Hit@k；Hit@10最高82.2%、MRR@10达0.74；Brand Mention Rate（BMR）与Conditional Propagation（CP）分别最高达62.7%和75.9%。

Conclusion: 单次品牌提及即可同时提升检索曝光与生成回答中的品牌可见性，说明隐式内容操纵对生成式检索构成现实威胁，亟需针对性防御机制。

Abstract: Generative search engines increasingly shape how users discover products, services, and commercial entities by generating answers from retrieved web content. This creates an opportunity to influence which brands appear in these answers by modifying the content available to the retrieval system. In this work, we study an adversarial generative engine optimization (GEO) setting in which a predefined brand is added to an article through a single contextually integrated mention. The challenge is to make the mention fit the surrounding article without making the content overtly promotional, while keeping it relevant enough to be retrieved and to influence the generated answer. To study this, we introduce Implicit Brand Injection (IBI), a multi-agent framework that identifies insertion locations, rewrites the selected sentence, verifies the edit, and selects the final candidate. To evaluate IBI at scale, we construct IBIBench, which contains 1,000 web articles and 60 synthetic brands across 12 diverse domains, with both endemic and non-endemic brand placements. We compare IBI with Direct Prompting and Best-of-N with ranking under an equivalent budget, evaluating insertion quality, target-chunk retrieval, and downstream brand propagation. Compared with the baselines, IBI produces insertions that better preserve semantics and disrupt perplexity less, while improving Hit@k across all evaluated cutoffs, query regimes, and placement settings. IBI achieves up to 82.2% Hit@10 and 0.74 MRR@10, while Brand Mention Rate (BMR) and Conditional Propagation (CP) reach up to 62.7% and 75.9%, respectively. These results show that a single brand mention can increase both retrieval exposure and brand visibility in generated responses, highlighting the need for defenses against implicit content manipulation in generative search.

</details>


### [6] [IDs That Mean Something: A Hands-On Tutorial on Semantic IDs for Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04140)
*Roger Zhe Li,Aghiles Salah,Mete Sertkan,Changhong Jin,Yingjie Niu,Ruihai Dong,Barry Smyth*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本教程综述从传统整数ID到语义ID的演进，系统回顾语义ID在多模态、冷启动和跨域推荐中的发展与应用，并提供动手实践与开放挑战讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统使用无语义的任意整数ID标识物品，模型须完全从交互数据学习表示，导致冷启动问题；虽可用内容信息部分缓解，但存在嵌入表爆炸和场景专用架构的问题。语义ID将内容、层次和关系信息直接编码进物品身份，为大规模语义信息利用提供统一范式。

Method: 以教程（tutorial）形式，梳理从原始ID到语义ID的演进脉络，系统评述其在多模态、冷启动与跨域推荐中的研究与应用；并设计动手练习，指导参与者构建与评估语义ID、训练模型及探索跨域迁移。

Result: 指出语义ID框架可同时服务于生成式与判别式应用，缓解嵌入表膨胀与场景专用架构问题，并促进跨模态、跨域与跨任务的统一化；配套提供教程官方材料网站。

Conclusion: 语义ID是提升信息利用率的可扩展手段，但仍存在开放挑战，包括动态语义ID与多利益相关方效用等值得进一步研究的方向。

Abstract: Traditional recommender systems rely on arbitrary integer IDs to identify items, which carry no semantic information and force models to learn representations from interaction data. While leveraging content information can partially mitigate cold-start, this paradigm inherently suffers from exploding embedding tables and scenario-specific architectures. Recent years have seen the emergence of semantic IDs, which encode semantic information directly into the item identity, reflecting content, hierarchy, and relationships. This framework addresses the above limitations by offering a principled way to encode large-scale semantic information into item identity, benefiting both generative and discriminative applications and unlocking more native unification across modalities, domains, and tasks. This tutorial traces the evolution from raw IDs to semantic IDs as a scalable means of information utilization, offering a comprehensive review of their development and applications across multi-modality, cold-start, and cross-domain recommendation. Hands-on exercises guide attendees through building and evaluating semantic IDs, training models, and exploring cross-domain transfer. We conclude by discussing open challenges, including dynamic semantic IDs and multi-stakeholder utility. All materials of the tutorial could be found at the official website https://semantic-id-tutorial.github.io.

</details>


### [7] [IntentGEO: Intent-aware Generative Engine Optimization for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04225)
*Runlin Lei,Shiqi Zhang,Xiaokui Xiao,Zhewei Wei*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出IntentGEO基准，揭示共享改写在不同用户意图下可见性增益差异大且学习式提示优化会漂移，提出AMO（锚定元优化）以固定推荐指令为核心、从引用反馈学习附加策略，平均引用排名增益提升35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GEO研究以内容为中心、面向通用查询优化可见性，但同一条目描述需服务不同需求与决策目标的用户，可见性收益依赖具体请求；且发现学习式提示优化平均甚至劣于手写种子提示。

Method: 将意图（需求具体度、决策进程）纳入GEO评估，形式化IntentGEO任务以评测跨意图的共享改写；诊断提示漂移问题（推荐指令被泛化促销话术取代）；提出AMO，将推荐指令保留在固定核心中，仅从引用反馈学习附加策略。

Result: 覆盖4个领域、10种意图状态：AMO 相对最强学习式基线平均引用排名增益提升35%，在9个状态上领先；消融显示学习附加优于固定核心单独使用，且优于以该核心初始化的全提示优化；在智能体推荐流水线与（单独适配后）商用闭源在线助手Doubao上取得最高增益。

Conclusion: 用户意图是GEO评估的关键维度，锚定式适配可跨推荐需求与决策阶段泛化，为生成式引擎优化提供稳健方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly shape recommendations across chatbots, e-commerce, and user-generated content (UGC) platforms, creating demand for Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) to improve item visibility in generated responses. Existing GEO studies are mainly content-centric, optimizing visibility under general-purpose queries. However, a shared item description usually serves users with different needs and decision goals, resulting in its visibility gains being dependent on the request. To investigate how changing intent affects visibility, we formulate IntentGEO to evaluate shared rewrites across need specificity and decision progression. We revisit existing GEO methods and find that their visibility gains vary across user intents. More critically, learning-based prompt optimization can underperform its handwritten seed on average. Tracing these updates reveals prompt drift from explicit recommendation instructions toward generic promotional tactics. To address this drift, we propose AMO (Anchored Meta-Optimization), which preserves recommendation instructions in a fixed core while learning appended strategies from citation feedback. Across four domains and ten intent states, AMO improves average citation-rank gain by 35% over the strongest prior learning-based baseline and leads on nine states. Ablations show that learned additions improve on the fixed core alone and that AMO outperforms full-prompt optimization initialized from that core. It also achieves the highest gains in an agentic recommendation pipeline and, after separate adaptation, on Doubao, a commercially deployed, closed-source online assistant. These findings highlight user intent as a key dimension of GEO evaluation and support anchored adaptation across recommendation needs and decision stages.

</details>


### [8] [ReuseGR: Offline--Online Decoupled Generative Retrieval via Adaptive Prefix Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04259)
*Xujing Wang,Yufeng Chen,Boxuan Zhang,Chao Wei,Cai Xu,Jie Zhao,Weiru Zhang,Wei Zhao,Ziyu Guan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ReuseGR 将生成式检索的查询级候选规划与请求级个性化补全解耦：7B 离线规划器生成共享 SID 前缀集合，0.5B 在线模型结合用户历史选择前缀并补全剩余 token，并配合 ReuseCut、Path KD、BudgetCut（OPD 初始化 + GRPO 优化）实现计算复用与个性化检索的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大生成式检索模型质量好但解码开销大，难以低延迟服务；同一查询被多用户提交时重复生成完整 SID 浪费计算，而各用户历史又要求结果个性化，因此需在跨请求复用大模型计算的同时保持检索个性化。

Method: 提出 ReuseGR，分离查询级候选规划与请求级个性化补全：7B 离线规划器为同查询请求生成共享 SID 前缀集合；0.5B 在线模型基于请求历史选择前缀并生成剩余 token。ReuseCut 依据历史覆盖率按查询选择缓存深度，权衡计算复用与候选覆盖；Path KD 将 7B 后缀打分知识以路径级蒸馏迁移至 0.5B；BudgetCut 在固定分支预算下选择已打分分支以提升 top-100 召回，其选择策略由 on-policy distillation (OPD) 初始化并经 GRPO 细化。

Result: 在约 200 万 query–item 记录、338 万商品目录的工业搜索广告数据集上，前缀已准备时相对全在线 0.5B 基线 query 级 HR@100 提升 3.83 个百分点、平均在线时延降低 38.9%；HR@100 与全在线 7B 基线相当，在线时延低 57.7%。14 天用户级在线 A/B 测试相对 0.5B 基线 CTR 相对提升 0.93%，千次请求收入相对提升 1.05%。

Conclusion: 通过离线大模型共享前缀规划与在线小模型个性化补全，并结合缓存深度控制、知识蒸馏与预算受限的精排分支选择，ReuseGR 可在显著降低在线时延的同时保持或超越大模型检索质量，并在真实工业系统中获得点击率与收入正向收益。

Abstract: Larger generative retrieval models can improve retrieval quality, but their decoding cost makes low-latency serving difficult. When different users submit the same query, generating complete Semantic IDs (SIDs) for every request repeats computation, while their interaction histories call for different results. The challenge is to reuse large-model computation across requests while keeping retrieval personalized.
  We propose \textbf{ReuseGR}, which separates query-level candidate planning from request-level personalized completion. A 7B offline planner generates SID prefix sets shared across requests with the same query. A 0.5B online model uses each request's history to select prefixes and generate the remaining tokens. ReuseCut chooses a cache depth for each query based on historical coverage, balancing computation reuse and candidate coverage. To strengthen online completion, we transfer 7B suffix-scoring knowledge to 0.5B through path-level knowledge distillation (Path KD). BudgetCut then selects scored branches to improve final top-100 recall under a fixed branch budget. Its selection policy is initialized by on-policy distillation (OPD) and refined through group-relative policy optimization (GRPO).
  On an industrial search-advertising dataset with approximately 2M query--item records and 3.38M catalog items, ReuseGR improves query-level HR@100 by 3.83 percentage points and reduces mean online latency by 38.9\% relative to an all-online 0.5B baseline when prefixes are prepared. It achieves HR@100 comparable to an all-online 7B baseline with 57.7\% lower online latency. A 14-day user-level online A/B test against the 0.5B baseline shows relative gains of 0.93\% in click-through rate and 1.05\% in revenue per thousand requests.

</details>


### [9] [Do Retrieval Ranking Models Capture Event Order? A Study of Timeline-Constrained Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04343)
*Alon Eirew,Lidan Avisar,Kfir Bar,Ido Dagan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究"时间线约束的文档检索"任务，考察语义检索模型是否编码事件间的时间关系，发现现有稠密检索器与神经重排器难以捕捉时间约束，而显式建模时间顺序的方法带来显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义检索模型以匹配查询"语义"为目标，但其排序机制是否真正编码了事件之间的时间关系仍不清楚；需要验证这一能力缺口。

Method: 构建新的聚焦评测集合与受控实验设置；查询为有序的自然语言事件陈述序列，相关文档需以兼容的时间推进顺序匹配这些事件。测试主流稠密检索器与神经重排器，并与词法检索及时间线感知（timeline-aware）方法对比。

Result: 先进语义检索器与重排器相较词法检索仅带来有限提升，说明其语义匹配能力无法可靠捕捉时间约束；时间线感知方法相较语义匹配与标准重排器取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 时序顺序在该设定下是强排序信号，未来需在语义检索器中改进时间建模。

Abstract: Semantic retrieval models aim to match documents to a query's "meaning", yet it remains unclear whether they successfully encode temporal relations between events in their ranking mechanisms. We investigate this question by studying timeline-constrained document retrieval, where a query is an ordered sequence of natural-language event statements while a relevant document should match those events in a compatible temporal progression. We then test whether widely used dense retrievers and neural rerankers capture such constraints, and whether explicitly preserving temporal order improves ranking, using a controlled setting over a novel focused evaluation collection. Experiments show that state-of-the-art semantic retrievers and rerankers provide only limited improvements over lexical retrieval, suggesting that their semantic matching capabilities do not reliably capture temporal constraints. We then compare a timeline-aware method with both semantic matching and standard rerankers and find that it yields substantial improvements. This demonstrates that considering temporal order can provide a strong ranking signal in this setting, suggesting future research on improving temporal modeling in semantic retrievers.

</details>


### [10] [DualEvolve: Dual-Level Self-Evolution of Mechanism and Orchestration for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04439)
*Tianqi Gao,Chenxu Wang,Wenxuan Yang,Yueqi Mou,Biao Tang,Ming Liu,Peng Yan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DualEvolve是一个双层框架，让LLM智能体同时进化推荐机制与智能体编排，实现递归式自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体自动化推荐系统开发时，更新选择主要依据候选方案的最终任务分数，缺少针对“实验质量”的独立优化信号，难以实现持续自主改进。

Method: 内层进化利用任务反馈修改模型与训练代码以提升推荐性能；外层进化对内层实验过程打分，基于分数与执行轨迹更新harness与skill规则，并依据下一轮分数调整编排选择，从而提升后续实验质量。

Result: 在MovieLens-1M及三个Amazon Reviews 2023子集上，覆盖4种推荐方法与16种设置，共64项设置-指标对比中DualEvolve全部优于Base、OpenEvolve与DeepEvolve；相对DeepEvolve的宏平均相对提升为HR@5 7.6%、NDCG@5 9.0%。

Conclusion: 将“实验过程质量”作为独立优化信号并与任务性能协同进化，可有效实现推荐系统开发工作流的递归自我改进。

Abstract: Recommender systems are widely used in e-commerce and content platforms, where improvement requires repeated model design, training optimization, and experimental validation. Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly automate this development cycle. Sustained autonomous improvement requires agents to improve both recommendation models and their development workflows. However, task-performance-driven approaches select updates mainly by candidates' final scores, without a separate optimization signal for experimentation quality. We therefore present DualEvolve, a dual-level framework that co-evolves recommendation mechanisms and agent orchestration toward recursive self-improvement. Inner evolution revises model and training code using task feedback to improve recommendation performance. Outer evolution scores the inner-loop experimental process, updates harness and skill rules using these scores and execution traces, and adjusts orchestration choices based on next-round scores to improve the quality of subsequent experiments. Experiments on MovieLens-1M and three Amazon Reviews 2023 subsets cover four recommendation methods and 16 settings. DualEvolve achieves higher test means than Base, OpenEvolve, and DeepEvolve in all 64 setting-metric comparisons, with macro-averaged relative gains over DeepEvolve of 7.6% (HR@5) and 9.0% (NDCG@5).

</details>


### [11] [SourceFlow: Collaborative Source Coupling for Flow-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04455)
*Chenghao Wu,Yongqi Zhou,Jin Chen,Yifeng Zhou,Jinchang Zhu,Junwei Pan,Chao Zhou,Zhixiang Feng,Chengguo Yin,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 SourceFlow，将协同结构注入流式协同过滤的源分布，通过 Neighborhood Coupling、Collaborative Consensus 与 Stretched Restoration 使源向量携带目标历史相关的协同线索，在五个数据集上一致性优于基线，Recall@10 相对提升 15.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式推荐器通过去噪交互历史学习偏好，但注入的噪声会扭曲原始偏好信号；流式 CF 可在少步内完成推理，但现有方法仅用全局物品频率独立采样源，源与目标历史无关，预测器缺乏用户特定的源线索。

Method: 不再对预测器增加条件，而是改造源分布本身：Neighborhood Coupling 用未观测的协同替代物品替换历史物品，构造"位移二值源"；Collaborative Consensus 利用共享该物品的其他用户交互证据细化替换概率；Stretched Restoration 在训练中将替换保留至较晚流时间，增加恢复原始交互的机会。

Result: 五个推荐数据集上一致优于竞争基线，相对最强流式基线 Recall@10 平均相对提升 15.7%；受控实验显示收益依赖源与目标历史的正确配对，跨用户重分配同一批源向量后收益反转。

Conclusion: 源分布中嵌入协同结构可有效提升流式协同过滤性能，且源—目标配对的正确性是收益的关键前提。

Abstract: Diffusion-based recommenders learn user preferences by denoising interaction histories, but the added noise can distort the original preference signals. Flow-based collaborative filtering (CF) instead learns a discrete path from a sampled source to the user's observed training interactions (the target history), enabling inference in a few steps. Existing flow-based CF methods sample sources using global item frequencies to reflect the sparsity and popularity of observed interactions. Each source is sampled independently of its target history, leaving the predictor without user-specific source cues for learning to reconstruct that history. We propose SourceFlow, which brings collaborative structure into the source distribution itself rather than adding further conditioning to the predictor. Like inference from observed histories, training uses collaborative evidence to predict missing interactions. Specifically, Neighborhood Coupling constructs displaced binary sources by replacing historical items with unobserved collaborative alternatives. Collaborative Consensus further refines replacement probabilities using evidence from other interactions of users who share the observed item. Stretched Restoration retains these replacements at late flow times during training, providing more opportunities to recover the original interactions. Experiments on five recommendation datasets show consistent improvements over competitive baselines, including a mean relative Recall@10 gain of 15.7% over the strongest flow-based baseline. Controlled experiments confirm that these gains depend on correctly pairing collaborative sources with their target histories, as reassigning the same source vectors across users reverses the gains. Code will be publicly released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [12] [$τ$-Rec-Books: A Disclosure-Controlled Benchmark for Requirement Elicitation in Agentic Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04459)
*Bharath Sivaram Narasimhan,Karthik R. Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 $\tau$-Rec-Books 基准，通过固定用户请求、仅改变需求揭示时机（volunteer/mixed/hidden）来评估对话推荐系统的需求引导能力，发现当前 agent 在需主动询问与主动试探时成功率显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话推荐系统的核心挑战在于推荐满足用户前，需逐步发现用户需求，但缺乏针对"需求引导（elicitation）"能力的系统评估手段。

Method: 构建 $\tau$-Rec-Books：基于 Wikidata 2 万本书目生成 450 个任务，设置三种需求揭示条件（volunteer：全部前置给出；mixed：一项目开始给出、其余按询问给出；hidden：部分需求永不主动给出，仅能通过提案+接受/拒绝反馈推断）；采用基于规则的模拟用户回答 agent，并基于书目元数据（不依赖 LLM judge）对推荐结果打分；在 4 个 agent × 2 种搜索接口上评测。

Result: 所有 agent 与接口的成功率均随条件恶化而下降：平均从 volunteer 的 0.975 降至 mixed 的 0.756、hidden 的 0.283。mixed 失败主因是 agent 不主动提问，提示其提问后各 agent 成功率均达 0.931 以上。hidden 任务失败主因是推荐了从未向用户提案的书目；被要求先提案时，agent 仅与"推荐热门书"的简单基线持平，明显低于利用拒绝信号排除候选需求的基线。

Conclusion: 现有 agent 的需求引导能力仍不足：提问与主动探测是性能瓶颈所在，简单策略（提问、基于拒绝的排除）即可带来显著提升，表明该基准能有效暴露 elicitation 环节的短板。代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: While conversational recommendation systems provide a more natural user interface, they face the challenge of progressively discovering a user's needs before they can recommend an item that satisfies them. To evaluate the ability of AI agents at this elicitation process, we introduce $τ$-Rec-Books, a benchmark that holds each user request fixed while varying only when the user reveals their requirements: all at the start (volunteer), one at the start and the rest when asked (mixed), or some never (hidden). A hidden requirement can be found only by proposing books and inferring it from the user's accept or reject verdicts. We generate 450 tasks over a catalog of 20,000 books from Wikidata, develop a rule-based simulated user to answer the agent, and score recommendations against catalog metadata without an LLM judge. Across four agents and two search interfaces, success rates fall for every agent and interface, on average from 0.975 (volunteer) to 0.756 (mixed) and 0.283 (hidden). Mixed tasks fail mainly because agents do not ask, and when told to ask, every agent reaches at least 0.931. On hidden tasks, most failures come from agents recommending a book they never proposed to the user. When told to propose first, agents only match a simple baseline that proposes popular books, and stay well below one that uses rejection to rule out possible requirements. We release the catalog pipeline, task generator, simulated user, validators, reference agents, and all agent traces at https://github.com/nbharaths/tau-rec/tree/main/data/tracks/books-wikidata-v2.

</details>


### [13] [Collaborative Search Meets Large Language Models: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04619)
*Peiyuan Gong,Jiamian Li,Erhan Zhang,Fengming Liang,Kexin Liu,Yue Chen,Jiaxin Mao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 一项80人（40对）被试研究系统比较了LLM智能体（QAAgent、CoSearchAgent）、聊天内搜索插件与人工Google搜索在协作搜索中的表现：LLM智能体降低感知负荷、促进协作并重塑沟通与证据使用实践，促成更多独立改写但牺牲报告丰富度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体已可在共享通信空间中支持协作信息搜寻，但相较传统搜索工具，这类智能体如何影响搜索体验、用户行为与协作结果尚缺乏认识，需要系统的实证对比。

Method: 招募80名学生组成40对，每对在四种条件下各完成一次协作搜索任务：QAAgent（仅用LLM参数化知识答疑）、CoSearchAgent（利用多用户对话上下文改写查询、检索网页并生成有来源依据的答案，必要时提出澄清问题）、聊天内搜索插件（返回结果列表）和人工Google搜索。每任务后填写体验问卷并各自独立撰写报告，全部任务后进行个人访谈。

Result: LLM智能体降低了感知工作负荷，支持协作，并重塑了沟通与证据使用实践；同时鼓励更多独立改写（paraphrasing），但以报告丰富度下降为代价。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个跨搜索体验、用户行为与协作结果三个维度、系统比较LLM智能体与传统工具的协作搜索实证研究，揭示了LLM智能体在协作搜索中的收益与代价。

Abstract: Collaborative search enables users to address complex information needs jointly. Advances in large language models (LLMs) enable autonomous agents to support collaborative information seeking within shared communication spaces. However, little is known about how these agents shape search experiences, behaviors, and outcomes compared with conventional search tools. We conduct a user study with 80 students working in 40 pairs. Each pair completes four collaborative search tasks under four scenarios: QAAgent, which answers queries using an LLM's parametric knowledge; CoSearchAgent, which uses multi-user conversational context to reformulate queries, retrieve web information, and generate source-grounded answers, while asking clarifying questions when needed; an in-chat search plugin that returns result lists; and manual Google Search. After each task, participants complete several questionnaires assessing their search experience and independently write a report; after all tasks, they participate in individual interviews. Results show that LLM-based agents reduce perceived workload, support collaboration, and reshape communication and evidence-use practices. They also encourage more independent paraphrasing, albeit at the cost of report richness. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic empirical comparison of LLM-based agents and conventional tools for collaborative search across search experience, user behavior, and collaborative outcomes.

</details>


### [14] [QueryFlow: Multi-Hop Graph Retrieval with Adaptive Query Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04625)
*Manzong Huang,Chenyang Bu,Yi He,Jiapu Wang,Xindong Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 QueryFlow，将多跳证据检索视为证据表示与查询条件的耦合演化，用一个共享查询状态递归地整合图上下文摘要，并驱动后续图传播的边界输入与关系特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳 RAG 需在有限预算下跨页恢复互补证据。独立打分难以发现依赖中间事实才显现相关性的证据；迭代检索虽引入中间事实，图检索虽显式建模关系，但固定查询的图检索器在更新实体表示时不根据累积的关系上下文调整查询条件。

Method: QueryFlow：维护一个共享查询状态，采用循环方式将基于查询条件的图上下文摘要整合进该状态；该状态同时控制后续图传播的边界输入（boundary inputs）与关系特征（relation features），使"检索什么"与"如何检索"同步演化。

Result: 在四个代表性多跳 QA 数据集上，相较代表性基线，macro Recall@10 提升 4.98 个百分点，Full Recall@10 提升 8.40 个百分点，LLM-Acc 提升 8.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: 查询条件应随累积上下文动态演化而非保持固定；查询状态与证据表示的双向耦合能更有效地在有限预算内恢复多跳互补证据。

Abstract: Multi-hop retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) supports Web information seeking and fact verification by recovering complementary evidence across related pages within a limited retrieval budget. Scoring evidence independently against the original question can miss supporting units whose relevance emerges through intermediate facts. Iterative retrieval incorporates these facts into subsequent queries, while graph retrieval explicitly models relations among evidence units. Yet fixed-query graph retrievers update entity representations without adapting the query condition to accumulated relational context. We view multi-hop evidence recovery as the coupled evolution of evidence representations and query conditions, with accumulated context informing both what the retriever represents and how it searches for complementary evidence. QueryFlow realizes this perspective by recurrently integrating query-conditioned summaries of graph context into a shared query state, which controls boundary inputs and relation features for subsequent propagation. Across four representative multi-hop question answering (QA) datasets, QueryFlow achieves macro Recall@10 (R@10), Full Recall@10 (FR@10), and LLM-Acc gains of 4.98, 8.40, and 8.0 percentage points over representative baselines, respectively.

</details>


### [15] [SPADE: Spatiotemporal Preference Alignment and Diversity Enhancement for Local Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04662)
*Chenxu Wang,Wenxuan Yang,Yueqi Mou,Shuxian Bi,Biao Tang,Peng Yan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SPADE：一种端到端生成式主动查询推荐框架，联合优化生成空间、上下文表示、偏好分布与输出列表质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地服务应用首页的主动查询推荐需从百万级词表做开放集生成，输出须同时满足用户偏好对齐、细粒度时空匹配、长尾意图覆盖与少量候选位内的多样性。现有方法各自孤立地处理这些维度：常规分词抑制长尾查询，粗粒度位置编码丢失细粒度地理需求，通用偏好优化无法对齐线上曝光-点击分布，标准束搜索导致近重复输出。

Method: SPADE 包含四个组件：OA-BPE 将子词合并与业务价值信号对齐以提升长尾查询生成概率；STAR 用连续多尺度编码与软门控替代粗粒度位置 token，实现精确时空定位；MARS-DPO 结合跨域预训练与基于线上曝光-点击数据的偏好对齐，采用复合奖励与自适应难负采样；SDBS 在不牺牲召回的前提下提升意图覆盖。

Result: 大规模生产数据离线实验：相较最强生成基线 +5.6% HR@5，GeoC@5 降低 40.3%，+1.9% Exec@5。一周线上 A/B 测试：模块 CTR +1.88%，搜索量 +0.177%，搜索 DAU +0.105%。

Conclusion: SPADE 通过四组件协同优化，在离线与线上均验证了主动查询推荐的有效性，并带来实际生产收益。

Abstract: Proactive query recommendation aims to suggest relevant search queries to users on the homepage of local service apps. Unlike conventional recommendation over a fixed item set, this requires open-set generation from a vocabulary of millions, where outputs must simultaneously align with user preference, match fine-grained spatiotemporal context, cover long-tail intents, and provide diverse alternatives within a few candidate slots. Existing approaches address these axes in isolation: standard tokenization suppresses long-tail queries, coarse location encoding misses fine-grained geographic demand, generic preference optimization fails to align with production impression-click distributions, and standard beam search collapses to near-duplicate outputs. We present SPADE (Spatiotemporal Preference Alignment and Diversity Enhancement), an end-to-end generative framework that jointly optimizes the generation space, contextual representation, preference distribution, and output slate quality through four components: Objective-Aligned BPE (OA-BPE) aligns subword merging with business value signals to boost long-tail query generation probability; Spatio-Temporal Adaptive Representation (STAR) replaces coarse location tokens with continuous multi-scale encodings and soft gating for precise spatiotemporal grounding; MARS-DPO (Multi-dimensional Adaptive Reward Sampling DPO) combines cross-domain pre-training with preference alignment on production impression-click data via composite rewards and adaptive hard negative sampling; and Sigmoid Diversity Beam Search (SDBS) improves intent coverage without sacrificing recall. Offline experiments on large-scale production data show that SPADE outperforms the strongest generative baseline by +5.6% HR@5, -40.3% GeoC@5, and +1.9% Exec@5. A one-week online A/B test confirms production impact: +1.88% Module CTR, +0.177% Search Volume, and +0.105% Search DAU.

</details>


### [16] [OneTrans-GSR: A Generative Slate Ranking Paradigm Unifying Reranking and Mix-Ranking with One Autoregressive Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04720)
*Feng Cheng,Yu-Hsuan Huang,Fengyi Wang,Chaofan Zhou,Zhennan Zhu,Huan He,Bo Zhang,Ruoqi Zhang,Zhanfeng Mo,Ziwei Liu,Lanyi Li,Yifeng Pan,Junhua Long,Ziqiu Zhou,Yue Lin,Shaowei Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文将重排序与混排统一为可扩展的学习问题，提出单解码器策略 OneTrans-GSR 端到端生成曝光列表，以奖励模型+强化学习优化列表级长时目标，并在线A/B中显著提升商业指标同时降低时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业推荐系统在re-ranking与mix-ranking阶段依赖人工规则与低容量专用模型组装最终曝光列表（组合商品卡、短视频、直播、广告卡等多业务通道内容）。这些规则虽含领域知识，但限制候选列表组合空间、依赖人工调参权衡多目标，且难以利用更多数据与算力，因而无法随计算扩展而提升性能。

Method: 将re-ranking与mix-ranking重构为统一的列表生成学习问题，并在两阶段统一实例化：提出 OneTrans-GSR——基于已部署的 OneTrans 骨干扩展而来的单一 decoder-only 策略，端到端生成曝光列表。训练时用奖励模型对完整列表打分，指导强化学习优化列表级与长期目标；解码时显式施加业务约束。

Result: 离线评估显示列表质量随奖励模型与策略算力增加而持续提升；在大规模工业推荐系统上线，OneTrans-GSR 在统一列表内混合异构内容类型，在线A/B测试中 GMV/u 提升2.63%，广告主价值提升2.06%，复购率提升2.03%，同时re-ranking与mix-ranking端到端时延降低13%。

Conclusion: 结果表明列表生成范式发生转变：从人工设计启发式规则转向通用学习策略，为后续算法优化与算力驱动的性能提升提供了可扩展路径。

Abstract: Industrial recommender systems allocate substantial computation to pointwise candidate scoring, yet separate reranking and mix-ranking stages still assemble the final exposure slate using hand-crafted rules and specialized low-capacity models. Mix-ranking combines product cards, short videos, livestreams, and advertising cards supplied by distinct upstream business channels. Although these rules and models encode valuable domain knowledge, they are inherently limited in leveraging additional data and computation. They constrain the combinatorial space of candidate slates and determine objective trade-offs through manual tuning. To enable scalable and effective slate generation, we reformulate reranking and mix-ranking as a unified learning problem whose performance can improve with computation. Specifically, we instantiate this formulation across the reranking and mix-ranking stages and propose OneTrans-GSR, a single decoder-only policy that extends our deployed OneTrans backbone to generate the exposure slate end-to-end. During training, a reward model evaluates complete slates and guides reinforcement learning toward slate-level and long-term objectives. During decoding, business constraints are explicitly enforced. Offline evaluation shows that slate quality consistently improves as reward-model and policy compute increase. Deployed in a large-scale industrial recommendation system, OneTrans-GSR mixes heterogeneous content types within a unified slate. In online A/B tests, it improves GMV/u by 2.63%, advertiser value by 2.06%, and repurchase rate by 2.03%, while reducing end-to-end latency across the reranking and mix-ranking stages by 13%. These results mark a paradigm shift in slate generation: moving beyond human-engineered heuristics toward a general learned policy creates a scalable path for further algorithmic optimization and compute-driven improvement.

</details>


### [17] [Learning to Rank Explanations for Recommendation with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04775)
*Ben Kabongo,Vincent Guigue*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 LMStaR——将个性化解释排名表述为稠密检索的纯文本框架，用查询编码器与陈述编码器共享嵌入空间，并以多正例对比学习联合适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统解释需可理解且可评测；LLM 生成的自然语言理由在事实性与个性化相关性上难以评估。陈述级排名可借助成熟排序指标实现细粒度、可复现评测，但现有方法依赖协同表示，语义增强难以超越基于用户历史流行度的简单基线，且直接适配语言模型做个性化陈述排名尚属空白。

Method: LMStaR：查询编码器编码用户交互历史与目标物品元数据，陈述编码器将候选解释映射到同一嵌入空间，两编码器通过多正例对比学习联合适配以学习个性化相关性；整体为完全基于文本的稠密检索框架，并进行多骨干与跨域迁移、反事实分析。

Result: 在 Amazon Beauty、Amazon Toys、Yelp 上 NDCG@1 均为最高，较 CLER 提升 1.06–8.25 点（+11.8%–65.9%）；两个 Amazon 数据集上前五名排名领先，Yelp 上 CLER 仍更强。任务特定适配使五个被测骨干均获提升，且无需目标域微调即可跨域迁移；反事实分析显示用户特定历史的重要性。

Conclusion: 任务适配的语言模型在个性化解释排名上具备潜力，纯文本稠密检索式表述结合多正例对比学习可有效优于协同表示基线。

Abstract: Recommender systems are ubiquitous on the Web, making understandable explanations essential for users. Although large language models (LLMs) increasingly generate natural-language justifications, evaluating their factual grounding and personalized relevance remains challenging. Statement-level ranking offers an alternative by selecting concise, review-derived explanations, enabling fine-grained and reproducible evaluation using established ranking metrics. However, existing statement ranking methods largely rely on collaborative representations, while semantic enhancements often fail to outperform simple user-history popularity baselines. Moreover, directly adapting language models for personalized statement ranking remains largely unexplored. In this work, we introduce LMStaR (Language Models for Statement Ranking), a fully text-based framework that formulates personalized explanation ranking as dense retrieval. A query encoder represents the user's interaction history and target-item metadata, while a statement encoder maps candidate explanations into a shared embedding space. Both encoders are jointly adapted through multi-positive contrastive learning to learn personalized relevance. Experiments on Amazon Beauty, Amazon Toys, and Yelp show that LMStaR achieves the highest NDCG@1 across all three domains, outperforming CLER by 1.06--8.25 points (+11.8--65.9%). It also leads top-five ranking on both Amazon datasets, although CLER remains stronger on Yelp. Task-specific adaptation improves all five evaluated backbones and transfers across domains without target-domain fine-tuning. Counterfactual analyses further reveal the importance of user-specific histories. These findings highlight the potential of task-adapted language models for personalized explanation ranking.

</details>


### [18] [Scaling Laws For Data-Constrained Dense Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04979)
*Bowen Fu,Jingtao Zhan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 在数据受限的微调场景下，最佳可达检索性能可用关于非嵌入参数量与唯一样本数的乘法幂律（MAEL）精确刻画；模型增大比增加唯一域内数据在降低MAEL上约高效1.46倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索模型通常在有限的高质量标注或交互日志上反复多轮微调，这种数据受限情形偏离标准scaling law所假设的数据充足设定，因此模型规模与唯一微调数据量如何共同决定最优可达检索性能尚不明确。

Method: 定义最小可达评估损失（MAEL）为固定模型规模与数据预算下训练过程中达到的最低评估损失；分析学习率对优化轨迹的影响（重标定优化步轴后学习曲线坍缩，并用CKA验证达到MAEL的检查点表示高度相似）；在MS MARCO子集上微调35个BERT族稠密检索器，拟合MAEL对非嵌入参数与唯一样本的缩放形式；推导MAEL达到时训练步的缩放律，并分析最优权重衰减与EMA时间尺度的关系。

Result: 学习率显著改变优化轨迹但几乎不影响最终MAEL；MAEL可由非嵌入参数与唯一样本的简单乘法幂律准确描述，优于常用加性缩放公式；MAEL达到步数亦服从缩放律；最优权重衰减近似对应不变的EMA时间尺度，可在域内与域外基准上降低MAEL并提升检索性能；综合损失与步数缩放表明，增大预训练模型规模在降低MAEL上约为增加唯一域内数据的1.46倍微调算力效率。

Conclusion: 数据受限的稠密检索微调中，可达性能由乘法幂律支配，学习率主要影响路径而非终点；通过步数缩放律与EMA时间尺度不变的权重衰减设定可进一步优化；在观测区间内，扩展模型规模比扩展唯一域内数据更具算力效率，为数据受限检索的资源配置提供定量依据。

Abstract: Dense retrieval models are typically obtained by fine-tuning pretrained language models on supervised retrieval data, where high-quality annotations or interaction logs are often limited and reused across many epochs. This data-constrained regime differs from the abundant-data setting assumed by standard scaling laws, making it unclear how the best attainable retrieval performance changes with model size and unique fine-tuning data. We study this question through the minimum achievable evaluation loss (MAEL), defined as the lowest evaluation loss reached during training under fixed model-size and data-budget constraints. We first show that learning-rate choices strongly affect optimization trajectories but have little effect on the final attainable MAEL: after rescaling the optimization-step axis, learning curves collapse across learning rates, and CKA analysis shows that checkpoints reaching MAEL learn highly similar representations. We then fine-tune 35 BERT-family dense retrievers on MS MARCO subsets and find that MAEL is accurately described by a simple multiplicative power law over non-embedding parameters and unique samples, outperforming commonly used additive scaling formulations. We further derive a scaling law for the training step at which MAEL is reached and show that optimal weight decay follows an approximately invariant EMA timescale, yielding lower MAEL and stronger retrieval performance on both in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks. Finally, combining loss and step scaling suggests that, in our observed regime, increasing pretrained model size is approximately 1.46 times as fine-tuning-compute-efficient as increasing unique in-domain data for reducing MAEL.

</details>


### [19] [When Category Means Move: A Paired-Support Audit of Recommendation Logs with Random Insertions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05006)
*Yu Zhang,Zhihan Wang,Yuhang Lu,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文利用KuaiRand随机插入曝光日志，对内容类别仪表盘所报告的均值进行审计：同一批12,472名用户与5,486条视频在两类日志中对照。标准均值绝对/中心化MAE为0.1935/0.0491，秩相关0.7833。用户去均值加随机总均值锚点使绝对MAE降80.0%，但中心化MAE仅降21.0%，且秩相关未可靠改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内容类别仪表盘以均值汇总生产推荐日志，但服务策略实际上在挑选用户、物品与上下文，使均值可能由支撑集（overlap）差异而非真实类别效应驱动。作者希望审计这些汇总在随机化参照下的可靠性，并量化误差来源与决策后果。

Method: 基于KuaiRand的随机插入印象，在同一用户-视频样本上对比生产日志与随机日志；采用用户去均值加随机总均值锚点、完整支撑账本分解（加权平方误差分解为视频内残差、视频组合、随机支撑三部分）、早期类别校准与标量校准对比、以及top-k遗憾的bootstrap区间与六对比族错误率校正；另做无结果变量的不相交随机参照切分与已知真值压力测试（均值可加正对照、物品组合、弱重叠）。

Result: 完整支撑账本将加权平方误差主要分配给视频内残差（72.41%）与视频组合（23.90%），随机支撑仅3.65%。冻结后测中早期类别校准MAE 0.0251，标量校准0.0531，差值-0.0280 [-0.0296, -0.0172]。决策证据依cutoff而定：行加权去均值相对朴素法的名义top-10遗憾变化为-0.0062 [-0.0084, -0.0003]，而top-5与top-20区间含零；六对比族错误率校正后的top-10区间[-0.0136, 0.0013]含零；无结果变量切分下六个名义对比均覆盖零。已知真值压力测试显示均值可加正对照下改进不完整，物品组合与弱重叠下失败。

Conclusion: 所有分解均为描述性，且每个结果以重叠队列为条件；KuaiRand区间量化的是在已实现目录与日历条件下的用户簇变异，作者不主张内在类别效应，也不主张普适有效的校正方法。

Abstract: Content-category dashboards summarize production recommendation logs, although the serving policy selects the users, items, and contexts behind each mean. Using KuaiRand random-insertion impressions, we audit these summaries on the same 12,472 users and 5,486 videos in both logs. Standard means have absolute/centered mean absolute error (MAE) 0.1935/0.0491 and rank correlation 0.7833. User demeaning plus a random grand-mean anchor reduces absolute MAE by 80.0%, but centered MAE by only 21.0%, without reliably improving rank. A complete-support ledger allocates weighted squared error mainly to within-video residual (72.41%) and video mix (23.90%), with 3.65% from random support. In a frozen later test, early category calibration reaches MAE 0.0251 versus 0.0531 for scalar calibration; their direct difference is -0.0280 [-0.0296, -0.0172]. Decision evidence is cutoff-specific: the nominal top-10 regret change for row-weighted demeaning versus naive is -0.0062 [-0.0084, -0.0003], while top-5 and top-20 intervals include zero. The six-contrast family-wise top-10 interval [-0.0136, 0.0013] includes zero. An outcome-free disjoint randomized-reference split also leaves all six nominal contrasts covering zero. A known-truth stress test shows incomplete improvement under a mean-additive positive control and failure under item mix and weak overlap. All decompositions are descriptive and each result is conditional on its overlap cohort. KuaiRand intervals quantify user-cluster variation conditional on the realized catalog and calendar; we claim neither intrinsic category effects nor a universally valid correction.

</details>


### [20] [Dataset Recommendation for Heterogeneous and Dynamic Scholarly Graphs via Multimodal Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05071)
*Ornella Irrera,Matteo Lissandrini,Daniele Dell'Aglio,Gianmaria Silvello*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出多模态学术注意力网络 MM-SAN，一种面向真实学术知识图谱的异质多模态图表示学习方法，用拓扑感知与文本嵌入相结合实现论文到数据集的推荐，并支持转导、半归纳与全归纳三种设定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据规模激增使研究者难以找到相关数据集；人工检索耗时且难以发现无直接链接、不受关注度指标青睐的有价值数据集。开放学术知识图谱虽按学术成果组织知识，但数据集之间缺乏有意义连接，数据集节点孤立、未被充分利用，且图谱存在元数据缺失或不准确、记录重复、数据集整合薄弱等问题，使数据集推荐仍具挑战性。

Method: MM-SAN（Multimodal Scholarly Attention Network）：异质多模态图表示学习方法，融合拓扑感知嵌入与基于文本的嵌入，在文本元数据有限或缺失时仍可有效推荐；可在转导、半归纳、全归纳三种设定下运行，适应演化中的 SKG 而无需重训练。

Result: 实验表明 MM-SAN 优于现有最先进方法，为数据集推荐提供了稳健且可扩展的解决方案。

Conclusion: MM-SAN 通过多模态异质图学习有效缓解 SKG 中数据集孤立与元数据不完整的问题，兼顾可扩展性与对动态图谱的适应性，是面向真实学术知识图谱的数据集推荐可行方案。

Abstract: Researchers struggle to find relevant datasets amid the growing volume of data across repositories and scholarly publications. Manual dataset retrieval is time-consuming and often fails to identify valuable resources that are not easily discoverable through direct links or popularity metrics. Dataset recommendation, which aims to identify datasets relevant to a paper, author, or query, has become essential for improving dataset discoverability and reuse. However, this task remains challenging because Scholarly Knowledge Graphs (SKGs) are large-scale, heterogeneous, dynamic, and incomplete. Although openly available SKGs organize scientific knowledge by linking scholarly outcomes, they often lack meaningful connections between datasets, leaving them isolated and underutilized. These graphs contain millions of evolving relations but still suffer from missing or inaccurate metadata, duplicate records, and weak dataset integration. This work presents the Multimodal Scholarly Attention Network (MM-SAN), a heterogeneous multimodal graph representation learning method for recommending datasets relevant to research publications within real-world SKGs. MM-SAN combines topology-aware and text-based embeddings, enabling effective recommendations even when textual metadata is limited or absent. It operates in transductive, semi-inductive, and fully inductive settings, adapting to evolving SKGs without requiring retraining. Experiments show that MM-SAN outperforms state-of-the-art methods, providing a robust and scalable solution for dataset recommendation.

</details>


### [21] [HARP: Training-Free Dual-Profile Agentic Communication for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05319)
*Zhen Tao,Xun Zhou,Xuhui Chen,Ziyue Qiao,Qingqiang Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 将LLM推荐重构为"智能体通信"四阶段框架，识别出单向BUILD、提示受限UNDERSTAND、缺失SYNC三大通信缺陷，并提出免训练三算子方案HARP补齐这些环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐中记忆机制存在用户-物品不对称：仅用户侧有结构化画像；画像信号只进入带宽受限的重排器提示而未参与检索；用户画像在预热后被冻结且缺乏有原则的更新规则。

Method: 把LLM推荐形式化为BUILD（自描述构建）→TRANSMIT（沿协同图传输）→UNDERSTAND（理解对方）→SYNC（从下次交互同步）四阶段。HARP作为免训练、冻结LLM骨干上的三算子增量：(1)规则抽取的对称物品画像，零额外LLM调用；(2)通道可分解的画像级同质性H(phi_u, phi_i)，在检索时而非重排提示内消费；(3)单遍反思式上下文内画像演化，无梯度更新。

Result: 在Yelp、Books、MovieTV、Goodreads四个公开基准上，Hit@1较最强已发表基线提升6.9%–24.0%，各数据集Holm校正后p<1e-9。消融与逐案例归因显示三算子互补，检索侧门控为主要增益来源，并报告了反思式更新带来的权衡。

Conclusion: 提出智能体通信式问题形式化，并给出免训练可落地的HARP实例；代码开源。

Abstract: In LLM-based recommendation, memory acts as a user-level agent in a memory-augmented backbone. Yet users and items remain asymmetric: only the user is given a structured profile; it enters a bandwidth-limited reranker prompt; and it is frozen after warmup. We recast LLM-based recommendation as an agentic-communication process in four stages: BUILD a self-description, TRANSMIT it along the collaborative graph, UNDERSTAND the other side, and SYNC it from the next interaction. This formulation reveals three unresolved communication challenges: the item side never produces a comparable self-description (one-sided BUILD); profile-level signals enter only the bandwidth-limited reranker prompt rather than retrieval, where they would not compete for context tokens (prompt-trapped UNDERSTAND); and the user's description is frozen after warmup with no principled update rule (absent SYNC). We instantiate the missing stages as HARP (Homophily-aware Agentic Recommendation via dual Profiles), a training-free, three-operator addition to a frozen LLM backbone: a rule-extracted symmetric item profile (zero extra LLM calls), a channel-decomposable profile-level homophily H(phi_u, phi_i) consumed at retrieval time rather than inside the reranker prompt, and a single-pass reflective update realising in-context profile evolution without any gradient step. On four public benchmarks (Yelp, Books, MovieTV, Goodreads), HARP lifts Hit@1 over the strongest published baseline by 6.9%-24.0%, with family-wise Holm-corrected p < 1e-9 on every dataset. Ablations and a per-case attribution study show that the operators contribute complementary gains, with the retrieval-side gate as the main source; we also report the trade-off introduced by the reflective pass. We formulate agentic communication and provide a training-free instantiation. Code is available at https://github.com/Jove-Zhen/HARP.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [22] [Alignment Faking Depends on the Value, Not Just the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03650)
*Manodeep Ray,Vivek Srivastava,Shirish Karande,Murari Mandal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"价值依赖性对齐伪装"视角，构建VALET框架，在32个ValueBench价值维度与10个模型上按bare/unmonitored/monitored三条件量化对齐伪装，发现合规差距0.05–0.50且主要变异来自模型–价值配对交互，并用六类推理轨迹分类刻画行为转变，最终输出value cards。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅在有害内容目标与少数道德基础价值上证实Alignment Faking（被感知监控时遵从、无监控时放弃指令），尚不清楚该现象在更广价值空间中的分布规律。作者主张对齐伪装是模型–价值配对的属性而非模型固有属性，需逐价值系统性刻画。

Method: 提出逐价值profiling框架，对每个模型–价值配对在三种条件下评测：bare（无系统提示）、unmonitored（无监控）、monitored（监控），以条件间合规率差异度量alignment-faking gap。覆盖32个ValueBench维度、10个开源与闭源模型（8B–约675B）。对推理轨迹做六类分类以追踪行为转变来源，并将结果封装为value cards。

Result: 合规差距跨度0.05–0.50；蒸馏70B模型在部分价值上优于约675B模型。结构方差大部分归因于"哪个模型被问及哪个价值"的交互项，而非模型或价值的单独主效应。推理轨迹分类显示：监控条件下训练信号推理占主导，无监控条件下价值冲突推理占主导。

Conclusion: 对齐伪装具有价值依赖性，必须以模型–价值配对为单位刻画；单一模型层面的安全结论不足以迁移至具体价值维度。value cards将行为倾向纳入模型卡，为细粒度行为披露与评测提供可复用的开源工具（代码与数据见VALET仓库）。

Abstract: Language models can follow a directive when they believe they are monitored and abandon it when they believe they are not, a pattern termed Alignment Faking. Prior work establishes this for harmful-content objectives and a handful of moral-foundations values, leaving open how it distributes across a wider value space. We argue it is value-dependent: whether compliance survives a change in perceived oversight is a property of the model--value pairing, not of the model alone. Our framework profiles Alignment Faking per value, scoring each model-value pair under three conditions: bare (no system prompt), unmonitored, and monitored. On 32 ValueBench dimensions across ten open- and closed-weight models (8B-~675B), compliance gaps span 0.05-0.50, with a distilled 70B above a ~675B model. We observe that majority of the structural variance depends on which model was asked about which value and not on the model or value alone. A six-category taxonomy over reasoning traces tracks the behavioural shift: training-signal reasoning dominates under monitoring and value-conflict reasoning dominates when unmonitored. We package per-model results as value cards, extending model cards with behavioral disposition. Code and data are available at https://github.com/respailab/VALET.

</details>


### [23] [CARD: A Proof-Carrying Certificate-to-Action Interface for Relative-Measurement Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03651)
*Yushan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出CARD——一种"证明携带（proof-carrying）"接口，严格区分几何证书、物理变换与服务决策三类对象并记录其组合方式，将证书语义、物理执行、证明获取与图计算通过显式系统对象连接起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何证书只对特定估计器对象给出数学断言，而机器人行动需要物理变换、服务决策与及时的证明支撑，二者对象不同却常被混同。作者需要一个接口将语义运动与见证修复分离，并刻画投影代价与证明携带代价何时一致、何时能保证可执行的有用服务。

Method: 提出lifted-fiber形式化：区分语义运动与witness repair，给出投影代价与证明携带代价一致的条件；推导精确的有用服务律——无放回均匀抽样产生超几何产出分布，从合格总体抽样则在活体验证条件下每次请求产生一次有用服务。实证基于AA15-X500空中测绘系统20架次的404个Full16案例，并做配对因果导航变换比较、五档匹配请求预算实验，及11个KITTI/EuRoC/TUM SLAM案例的公开下游评估。

Result: 404个经检查器确认的优化器点全部reference-discrepant（旋转/平移中位数1.9891°与21.6322 m），而配对因果导航变换全部reference-consistent（0.0036566°与0.0104519 m）。五档预算下超几何预测与普通随机均值最大差0.8412次服务；普通匹配随机达0/50目标成功，actionability匹配随机达50/50并与CARD策略一致。SLAM案例中随机与CARD图干预的优化器墙钟时间比为1.128x–67.65x，原生求解器记录显示差异源于消元填充、Cholesky因子规模与数值分解。

Conclusion: CARD通过显式区分证书对象、物理变换与服务决策并提供可执行的actionability保证，使证明携带接口在真实航空与SLAM场景中兼顾正确性与计算效率，从而将证书语义、物理执行、证明获取与图计算统一到同一系统框架中。

Abstract: Geometric certificates make mathematical claims about specified estimator objects, while robotic action requires a physical transform, a service decision, and timely proof support. We present CARD, a proof-carrying interface that keeps these objects distinct and records how they combine. Its lifted-fiber formulation separates semantic motion from witness repair and characterizes when projected and proof-carrying costs coincide. Its actionability result gives an exact useful-service law: uniform sampling without replacement produces a hypergeometric yield, while sampling from the eligible population gives one useful service per request under the observed live-confirmation condition. The primary study contains 404 Full16 cases from 20 sorties of an AA15-X500 airborne survey system. All 404 checker-confirmed optimizer points are reference-discrepant, with median rotation and translation discrepancies of 1.9891 degrees and 21.6322 m. The paired causal-navigation transforms are reference-consistent in all 404 cases, with medians of 0.0036566 degrees and 0.0104519 m. Across five matched request budgets, the largest difference between the hypergeometric prediction and the observed ordinary-random mean is 0.8412 services. Ordinary matched random reaches 0/50 target successes, while actionability-matched random reaches 50/50 and matches the CARD policy. Public downstream evaluation on 11 KITTI, EuRoC, and TUM SLAM cases measures optimizer wall-time ratios of 1.128x-67.65x for random versus CARD graph interventions. Native solver records locate this difference in elimination fill, Cholesky factor size, and numeric factorization. Together, these results connect certificate semantics, physical execution, proof acquisition, and graph computation through explicit system objects.

</details>


### [24] [MCPGuard: Finding Conformance Gaps in MCP SDKs via Dual-Track Analysis and Dynamic Adjudication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03662)
*Xikai Sun,Kebin Liu,Fan Dang,Yawen Zheng,Zihao Yang,Yunhao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MCPGuard：基于LLM的MCP SDK规范一致性检测框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 官方SDK实现了消息校验与会话逻辑，但可能遗漏规范约束并传播至下游应用，因此SDK级一致性测试至关重要；然而现有工具主要测试运行中的端点，难以发现SDK模型是否真正落实规范要求。

Method: 双轨设计：(1) 结构轨——将JSON Schema约束与归一化的SDK模型对齐，产出约束级证据；(2) 行为轨——抽取规范性自然语言规则，检索有界的函数级代码切片，交由LLM审计候选遗漏；随后由证据感知的动态裁决器检查完整源码上下文并构造PoC验证。共享内核+语言适配器支持跨SDK分析。

Result: 在官方Python 1.26.0与TypeScript 1.29.0 SDK上分别确认13项与19项缺陷，两个黑盒基线均未发现其中任何一项；在开源MCP服务器上的测试进一步表明这些缺口会系统性地向下游传播。

Conclusion: MCPGuard使规范约束在SDK层面的一致性可直接测试，与传统面向端点的一致性测试套件形成互补。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) connects large language model agents to external tools and services through official SDKs that implement message validation and session logic. Specification constraints omitted by these SDKs may propagate to downstream applications, highlighting the importance of SDK-level conformance testing. Yet existing MCP conformance tools mainly exercise running endpoints and reveal little about whether SDK models enforce specification requirements. We present MCPGuard, an automated LLM-based framework for detecting specification-to-SDK conformance gaps. Its structural track aligns JSON Schema constraints with normalized SDK models and produces constraint-specific evidence. Its behavioral track extracts normative prose rules, retrieves bounded function-level slices, and audits them with LLMs for candidate omissions. An evidence-aware dynamic adjudicator then inspects complete source context and builds PoCs to validate candidates. A shared kernel with language-specific adapters enables analysis across SDKs. On the official Python 1.26.0 and TypeScript 1.29.0 SDKs, MCPGuard confirms 13 and 19 findings, respectively, none of which are exposed by two black-box baselines. Tests on open MCP servers further show that these gaps can systematically propagate downstream. By making SDK conformance of specification constraints directly testable, MCPGuard complements conventional endpoint-oriented conformance suites.

</details>


### [25] [Functional-Group Knowledge Is Structured in Language Models Before It Is Spoken](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03684)
*Tran Nhat Khang Nguyen,Anh Nguyen,Obed Issakah,Janghoon Ock*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过读取五个开源模型的内部表示，发现模型在未经化学训练时就已按化学家族组织官能团，并以一致方向编码化学差异；这种结构在中层突然涌现，且化学微调使官能团而非提示措辞主导表示。仅凭输出评估会低估模型编码的化学信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型是化学与材料科学智能体的推理核心，需先理解分子结构。但输出只反映“说了什么”，无法区分是缺乏知识还是表达失败（如把1-氟丁烷的官能团答成炔基）。作者希望用内部表示分析来区分这两种情况。

Method: 针对覆盖92个分子、20个官能团的提示，读取五个开源模型（含base模型）的内部表示，分析官能团在表示空间中的组织方式、编码方向及随层数的变化，并比较化学微调前后的表示结构。

Result: 即使无化学训练，模型也按化学家族组织官能团，并将化学差异编码为一致方向（如硫醚→硫醇的位移最接近醚→醇）。该结构在中层突然涌现，表示从跟踪提示措辞转向跟踪官能团。base模型正确率最低（58.5%），但仍编码官能团家族与化学上有意义的模式，其错误多为“未作答”而非“答错”。

Conclusion: 官能团知识在被表达之前就已在模型中有结构地存在。化学微调会重组该结构，使官能团主导表示。仅以输出评估化学语言模型会低估其编码的化学信息；表示分析可区分知识缺失与表达失败，对为化学智能体选择或适配模型具有重要意义。

Abstract: Language models serve as the reasoning core of agents for chemistry and materials science, where they must interpret molecular structure before planning syntheses or proposing materials. Yet their outputs show only what they say, not what they know. Given a prompt such as ``The primary functional group in 1-Fluorobutane (CCCCF) is'', a small general-purpose model may answer ``the alkyne group'' instead of an alkyl fluoride. The output alone cannot tell whether the model failed to recognize the fluorine or recognized it and failed to say so. We address this by reading the internal representations of five open-source models on prompts covering 92 molecules and 20 functional groups. Even without chemistry training, the models organize functional groups by chemical family, and they encode chemical differences as consistent directions. The shift from thioether ($\mathrm{R{-}S{-}R'}$) to thiol ($\mathrm{R{-}SH}$), added to ether ($\mathrm{R{-}O{-}R'}$), lands nearest alcohol ($\mathrm{R{-}OH}$). This structure emerges abruptly in the middle layers, where representations shift from tracking the wording of the prompt to tracking the functional group. The base model names the correct functional group in only 58.5\% of prompts, the lowest of the five, yet it encodes functional-group families and preserves chemically meaningful patterns. Its errors are mostly failures to answer rather than wrong answers. Functional-group knowledge is structured in the model before it is spoken. Chemistry fine-tuning reorganizes it, making functional groups rather than prompt wording dominate the representation. These results suggest that evaluating chemical language models through outputs alone can underestimate the chemical information they encode. Representation analysis can distinguish a lack of knowledge from a failure to express it, which matters when choosing or adapting models for chemical agents.

</details>


### [26] [LOLPlay: Benchmarking Real-Time Visual Agents in League of Legends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03685)
*Zhaojian Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出原生《英雄联盟》基准LOLPlay（36个英雄特定任务，涵盖资源管理、战斗、协作与目标），通过截图与鼠标键盘交互，含自动化初始化与82项可执行子目标检查以区分部分进度与完成；评测三模型三档推理强度共324次，GPT-6 Astra任务成功率最高25.0%（低/中强度），子目标成功率最高40.3%（中强度），但高强度未带来提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉智能体需在推理过程中环境持续变化时协调推理与行动，现有基准缺乏真实实时环境下细粒度进度度量与推理强度—时效权衡的考察。

Method: 构建原生《英雄联盟》基准LOLPlay，含36个英雄特定任务，覆盖资源管理、战斗、协作和目标四类；智能体仅通过截图观测并以鼠标—键盘动作交互；提供自动化初始化消除人工干预，并设计82个可执行子目标检查以区分部分进度与真正完成；在三个模型、三档推理强度下进行324次任务评测，并分析轨迹与交互轮次。

Result: GPT-6 Astra在低、中推理强度下取得最高任务成功率25.0%，中等强度下取得最高子目标成功率40.3%；所有模型高强度均未优于低强度；Astra中更长的推理请求导致交互轮次减少，表明额外推理在实时控制中存在代价；轨迹显示子目标组合不完整，以及在观测场景变化后仍执行动作的问题。

Conclusion: 实时游戏环境揭示了当前视觉智能体在子目标分解与及时动作方面的不足，凸显推理深度与行动时效之间的张力，呼吁将视觉推理与及时行动相结合的智能体设计，并以细粒度子目标检查作为更可靠的进度评估手段。

Abstract: Visual agents need to coordinate reasoning and action while their environment evolves during inference. We introduce LOLPlay, a benchmark in native \emph{League of Legends} with 36 champion-specific tasks spanning resource management, combat, cooperation, and objectives. Agents interact through screenshots and mouse--keyboard actions; automated initialization and 82 executable subgoal checks distinguish partial progress from completion. Across 324 task evaluations of three models at three reasoning-effort settings, GPT-6 Astra achieves the highest task success rate of 25.0\% at low and medium effort, and the highest subgoal success rate of 40.3\% at medium effort. However, high effort improves neither metric over low effort for any model. For Astra, longer requests leave fewer interaction rounds, suggesting a cost to additional reasoning in real-time control. Trajectories reveal incomplete subgoal composition and actions executing after the observed scene changes, motivating agents that combine visual reasoning with timely action.

</details>


### [27] [Training LLM Agents to Automate LLM Post-Training with RSI-Dojo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03698)
*Shuzheng Gao,Zhaojian Yu,Penghao Yin,Yuling Shi,Kai Cai,Shilin He,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RSI-Dojo——一套可执行的模型训练环境集合，用于让LLM智能体学会执行后训练（post-training）；并配套提出自动构建流水线RSI-Craft，将人类研究论文自动转化为可执行任务，最终得到281个种子训练任务，并据此采集高质量轨迹微调出RSI-Dojo-9B/35B-A3B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归自我改进（RSI）的核心能力之一是让智能体自主训练与改进基础模型，但已有自动化模型训练研究多停留在工作流或harness层面的优化，未将后训练能力内化进模型本身；而内化该能力的关键瓶颈在于缺乏可执行、可产出高质量轨迹的模型训练环境。

Method: 1) RSI-Craft自动流水线：从顶会采集研究论文，将数据与指标实例化为统一接口，复现评测依赖，再按数据规模、执行可靠性与判别性筛选任务，产出含281个种子模型训练任务的RSI-Dojo；2) 基于RSI-Dojo，用改进的harness与轨迹采集方法从前沿模型采集训练轨迹，并通过基于评分表（rubric）与基于结果的双重过滤保留样本；3) 用这些轨迹微调Qwen3.5-9B与Qwen3.6-35B-A3B。

Result: RSI-Dojo-9B与RSI-Dojo-35B-A3B相对各自基座模型在PostTrainBench上分别提升8.74与16.87分；RSI-Dojo-35B-A3B取得PostTrainBench 29.95分、PostTrainBench-Lite 53.29分，与GLM-5.2、GPT 5.5等前沿模型持平。

Conclusion: 通过构建可执行训练环境并采集高质量后训练轨迹进行微调，可将后训练能力内化到中小规模模型中，使其在模型训练基准上达到前沿水平，为RSI方向提供了可复用的环境构建与能力蒸馏范式。

Abstract: Recursive Self-Improvement (RSI) aims to enable AI systems to autonomously build and improve AI systems themselves, including models, harnesses, and underlying infrastructure. Within this broader agenda, a central capability is enabling agents to train and improve base models. However, existing studies on automated model training primarily focused on workflow- or harness-level optimizations, rather than internalizing the ability to conduct post-training into the model itself. Developing this capability requires executable model training environments that can produce high-quality trajectories demonstrating how to select data, train models, diagnose failures, and iteratively improve results. In this paper, we introduce RSI-Dojo, a collection of executable model training environments designed to teach LLM agents to conduct post-training. To construct RSI-Dojo, we develop RSI-Craft, an automatic pipeline that converts human research artifacts into executable model training environments. RSI-Craft automatically collects research papers from top venues, instantiates datasets and metrics into a unified interface, and reproduces evaluation dependencies. It then filters tasks by data size, execution reliability, and discriminability, yielding RSI-Dojo with 281 seed model training tasks. Building on RSI-Dojo, we employ a trajectory collection method with an improved harness to collect high-quality model-training trajectories from frontier models, then retain examples through rubric-based and outcome-based filtering. We fine-tune Qwen3.5-9B and Qwen3.6-35B-A3B on these trajectories. The resulting RSI-Dojo-9B and RSI-Dojo-35B-A3B improve over their base models by 8.74 and 16.87 PostTrainBench points, respectively. RSI-Dojo-35B-A3B reaches 29.95 on PostTrainBench and 53.29 on PostTrainBench-Lite, matching the performance of frontier models such as GLM-5.2 and GPT 5.5.

</details>


### [28] [Unsatz: Evaluating Scientific Discovery Without a Given Ansatz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03713)
*Ethan Ji,Wei Wang,Yizhou Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Unsatz基准：让LLM智能体在预算内自主设计实验、自行构建方程/模型结构的"ansatz"，仅以对未见条件的预测表现评分；9个模型在80个系统中成功率21%–60%，主要困难在结构发现而非参数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动方程发现基准通常直接给定ansatz（变量、候选项库或待修正的已知定律），把最需要科学判断的"建模假设构建"环节剥离了；作者希望评估智能体能否像科学家一样自行决定模型中包含哪些量、哪些是隐变量、如何相互作用与测量。

Method: 每个任务是由私有随机种子生成的模拟系统，用小型建模语言编写，涵盖ODE、延迟微分方程、PDE、离散映射、静态与因果模型、随机过程，并可含隐状态、失真传感器与无信息测量通道。智能体在预算约束下购买实验数据，用同一语言写候选程序，由基准的fitter给出系数，最终仅按其对分布外实验（超出探索范围的条件、干预、更长时序）的预测能力评分；共80个系统、20个领域，评测9个语言模型，并额外提供"相关系统已发现程序"的迁移设置，以及44个系统上经典方程发现方法的对照。

Result: 9个LLM得分介于21%与60%；在44个系统上经典方程发现方法几乎从不成功，而若给定真实方程仅留未知系数，同样数据通常足够，说明瓶颈在于结构发现而非系数估计。跨系统复用已发现程序仅在机制相同且前次探索成功时有效。更强智能体犯的"非科学"错误更少；但普遍存在两类缺陷：能识别系统有哪些变量，却找不出它们如何作用；倾向于以降低训练误差为准则修改模型，而非以提升预测为目标。

Conclusion: 基准揭示了当前LLM智能体在自主科学建模上的关键短板：变量辨识尚可，机制/函数形式构建薄弱，且优化目标偏离预测泛化。作者据此主张将"ansatz构建"作为独立评测维度，并提示需改进智能体的假设生成与模型选择准则。

Abstract: Before a scientist fits any parameter, they must decide what the model contains: which quantities exist, which are hidden, how they interact, and how they are measured. Physicists call such a trial form an ansatz. Benchmarks for automated equation discovery mostly supply it, by fixing the variables, a library of candidate terms, or a law to be modified. We introduce Unsatz, a benchmark in which an LLM agent must construct the ansatz itself. Each task is a simulated system, generated from a private seed as a program in a small modeling language: ordinary, delay, or partial differential equations, discrete maps, static and causal models, or stochastic processes, possibly with hidden states, distorted sensors, and uninformative measurement channels. The agent buys experiments within a budget, writes candidate programs in the same language, obtains their coefficients from the benchmark's fitter, and is scored only on how its final program predicts experiments it never saw: conditions outside the range it could explore, interventions, and longer runs. Across 80 systems in 20 domains, nine language models solve between 21% and 60%. On 44 of these systems, classical equation-discovery methods almost never succeed, although the same data usually suffice once the true equations are given with unknown coefficients; the difficulty lies in finding the structure, not in estimating it. Because mechanisms recur across systems, we also give agents the program they discovered for a related system; it helped only when the new system shared the mechanism and the first investigation had succeeded. Stronger agents make fewer unscientific errors; beyond these, agents find which variables a system has but not how they act, and prefer revisions that lower the training error to revisions that predict better.

</details>


### [29] [Counterexample-driven autonomous research with application to excess radiated energy in a Z-pinch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03721)
*Bowen Zhu,Jian Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出开源框架 Simjecture，让语言模型智能体围绕人类设定的物理猜想开展研究，以反例驱动的证伪与假设修复为核心逻辑、以计算物理作为可执行检验台，并在 82.6 小时的研究中应用于 Z-pinch 中辐射能量超过内爆壳动能这一长期问题，揭示能量转换、滞留与释放时序的竞争机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 辅助数学已显示开放式机器搜索可超越人类数学家，但将该范式迁移到物理需重构研究流程：物理是经验性的、模型无终局，且理论必须与真实世界交互，因此需要新的研究组织方式。

Method: Simjecture 框架：语言模型智能体追寻人类定义的物理猜想；以反例驱动的证伪与前瞻性假设修复组织探究；以计算物理作为可执行检验台，并将"数值工具的资格认定"与"科学主张评审"分离，使求解器与诊断工具可在被记录的研究过程内自适应调整。随后在 82.6 小时的研究中针对 Z-pinch 中辐射能量超过内爆壳动能的问题开展调查。

Result: 揭示能量转换、滞留与释放时序的竞争效应：更强的相干螺旋形变提高电阻性转换，同时降低辐射并增加滞留能量；更宽的尾随等离子体剖面推迟后段辐射峰值并弥补早期辐射热计测量的大部分亏缺，且谱响应在脉冲期间发生变化。

Conclusion: 结果为高能量密度等离子体物理中的能量转换提供新认识，并展示了物理学研究的一种新范式。

Abstract: Recent breakthroughs in artificial-intelligence-assisted mathematics show that open-ended machine search can outperform human mathematicians, yet transferring this paradigm to physics requires refactoring the research workflow. We introduce Simjecture, an open-source framework in which language-model agents pursue human-defined physical conjectures. The framework tackles two central challenges in this transfer: first, because physics is empirical and no model is final, it structures inquiry around counterexample-driven falsification and prospective hypothesis repair; second, because physical theories must interact with the real world, it uses computational physics as an executable testbed while separating the qualification of numerical instruments from scientific claim review so solvers and diagnostics can be adapted within a recorded investigation. We apply the framework in an 82.6 h investigation to tackle the long-standing problem of radiated energy exceeding the imploding-shell kinetic energy in a Z-pinch. The investigation reveals competing effects of energy conversion, retention, and release timing. Stronger coherent helical deformation increases resistive conversion while reducing radiation and increasing retained energy. A broader trailing-plasma profile delays the later radiation maximum and recovers much of its early bolometric deficit, with a spectral response that changes during the pulse. These results provide new insight into energy conversion in high-energy-density plasma physics and demonstrate a new paradigm for physics research.

</details>


### [30] [EMBERS: Empirically Motivated Robustness Testing of Deep Research Systems via Defect-Guided Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03723)
*Shuochuan Li,Zan Wang,Dong Wang,Yuhang Jiang,Huajin Cui,Chen Zhang,Gang Wang,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对1932条深度研究(DR)系统执行轨迹开展实证研究，归纳出九类跨子问题生成、在线搜索与最终推理阶段的反复出现缺陷，并据此提出缺陷引导的模糊测试框架EMBERS，用于DR系统鲁棒性测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DR系统将大模型扩展为自主智能体，多阶段执行（分解信息需求、迭代检索、证据综合）可能带来鲁棒性风险：用户问题或中间检索输入的细微变化会跨阶段传播并导致错误答案。现有评测多关注任务求解能力或对外部证据扰动的鲁棒性，对DR执行过程的内在脆弱性探索不足。

Method: 1) 对来自3个代表性DR系统的1,932条执行轨迹进行实证分析，识别缺陷类别；2) 提出EMBERS框架：以正确回答的种子问题为起点，采用缺陷引导变异、网络搜索辅助的保真性保障、多样性感知的种子维护，生成面向特定阶段的测试输入。

Result: 在16种"框架—模型—数据集"设置中，EMBERS在相同测试预算下的失败发现吞吐量达到最强基线的3.21倍；人工与LLM辅助评估表明多数生成输入保留了原始信息需求与必要上下文约束；模型微调实验显示一致回答（consistent-answer）性能稳定提升。

Conclusion: EMBERS能有效暴露DR系统的内在执行缺陷，且生成的高质量对抗输入不仅可用于鲁棒性测试，还可作为数据用于模型改进。

Abstract: Deep Research (DR) systems extend large language models into autonomous agents that decompose complex information needs, iteratively search the web, and synthesize evidence to answer challenging questions. However, their multi-stage execution may introduce robustness risks, as variations in user questions or intermediate search inputs may propagate across research stages and lead to incorrect answers. Existing evaluations focus on task-solving capabilities or robustness against external evidence disturbances, leaving intrinsic weaknesses in DR execution insufficiently explored. Thus, we conduct an empirical study of 1,932 execution trajectories from three representative DR systems and identify nine recurring defect categories across subquestion formulation, online search, and final reasoning. Guided by these findings, we propose EMBERS, an empirically motivated, defect-guided fuzzing framework for robustness testing of DR systems. Starting from correctly answered seed questions, EMBERS generates stage-targeted test inputs using defect-guided mutation, web-search-assisted fidelity assurance, and diversity-aware seed maintenance. Experiments across 16 framework--model--dataset settings show that EMBERS achieves 3.21 times the failure-discovery throughput of the strongest baseline under identical testing budgets. Human and LLM-assisted evaluations demonstrate that most generated inputs preserve the original information need and necessary contextual constraints. Moreover, model fine-tuning experiments show consistent-answer performance improvement, highlighting their value for robustness testing and model improvement.

</details>


### [31] [From Logs to Faults: Reference-Free Fault Localization for Retrieval-Augmented Generation Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03725)
*Hira Naveed,Scott Barnett,Stefanus Kurniawan,John Grundy,Chetan Arora,Hourieh Khalajzadeh,Omar Haggag*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RAGLog 利用执行日志在无需参考答案和代码插桩的情况下，跨 RAG 各阶段检测并定位故障；在自然故障运行上 F1 达 0.73，优于 Claude Code（0.58）与 Codex（0.52），单次分析约 5 秒、成本 0.005 美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 流水线广泛用于领域问答，但故障定位依赖人工且耗时。由于各阶段相互依赖，故障会向下游传播而掩盖源头，甚至在最终答案正确时仍未被发现；现有评估方法多针对单一阶段，且依赖参考答案或代码插桩。

Method: 提出 RAGLog：将异构 API 日志转换为统一的 RAG schema，并融合基于规则、基于嵌入和基于 LLM 的检查来识别故障阶段；同时构建实时故障注入器与 RAGLog Faults 数据集（5 个 RAG 流水线、3,134 次运行、带阶段级故障标签）。

Result: 基于 Claude Haiku 5.5，自然故障运行上 pooled F1 为 0.73（Claude Code 0.58、Codex 0.52）；注入故障运行上为 0.84（对比 0.80 与 0.82）。单次分析约 5 秒、约 0.005 美元，成本与延迟总体低于 agent 基线。

Conclusion: 日志驱动的多检查融合可在无参考答案、无插桩条件下有效定位 RAG 阶段级故障，且成本与延迟具优势；RAGLog 已作为开源工具发布，用于 RAG 流水线的调试、评估与监控。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipelines are widely used for domain-specific question answering, yet locating faults remains manual and time-consuming. Fault localization is challenging because RAG pipelines consist of interdependent stages, where faults can propagate downstream, masking their origin, or remain undetected even when the final answer is correct. Existing evaluation approaches often focus on individual stages and rely on reference answers or code instrumentation. We present RAGLog, which detects and localizes faults across RAG stages using execution logs, without requiring reference answers or code instrumentation. RAGLog transforms heterogeneous API logs into a common RAG schema and combines rule-based, embedding-based, and LLM-based checks to identify faulty stages. We introduce a live fault injector and RAGLog Faults, a dataset of 3,134 runs from five RAG pipelines with stage-level fault labels. With Claude Haiku 5.5, RAGLog achieves a pooled F1 score of 0.73 on runs with naturally occurring faults, outperforming Claude Code (0.58) and Codex (0.52), and comparable effectiveness on runs with injected faults (0.84 versus 0.80 and 0.82, respectively). It analyzes runs in approximately five seconds at US\$0.005 each, with overall lower cost and latency than agent baselines. RAGLog is available as an open-source tool for debugging, evaluating, and monitoring RAG pipelines.

</details>


### [32] [Reasoning is topologically simple](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03739)
*Chris Kapulkin,Nathan Kershaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出并检验一个假设：推理文本的共现拓扑比非推理文本更简单，且高质量推理的共现拓扑比低质量推理更简单，并探讨其在AI安全与AI评估中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望寻找一种可量化的文本结构特征来刻画"推理"这一属性，并借此服务于AI安全与AI评估，因此提出以共现拓扑复杂度作为推理文本的判别性信号。

Method: 从共现拓扑（co-occurrence topology）角度构建文本表示，比较推理文本与非推理文本、以及不同质量推理文本的拓扑复杂度，并设计相应的检验实验，进而将其应用于AI安全与AI评估场景。

Result: 摘要仅陈述假设的提出与检验，未给出具体数值或统计结论，因此无法从摘要中提取量化结果。

Conclusion: 推理文本可能具有更简单的共现拓扑结构，且拓扑简洁性可能与推理质量正相关，这一特征有望用于AI安全与AI评估。

Abstract: We formulate and investigate the hypothesis that reasoning text has simpler co-occurrence topology than non-reasoning text, and that higher-quality reasoning has simpler co-occurrence topology than lower-quality reasoning. We discuss and test applications to AI safety and AI evaluation.

</details>


### [33] [VagueBench: Benchmarking the Robustness of Embodied Agents on Ambient Context Illusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03765)
*Yang Qu,Qiang Fu,Zonghao Ying,Jiayi Zhao,Yaoyuan Zhang,Jinyang Guo,Jian Yang,Aishan Liu,Yingxun Wang,Xianglong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"环境上下文错觉"现象并构建VagueBench基准，揭示具身智能体在语义等价但上下文极简指令下的显著性能退化与对辅助语言信号的过度依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体鲁棒性评估偏重危险指令与越狱攻击，忽视了更基础的问题：智能体在语义充分但上下文极简（缺少非必要语境线索）的指令下是否仍能正确执行，而这类指令在真实人际交流中极为常见。

Method: 基于AI2-THOR仿真环境构建VagueBench基准；从实体、流程、约束、先验知识等语义成分出发，建立4大类9子类的统一分类体系以削减辅助信息；采用人机协同生成框架（语义冗余估计、分类引导式改写、严格语义不变性验证）构造1,303对指令；并在6个先进具身智能体及真实机器人系统上评估。

Result: 6个SOTA智能体在VagueBench上任务成功率相对下降19.65%，空计划率相对上升54.10%；该现象在真实机器人系统中同样存在，可导致错误决策与潜在安全风险。

Conclusion: 具身智能体依赖辅助语言线索而非核心任务语义；上下文极简指令应成为鲁棒性评估的关键维度，VagueBench为该方向提供系统化测试平台。

Abstract: Embodied agents are increasingly deployed in open-world environments, yet current robustness evaluations remain biased toward hazardous instructions and jailbreak vulnerabilities. A more fundamental issue is largely overlooked: agents are rarely tested under semantically sufficient but contextually minimal instructions (i.e., common in real-world human communication), where auxiliary contextual cues are absent.
