Result: 理论分析表明现有dissimulation方法存在显著隐私泄漏；所提simulation-based规划算法在提供期望奖励保证的前提下有效缓解了该泄漏。在多个基准问题上的实验显示，该方法在奖励函数隐私保护方面优于此前方法。

Conclusion: 在MDP序列规划中保护奖励隐私，应超越传统差分隐私/隐藏式(dissimulation)思路，转向主动欺骗(simulation)的规划策略；该思路可在不牺牲（并有保证地约束）期望奖励的条件下，更好地抵御基于IRL的观测者，实现隐私与效用的兼顾。

Abstract: Preserving the privacy of preferences (or rewards) of a sequential decision-making agent when decisions are observable is crucial in many physical and cybersecurity domains. For instance, in wildlife monitoring, agents must allocate patrolling resources without revealing animal locations to poachers. This paper addresses privacy preservation in planning over a sequence of actions in MDPs, where the reward function represents the preference structure to be protected. Observers can use Inverse RL (IRL) to learn these preferences, making this a challenging task.
  Current research on differential privacy in reward functions fails to ensure guarantee on the minimum expected reward and offers theoretical guarantees that are inadequate against IRL-based observers. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel approach rooted in the theory of deception. Deception includes two models: dissimulation (hiding the truth) and simulation (showing the wrong). Our first contribution theoretically demonstrates significant privacy leaks in existing dissimulation-based methods. Our second contribution is a novel RL-based planning algorithm that uses simulation to effectively address these privacy concerns while ensuring a guarantee on the expected reward. Experiments on multiple benchmark problems show that our approach outperforms previous methods in preserving reward function privacy.

</details>


### [138] [On Discovering Algorithms for Adversarial Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00922)
*Shashank Reddy Chirra,Jayden Teoh,Praveen Paruchuri,Pradeep Varakantham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出对抗式模仿学习(AIL)中"奖励赋值(RA)"环节被长期忽视，提出用LLM引导的进化框架数据驱动地发现RA函数，得到首个元学习AIL算法DAIL，可跨环境与优化算法泛化并超越人工设计基线，并分析其训练更稳定的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIL在专家示范有限时有效但训练不稳定；现有研究多聚焦于密度比(DR)估计的改进，而将密度比转化为奖励信号的奖励赋值(RA)环节对训练动态与最终策略性能的影响被严重忽视。传统RA函数源于散度最小化目标，高度依赖人工设计与直觉，缺乏基于策略实际表现的数据驱动设计。

Method: 将AIL分解为密度比估计ρ_E/ρ_π（判别器估计）与奖励赋值RA（将比值变换为奖励）两部分；固定DR部分，转而以最终模仿策略的性能为直接目标，搜索RA函数空间。具体采用LLM引导的进化框架高效探索RA函数空间，元学习得到RA函数，形成DAIL算法。

Result: DAIL在未见过的环境和不同策略优化算法上均能泛化，性能超越当前最先进的人工设计基线；论文进一步分析了DAIL为何带来更稳定的训练，给出了关于RA函数在AIL稳定性中作用的新见解。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 奖励赋值是影响AIL训练稳定性与性能的关键但被忽视的环节；以策略表现为目标、借助LLM引导进化进行数据驱动的RA函数发现是可行且有效的，DAIL作为首个元学习AIL算法展现出良好泛化性与稳定性，并为理解AIL稳定性机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: Adversarial Imitation Learning (AIL) methods, while effective in settings with limited expert demonstrations, are often considered unstable. These approaches typically decompose into two components: Density Ratio (DR) estimation $\frac{ρ_E}{ρ_π}$, where a discriminator estimates the relative occupancy of state-action pairs under the policy versus the expert; and Reward Assignment (RA), where this ratio is transformed into a reward signal used to train the policy. While significant research has focused on improving density estimation, the role of reward assignment in influencing training dynamics and final policy performance has been largely overlooked. RA functions in AIL are typically derived from divergence minimization objectives, relying heavily on human design and ingenuity. In this work, we take a different approach: we investigate the discovery of data-driven RA functions, i.e, based directly on the performance of the resulting imitation policy. To this end, we leverage an LLM-guided evolutionary framework that efficiently explores the space of RA functions, yielding \emph{Discovered Adversarial Imitation Learning} (DAIL), the first meta-learnt AIL algorithm. Remarkably, DAIL generalises across unseen environments and policy optimization algorithms, outperforming the current state-of-the-art of \emph{human-designed} baselines. Finally, we analyse why DAIL leads to more stable training, offering novel insights into the role of RA functions in the stability of AIL. Code is publicly available: https://github.com/shshnkreddy/DAIL.

</details>


### [139] [A Year in LLM Serving: Workload Evolution, Caching and Load-Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13573)
*William Nixon,Jon Durbin,Florian Standhartinger,Haryadi S. Gunawi,Juncheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文基于CompanyX一年期生产trace，对真实LLM serving工作负载进行全局刻画与纵向研究，并公开完整trace以支持后续研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM serving工作负载研究在规模与范围上受限，通常只观察短时间段，对生产中用户与模型交互的可见性不足，难以反映负载随时间的演化以及用户-模型交互如何塑造实际流量。

Method: 对来自CompanyX的为期一年的生产trace进行全局刻画与纵向分析，覆盖多个模型与用户（含热门模型与长尾模型），从聚合层面、时间层面、模型层面和用户层面四个视角剖析负载。

Result: 揭示了常被聚合视角掩盖的工作负载演化规律与用户-模型结构特征；并公开释放完整的一年期trace。

Conclusion: 该工作深化了对真实LLM serving工作负载的理解，为serving系统的动机论证与基准测试提供了无需依赖采样或合成负载的真实生产数据基础。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) serving has become a critical cloud workload, and realistic traces are essential for motivating and benchmarking serving systems. However, existing LLM serving workload studies remain limited in scale and scope. They often observe short time periods and provide limited visibility into how users interact with models in production. As a result, they do not fully capture how LLM serving workloads evolve over time or how user-model interactions shape production traffic. In this work, we further the understanding of real-world LLM serving workloads through both a global characterization and a longitudinal study of a one-year production trace from CompanyX. Unlike prior studies, our trace captures full production behavior across many models and users, including both popular and long-tail models. We analyze the workload from aggregate, temporal, model-level, and user-level perspectives, revealing workload evolution and user-model structure that are typically hidden behind aggregate views. To support future research, we publicly release the full one-year trace, enabling downstream studies of production behavior without relying on sampled or synthetically generated workloads. The trace is available at https://github.com/HarvardMadSys/chutes_workload.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [Latent Cluster Separation as a Regularizer for Lifelong Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03652)
*Sabarish Radhakrishnan,Nathalie Japkowicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文解耦了终身异常检测中"潜在聚类正则化"与"基于质心距离的打分"两种设计，发现前者作为训练期正则即可提升检测质量与后向迁移，而后者才是性能不稳定性的主要来源，并给出选择指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在终身（持续）异常检测中，提升后向迁移的常见做法是把模型内部表征重塑到可学习的概念质心上，并配套使用基于质心距离的异常打分。由于这两种设计通常同时引入，无法判断性能增益究竟来自哪一方，因此需要解耦分析。

Method: 在基于VAE的终身异常检测器中，将"潜在聚类分离"与"距离打分"两个机制拆开，分别单独研究：将聚类分离仅作为训练期正则项而保持原异常打分不变，并单独考察改用距离打分带来的影响。

Result: 仅作为训练期正则使用时，潜在聚类分离即可提升检测质量与后向迁移；但能否优于替代打分方法取决于数据集的内在表征结构。改用距离打分则是该类方法中主要不稳定性来源，论文识别并解释了其内在机制。

Conclusion: 两种设计选择的收益依赖于数据集的内在表征；论文将其作为在两者之间做取舍的实用指导，而非单纯罗列注意事项。

Abstract: Lifelong (continual) anomaly detection systems must learn a sequence of tasks without forgetting how to recognize anomaly types seen earlier -- a property known as backward transfer. A common way to try to improve backward transfer is to reshape a model's internal representation around learnable concept centroids, typically coupled with a new way of scoring anomalies based on distance to those centroids. Because these two design choices are usually introduced together, it is unclear which one is actually responsible for any performance gain. In this paper, we unbundle the two mechanisms within a VAE-based lifelong anomaly detector and study them separately. We find that used purely as a training-time regularizer -- while leaving anomaly scoring unchanged -- latent cluster separation can improve detection quality and backward transfer, though whether it outperforms the alternative scoring approach depends on the dataset's underlying representation. Switching to distance-based scoring is instead where most of the instability commonly associated with this class of method actually originates; we identify and explain the underlying mechanism. This benefit depends on the dataset's underlying representation, and we report this as practical guidance for choosing between the two design choices, rather than as a list of caveats.

</details>


### [141] [Four Extensions of the Universal Law of Robustness: Log-Concave Covariates, Heterogeneous Mixtures, Vector-Valued Predictors, and Markovian Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03653)
*Tobias J. Peter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文推广Bubeck–Sellke“通用鲁棒性定律”，将等周型协变量假设放宽为指数集中、混合分布、向量输出与马氏相关样本，在统一主定理下给出显式常数与容量“预热”项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bubeck–Sellke的通用鲁棒性定律刻画：以p个实参数拟合n个噪声水平以下的带噪样本，当协变量分布满足等周性质时，Lipschitz常数被迫达到√(nd/p)量级，即光滑插值需付出d倍过参数化代价。但其假设较强——高斯型集中、同质协变量律、标量输出、独立样本——限制了适用范围，本文意在逐条放松这些假设。

Method: 采用统一的“主定理”（master theorem）承载共同论证结构：(1) 将等周假设弱化为指数集中，速率与绝对常数不变，代价是引入容量“预热”（burn-in）项，并证明某种形式的预热是必要的；(2) 异质混合分布中，以各分量常数的加权平均替代最坏分量常数，需混合组成的“典型性”条件；(3) 标量输出推广到向量输出，Frobenius Lipschitz尺度下的界与输出维度K无关，Euclidean尺度下仅损失K^{-1/2}，且协变量无关噪声可消除该损失；(4) 独立样本推广到平稳马氏协变量，i.i.d.速率保持，伪谱隙仅进入样本量条件。

Result: 指数集中条件下保持原速率与绝对常数，并给出容量预热必要性的证明，从而得到归一化对数凹协变量下的定律（相对当前KLS界仅损失log d）；混合情形下界由加权平均常数控制；向量输出的Frobenius界不依赖K，Euclidean界仅损失K^{-1/2}（协变量无关噪声下无损失）；平稳马氏情形保持i.i.d.速率，伪谱隙只影响样本量要求。全文常数均为显式。

Conclusion: 通用鲁棒性定律在更弱的集中性、非齐次混合、多维输出与相依采样下依然成立，本质机制由单一主定理统一刻画；显式常数与必要的容量预热项揭示了该现象的普适性及其代价边界。

Abstract: Bubeck and Sellke's universal law of robustness says that fitting $n$ noisy samples below the noise level with a class of $p$ real parameters forces a Lipschitz constant of order $\sqrt{nd/p}$, when the covariate distribution on $\mathbb{R}^d$ is isoperimetric: smooth interpolation costs a factor $d$ of overparametrization. Its hypotheses are Gaussian-type concentration, a homogeneous covariate law, scalar outputs, and independent samples; we relax each one. Exponential concentration suffices, at the unchanged rate and absolute constant and at the price of a capacity burn-in, some form of which we show is necessary; this gives the law for normalized log-concave covariates, with a $\log d$ loss from the current KLS bound. For heterogeneous mixtures the worst component constant is replaced by the weighted average of the component constants, under a typicality condition on the mixture composition. For vector-valued predictors the bound at the Frobenius Lipschitz scale is free of the output dimension $K$, and the Euclidean bound loses only $K^{-1/2}$, a loss that covariate-independent noise removes. Stationary Markov covariates retain the i.i.d. rate, the pseudo-spectral gap entering only the sample-size conditions. One master theorem carries the shared argument, and constants are explicit throughout.

</details>


### [142] [Towards Automatic Heart Murmur Characterization from Multi-spot Phonocardiograms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03654)
*Marcelo Nogueira,Jorge H. Oliveira,Carlos G. Ferreira,Miguel T. Coimbra,Alípio M. Jorge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将心脏杂音特征化建模为五个患者级分类任务，比较三种多听诊点策略，验证多听诊点杂音特征化的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动心音图分析多止步于二分类杂音检测，未估计时机、形态、音调、音质、分级等更丰富的临床描述信息，缺乏结构化杂音报告能力。

Method: 基于CirCor DigiScope中735例具备完整四听诊点（主动脉、肺动脉、二尖瓣、三尖瓣）记录的患者，构建五项患者级分类任务，比较三种多听诊点策略：①各听诊点独立训练传统与深度学习分类器并经最大严重度晚期融合；②同步多输入CNN逐任务处理各点代表片段；③统一多输入多输出CNN共享表征同时预测五个属性；并分析按类别平衡数据的影响。

Result: 最佳晚期融合配置在五项任务上取得85.56%–93.81%的非加权平均灵敏度，各类别召回率80.00%–100.00%；多输入CNN较晚期融合低7–16个百分点；多输出模型为72.59%–79.40%。朴素加入全部少数类片段进行平衡会因引入未被聚类片段筛选过滤的非代表性噪声片段，在最大严重度规则下降解患者级性能。

Conclusion: 多听诊点杂音特征化在融合式与联合学习架构下均可行，为在二分类筛查之外提供自动结构化杂音报告指明路径。

Abstract: Cardiac auscultation traditionally detects the presence of a heart murmur, but clinicians further characterize it by timing, shape, pitch, quality, and grade. Most automatic phonocardiogram analysis systems stop at binary detection and do not estimate this richer description. In this work, we formulate automatic murmur characterization as five patient-level classification tasks and compare three multi-spot strategies on recordings from the aortic, pulmonary, mitral, and tricuspid auscultation locations. First, classical and deep-learning classifiers are trained independently at each location, and predictions are combined via a maximum-severity late-fusion rule. Second, a synchronous multi-input CNN jointly processes one representative segment per location for a single task. Third, a unified multi-input, multi-output CNN predicts all five attributes from one shared multi-spot representation. Using 735 patients from the CirCor DigiScope dataset with complete four-spot recordings, the best late-fusion configurations reach an unweighted mean sensitivity between 85.56% and 93.81% across the five tasks, with class-specific recalls between 80.00% and 100.00% for every relevant category. The multi-input CNN trails late fusion by 7-16 points depending on the task, while the multi-output model reaches values between 72.59% and 79.40%, estimating all five attributes from one shared representation. We further show that naively balancing training data by adding all minority-class segments degrades patient-level performance under the maximum-severity rule, since it introduces non-representative, noisy segments not filtered by the clustering-based segment selection used for evaluation. These results indicate multi-spot murmur characterization is feasible with both fusion-based and jointly learned architectures, outlining a path toward automatic, structured murmur reports complementing binary screening.

</details>


### [143] [Mixture of Wide Experts: Sparse Routing for Interpretable Native Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03655)
*Tavish Mankash,Vardhaman Kalloli,Keshava Prasad,Deepan Muthirayan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Mixture of Wide Experts (MoWE)，一种让选中的专家读取完整表示但写入固定、互不重叠输出坐标的稀疏前馈层，使模型在保持预测性能的同时直接学到可解释的原生坐标，其可读性接近在稠密模型上训练后的 SAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型把同一概念分散在众多内部维度上，导致特征难以解释。现有做法是先训练再用 SAE 事后恢复较清晰的特征，作者转而探究模型能否在训练中直接学到可解释的输出坐标。

Method: 设计 MoWE 稀疏前馈层：被选中的专家读取完整隐表示，但写回固定且互不重叠的坐标子集。在隐藏原因已知的受控任务上验证定位能力；在约 19 层、算力匹配、10B token 的设置下与 dense 和标准 MoE 对比。用语言模型裁判基于强激活片段描述各坐标，并在新片段上测试，校正后得到特异性分数；同时在相同 FFN 更新上训练 TopK SAE 作对照。另做两组案例研究：角度推理中的四坐标因果路径及七坐标复制干预，以及危险请求检测与拒绝转向。

Result: 受控任务中 MoWE 在保持预测性能的同时比 dense 和标准 MoE 更清晰地定位隐藏原因。10B token 训练后评估交叉熵：MoWE 2.417、dense 2.452、标准 MoE 2.22。原生坐标特异性得分：MoWE 0.523、dense 0.375、标准 MoE 0.268；相同 FFN 上的 TopK SAE 为 dense 0.489、MoWE 0.556。案例中识别出支持"直角为 90 度"预测的四坐标因果路径，并将七个角度相关坐标值复制进模型，使"A rectangle has four"的下一词从 sides 变为 degrees；另有固定坐标集检测危险请求、另一坐标集引导拒绝。

Conclusion: MoWE 将稀疏条件计算与可直接识别和复用的坐标结合起来：原生坐标可读性接近稠密模型上的 SAE，且坐标可用于因果干预和电路分析，但纯预测性能仍落后于标准 MoE（2.417 vs 2.22）。

Abstract: Language models often spread a concept across many internal dimensions, making their features difficult to interpret. Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) recover clearer features after training. We ask whether models can instead learn interpretable output coordinates directly. We introduce Mixture of Wide Experts (MoWE), a sparse feed-forward layer in which selected experts read the full representation but write to fixed, disjoint output coordinates. On a controlled task with known hidden causes, this design preserves predictive performance while localizing causes more clearly than dense and standard mixture-of-experts (MoE) models.
  At approximately matched compute, we train two 19-layer runs of each architecture for 10 billion tokens. MoWE achieves evaluation cross-entropy 2.417 versus 2.452 for dense, while standard MoE reaches 2.22.
  We measure native-coordinate readability by asking a language-model judge to describe each coordinate from passages where it is strongly active and test that description on new passages. Correcting for descriptions that also fit other coordinates gives a specificity score of 0.523 for MoWE, 0.375 for dense, and 0.268 for standard MoE. TopK SAEs trained on the same FFN updates score 0.489 for dense and 0.556 for MoWE, making native MoWE comparable to the dense SAE.
  In the first case study, we identify a four-coordinate causal path supporting the model's prediction of degrees after the prompt A right angle is 90. We then copy seven angle-related coordinate values into the model as it processes A rectangle has four, changing its next-token prediction from sides to degrees. In the second, one fixed coordinate set detects hazardous requests and another steers the next token toward refusal. These examples show that MoWE combines sparse conditional computation with coordinates that can be identified and reused directly.

</details>


### [144] [De Rham cohomological architectures for PINNs and PDEs with topological holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05276)
*Andrew Gracyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Hodge-de Rham PINNs：利用 Hodge-Morrey-Friedrichs (HMF) 分解参数化 1-形式，将标量 PDE 解通过积分算子映射表示为 1-形式，并在关联 PDE 上施加 PINN 损失，从而缓解经典 PINN 在穿孔、孔洞、极点、空腔等拓扑病态场景下的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 PINN 在含孔洞区域常因界面不连续、闭合路径积分非零、路径依赖、与外微分精确形式对应等问题失效，需要从 de Rham 上同调角度修正。

Method: 对带边流形采用 HMF 分解参数化 1-形式，借助 HMF 同构定理建立与 de Rham 上同调的联系；将标量 PDE 解表示为 1-形式的积分算子映射，在关联 PDE 上构造 PINN 损失；利用 Sobolev 正则算子与 Fréchet 微分关联保证良态性；当损失不满足可积性条件时引入 exhaustion 函数辅助训练。

Result: 给出理论保证，并在 Aharonov-Bohm 效应及一系列拓扑不利问题（非精确形式、非平凡调和项与余闭项等场景）上验证了病态防护效果；相关算子的闭式表达或积分形式在实验中逐一讨论。

Conclusion: 基于上同调增广的 PINN 能系统性地规避孔洞类拓扑导致的失败模式，扩展了 PINN 在非平凡拓扑域上的适用性。

Abstract: We attempt to alleviate failure modes of classical physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) in scenarios of topological pathologies corresponding to punctures, holes, poles, and voids by introducing de Rham cohomological augmentations, or Hodge-de Rham PINNs. Vanilla PINNs often encounter pitfalls in hole-based settings due to discontinuous interfaces, nonzero closed path integration, path dependence, correspondence to exact forms via the exterior derivative, etc., therefore we absolve traditional failures via parameterizing a 1-form by the Hodge-Morrey-Friedrichs (HMF) decomposition for manifolds with boundary, drawing a connection to de Rham cohomology such as through the HMF isomorphism theorem. 1-forms can act as representations of scalar-valued PDE solutions through integral operator mappings. We propose performing a PINN-based loss on the parameterized 1-form through an associated PDE, which subsequently solves the potential-based PINN problem and moreover remedies the failures of vanilla PINNs via the Sobolev-regular operator under a Fréchet differentiation interconnection. The operator relationship often has a nontrivial closed-form and often requires integration or a case-by-case analysis, which we address thoroughly in our experiments. When an integrability condition is not met on our losses, we can equip exhaustion functions to train the networks. We demonstrate our methods on the Aharonov-Bohm effect, which, to our understanding, is largely nonexistent in PINN literature. We develop theoretical guarantees of our methods and demonstrate our pathological safeguards on a broad selection of topologically-adverse problems, including scenarios of inexact forms, and those of nontrivial harmonics and coexact terms.

</details>


### [145] [Stress-Testing the Robustness of LLM-Based Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03656)
*Ankitkumar Patel,Jigarkumar Patel,Amit Kumar,Supreetha Sreeram,Venkatesh Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出六维度压力测试框架，系统揭示LLM在因果发现中的"乐观偏差"、传统指标失真及规模扩展收益脆弱三类失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型被寄望以广泛知识与推理能力承担因果推断，但实际多依赖表层模式匹配而非真正的因果逻辑；已有研究只知LLM在该领域会失误，却缺乏对失误"何处、为何、程度如何"的精确刻画。

Method: 构建系统性压力测试框架，围绕六个关键维度展开评测：模型规模、因果图复杂度、提示策略、元数据质量、数据质量、以及对不确定性的敏感性。

Result: 发现显著的"乐观偏差"——模型系统性高估因果连边，导致整体精确率偏低；传统评估指标具误导性，推理型模型表面领先，但其高分往往源于忽略推断不确定性，一旦将不确定性纳入评估，性能明显下降；规模扩展虽带来提升，但增益脆弱，随元数据质量下降性能急剧退化。

Conclusion: 本文为LLM的盲区提供了实用指南，明确了何时可将其纳入因果发现流程、何时可能误导研究者。

Abstract: Why do some of the world's most powerful Artificial Intelligence (AI) systems still struggle to reason about cause and effect? Causal discovery, the ability to discern the underlying drivers of a system, is fundamental to scientific inquiry and has traditionally relied on structured data and statistical methods. Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have been explored as an alternative paradigm for causal inference, leveraging their broad knowledge and reasoning capabilities. Yet, despite their linguistic fluency, LLMs often rely on surface-level pattern recognition rather than genuine causal logic. While it is known that LLMs stumble in this domain, we lack a precise map of where, why, and how significantly these failures occur. In this paper, we propose a systematic stress-test framework across six critical dimensions: model scale, causal graph complexity, prompting strategy, metadata quality, data quality, and sensitivity to uncertainty. Our findings reveal a significant "optimism bias" in LLMs; models consistently overestimate causal links, leading to low precision across the board. Furthermore, we demonstrate that traditional evaluation metrics can be deceptive: while reasoning-heavy models lead in performance, the highest scores often occur when uncertainty in inference is overlooked. Once uncertainty is integrated into the evaluation, performance drops, providing a more honest reflection of causal reliability. We also find that, while scaling model size improves outcomes, these gains are fragile; performance degrades sharply as the quality of metadata decreases. This work serves as a practical guide to the blind spots of LLMs, offering a clear-eyed assessment of when these tools can be integrated into a causal discovery pipeline and when they are likely to lead researchers astray.

</details>


### [146] [Learning Directional Randers Geometry for Latent-Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03675)
*Théau Blanchard,Stéphanie Allassonnière*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Finsler-Randers度量学习框架，在深度网络潜空间中引入方向依赖的非对称距离，用单调序列约束实现更可靠的插值与外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络潜空间缺乏显式几何结构，插值保真度低、外推易误导；现有潜空间插值假定转移代价对称，无法刻画疾病进展等有向过程（如肿瘤生长、认知不可逆变化），而黎曼度量虽引入曲率仍保持对称性，故需强调"非对称性"而非仅曲率。

Method: 提出Finsler-Randers度量学习框架，在潜空间中学习方向依赖的距离（非对称Finsler度量，Randers型由黎曼度量与线性1-形式组合构造），从而计算满足单调时序约束的轨迹，用于插值与外推。

Result: 在受控横截面数据集上验证了该方法在插值与外推任务中的潜力，表明刻画非对称性可有效提升有向过程建模效果。

Conclusion: 关键不在曲率而在非对称性：引入方向依赖距离即可直接解决插值保真与外推可靠性问题，为疾病进展等有向潜空间分析提供几何工具。

Abstract: Deep neural networks extract powerful latent representations from images, enabling fast and efficient downstream analysis. However, these latent spaces lack explicit geometric structure, leading to possibly poor interpolation fidelity and misleading extrapolation. Latent interpolation techniques assume symmetric transition costs which are often not the case for real world data. Their symmetric nature cannot capture directional processes, such as disease progression, where certain transitions are impossible (e.g. tumor growth dynamics or cognitive improvement in Alzheimer's disease). Although Riemannian metrics extend Euclidean geometry by incorporating curvature, they remain symmetric. We show that understanding asymmetry and not curvature alone directly addresses these problems. To do so we propose a new Finsler-Randers metric learning framework. It incorporates direction-dependent distances, thus enabling the computation of trajectories respecting monotonic sequential constraints. We demonstrate its potential for interpolation and extrapolation on controlled cross-sectional datasets.

</details>


### [147] [Does the Trust Boundary Hold over Time? A Long-Horizon Benchmark for Receiver-Directed Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03724)
*Ting Xu,Henry Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"接收方主导"的联邦学习信任边界框架，用13站点基准（含精确oracle、配对种子、攻击协议、14方法/600轮、Qwen3-1.7B LoRA/500轮）系统评估准入判据在长周期下的稳健性，并引入与门控解耦的事后审计下线机制。核心发现：门控失败取决于所测目标（起始模型 vs 当前模型），二者互有盲区；独立数据的事后审计可在任意训练历史后保持保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在接收方主导的联邦学习中，每个站点自行划定信任边界：针对每个来源与模型组件判断该迁移"有利（准入）／有害（拒绝）／尚不可判断（弃权）"。作者关注这一边界能否在数百轮训练中长期成立，即门控判据是否会随模型漂移而失效、以及何种测量目标下失效方式不同。

Method: 构建半合成十三站点联邦基准，为每个被评估迁移提供精确oracle，采用配对随机种子与攻击协议；横跨14种方法训练600轮，并在Qwen3-1.7B LoRA站点上训练500轮（另设60个迁移、100轮的公共目标对比）。对比两类门控：以固定参考模型为判据 vs 以站点当前模型为判据；并用两个简短命题刻画其失败模式。另设计留出审计机制：不改动门控判决，在训练后决定各最终模型部署或回退到本地训练，并报告已失效的准入；在独立审计数据下给出对任意训练历史的保证。

Result: CPU层20个配对种子下，固定参考门控更能识别相对站点起始模型有用的迁移，却使29%对当前模型有害的迁移被准入；当前模型门控在CPU层仅准入2%对该目标有害的迁移，但漏掉大量对起始模型有用的迁移。600轮时审计部署了65–72%的模型，其中无一在其目标上为负收益，并保留98%的增益；所有运行中失效报告均未出现符号错误。2万次审计观测下，方差自适应界以99%召回识别出log loss劣化≥0.01的失效迁移。在38%对抗站点下，FedFomo与CS-Gate是唯一在所有诚实组中平均增益为正的受评方法。

Conclusion: 接收方信任边界的有效性取决于所测量的目标：针对起始模型与针对当前模型的门控存在结构性张力，单一准入判据无法同时覆盖；将"准入"与"部署"解耦、辅以独立数据上的事后审计与方差自适应界，可在长期训练中提供可靠保证。该张力在Qwen3大模型LoRA设定上同样复现。

Abstract: In receiver-directed federated learning, each site draws a trust boundary. For every source and model component it judges, on its own data, whether the transfer helps (admit), harms (reject) or cannot yet be told (abstain). We ask whether this boundary holds over hundreds of rounds. Our benchmark has a federation of thirteen semi-synthetic sites, an exact oracle for every evaluated transfer, paired seeds, an attack protocol, fourteen methods over 600 rounds and Qwen3-1.7B LoRA sites over 500. The answer depends on which boundary is measured. Across 20 paired CPU seeds, a gate judging against a fixed reference better identified transfers useful relative to the site's starting model. Yet it admitted 29% of transfers harmful to the model the site now has. A gate judging against the current model admitted 2% of harmful transfers on that target in the CPU tier, but missed many transfers useful to the starting model. Two short propositions explain these distinct failures. We also evaluate a held out audit that leaves gate verdicts intact. After training, it decides whether each final model is deployed or the site falls back to local training, and reports admissions that have gone stale. With independent audit data its guarantees hold after any training history. At 600 rounds the audit deployed 65 to 72% of models, none with negative benefit on its target, and kept 98% of the gain. No stale report had the wrong sign in our runs. With 20,000 audit observations, a variance-adaptive bound found 99% of stale transfers that cost at least 0.01 in log loss. A common-target Qwen3 comparison on 60 selected transfers shows the same tension over 100 rounds. With 38% adversarial sites, FedFomo and CS-Gate were the only evaluated methods with positive mean gain in every honest group.

</details>


### [148] [Auditing a Model-Hub Benchmark: Data Quality, Training Budgets, and Side Information in HuggingBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03727)
*Michal Podstawski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以数据产品视角审计推荐基准 HuggingBench，发现其外源知识图谱存在条目-实体身份约束违背、训练预算不足及文本信号未被利用等问题，并给出修正与可复用校验实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由模型枢纽构建的基准同时包含交互数据、知识图谱与资源文档，但“文件各自合法”并不保证它们描述同一对象。作者希望检验此类基准在多源拼接下的身份一致性与协议合理性，并揭示潜在的可复现性风险与被忽视的信号。

Method: 将发布物视为数据产品，对 HuggingBench（SIGIR 2025）做数据校验、复现其11种推荐方法，并用3个随机种子进行控制实验；检查键与取值范围约束之外的身份约束，修正连接后重跑KG类方法；分析100轮训练预算与早停的关系；再以线性模型结合条目文本与条目图建立强基线；并对其他推荐发布物做标识符一致性抽查。

Result: 四个外源KG中有三个虽通过键与范围校验，却违背条目-实体身份约束；修正连接后，所有KG类方法在全部种子下均有提升，Mean Recall@10最高提升73%，并一致翻转27组KG配置对比中的10组。100轮预算在29个验证集选定设置中有7–8个早停未触发，其中21/23个设置-种子对在更长预算下改善。未被任何已报告方法使用的条目文本是最强信号：含文本与条目图的闭式线性模型在CPU上数分钟训练即超过最佳报告Recall@10达18%。其他推荐发布物也存在同一数据集不同发布版本使用不兼容标识符的情况。

Conclusion: 文件级合法性不足以保障跨产物语义一致性；身份与出处校验应作为基准与模型湖发布流程的必备环节。作者公开修正后的文件、映射、校验器与日志，并总结在基准构建中保持身份与出处一致性的实践准则。

Abstract: Benchmarks built from model hubs combine interaction data, knowledge graphs (KGs) and resource documentation, but valid files do not ensure that these artifacts describe the same objects. Treating the release as a data product, we audit HuggingBench (SIGIR 2025), a benchmark for recommending Hugging Face resources, through data validation, a replication of its eleven recommendation methods and controlled experiments with three training seeds. In three of its four external KGs, the files pass key and range checks but violate the item-entity identity constraint on which KG recommenders rely. Correcting this join improves every KG-based method in every seed, by up to 73% in mean Recall@10, and consistently reverses ten of 27 comparisons between KG configurations. The protocol matters as well: the 100-epoch budget ends before early stopping in seven to eight of 29 validation-selected settings, and 21 of 23 such setting-seed pairs improve with a longer budget. The released item text, which no reported method uses, is the strongest signal: closed-form linear models with text and the item graph exceed the best reported Recall@10 by 18% while fitting in minutes on a CPU. Checks of other recommendation releases show that individually valid releases of one dataset can use incompatible identifiers. We release corrected files, mappings, validators and logs, and derive practices for preserving identity and provenance across the artifacts of benchmarks and model lakes.

</details>


### [149] [Minimal Superior Penalties: Characterising and Calibrating Decision-Aware Proper Scoring Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03734)
*Rouhollah Ahmadian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究分类器训练中的"优势性"(superiority)要求:正确分类的预测得分应严格优于任何错误分类的预测。作者指出Brier与对数评分均不满足该性质,将其归结为两个端点(正确预测得分上确界与错误预测得分下确界)之差,即"最小惩罚"(infimal penalty),给出闭式解,并构造含已发表惩罚与最小惩罚的一参数族,证明其严格proper,再用两数据集案例研究检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练分类器依赖验证指标决定保留哪个模型及何时停止训练,因此该指标需满足"优势性":无论概率多接近,任何正确分类的预测都应优于任何错误分类的预测。然而Brier评分与对数评分单独均不满足此性质;已有工作仅用第一个端点校准惩罚,可能过度惩罚。作者希望给出优势性的精确刻画并求得所需的最小惩罚。

Method: 将优势性刻画为正确预测得分上确界与错误预测得分下确界两个端点之间的间隔,对Brier与对数评分分别推导闭式解,其差值为最小惩罚;与已发表(基于单端点校准)的惩罚比较,并将两者嵌入一参数族,在单值平局约定下证明族内每个成员均严格proper;最后开展预先设定的两数据集案例研究(10类与47类等多场景)。

Result: 最小惩罚在Brier基底下比已发表惩罚更小:三分类时为其1/4.00(即小4.00倍),二十类时为1/2.11。案例研究中,在报告的十类场景下所选模型的平均宏F1随惩罚增大而上升,但在考虑种子/选择模式的嵌套结构后与噪声不可区分;47类场景未复现该描述性规律。作为无优势性基线的对照在代数上是受测族的重缩放,故实验无法将观察到的模式特异归于优势性。

Conclusion: 优势性由两个端点之差精确决定,最小惩罚为恢复优势性所需惩罚的严格下界;当目标是优势性本身时,任何严格高于最小惩罚的惩罚均已足够,选择更大惩罚属于独立的校准权衡而非保证所需。实验证据不足以将模型选择效果归因于优势性,需谨慎解读。

Abstract: Training a classifier means letting a validation metric decide which model is kept and when training stops. A natural requirement on that metric is superiority: every correctly classified forecast should score better than every incorrectly classified one, however close the underlying probabilities are. Neither the Brier score nor the logarithmic score satisfies this on its own. We show that superiority is governed by the separation between two endpoints -- the supremum of the score on correct predictions and the infimum on incorrect predictions -- and derive both in closed form for each score. Their difference is the infimal penalty, the greatest lower bound on the penalty needed to restore superiority. Prior work restores superiority with a penalty calibrated from the first endpoint alone; for the Brier base, our infimal penalty is smaller than that published penalty by a factor ranging from 4.00 at three classes to 2.11 at twenty. We further embed the published and infimal penalties in a one-parameter family and show that, under a single-valued tie convention, every member of the family is strictly proper. In a pre-specified two-dataset case study, mean selected-model macro-F1 rises with the penalty in the reported ten-class regimes, though not distinguishably from noise once seed/selection-mode nesting is accounted for; a fifth regime at forty-seven classes does not reproduce even this descriptive pattern. The intended no-superiority baseline is algebraically a rescaling of the family under test, so the experiment cannot attribute the observed pattern specifically to superiority. When superiority itself is the objective, any penalty strictly above the infimal value suffices; choosing a larger penalty is a separate calibration trade-off rather than a requirement for the guarantee.

</details>


### [150] [SSPE: Robust Sequential Staypoint Extraction in Degraded Trajectory Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03737)
*Yueyang Liu,Lance Kennedy,Andreas Züfle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出序列化停留点提取算法SSPE，用状态驱动架构、动态质心、n步前瞻与时间桥接机制替代滑动窗口，并配合无监督共识参数估计，在ExtraSensory数据集上F1与效率均优于Trackintel滑动窗口基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实轨迹存在严重空间噪声与长时间时间缺失，经典阈值型方法（如滑动窗口）对此高度敏感，常将连续的静止时段割裂为碎片，影响移动性分析的基础质量。

Method: SSPE采用状态驱动的序列架构：以动态质心取代静态空间锚点，引入n步前瞻启发式以结构性容忍瞬时坐标漂移，并用时间桥接机制防止信号长时间中断造成的伪碎片化；另提出完全无监督的共识式参数估计框架，从原始轨迹中提取基线统计尺度，通过最大化结构一致性确定最优参数，无需标注真值。

Result: 在具人工核验真值的ExtraSensory真实移动数据集上，与Trackintel滑动窗口实现对比，SSPE获得更高F1分数，同时计算效率更优。

Conclusion: 状态驱动+前瞻+时间桥接的设计可有效缓解噪声与缺失导致的碎片化，且无监督共识参数估计可摆脱对标注真值的依赖，使SSPE在精度与效率上均优于主流基线，适合作为停留点提取的通用方案。

Abstract: Extracting semantically meaningful staypoints from raw trajectory streams is a foundational prerequisite for mobility analytics. However, real-world trajectory data is frequently degraded by severe spatial noise and protracted temporal dropouts. Classic threshold-based extraction methods, such as the widely adopted sliding-window algorithm, are highly sensitive to these anomalies, routinely fracturing continuous stationary episodes into disjointed fragments. To overcome this limitation, we propose the Sequential Staypoint Extraction (SSPE) algorithm. SSPE employs a resilient, state-driven architecture that replaces static spatial anchors with a dynamic centroid and introduces an $n$-step lookahead heuristic to structurally tolerate transient coordinate drift. Concurrently, it utilizes temporal bridging mechanisms to prevent artificial fragmentation during prolonged signal loss. Since optimal extraction parameters vary significantly across datasets, we further introduce a fully unsupervised, consensus-based parameter estimation framework. This framework derives baseline statistical scales directly from the raw trajectory data and identifies the optimal parameter configuration by maximizing structural agreement, thereby eliminating the reliance on annotated ground truth. We evaluate SSPE on the ExtraSensory dataset, a real-world mobility corpus with manually verified ground-truth staypoints, against the sliding-window implementation of Trackintel, one of the most widely adopted extraction baselines in the field. Empirical results demonstrate that SSPE achieves superior accuracy, yielding higher F1-scores while simultaneously improving computational efficiency over this standard.

</details>


### [151] [RATSIFT: Resolution-Adaptive Transformer for Solar Irradiance Forecasting and Temporal Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03738)
*Jesto Peter,Timothy M. Hansen,Hossein Moradi Rekabdarkolaee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Transformer的架构，可同时进行太阳辐照度预测与时间降尺度，输入可为任意分辨率、输出为训练数据中出现过的任意分辨率；并采用遗传算法优化超参数以降低计算成本。在德州达拉斯实测数据上，15/30/60分钟及不规则间隔输入、5分钟分辨率输出的RMSE分别为28.785、29.544、30.157、32.132 W/m²，且能给出可靠的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳辐照度的准确预测与时间降尺度对可再生能源并网与电网调度至关重要；传统方法难以刻画辐照度的快速变化，且缺乏能在多输入/输出分辨率下统一预测与降尺度的框架，同时穷举式超参数网格搜索计算代价高。

Method: 构建Transformer架构，利用全局与局部上下文信息，支持任意分辨率输入并生成训练数据涵盖的任意分辨率输出，实现预测与时间降尺度一体化；引入基于遗传算法的超参数优化框架，替代穷举网格搜索以提升训练效率。

Result: 在德州达拉斯实测数据上，以15、30、60分钟及不规则间隔的全局输入、5分钟分辨率输出时，RMSE分别为28.785、29.544、30.157、32.132 W/m²；模型给出可靠的不确定性估计，在辐照波动条件下高分辨率输入对应更宽的预测区间，粗分辨率输入则区间更窄且训练时间与计算成本显著降低。

Conclusion: 该工作为多分辨率太阳辐照度预测与时间降尺度提供了可扩展、高效的框架，适用于实际能源预测与电网管理应用。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting and temporal downscaling of solar irradiance is critical for reliable renewable energy integration and grid management. Traditional methods often struggle to capture rapid irradiance variability. In this work, we develop a Transformer-based architecture for simultaneous forecasting and temporal downscaling. The model takes input data at arbitrary resolutions and generates outputs at any resolutions represented within the training data, leveraging both global and local contextual information. To improve training efficiency, a genetic algorithm-based optimization framework is implemented to identify suitable model hyperparameters, while reducing the computational cost of exhaustive grid search. The effectiveness of the proposed model is shown by analyzing real data in Dallas, Texas. The proposed model achieved root mean square error (RMSE) values of 28.785, 29.544, 30.157, 32.132~W/m\(^2\) for 15-, 30-, 60-minute, and irregularly spaced global input and producing forecast at 5-minutes resolution, respectively. Furthermore, this model produced reliable uncertainty estimates, exhibiting wider prediction intervals for high-resolution inputs during volatile solar conditions, whereas coarser-resolution inputs result in narrower intervals and significantly lower training time and computational cost. Our approach provides a scalable and efficient framework for multi-resolution solar irradiance forecasting and temporal downscaling, suitable for practical energy forecasting and grid management applications.

</details>


### [152] [CaRe-KT: Continuity-Aware Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning for Knowledge Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03744)
*Mehrnoush Mohammadi,Kamal Berahmand,Mohammad Morsali,Shazia Sadiq,Wojtek Tomaszewski,Hassan Khosravi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CaRe-KT：一种查询自适应的检索增强知识追踪框架，通过证据图缓存与结果监督对比学习维持"相关性连续性"，使检索相关性引导而非决定预测贡献，在五个基准上优于强基线并在稀疏历史与冷启动场景下保持稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强知识追踪将证据检索与下游预测分离，形成"检索—推理对齐鸿沟"：证据相关性随学习者知识状态的演变和预测上下文而变化，而检索阶段使用的相关性未必等于其对预测的实际效用。

Method: 1) 证据图缓存（EGC）：将持久化的题目级关系兼容性与交互级时间有效性结合，为每个查询构建查询特定的证据子图；2) 通过子图检索到的历史学习轨迹与目标学习者历史在同一表示空间中编码，采用结果监督的对比学习进行对齐；3) 注意力机制将检索获得的相关性与学习到的查询—证据兼容性融合，使相关性"告知"而非"决定"每条轨迹的预测影响。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上，CaRe-KT 优于强知识追踪基线；在历史稀疏与冷启动条件下仍保持稳健性能。

Conclusion: 结果支持将"相关性连续性"作为检索增强知识追踪的设计原则，即证据获取与推理过程之间的相关性应保持连续一致，但不应将检索相关性等同于预测效用。

Abstract: Knowledge tracing (KT) estimates learners' evolving knowledge states from interaction histories to support adaptive learning. Retrieval-augmented KT improves these estimates using cross-learner behavioral evidence, but existing approaches typically separate evidence retrieval from downstream prediction. This creates a retrieval-inference alignment gap because evidence relevance varies with the learner's evolving state and prediction context. We propose CaRe-KT, a query-adaptive framework that maintains relevance continuity between evidence acquisition and inference without equating retrieval relevance with predictive utility. CaRe-KT introduces an Evidence-Graph Cache (EGC) that combines persistent question-level relational compatibility with interaction-level temporal validity to construct query-specific evidence subgraphs. Historical learning trajectories retrieved through these subgraphs are encoded alongside the target learner's history in a shared representation space using outcome-supervised contrastive learning. An attention mechanism combines acquisition relevance with learned query-evidence compatibility, allowing relevance to inform, rather than determine, each trajectory's predictive influence. Experiments across five benchmarks show that CaRe-KT outperforms strong KT baselines and remains robust under sparse-history and cold-start conditions. These findings support relevance continuity as a design principle for retrieval-augmented knowledge tracing.

</details>


### [153] [Honest Small Decision Models for Finance: Scale, Specialization Collapse, and Mixed-Corpus Remediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03755)
*Ajitabh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 训练0.8B-32B小模型做单次前向的"System-1"决策模型（choice/score/noul输出概率），在公开决策基准与两个金融语料上评测。发现窄域微调损失通用决策能力9-45个百分点（随规模增大而缩小）；在现实标注问题上，各规模模型均退化为按horizon条件先验基线（0.37-0.38），但给出平坦且良好校准的概率（2B处ECE 0.026最优，9-32B因先验漂移升至0.043-0.044），呈现"自然的诚实不确定性"。混合语料修复用一个adapter保留全部能力，输出可部署工件并给出分带调度（band-dispatch）升级分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融工作流需要快速、校准良好且可审计的决策，而非单纯生成文本。作者希望验证：小语言模型能否以单次前向、带概率输出的形式承担类型化决策，并在真实金融数据上是否具备条件性预测能力与校准可靠性。

Method: 训练两族模型（K2-Horizon、Qwen3.5/3.8），9个规模档（0.8B-32B），统一冻结LoRA配方。数据：公开决策基准 + 两个新金融语料——程序化金标签的政策语料（规则阅读，精确标签）与现实标签结果语料（19,640条walk-forward前向收益问题，外加1,360条封存的2026测试集）。模型以单次前向输出choice/score/noul概率；设置对照以检测条件性边际，并分析EC E与先验漂移、梯度体量效应，最后做混合语料重训练。

Result: 窄域微调使通用决策能力下降9-45个百分点，该数据混合效应随模型规模增大而减小，并与"窄梯度体量"相关。在现实标签问题上，0.8B-32B各档均落在horizon条件先验基线（准确率0.37-0.38），对照下无可检测的条件性优势；概率平坦且校准良好并跟踪各horizon基率。校准存在中间规模甜点：2B处ECE 0.026，9-32B在先验漂移下退化至0.043-0.044。混合语料修复在一个adapter中保留全部能力：公开基准上匹配或略超通用专家，保持政策上限与接近专家级的结果校准。

Conclusion: 小模型可作为校准良好、可审计的金融决策工件，但其在现实结果问题上的价值在于"诚实的不确定性"而非条件性预测优势。混合语料单adapter可缓解窄域微调的能力侵蚀，配合分域温度机制与分带调度分析（规则决定答案处为锐利可派发带；现实不可判定处为平坦复核路由带），形成可部署方案。

Abstract: Financial workflows need fast, calibrated, auditable decisions, not just generated text. We train System-1 decision models, small language models fine-tuned to answer typed questions (choice/score/noul) with a probability in a single forward pass, across two model families (K2-Horizon and Qwen3.5/3.8) and nine rungs from 0.8B to 32B parameters, using one frozen LoRA recipe on a public decision benchmark and two new finance corpora: a programmatic-gold policy corpus (rule-reading with exact labels) and a reality-labeled outcome corpus (19,640 realized forward-return questions in walk-forward splits, plus a sealed 2026 test of 1,360 questions). Narrow fine-tuning costs general decision competence 9-45 percentage points, a data-mixture effect that shrinks with model scale and is associated with narrow-gradient volume. On reality-labeled questions, every trained rung from 0.8B to 32B lands at the horizon-conditioned prior baseline (accuracy 0.37-0.38, no detectable conditional edge under our controls); the models respond with flat, well-calibrated probabilities that track per-horizon base rates: naturally honest uncertainty, with a mid-scale calibration sweet spot (ECE 0.026 at 2B, degrading to 0.043-0.044 at 9-32B under prior drift). A mixed-corpus remediation carries all competencies in one adapter at modest cost: it matches or slightly exceeds the generalist specialist on the public benchmark while holding the policy ceiling and near-specialist outcome calibration, yielding a deployable artifact with per-domain temperature regimes and a band-dispatch escalation analysis: sharp, dispatchable bands where rules determine answers; flat, review-routing bands where reality does not.

</details>


### [154] [Language Models Can Benefit from Domain-Specific Inductive Biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03756)
*Damien Teney,Liangze Jiang,Zachary Shinnick,Hemanth Saratchandran,Simon Lucey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过将激活函数替换为按数据集调优的学习样条，作者构建了领域特化的 Transformer 变体，并以"架构×数据"的交叉组合评估跨域迁移，发现调优收益具有明显的领域特异性，说明预训练数据的不同子集可能需要不同的学习机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型普遍采用单一架构在大规模异构数据上训练，Transformer 也主要针对通用学习做了大量优化。但作者质疑：这类通用架构在不同预训练数据子集（如网页文本、代码、数学）上是否同等有效？即是否存在"通用架构掩盖了数据子集间的学习机制差异"这一问题。

Method: 1) 从头训练小型语言模型，使用具有代表性的预训练数据子集（网页文本、代码、数学等，共七个数据集，含 FineWeb、TinyStories 等）。2) 提出 Transformer 变体：将标准激活函数替换为在特定数据集上调优的学习样条（learned splines），以此简单直接地修改模型的归纳偏置。3) 采用架构与数据集的"混搭"（mix-and-match）实验设计，系统评估跨域迁移，即针对某一领域调优的架构在另一领域数据上的表现。4) 进一步分析改进背后的机制，包括损失盆地宽度与"彩票票据"（lottery tickets）的丰富程度。

Result: 调优后的 Transformer 变体在全部七个测试数据集上均提升了训练效率或泛化性能，但收益往往是领域特异的：在自然语言数据集（如 FineWeb）上调优的架构在 TinyStories 上表现良好，而在数学数据上调优的架构在自然语言上表现较差。机制分析表明，改进源于更宽的损失盆地和更丰富的"彩票票据"。

Conclusion: 预训练数据的不同子集可从不同的学习机制中获益，这揭示了当前主流追求"小规模通用架构与优化器集合"趋势的局限。作者据此指出，通过发展领域特化的学习策略、同时保留训练通用模型的能力，存在提升数据效率的机会。

Abstract: Language models are typically trained with a single choice of architecture on diverse domains and data sources. Modern transformers have been extensively refined for such general-purpose learning, but are they equally effective across different subsets of the pretraining data? We investigate this question by training small language models from scratch on representative subsets of pretraining data such as web text, code, and mathematics. We introduce variants of transformers in which standard activation functions are replaced with learned splines tuned on specific datasets, providing a simple way to modify their inductive biases. Using a mix-and-match of architectures and datasets, we evaluate cross-domain transfer, i.e. how well does an architecture tuned for one domain perform on data from another? We find that the tuned transformer variants improve training efficiency or generalization on all seven of our tested datasets, but the gains are often domain-specific. For example, architectures tuned on a natural language dataset such as FineWeb perform well on TinyStories, whereas those tuned on mathematics perform poorly on natural language. We identify several mechanisms underlying these improvements, including wider loss basins and more abundant "lottery tickets". Our results show that different subsets of pretraining data can benefit from different learning mechanisms, highlighting a limitation of the prevailing trend towards a small set of general-purpose architectures and optimizers. This points to an opportunity to improve data efficiency by developing domain-specific learning strategies that retain the ability to train general-purpose models.

</details>


### [155] [The role of injected noise in diffusion model generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03764)
*Vincent Emmerling,Felix Günther,David Kappel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究扩散模型中采样噪声对跨数据集样本相似性（泛化效应）的作用，发现注入噪声在采样中期影响最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同数据集训练的扩散模型在相同初始噪声输入下会生成高度相似的样本，暗示其学习到某种泛化的数据分布；作者希望厘清这一强泛化效应由何决定。

Method: 考察采样过程中注入噪声的作用（对比/扰动注入噪声），并分析样本相似性对噪声变化的鲁棒性，定位影响最大的采样阶段。

Result: 注入噪声是维持或破坏该泛化效应的关键因素；其影响在“粗结构已形成但样本身份尚未确定”的采样区间最大。

Conclusion: 采样噪声并非中性，而是控制跨模型样本一致性的重要机制；泛化相似性在特定采样阶段最敏感，为理解与调控扩散模型泛化提供依据。

Abstract: Diffusion models trained on distinct datasets can produce highly similar samples when prompted with the same noise input, suggesting that they learn a generalized data distribution. We investigate the role of noise injected during the sampling process, and show that it plays an important role in making or breaking this strong generalization effect. Based on this finding we investigate the robustness of sample similarity to changes in the injected noise, and find that its impact is largest in the sampling regime where broad structure already exists but the precise identity of the sample is not yet determined.

</details>


### [156] [Quantifying out-of-distribution data severity with Wasserstein distances: benchmarking large language models for alloy property prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03767)
*YuJie Liu,Dezhen Xue,Jun Sun,Xiangdong Ding,Zhibin Gao,Turab Lookman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通用大语言模型（Gemini、DeepSeek）在 Ti、钢、高熵合金、Al 的性能预测上超越 SteelBERT、MatSciBERT、SciBERT 等材料领域专用模型；提出的"先验模型数值基线 + LLM 推理校正"混合范式进一步超过先验模型与无引导模式，且数据质量优于数量、置信度校准良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 Gemini、DeepSeek 等通用 LLM 推理能力增强，需回答一个关键问题：其性能（性质）预测能力是否可与材料领域专用预训练模型相比，以及能否以更低成本替代昂贵的领域专用预训练。

Method: 在 Ti、钢、高熵合金（HEA）、Al 四类体系上对比通用 LLM 与 SteelBERT/MatSciBERT/SciBERT；提出混合方法，将 CatBoost 或 SteelBERT 等先验模型的数值估计作为 prompt 输入，使通用 LLM（DeepSeek V3.1、Gemini-2.5-Flash）充当"推理校正器"；并用基于相似度的指标研究数据策略（用训练集 5% 高相似参考数据 vs 100%），同时评估 LLM 自报置信度的不确定性校准。

Result: 通用 LLM 出乎意料地优于三个领域专用 BERT 模型；混合方法精度高于先验模型与"无引导模式"；仅用 5% 高相似度参考数据即可达到使用全部训练集相当的性能，说明数据质量主导数量；LLM 自报"高"置信度与更小的真值偏差强相关，表现出良好的不确定性校准。

Conclusion: 将通用 LLM 与先验模型数值基线、高质量数据筛选协同，可构成低成本且准确的材料发现范式，降低对昂贵领域专用预训练的需求。

Abstract: As general-purpose Large Language Models (LLMs), such as Gemini and DeepSeek, exhibit increasingly advanced reasoning capabilities, a critical question arises: how do their property prediction abilities compare to those of domain-specific pretrained materials models? In this study, we evaluate predictions for Ti, steels, high-entropy alloy (HEA) and Al, demonstrating that general-purpose LLMs surprisingly outperform SteelBERT, MatSciBERT, and SciBERT. To further enhance performance, we introduce a hybrid approach that utilizes numerical priors from a prior model, such as CatBoost or SteelBERT, to guide the general LLMs (Deepseek V3.1, Gemini-2.5-Flash). By inputting these initial estimates from a prior model as prompts, the LLM acts as a reasoning corrector, achieving accuracy superior to the prior model and "unguided mode". Furthermore, we investigate the impact of data strategy using a similarity-based metric, revealing that data quality dominates quantity; using merely 5% of high-similarity reference data of training set as input yields performance similar to the results of using 100% of training set. Additionally, the LLMs demonstrate excellent uncertainty calibration, where self-reported "high" confidence strongly correlates with minimal deviation from ground truth. Our findings suggest that synergizing general-purpose LLMs with prior model numerical baselines and high-quality data curation creates a cost-effective and accurate paradigm for materials discovery, reducing the need for expensive domain-specific pretraining.

</details>


### [157] [Multi-layer Indexer: Overlapping Selection and Attention in DeepSeek Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03768)
*Serdarcan Dilbaz,Arjun Krishna,Kiran Kamble,Daniel M. Bikel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多层索引器（MLI）：让每层复用上一层索引器的token选择，使索引器扫描与该层注意力并行执行，无需重训，与IndexCache组合后在长上下文解码中显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在DSA等稀疏注意力中，每层前的lightning indexer需全量扫描KV cache，长上下文解码时该扫描而非稀疏注意力本身成为瓶颈。

Method: 观察到相邻层选择的token高度相似，故令某层直接使用上一索引器层的选择结果，解除层内索引器与注意力的串行依赖，实现并发执行；默认实现不改kernel、可直接嵌入SGLang生产decode图，另提供可选融合decode kernel。可与IndexCache（GLM-5.2的IndexShare）组合：IndexCache减少保留索引器的层数，MLI隐藏剩余层的扫描开销。

Result: 在DeepSeek-V3.2与GLM-5、10K–195K上下文、保留率1/2至1/32下，同保留率解码比IndexCache快最多14.5%（DeepSeek-V3.2）与9.8%（GLM-5，均1/2），1/4时为7.9%与5.1%，增益随上下文增长；1/r保留率下比IndexCache 1/(2r)更快且保留两倍新鲜选择，128K时达原模型吞吐1.32x（1/4），优于IndexCache 1/8的1.28x；LongBench、RULER、LongBench v2精度与IndexCache相差数个百分点内。

Conclusion: MLI以零重训、零新权重的轻量改动消除索引器串行瓶颈，与跨层索引复用互补，在保持精度的同时提升长上下文解码吞吐。

Abstract: Sparse attention cuts the cost of long-context inference by making the attention matrix sparse: each query attends only a subset of the sequence, chosen by a fixed pattern or, in recent production LLMs, learned per query. In DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA), the attention of DeepSeek-V3.2 and the GLM-5 family, every layer is preceded by a lightning indexer that scores the whole cache and keeps the k=2048 highest-scoring tokens for the layer to attend. At long context this indexer scan, not the sparse attention it feeds, is where the decode time goes. But adjacent layers select nearly the same tokens, so a layer can attend the selection of the previous indexer layer instead of its own. This removes the dependency: each indexer then runs concurrently with the attention of its layer. The resulting Multi-layer Indexer (MLI) needs no retraining and no new weights. Its default realization changes no kernel, running inside SGLang's production decode graph; an optional fused decode kernel accelerates it further on DeepSeek-V3.2. MLI composes with IndexCache (the cross-layer reuse GLM-5.2 ships as IndexShare): IndexCache drops the indexer from some layers, and MLI hides the scan on the rest. We serve DeepSeek-V3.2 and GLM-5 under IndexCache's protocol at retentions 1/2 to 1/32 (the fraction of layers that keep an indexer) and contexts 10K to 195K. At equal retention the composition decodes faster than IndexCache: up to 14.5% faster on DeepSeek-V3.2 and 9.8% on GLM-5 at 1/2, and 7.9% and 5.1% at 1/4, largest where IndexCache keeps the most indexers and growing with context. At retention 1/r it is faster still than IndexCache at 1/(2r) while keeping twice the fresh selections: at 128K it reaches 1.32x the unmodified model's decode throughput at 1/4, above IndexCache's 1.28x at 1/8. Its LongBench, RULER and LongBench v2 accuracy stays within a few percentage points of IndexCache's.

</details>


### [158] [Probabilistic Steering of Generative Priors for Lab-in-the-Loop Antibody Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03844)
*Anisha Parsan,Sebastian Ober,Calvin McCarter,Nick Bhattacharya,Hunter Elliott*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将抗体发现的生成模型与后期迭代优化统一为一个平滑过渡的流程，用概率代理模型的后验不确定性指导结构先验与湿实验监督之间的交接，在低数据量下提升优化效率与分子多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗体开发通常被割裂为两个阶段：先用生成式结构模型发现新结合体，再用概率黑盒优化方法迭代精修。这种分离使模型与方法各自为政，难以在数据稀缺的精修阶段高效衔接，因此作者希望把两阶段统一起来。

Method: 将生成式发现模型平滑过渡为迭代优化：以概率代理模型的后验不确定性作为调度信号，控制从基于结构的先验向湿实验数据监督的交接过程。

Result: 在低N（小样本）情形下优化效率提升，且优化后分子之间的多样性也有所改善。

Conclusion: 两阶段的统一式框架优于将发现与精修割裂处理的常规做法，尤其适用于湿实验数据有限的抗体优化场景。

Abstract: Therapeutic antibody development is often framed as comprising two discrete stages: initial discovery of novel binders followed by iterative optimization to refine these hits into therapeutic-grade molecules. Models and methods often reflect these stages, with generative structure models applied to discovery and probabilistic black-box optimization methods applied to refinement. Here we propose to unify these stages by smoothly transitioning a generative discovery model into iterative optimization, allowing the handoff between the structure-based prior and supervision from wet lab data to be guided by the posterior uncertainty of a probabilistic surrogate. We show that this yields improvements in optimization efficiency in the low-N regime, as well as improved diversity amongst the optimized molecules.

</details>


### [159] [Dr. No: Feature Steering for Abstention in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03862)
*Farid Hasanov,Riccardo Renzulli,Bahram Zonooz,Mykola Pechenizkiy,Decebal Constantin Mocanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Dr. No——一种基于稀疏自编码器(SAE)特征引导的推理时方法，挖掘与"弃答/作答"相关的特征并在prefill阶段调制，无需重训练即可改善大模型的证据不足感知与弃答行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在视觉或文本证据不足时仍倾向给出自信但无依据的答案，在高风险或信息检索场景中难以信任；作者希望探究这类行为背后的内部机制能否被识别并在推理时加以控制。

Method: 利用SAE挖掘与abstention（弃答）和commitment（作答）相关的稀疏特征，在prefill前向传播中对少量相关特征进行调制（feature steering），不重训练基座模型。在LLaVA-1.6 Mistral 7B上评测UNK-VQA、MM-UPD等多模态弃答基准；在Qwen3.5-2B上评测AbstentionBench子集的语言推理任务，并与多种基于不确定性的后验拒答基线对比。

Result: 相比后验拒答方法，特征引导在"正确作答—适当弃答"之间取得更优平衡；在UNK-VQA以及MM-UPD中涉及图文/问答不兼容的样本上增益最明显，并较清晰地迁移到AbstentionBench的数学推理子集；但在缺失答案检测与检索密集型多跳QA（如MuSiQue）上表现有限。

Conclusion: 稀疏内部特征可作为轻量的控制接口来塑造大模型的不确定性感知行为；同时表明弃答控制依赖于不确定性来源，而非由单一通用信号支配。

Abstract: Large foundation models often answer even when the available visual or textual evidence is insufficient to support a reliable conclusion. This failure to abstain makes them hard to trust in high-stakes or information-seeking settings, where a confident but unsupported answer can be worse than no answer at all. It also raises a natural question: can the internal mechanisms behind this behaviour be identified and controlled at inference time? We present Dr. No, a feature-steering approach that mines sparse autoencoder (SAE) features associated with abstention and commitment, then modulates a small set of them during the prefill pass, without retraining the base model.
  Dr. No is evaluated on LLaVA-1.6 Mistral 7B across multimodal abstention benchmarks, including UNK-VQA and MM-UPD, and additionally benchmarked on language reasoning tasks using Qwen3.5-2B on AbstentionBench subsets. Against multiple uncertainty-based abstention baselines, feature steering achieves a more favourable balance between correct answering and appropriate abstention than post-hoc rejection methods. The gains are strongest on UNK-VQA and on MM-UPD cases involving image--question or question--answer incompatibility, and transfer most clearly to mathematical reasoning subsets of AbstentionBench. However, performance is more limited on absent-answer detection and retrieval-heavy multi-hop QA, such as MuSiQue. These results suggest that sparse internal features provide lightweight control handles for shaping uncertainty-aware behaviour in foundation models, while also showing that abstention control is source-dependent rather than governed by a single universal signal.

</details>


### [160] [ExaTune: Exhaustive Hyperparameter Landscape Exploration at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03867)
*Kevin Dick,Katie Lonsway*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出开源HPC系统ExaTune，通过穷举离散超参数网格生成"超参数景观地图"这一新数据制品，并系统化应用五种适应度景观分析指标；发布8个完整景观地图（285,080个配置）基准，实证表明常规模拟优化器会系统性遗漏景观中可见的不稳定区域、次优解与参数交互效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超参数优化（HPO）只返回单一最优配置，丢弃了刻画性能如何依赖配置选择的完整景观信息。适应度景观分析（崎岖度指标、自相关函数、适应度-距离相关）在进化计算中成熟，但因缺乏细粒度真值枚举数据，从未在大规模ML超参数空间上应用。

Method: 构建ExaTune开源HPC系统：对离散超参数网格每一点做K折交叉验证穷举评估，结果聚合为可复现的parquet基准；并实现五个形式化景观指标——空间自相关（Moran's I）、崎岖度、局部最优密度、适应度-距离相关（FDC）、基于方差的参数重要性。

Result: 发布8个完整景观地图（共285,080个穷举评估配置），覆盖5类模型家族（SVM、随机森林、XGBoost、岭回归、k近邻）与6个分类、2个回归基准，托管于HuggingFace。实证显示贝叶斯优化、Optuna、Hyperband与随机搜索在标准试验预算下会系统性遗漏不稳定区域、次优解与参数交互效应。

Conclusion: 完整景观地图揭示了优化器在结构上无法获取的信息；ExaTune将适应度景观分析引入大规模HPO研究，为可复现的超参数空间诊断提供基准与工具。

Abstract: Hyperparameter optimization (HPO) returns a single optimal configuration, systematically discarding the explored performance landscape that encodes how results depend on configuration choices. Fitness landscape analysis comprises a robust collection of methods from evolutionary computation offering ruggedness indices, autocorrelation functions, and fitness-distance correlation that have never been applied to machine learning hyperparameter spaces at scale, given that fine-grained ground-truth enumeration data has not existed. We present ExaTune, an open-source HPC system to produce a new artifact class: the hyperparameter landscape map. ExaTune exhaustively evaluates every point on a discretized hyperparameter grid via $K$-fold cross-validation, aggregates results into reproducible parquet benchmarks, and applies a suite of five formalized landscape metrics: spatial autocorrelation (Moran's $I$), ruggedness, local optima density, fitness-distance correlation (FDC), and variance-based parameter importance. We release a benchmark collection of 8 complete landscape maps (totalling 285,080 exhaustively evaluated configurations) spanning five model families (SVMs, random forests, XGBoost, ridge regression, and $k$-nearest neighbors) across six classification and two regression benchmarks, hosted on HuggingFace. Empirical analysis demonstrates that Bayesian optimization, Optuna, Hyperband, and Random Search at standard trial budgets systematically miss instability regions, secondary optima, and parameter interaction effects that are visible in complete landscapes; information that is structurally unavailable to any optimizer by design.

</details>


### [161] [Embedding Semantic Entropy: NLI-Free Semantic Uncertainty Estimation with Geometry-Aware Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03876)
*Akshiya Raveendran,Praveenasarma Baskarakurukkal,Pragatheeswaran Vipulanandan,Kamal Premaratne,Uthayasanker Thayasivam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Emb-SE，用语义嵌入空间中的K-Medoids聚类与动态簇选择替代语义熵中基于NLI蕴含的硬聚类，在多数据集与多模型上与SE性能相当且语义分组更可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM常产生流畅但错误的回答，需不确定性估计以保障可靠部署。现有Semantic Entropy依赖蕴含关系的硬聚类，黑箱且不易检视，聚类质量受NLI判定影响。

Method: 用语义嵌入空间中的K-Medoids替代蕴含式聚类；引入动态簇选择（依据簇内紧致度与簇间分离度）；在TriviaQA、SQuAD、NQ-Open、SVAMP、BioASQ上，用Falcon-7B-Instruct、LLaMA-2-7B、LLaMA-2-7B-Chat、Gemma-7B评估。

Result: AUROC与AUARC具竞争力，与SE相当；性能仅有有限下降，同时语义分组更可检视、簇更紧致且可分。

Conclusion: NLI蕴含聚类可用嵌入空间聚类替代；将不确定性估计从黑箱蕴含判定转化为可检视的聚类问题，因为语义不确定性分数本质上依赖底层语义分组。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often generate fluent yet incorrect responses, making un- certainty estimation important for reliable deployment. Semantic Entropy (SE) estimates uncertainty by clustering responses based on semantic equivalence, but it relies on hard entailment-based clustering. We propose Embedding Semantic Entropy, which replaces entailment-based clustering with K-Medoids in semantic embedding space and uses dynamic cluster selection to account for intra-cluster compactness and inter-cluster separation. We evaluate the method on TriviaQA, SQuAD, NQ-Open, SVAMP, and BioASQ using Falcon- 7B-Instruct, LLaMA-2-7B, LLaMA-2-7B-Chat, and Gemma-7B. Results show competitive AUROC and AUARC performance, suggesting that NLI-based entailment clustering can be replaced with embedding-space clustering with limited degradation while improving the inspectability of semantic grouping. This matters because semantic uncertainty scores depend on underlying semantic groups; by making these groups explicit, compact, and separable in embedding space, Emb-SE turns uncertainty estimation from a black-box en- tailment decision into an inspectable clustering problem.

</details>


### [162] [The Information Limits of Neural Controlled Differential Equations Under Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03887)
*Rostislav Gusev,Evgenia Romanenkova,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对平面驱动、速度有界的Neural CDE中由稀疏观测导致的终端状态不确定性进行刻画，给出基于带符号面积与传输Lie括号的zonotope近似与可计算歧义证书，并分析采样数N下的最优最坏情形半径标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Neural CDE通过插值离散观测构造连续路径演化隐状态，但稀疏测量使观测点之间的输入路径不确定；与同一数据一致的多种可行连续路径可导致不同终端状态，因此需要量化这种由插值歧义引起的不确定性。

Method: 针对速度有界的平面驱动情形，刻画与观测兼容的终端状态集合；用可行带符号面积与传输Lie括号构造其主导阶zonotopic近似；将该集合投影到标量预测上得到目标特定的歧义证书；并研究确定性自适应采样策略下所需测量数，推导非退化平面CDE的最优最坏情形半径随观测区间数N的标度。

Result: 兼容终端状态集合允许由可行带符号面积和传输Lie括号确定的主导阶zonotope近似，其标量投影给出可计算的歧义证书。对非退化平面CDE，最优最坏情形半径在速度有界时为N^{-1}量级，在速度与加速度均有界时为N^{-2}量级。

Conclusion: 插值歧义可用zonotope与Lie括号结构有效刻画并形成可计算证书；路径正则性（加速度约束）显著提升不确定性随采样密度的收敛阶，为稀疏观测下Neural CDE的可靠性评估与采样设计提供理论依据。

Abstract: Neural controlled differential equations (Neural CDEs) evolve hidden states along continuous paths constructed by interpolating discrete observations. Sparse measurements leave the input between observation times undetermined, and admissible continuous paths consistent with the same data can produce different terminal states. We characterize this ambiguity for planar drivers with bounded speed. The set of compatible terminal states admits a leading-order zonotopic approximation determined by feasible signed areas and transported Lie brackets. Its projection onto a scalar prediction yields a computable, target-specific ambiguity certificate. We also study the number of measurements needed to reduce the remaining uncertainty. For nondegenerate planar CDEs, the optimal worst-case radius over deterministic adaptive sampling policies scales as $N^{-1}$ when speed is bounded and $N^{-2}$ when both speed and acceleration are bounded, where $N$ is the number of observation intervals. Synthetic experiments assess the certificate using admissible continuous completions and examine the role of path regularity.

</details>


### [163] [When Latent Dynamics Meet Data Assimilation: A Benchmark of Koopman-Type Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03916)
*Zhiwei Zhang,Wenxuan Yuan,Xiaoyuan Cheng,Pouria Behnoudfar,Chuanqi Chen,Charlotte Moser,Yuta Tarumi,Jin-Long Wu,Rossella Arcucci,Takemasa Miyoshi,Nan Chen,Marc Bocquet,Sibo Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了Koopman表示在深度潜变量数据同化中的实际效益，发现表示灵活性与优化可解性之间存在权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koopman表示以线性演化与通用性见长，但作者质疑这些理论优势能否真正转化为深度潜变量数据同化（LDA）中的性能提升，故需系统性验证。

Method: 在5个系统（约周期到强混沌、状态维度跨度大）上对比7种代表性方法与结构基线，评估预测与同化精度、物理状态恢复、潜轨迹行为、动力学一致性，并考察高斯误差假设、目标凸性与分析解唯一性。

Result: 潜状态线性演化可稳定短期预测，但在强混沌与高维系统中限制表达能力，且未一致提升同化精度；参数化线性算子更灵活但与编解码器联合优化非凸；具闭式分析更新的方法表现更优更稳健，但矩阵求逆是计算瓶颈。

Conclusion: Koopman结构的实际收益取决于充分的观测信息与同化流程的选择，而非仅依赖线性演化结构本身。

Abstract: Koopman representations describe nonlinear dynamics through linear evolution in a transformed space, offering an attractive framework for their linear simplicity and generality. Yet these advantages do not necessarily translate into improved performance in deep latent data assimilation (LDA). We systematically compare seven representative methods and structural baselines across five systems spanning approximately periodic to strongly chaotic dynamics and a broad range of state dimensions. We evaluate forecast and assimilation accuracy, physical state recovery, latent-trajectory behavior, and dynamical consistency, and assess Gaussian error assumptions, objective convexity, and uniqueness of analysis solutions. Our results reveal a trade-off between representational flexibility and optimization tractability. Linear evolution of the latent state can stabilize short-term forecasts but restrict expressiveness in strongly chaotic and high-dimensional systems, without consistently improving assimilation accuracy. Parameterized linear operators offer greater flexibility, yet their joint optimization with the encoder--decoder remains nonconvex. Methods with closed-form analysis updates generally perform better and exhibit greater robustness in our experiments, although matrix inversion remains a computational bottleneck. These findings show that the practical benefits of Koopman structure depend on both sufficient observational information and the choice of assimilation procedure.

</details>


### [164] [Sequential auditing of connected forecast errors under partial observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03928)
*Yuanbo Zhou,Junjie Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究在标签部分缺失的图时间序列预测中，审计者如何选择性查询缺失结果，以判定是否存在一个连通节点组，其真实值同时落在各自预测区间的同一侧（同向违反）。MOMS在30%标签隐藏下相比筛选随机采集减少19.6%–38.5%的平均查询量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图预测系统用预测区间刻画相连位置的不确定性，但用于评估的真实结果往往只能部分观测，因此需要以尽量少的查询完成对"同向违反连通组是否存在"这一事件的判定，并厘清查询效率、早期事件发现与局部计算成本之间的关系。

Method: 将问题建模为带查询预算的序贯审计：每步选择待检索标签，当已观测标签足以判定事件或预算耗尽时停止。方法包括MOMS采集策略、shortest witness（最短见证）策略，以及直接分量遍历（direct component traversal），后者无需枚举连通子集即可复现支路。

Result: 在三个公开图时间序列数据集上，30%标签隐藏时MOMS相对screened random acquisition将平均查询数降低19.6%–38.5%；在METR-LA局部化掩码下，shortest witness在十次查询内发现更多正例，但差异依赖节点排序；直接分量遍历复现了全部9,696条screened random trace而无需枚举连通子集。

Conclusion: 减少判定所需查询并不必然带来更早的事件发现，也未必降低局部计算开销；查询效率、发现速度与计算成本需分别评估。

Abstract: Graph forecasting systems often rely on prediction intervals to quantify uncertainty across connected locations, but the true outcomes needed for evaluation may be only partially observed. We study how an auditor can selectively retrieve missing outcomes to determine whether there exists a connected group whose outcomes violate the same side of their respective forecast intervals. The audit stops when the observed labels determine the event or the query budget is exhausted. On three public graph time series, MOMS reduces mean queries by 19.6% to 38.5% relative to screened random acquisition when 30% of labels are hidden. Under localized masking on METR-LA, shortest witness finds more positive cases within ten queries, although the difference depends on node ordering. Direct component traversal reproduces all 9,696 screened random traces without enumerating connected subsets. The experiments show that reducing queries to a decision does not necessarily improve early event discovery or reduce local computation.

</details>


### [165] [Accelerating FastDEC Clustering Algorithm with GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03934)
*Mi Li,Eibe Frank,Bernhard Pfahringer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SymWarp-FastDEC，一种 GPU 加速的 FastDEC 实现，核心为利用搜索对称性的精确自 k-NN 核 SymTrikNN 与 warp 并行支配分量归约算法 WP-DC，在保证聚类结果等价的前提下获得显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FastDEC 通过基于支配的密度估计获得高质量聚类，但精确 k-NN 构建与支配分量（DC）处理的计算开销大，成为性能瓶颈，限制了其可扩展性。

Method: 1) SymTrikNN：精确自 k-NN 核，利用搜索对称性采用上三角遍历，并配合 warp 协作式 top-k 选择；2) WP-DC：面向现代 GPU 的 warp 并行 DC 归约算法，消除 DC 归约瓶颈；3) 结合内存自适应执行规划与流调度。

Result: 在所有等价性基准上与原始 FastDEC 输出完全一致；相较使用 FAISS GpuIndexFlatL2 做精确 k-NN 的 FastDEC 变体，端到端性能提升约 1.7x–4.9x，且优势随维度增加而扩大；相较单线程 Python FastDEC，在高维（k-d 树退化）情形下加速约 2.5×10^4（作者声明此为极端个例，不具代表性）。

Conclusion: 通过对称性利用与 warp 级并行设计，可在不改变聚类结果的前提下有效加速 FastDEC；主要贡献在于精确 k-NN 与 DC 归约两个瓶颈的 GPU 化，其中高维场景收益尤为明显。

Abstract: Fast Dominance Estimation Clustering (FastDEC) is an efficient variant of Density Peak Clustering (DPC) that achieves high clustering quality through dominance-based density estimation, but remains constrained by the computational cost of exact k-nearest-neighbor (k-NN) construction and dominance component (DC) processing. We present SymWarp-FastDEC, a GPU-accelerated implementation of FastDEC. At its core is SymTrikNN, an exact self-k-NN kernel that exploits search symmetry using upper-triangular traversal and warp-cooperative top-k selection. We also introduce WP-DC, a warp-parallel DC reduction algorithm designed for modern GPUs. Combined with memory-adaptive execution planning and stream scheduling, WP-DC eliminates DC reduction as a runtime bottleneck. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show that SymWarp-FastDEC produces clustering outputs identical to the original FastDEC on all evaluated equivalence benchmarks. Compared with a FastDEC variant using FAISS GpuIndexFlatL2 for exact k-NN search, our approach achieves approximately 1.7x to 4.9x higher end-to-end performance, with the advantage generally increasing with dimensionality. Compared with the original single-threaded Python FastDEC implementation, speedups reach approximately 2.5 x 10^4 in a very high-dimensional case where its k-d-tree-based search degrades; this extreme result is not representative of typical acceleration.

</details>


### [166] [Auditing Multi-Proxy Explanations of Web User Preferences: Identification Boundaries and Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04997)
*Yu Zhang,Zhihan Wang,Yuhang Lu,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出区分点识别、部分识别与弃权的审计框架，在特征依赖、任意符号的代理偏差下证明"严格多数未污染代理归因→逐坐标中位数可恢复潜在归因"的阈值并给出尖锐极小极大分析；冻结已知真值基准与亚马逊时间分离研究支持该结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web系统从行为、评分、文本及LLM生成画像推断用户偏好，但对代理（proxy）的解释可能揭示的是测量过程本身而非所关注的偏好；因此需要一套能刻画"何时可下结论、何时只能部分识别、何时应弃权"的审计方法。

Method: 构建相对显式参考分布的审计框架（点识别／部分识别／弃权）；在特征依赖且符号任意的代理偏差假设下，用未污染代理归因的逐坐标中位数恢复潜在归因的支持方向（外部合理正尺度下恢复幅度）；以响应有效的不可区分世界证明阈值尖锐性；刻画近似洁净与尺度不确定下的尖锐极小极大误差与精确相容目标集，并传播清洁代理归因误差。实验采用冻结已知真值基准（2个响应族、4个代理数量、全部污染计数、3种偏差强度、11种方法，焦点比较在20个未见种子上确认）及时间分离的亚马逊研究。

Result: 正确预算下，直接归因张量审计达到100%覆盖率、95.40%–97.05%的活跃驱动符号判定、无误判符号；亚马逊研究中中位数聚合在四个目标上均降低相对比较器的条件符号误差，8个预设目标-指标对比中6个Bonferroni区间排除零；受控真实协变量扰动在预设焦点指标上四个目标均支持中位数法。相容集显示现有代理配置很少允许严格符号结论。

Conclusion: 代理归因的可解释性应被表述为"识别＋弃权"的审计问题而非无条件点估计；严格多数阈值提供了可证明的恢复条件与尖锐性能界。鉴于比较器校准较弱，亚马逊证据仅支持拟合曲面对齐而非潜在偏好恢复，后续需更强校准以检验真实偏好恢复。

Abstract: Web systems infer user preferences from behavior, ratings, text, and LLM-generated profiles, yet explaining a proxy may reveal its measurement process rather than the preference of interest. We develop an audit framework distinguishing point identification, partial identification, and abstention relative to an explicit reference distribution. Under feature-dependent, arbitrarily signed proxy bias, a strict majority of uncontaminated proxy attributions lets the coordinatewise median recover latent-attribution support and direction and, with externally justified positive scales, magnitude. We prove this threshold sharp using response-valid indistinguishable worlds, characterize sharp minimax error and exact compatible-target sets under approximate cleanliness and scale uncertainty, and propagate clean-proxy attribution error. A frozen known-truth benchmark spans two response families, four proxy counts, all contamination counts, three bias strengths, and eleven methods, with a focal comparison confirmed on twenty unseen seeds. Under correct budgets, a direct attribution-tensor audit achieved 100% coverage, 95.40%-97.05% active-driver sign decisions, and no false signs. In a temporally separated Amazon study, median aggregation reduced comparator-relative conditional sign error for all four targets; six of eight prespecified target-metric contrasts had Bonferroni intervals excluding zero. A controlled real-covariate perturbation favored the median on the prespecified focal metric for all four targets. Compatible sets showed that the current proxy configuration rarely licensed strict-sign conclusions. Weak comparator calibration limits the Amazon evidence to fitted-surface alignment rather than latent-preference recovery.

</details>


### [167] [P$^2$-MOPD: Accelerating Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation via Parameter Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03953)
*Sinuo Wang,Jiacheng Lu,Zeyu Zhang,Maojia Song,Rui Sun,Beidi Luan,Yutong Xie,Jing Li,Daxin Jiang,Qi Wu,Zuo Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 P²-MOPD，将学生与专家参数的线性组合视为单一可微模型，在同一 on-policy 蒸馏目标下联合优化混合系数与学生权重，兼顾 task arithmetic 的无训练式参数组合与多教师蒸馏的灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多教师 on-policy 蒸馏虽能整合专家能力，但需训练；task arithmetic 虽无训练，但混合系数只能停留在冻结学生与专家参数的张成空间内，表达能力受限。作者希望在保留 on-policy 蒸馏目标的同时，获得可训练的参数组合自由度。

Method: 将学生参数与专家参数的线性组合整体作为一个可微模型，联合优化混合系数与学生权重，共用同一 on-policy 蒸馏目标；学生保持可训练，专家仅作为参数先验，不将其限制在参数张成空间内。

Result: 在 1.7B 与 4B 规模、四个领域上，P²-MOPD 从相同初始化出发比 MOPD 恢复更快、更均衡，用一半训练步数即超过 MOPD 的最终平均恢复率；训练结束时 1.7B 模型平均恢复率达 94.7%，MOPD 为 80.9%。

Conclusion: 把参数线性组合的可微化与 on-policy 蒸馏联合优化，可在不牺牲可训练性的前提下利用专家先验，显著提升多领域能力恢复的效率与最终效果。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation consolidates expert capabilities by supervising student-generated trajectories, whereas task arithmetic offers a training-free alternative through linear parameter combinations. We view the linear combination of student and expert parameters as a single differentiable model and propose P$^2$-MOPD, which jointly optimizes the mixing coefficients and the student weights under the same on-policy distillation objective. Unlike task arithmetic, which stays within the span of the frozen student and expert parameters for any choice of merging coefficients, P$^2$-MOPD keeps the student trainable, so the experts act as parameter priors without confining it to this span. Across four domains at 1.7B and 4B scales, P$^2$-MOPD achieves faster and more balanced recovery than MOPD from the same initialization, surpassing its final average recovery in half the training steps. At the end of training, our 1.7B model reaches 94.7% average recovery, versus 80.9% for MOPD.

</details>


### [168] [Forget What Comes Next: LLM Unlearning via Statistical Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03955)
*Andrii Shkabrii,Claudia Plant,Martin Pawelczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PINT：基于HSIC的机器遗忘算法，通过降低待遗忘token与内部表示之间的统计依赖实现遗忘，并与精确记忆(Exact Memorization)建立可计算上界联系；在8种设定、最大70B模型上取得最佳遗忘-效用权衡，且是唯一使高斯毒化样本不可检测的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM会记忆并复现训练数据片段，带来隐私与法律风险，因此需要机器遗忘。但被投毒的样本在遗忘后仍可被统计检测出来，说明现有遗忘目标可能并未真正去除模型已记忆的内容，需要重新审视遗忘目标的有效性。

Method: PINT使用Hilbert-Schmidt独立性准则(HSIC)来降低待遗忘token与其用于预测的内部表示之间的统计依赖性；并从理论上通过一个可计算的上界将HSIC目标与Exact Memorization关联，把训练目标直接与模型复现遗忘token的能力联系起来。

Result: 在8种设定、最高7B参数模型上比较遗忘-效用权衡，并验证可扩展至70B模型；评测覆盖高斯毒化、个人信息、生物与网络安全领域危险知识、受版权保护的书籍与新闻文章。PINT平均取得最强的遗忘-效用权衡，且是唯一使高斯毒化不可检测同时保持接近原始效用的方法。

Conclusion: 以统计独立性为核心的HSIC目标能更彻底地移除记忆内容，PINT在多种遗忘场景下兼顾遗忘效果与模型效用，并具备良好扩展性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can memorize and reproduce fragments of their training data, raising privacy and legal concerns that motivate machine unlearning. Yet poisoned training examples can remain statistically detectable after unlearning, which raises the question of whether current unlearning objectives remove what the model has memorized at all. In this work, we introduce PINT, a novel algorithm for machine unlearning that uses the Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion (HSIC) to reduce the statistical dependence between tokens to be forgotten and the internal representations used to predict them. We formally relate our HSIC-based objective to Exact Memorization through a computable upper bound, connecting the training objective directly to the model's ability to reproduce the forgotten tokens. In extensive experiments, we compare forgetting-utility trade-offs across eight settings with models up to 7B parameters and demonstrate scalability to 70B parameter models. Our evaluations cover unlearning Gaussian poisons, personal information, hazardous knowledge in biology and cybersecurity, and copyrighted books and news articles. PINT achieves the strongest forgetting-utility trade-off on average and is the only method to make Gaussian poisons undetectable while maintaining near-original model utility. Code is available at: https://github.com/shkabrii/PINT

</details>


### [169] [Sampling Interventional Distributions with Orthogonal MMD Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03962)
*Bariscan Bozkurt,Hugh Dance,Zonghao Chen,Alexandre Galashov,Arthur Gretton,Houssam Zenati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 IMMD-Flow：用核均值嵌入表示干预后分布，配合双稳健 Neyman 正交估计与 MMD 梯度流采样，支持硬/软干预与高维图像结局，速度快于生成式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果推断中常需从干预后结局分布采样，而非仅估计低维汇总统计量。现有方法需拟合目标分布生成模型及额外 nuisance 组件，在高维结局下计算代价高。

Method: 基于粒子的方法：以核均值嵌入表征目标干预分布；该嵌入具有双稳健、Neyman 正交的估计量并提供有限样本保证；再通过 MMD 梯度流从该嵌入高效采样。方法兼容硬干预与软干预，并借助冻结编码器扩展到高维图像结局。

Result: 在图像基准上，IMMD-Flow 与最先进生成式方法相当或更优，且具有显著速度优势；同时给出估计与粒子近似的理论保证。

Conclusion: 以核均值嵌入为核心的粒子式框架可兼具统计稳健性与采样效率，为高维干预分布采样提供了可替代生成模型的实用方案。

Abstract: Many causal inference problems require sampling from interventional outcome distributions rather than estimating low-dimensional summaries. Existing approaches often rely on fitting generative models of the target distribution together with additional nuisance components, which can be computationally demanding for high-dimensional outcomes. We propose Interventional Maximum Mean Discrepancy Flow (IMMD-Flow), a particle-based method that instead represents the target interventional distribution through its kernel mean embedding. This kernel embedding admits a doubly robust, Neyman-orthogonal estimator with finite-sample guarantees and can be efficiently reused to sample from the interventional distribution via MMD gradient flow. IMMD-Flow accommodates both hard and soft interventions, admits theoretical guarantees for estimation and particle approximation, and extends to high-dimensional image outcomes through frozen encoders. Across image-based benchmarks, IMMD-Flow matches or improves upon state-of-the-art generative approaches while showing significant speed advantages.

</details>


### [170] [Ermakov--Based embeddings as a parameter-free layer for physics-informed neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03964)
*Roberto Bernal-Jaquez,Roberto Romero Huerta,Alexander Schaum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将经典 Ermakov–Pinney 尺度函数 ρ 以无参数嵌入方式写入 PINN 输入层，用统一构造覆盖三类含时系数演化方程，并给出各族问题对照式消融解剖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含时系数演化方程迫使神经求解器把容量浪费在方程本身已决定的尺度上；作者希望把这一确定尺度从学习中剥离，通过经典尺度变量实现与解无关的架构化约化。

Method: 以 Pinney 常数 c 的两个符号统一三类问题：线性扩散（c=-1, ω≡0）、含时束缚势阱中的扩散（c=-1, ω≠0）、含时频率量子振子（c=+1）。利用精确矩恒等式仅由初始数据得到 ρ 的闭式表达，无需辅助网络或解信息；对每族问题分别设计诚实的对照组并做消融。

Result: 族 I 中仅扩散时间重参数化即可复现大部分增益，说明该族不适合作检验；族 II 不存在时间重参数化，对照组反而降低精度，振幅前因子至关重要（40.8x）；族 III 绝热近似失效，二次相位因子是关键（5.7x）。当约化解不收敛到不动点时，外推优势消失、方法失效。

Conclusion: 约化本身是自相似渐近文献中的经典尺度变量变换；新意在于使该约化对含时系数"架构化"的矩恒等式，以及上述比较性解剖。结论强调基线选择与消融的必要性，并明确方法的适用边界。

Abstract: Evolution equations with time-dependent coefficients force a neural solver to spend capacity on a scale that the equation already determines. We show that a single classical object, the Ermakov-Pinney scale function $ρ$, can be written into the input layer of a physics-informed neural network as a parameter-free embedding. One construction covers three problem families through the two signs of the Pinney constant $c$: linear diffusion ($c=-1$, $ω\equiv0$), diffusion in a time-dependent confining trap ($c=-1$, $ω\neq0$), and the quantum oscillator with time-dependent frequency ($c=+1$). An exact moment identity gives $ρ$ in closed form from the initial data alone, so no auxiliary network or knowledge of the solution is needed. Our central finding is comparative: each family requires a different component of the construction and has a different honest control. In family I, the diffusive-time reparametrisation alone reproduces most of the gain, so that family is a poor test of the idea and shows why an ablation is mandatory. In family II no time reparametrisation exists, the control degrades accuracy, and the amplitude prefactor becomes essential (40.8x). In family III the adiabatic approximation also fails, and the quadratic phase factor is what matters (5.7x). The method does not help when the reduced solution does not converge to a fixed point: its extrapolation advantage then disappears. The reduction itself is the classical scaling-variable change of the self-similar-asymptotics literature; what is new is the moment identity that makes it architectural for time-dependent coefficients, and the comparative anatomy above.

</details>
