### [171] [Stacking-Based Weighting for Large Language Bayes in M-Open Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03966)
*Anant Bhide,Akarsh Gupta,Edmond Cunningham,Justin Domke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向 LLB 的 stacking 聚合方法，用变分下界构造可处理的 ELBO-based stacking 目标，替代边际似然加权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLB 由自然语言描述生成多个候选概率程序并做贝叶斯模型平均，但候选模型集常存在误设，基于似然的聚合会过度集中到单一模型，低估跨模型不确定性。

Method: 以预测性能而非边际似然优化模型权重（stacking），保留原有语言模型驱动的模型生成与模型内推断流程；针对隐变量模型中留一预测量不可解的问题，推导变分下界，得到基于 ELBO 的可处理 stacking 目标。

Result: 在真实概率推断任务上，预测性能与贝叶斯模型平均相当，同时模型权重更分散，在误设情况下降低了对单一候选模型的集中。

Conclusion: stacking 聚合可在不改变 LLB 生成-推断管线的前提下，缓解似然聚合的过度集中问题，更好地体现候选模型间不确定性。

Abstract: Large Language Bayes (LLB) transforms a natural language problem description into a collection of candidate probabilistic programs and performs inference within each model, combining their predictions using Bayesian model averaging. While this provides a flexible framework for probabilistic inference without manual model specification, the resulting candidate model set is often misspecified in practice, and likelihood-based aggregation can concentrate on a single model, under-representing uncertainty across the sampled models. We propose a stacking-based aggregation method for LLB that selects model weights by optimizing predictive performance rather than marginal likelihood, while preserving the original language-model-driven model generation and within-model inference pipeline. Because leave-one-out predictive quantities are intractable in latent-variable models, we derive a variational lower bound that yields a tractable ELBO-based stacking objective. Across real-world probabilistic inference tasks, our method achieves comparable predictive performance to Bayesian model averaging while yielding more dispersed model weights, reducing concentration on a single candidate model under misspecification.

</details>


### [172] [One Mistake, Three Layers: Persistence Rules Under Depth Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03968)
*Maryam Rahimimovassagh,Michael Brown,Sanjeda Sara Jennifer,Niloofar Yousefi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出现行的"连续若干层超过阈值即判定信号开始可解码"的onset读取规则在层间相关下失效（合成曲线上提前19层），提出以置换构造零轨迹、逐层序统计量阈值与校准化持续性要求的误差可控估计器，并在一冻结co-folding trunk的pocket信息探针上复现该失败、给出累积深度作为判别指标，同时报告某公开checkpoint缺失128个trunk参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐层探测冻结表征已成常规，但"信号首次可解码"的判定依赖对深度曲线的读取规则；常用规则（连续K层超零校准阈值）未考虑相邻层分数相关这一在神经影像学中已知的风险，可能导致严重偏早的onset估计且无法自检。

Method: 1) 在已知真值的合成曲线上量化三阶段规则的偏差；2) 提出校准型估计器：在每个评估单元内置换标签并将置换固定贯穿所有深度以构造保留相关结构的零轨迹，采用逐层序统计量作为阈值，持续性要求亦经校准而非人为选定；3) 应用于冻结co-folding trunk的pocket membership探针，按蛋白家族与pocket熟悉度分层1,820个cutoff后靶标、四种regime、48个block，比较onset与预声明的累积深度指标；4) 对公开checkpoint做参数加载一致性检查。

Result: 合成曲线上三阶段规则在类Transformer深度相关下提前19层报出onset，且每次重采样均返回onset。真实数据上五阶段规则在零轨迹上的触发率为4–7%（名义5%）、三阶段为12–17%，复现合成失败。pocket membership在全部48个block、四种regime下均超阈值可解码，故onset无判别力；预声明累积深度有判别力，且在四种特征集中均以"熟悉家族+结构新颖pocket"最晚；该regime亦最弱，差距在控制长度、pocket度量与比对深度后仍成立。另发现某广泛使用的公开co-folding checkpoint在其代码下加载时缺失128个trunk参数，将被随机初始化并使所提取表征部分含噪而表面正常。

Conclusion: 深度曲线的onset读取规则需以保留层间相关的置换零分布进行误差校准，否则系统性偏早且具误导性；在冻结co-folding trunk上pocket信息近乎全程可解码，onset不具区分度，累积深度等聚合统计量更适合作判别量；表征研究报告应包含checkpoint完整性核验，参数缺失会以不可见方式污染结论。

Abstract: Probing frozen representations layer by layer is now standard practice, but reporting when a signal first becomes decodable requires a rule for reading a depth curve, and the obvious rule is wrong. On synthetic curves with known ground truth, requiring a probe score to exceed a null-calibrated threshold for three consecutive stages reports an onset nineteen stages early under transformer-like depth correlation, with every resample returning an onset. The failure follows from correlation between neighboring layers, a hazard known in neuroimaging but not, to our knowledge, checked for depth curves.
  We give an estimator whose error rate is calibrated rather than assumed. Null trajectories are built by permuting labels within each evaluation unit and holding each permutation fixed across depth, preserving the correlation structure the rule must survive; thresholds are per-layer order statistics; and the persistence requirement is calibrated rather than chosen.
  Applied to pocket information in a frozen co-folding trunk, stratifying 1,820 post-cutoff targets by protein-family and pocket familiarity, the five-stage rule fires on 4 to 7 per cent of null trajectories against a nominal 5 per cent and the three-stage rule on 12 to 17: the synthetic failure reproduces on real data. Pocket membership is decodable above threshold at all 48 blocks in all four regimes, so the onset cannot discriminate them; the pre-declared accumulation depth does, and is latest for familiar families with structurally novel pockets in every feature set. That regime is also the weakest, by a margin surviving controls for length, pocket measure and alignment depth.
  Separately, a widely used public co-folding checkpoint loads against its own code with 128 trunk parameters missing; those would be randomly initialized, and every representation extracted would be partly noise while appearing normal.

</details>


### [173] [Rapid Mean-Field Formation Transport with a Shared-Representation Neural Fokker-Planck Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03981)
*Zejian Zhou,Hao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种低在线计算代价的均场编队输运方法：用统一参数化非线性映射同时输出密度与控制，离线优化参数、在线固定系数，从而避免反复求解耦合前后向PDE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典均场规划器需反复求解耦合的正向与反向偏微分方程，在线重规划计算负担大，难以用于大规模多智能体系统的实时编队输运。

Method: 以同一参数化非线性映射近似密度与控制（标量密度输出+向量控制输出）；利用Fokker–Planck残差与初始条件残差、在数值仿真闭环粒子轨迹上的编队匹配代价以及控制代价联合优化参数；计算限制于状态空间有界区域，并对边界概率通量施加惩罚；参数在在线运行前优化并保持固定。对周期域模型证明诱导密度演化的适定性与密度逼近误差界（由动力学与初值残差刻画）。

Result: 实验量化了相对均场规划器的精度–运行时间权衡，显示输运误差低于经典反馈基线，并验证了统一参数化带来的收益。

Conclusion: 统一参数化+离线优化可显著降低在线计算量，同时保持编队输运精度，为大规模多智能体系统提供可行的实时均场输运方案。

Abstract: This paper develops a mean-field formation transport method with low online computational cost for large-scale multi-agent systems. Classical mean-field planners often require repeated solutions of coupled forward and backward partial differential equations, making online replanning computationally demanding. Density and control are therefore approximated by a common parameterized nonlinear map with separate scalar density and vector control outputs. Parameters are optimized using Fokker--Planck and initial-condition residuals, formation-matching costs on numerically simulated closed-loop particle trajectories, and control effort. Numerical computations are restricted to a bounded region of the state space, with an additional penalty on probability flux across its boundary. For the periodic-domain model, we establish well-posedness of the induced density evolution and bound the density-approximation error in terms of the dynamics and initial-condition residuals. Parameter optimization precedes online operation, during which controller coefficients remain fixed. Experiments quantify the accuracy--runtime trade-off relative to a mean-field planner, show lower transport error than classical feedback baselines, and demonstrate the benefits of the common parameterization.

</details>


### [174] [Physics-Informed Identification of Tensegrity Prestress across Taut-Slack Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04007)
*Sandesh Lamsal,Sumit Kumar,Rubi Bhandari,Subhamoy Sen,Andrzej Rutkiewicz,Leszek Małyszko,Landolf Rhode-Barbarigos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将张力集成结构预应力识别重新表述为逆问题，在约束笛卡尔坐标下用运动方程残差训练神经网络，保留仅受拉索法则，并结合Fisher信息分析预先判定成员参数可辨识性，在仿真与实测数据上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预应力决定张力集成结构的稳定性、刚度与动力学行为，但无法从稀疏测量直接观测且服役中可能下降；强激励下索松弛导致动力学非光滑，现有识别方法仅适用于张紧状态，而物理信息网络只解决正向预测问题。

Method: 在约束笛卡尔坐标下构建运动方程残差（质量矩阵恒定、力密度线性进入），将力密度、阻尼与轴向刚度设为可训练参数，并保持仅受拉索本构；利用Fisher信息分析在反演前判断记录可确定的成员参数；在仿真平面框架（至多五模块）与三维棱柱，以及仿生腿和足尺张力集成单形的实测记录上测试。

Result: 单模块框架上，在自由坐标一半的含噪加速度下，可辨识力密度误差在1.5%内，无论有无张紧-松弛转换；含转换记录可确定张紧记录无法确定的成员。五模块16索双跨桥中位力密度误差升至4.2%。实测中弹簧刚度平均误差0.1%内，单形13级预压与索力传感器对比RMSE为2.9%。

Conclusion: 该逆问题框架能在非光滑张紧-松弛动力学下从稀疏含噪测量中辨识预应力等参数，Fisher信息可先验指导可辨识性，仿真与实测均表明其精度良好，具备工程实用性。

Abstract: Prestress governs the stability, stiffness, and dynamic behavior of tensegrity structures, but it is not directly observable from sparse structural measurements, and it may decrease during service life. Under strong excitation, the cables may slacken and the dynamics become nonsmooth. Existing identification methods are limited to the taut regime, and physics-informed networks for tensegrities solve only the forward problem of predicting the response from known member parameters. This study formulates the inverse problem. Neural networks are trained on the equation-of-motion residual in constrained Cartesian coordinates, where the mass matrix is constant and the force densities enter linearly. The force densities, damping, and axial stiffness are trainable parameters, and the tension-only cable law is kept. A Fisher-information analysis shows, before inversion, which member parameters a record can determine. The method is tested on simulated planar tensegrity frames with up to five modules and a three-dimensional prism, and on measured records of a biomimetic leg and a full-scale tensegrity simplex. On the one-module frame, the identifiable force densities are recovered within 1.5% from noisy accelerations at half the free coordinates, on records with and without taut-slack transitions. Records with transitions determine members that taut records leave undetermined. On the five-module frame, a 16-cable two-span bridge, the median force-density error rises moderately, to 4.2%. On measured records, an identified spring stiffness is within 0.1% of its independently measured value on average, and the identified simplex prestress at 13 levels agrees with its cable force transducers to 2.9% root-mean-square.

</details>


### [175] [Decision Transformer with Task-Aware Representations for Offline Meta-Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04010)
*Xinbo Zhao,Yuhang Liu,Xin Zhang,Lei Le,Zhaohan Xi,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoTA-DT：基于Decision Transformer的离线元强化学习框架，通过对比学习、语言对齐、世界模型一步监督与Q函数长期监督联合学习任务表征，并以预训练LLM作为共享骨干，实现零样本/少样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线元RL泛化依赖对潜在任务表征的准确推断，以提供紧凑、可迁移的任务特征摘要来指导策略生成；现有方法对任务表征的监督信号不足、跨任务迁移能力有限。

Method: 提出CoTA-DT，四类互补监督联合学习任务表征：(i) 离线轨迹对比学习；(ii) 与自然语言任务描述对齐；(iii) 任务条件世界模型的一步监督；(iv) 任务条件Q函数的长期监督。学得的表征作为DT策略的条件信号实现任务感知元策略；设计语言grounded的自适应策略支持零样本/少样本适应；预训练LLM作为语言编码、世界建模、Q估计与策略学习的共享骨干。

Result: 在多个离线元RL基准上一致优于先前方法，在具有挑战性的零样本泛化场景中优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: 多源监督联合学习任务表征、LLM统一骨干的参数共享设计可有效提升离线元RL的效率与泛化能力。

Abstract: To enable effective generalization in offline meta-RL, it is essential to accurately infer the underlying task through latent task representations, which provide a compact and transferable summary of task-specific characteristics for guiding policy generation. In this paper, we propose Contrastive Task-Aware Decision Transformer (CoTA-DT), a DT-based offline meta-RL framework that learns task representations by jointly integrating four complementary components: (i) contrastive learning from offline trajectories, (ii) alignment with natural-language task descriptions, (iii) one-step supervision via a task-conditioned world model, and (iv) long-term supervision via a task-conditioned Q function. The learned task representations are used as conditioning signals for a DT-based policy, enabling task-aware meta-policy learning. To further support generalization in both zero-shot and few-shot settings, we design a language-grounded, self-adaptive strategy that enables effective and reliable adaptation to unseen tasks. Notably, in CoTA-DT, the pretrained LLM serves as a shared backbone for language encoding, world modeling, Q-function estimation, and policy learning. This unified design facilitates parameter sharing, improving both efficiency and generalization. Extensive experiments across diverse offline meta-RL benchmarks demonstrate that CoTA-DT consistently outperforms prior methods, particularly in challenging zero-shot generalization scenarios.

</details>


### [176] [Denoising Is Stein: Moment Access and a Resource Hierarchy for Learning Hidden Structure in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04043)
*Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究含隐藏非高斯成分的数据下的去噪对分数学习：协方差可见时可直取子空间；协方差各向同性时须从干净点及其噪声视图提取首个区分矩（阶为 k*），并给出所需样本量、特征数与 SGD 指数，以及达成固定 score gain 的两阶段学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清去噪分数学习在 data 含有隐藏非高斯成分时的可识别性与样本复杂度：明确何时可由协方差直接恢复成分，何时必须依赖更高阶矩，并刻画达到该矩所需的模型宽度、样本量与优化代价。

Method: 将去噪损失中的相关项解释为相对基线的 Stein 散度，对多项式场精确对应相应的矩差；在干净点条件下对新鲜噪声视图求平均，得到干净分布上的精确矩查询。分两种情形分析：协方差匹配的 regime 下分析满足 L^4/L^2 条件的朝向无关字典（需 d^{k*-1} 个特征）、点积核岭回归（同幂次样本）及单单元小步长在线 SGD（指数至对数因子）；并提出两阶段学习器——先用平滑去噪损失的曲率恢复隐藏方向，再锐化并拟合标量读出。对协方差可见 regime，给出冻结特征阈值 d^J/J!，并说明投影到主成分可使拟合模型、score risk 与生成律分布与环境的维数无关。

Result: 对每个偶数 k*，两阶段学习器用 O(d^{k*/2} log d) 个干净点即可相对高斯基线获得固定的 score gain。协方差匹配实验测得的 partial-trace overlap 与所提谱相变一致；宽度为 8 的网络在干净点复用下，在 d=64–192 上表现出类似 d^2 的训练集阈值，且验证与校准开销固定。

Conclusion: 隐藏非高斯成分的分数学习复杂度由首个区分矩的阶 k* 主导：矩阶越高，所需特征数/样本数以 d^{k*-1} 或 d^{k*/2} 形式增长；两阶段曲率引导方法在多项式级样本下即可获得非平凡增益，理论与实验在谱相变与阈值标度上相互印证。

Abstract: We study score learning from denoising pairs when the data contain a hidden non-Gaussian component. If covariance reveals the component, its subspace can be recovered directly. If covariance is isotropic, the first distinguishing moment has order $k^\ast$, and the learner must extract that moment from clean points and their noisy views.
  Correlations in the denoising loss are Stein discrepancies against a baseline; for polynomial fields they determine exactly the corresponding moment differences. Averaging fresh noisy views conditional on a clean point gives an exact moment query on the clean distribution. In the covariance-matched regime, orientation-independent dictionaries satisfying an $L^4/L^2$ condition need order $d^{k^\ast-1}$ features, and the stated dot-product kernel ridge models need the same power in samples. Small-step online SGD on one unit has this exponent up to logarithms. A two-stage learner uses the curvature of a smoothed denoising loss to recover the hidden direction, then sharpens it and fits a scalar readout. For every even $k^\ast$, it obtains a fixed score gain over the Gaussian baseline from $O(d^{k^\ast/2}\log d)$ clean points. In the covariance-visible regime, the frozen-feature threshold occurs at $d^J/J!$, while projection onto principal components makes the fitted-model, score-risk and generated-law distributions independent of ambient dimension.
  In the covariance-matched experiments, the measured partial-trace overlaps are consistent with the proposed spectral transition. A width-8 network trained with clean-point reuse shows a $d^2$-like training-set threshold over $d=64$--$192$, with fixed validation and calibration overhead.

</details>


### [177] [Structured Priors: Towards an algebra of component priors for prior-data fitted networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04056)
*Thomas Bartz-Beielstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出"结构化先验"替代PFN单一人造生成器：以测度论框架定义混合、卷积、幅度缩放、局部变换与投影五类算子并形式化证明其代数律，实现于macl2l软件包，经实验验证生成器复现恒等式、训练网络服从分配律、小分量混合先验在8个回归基准中7个不劣于单一分量，并在两个数据集上以1/9预算达到通用随机函数先验全预算仍达不到的误差；模型平均定理表明分量网络若为各自先验的贝叶斯预测器则其平均为混合先验的贝叶斯预测器，但预注册检验因网络过少识别生成分量而失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有先验数据拟合网络（PFN）依赖单一手工构造的生成器；当生成器含多组件时（如TabPFN、Mitra、TabICLv2），只能以人工指定权重的混合方式组合，权重对网络的影响须待预训练后才可见，缺乏可控性与可解释性。作者希望以具形式化代数保证的结构化组件先验替代手工生成器，使先验设计与训练效果之间建立可预测、可验证的联系。

Method: 在测度论框架下提出结构化先验：由一组具名的分量先验构成，通过混合、卷积、幅度缩放、局部变换、投影五类算子连接；形式化证明这些算子的支配代数律；在macl2l软件包中实现相应生成器；以置换检验验证恒等式复现精度；训练网络检验分配律；在八个公开回归基准上对比混合先验与各单一分量；提出并预注册检验模型平均定理（分量网络为各自先验的贝叶斯预测器时，其平均即混合先验的贝叶斯预测器）。

Result: 实现的生成器在置换检验分辨率内复现所证恒等式；训练后的网络服从分配律；由小分量构成的混合先验在8个回归基准中的7个上不劣于或优于任一单分量；在两个表格数据上，结构化先验以九分之一的数据预算即达到通用随机函数先验用满预算仍无法达到的误差；然而，针对平均预测器的预注册检验失败，原因是训练网络对生成分量的识别率过低。

Conclusion: 结构化先验为PFN提供了一种可形式化验证、可组合且实证有效的先验构造范式，在数据效率上显著优于通用随机函数先验；模型平均的理论保证虽成立，但受制于网络对分量识别的能力，未能实证兑现。作者据此提出以"结构化先验代数"为核心的研究纲领。

Abstract: Prior-data fitted networks are typically trained using synthetic data generated by a single, manually constructed generator. In cases where such a generator combines multiple components, e.g., in TabPFN, Mitra, or TabICLv2, this is achieved through a mixture using manually specified weights, the impact of which on the network only becomes apparent after pre-training. Instead of such a hand-built generator, this work proposes a structured prior: a small set of named component priors, linked by operators whose algebraic properties are formally proven and whose influence on the trained network has been validated. Within a measure-theoretic framework, the operators of mixture, convolution, amplitude scaling, local transform, and projection are defined. Their governing laws are proven. These operators are implemented in the macl2l software package. Experiments demonstrate that the implemented generators reproduce the identities within the resolution of a permutation test. Furthermore, it is shown that trained networks obey the distributive law; that a mixed prior composed of small components performs as well as or better than any individual component in seven out of eight open regression benchmarks; and that on two of these tables a structured prior reaches, with a ninth of the data budget, an error that a generic random-function prior does not reach even with the full budget. A theorem on model averaging demonstrates that networks trained on individual components constitute the Bayes predictor for the mixture, provided that the component networks are themselves the Bayes predictors for their respective priors. However, the pre-registered test of this averaged predictor with trained component networks failed, because the networks identify the generating component too rarely. The paper proposes a research program focused on the algebra of structured priors for prior-data fitted networks.

</details>


### [178] [StreamLTT: Risk Certification for Policy Selection under Stream Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04062)
*Jinwen Xu,Qin Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StreamLTT 将 Learn-then-Test 的固定总体风险替换为"平均可预测风险"（以决策前信息为条件的损失），通过条件中心化使偏离量成为鞅和，从而在依赖、非平稳的序贯部署流中实现风险认证的策略选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 LTT 针对由校准损失估计的固定总体风险，但序贯部署中损失存在依赖性与非平稳性，固定策略的风险会随观测历史演化，因此"应认证何种风险"以及"如何检验该风险"都需要重新界定。

Method: 提出 StreamLTT：以平均可预测风险（每个决策的损失条件于结果揭晓前可得信息）替代固定总体风险；条件中心化将经验偏离转化为鞅和，据此构造可适应可预测方差的检验，并给出有限样本 Hoeffding 型替代检验；结合族错误率（FWER）控制，得到可同时认证的策略区域以支持后续部署选择。

Result: 在 7 个预测数据集、7 种部署风险上，StreamLTT 认证的风险前沿比 IID、稳健方差、块自助法及基于集中不等式的替代方法更接近规定风险目标，经验 FWER 贴近规定水平；消融实验表明可预测风险形式带来更紧的风险前沿，而对可预测方差的适应更有效利用 FWER 预算。

Conclusion: StreamLTT 从"认证何种风险"与"如何检验"两方面入手，将风险认证的策略选择扩展到依赖型部署流。

Abstract: Learn-then-Test (LTT) provides a general framework for certifying deployment policies against a prescribed risk target, but its standard formulation targets a fixed population risk estimated from calibration losses. In sequential deployment, losses may instead be dependent and nonstationary, so the risk of a fixed policy can evolve with the observed history. We introduce StreamLTT, which replaces this fixed population risk with average predictable risk, defined by conditioning each decision's loss on the information available before its outcome is revealed. Conditional centering turns empirical deviations from this target into a martingale sum, enabling both a test that adapts to predictable variance and a finite-sample Hoeffding alternative. Combined with family-wise error rate control, StreamLTT yields a simultaneously certified policy region that supports subsequent deployment selection. Across seven forecasting datasets and seven deployment risks, StreamLTT places the certified frontier substantially closer to the prescribed risk target than IID, robust-variance, block-bootstrap, and concentration-based alternatives, while empirical family-wise error closely follows the prescribed level. A finite-sample ablation further shows that the predictable-risk formulation drives the tighter risk frontier, while adaptation to predictable variance makes more effective use of the available family-wise error budget. StreamLTT therefore extends risk-certified policy selection to dependent deployment streams by addressing both what risk should be certified and how it should be tested.

</details>


### [179] [TB-Transolver: Parallelized and Coherent Long-Horizon Rollouts via Temporal Bundling for Time-Dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04063)
*Sidharth S. Menon,Ameya D. Jagtap*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TB-Transolver（时间捆绑 Transolver），用 temporal bundling 将时间因果从串行自回归改为并行捆绑预测，使 Transolver 适用于瞬态 PDE 问题；并提出时空相干性指标 C(K) 用于先验选择最优捆绑宽度，实现最高 29× 训练加速与（多节点多 GPU）45.6× 聚合加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transolver 的 Physics-Attention 在非结构网格空间建模上表现优异，但处理时间相关问题须依赖时间条件化或自回归 rollout 以保持时间因果，导致计算成本随预测时域线性增长，串行执行成为瞬态问题的瓶颈。作者希望在不牺牲空间建模能力的前提下，消除该串行瓶颈。

Method: 核心为 temporal bundling：将时间轴切分为宽度为 K 的捆绑块，每块内多个快照一次性并行预测，从而在块级上保留因果、块内并行化。论文进一步定义“时空相干性” C(K) 作为量化时间捆绑内快照相关性的通用指标，仅用训练数据即可先验地选出最优捆绑宽度，避免昂贵的网格搜索；并结合分布式多节点、多 GPU 并行进一步放大收益。

Result: 在塑性力学、流体动力学、反应扩散与全球天气预报四个物理性质迥异的任务上验证计算效率：相对传统自回归与时间条件化基线，训练最高加速 29×；对时空相干性高的系统，在最优捆绑宽度下经多节点多 GPU 分布式并行可获最高 45.6× 的聚合加速（对比单 GPU 实现）。

Conclusion: TB-Transolver 通过时间捆绑在保留 Physics-Attention 空间骨干的同时消除了时间维的串行瓶颈，使 Transolver 可高效用于瞬态 PDE；C(K) 提供了低成本、数据驱动的捆绑宽度选择准则，且加速效果可随分布式并行进一步扩展，具备跨物理领域的通用性。

Abstract: Transolver has recently gained widespread adoption for solving diverse physical problems on unstructured geometric meshes by leveraging its Physics-Attention mechanism, which seamlessly extends standard softmax attention to handle physical domains at scale. For time-dependent problems, existing literature relies on time-conditioned or autoregressive rollouts to preserve temporal causality while retaining Physics-Attention as the spatial backbone. However, a fundamental limitation of this formulation is its sequential execution model, wherein computational costs scale linearly with the temporal horizon, rendering standard Transolver inefficient for transient problems despite its exceptional spatial efficacy. This bottleneck has historically hindered its broader application in time-dependent physical modeling. To address this limitation, we introduce TB-Transolver (also referred to as Temporally Bundled Transolver), an efficient framework built on temporal bundling that scales Transolver for time-dependent PDEs. Furthermore, we propose \emph{spatiotemporal coherence} (${\rm C(K)}$) as a general metric to systematically quantify snapshot correlation within a temporal bundle, enabling the \emph{a priori} selection of optimal bundle widths using training data alone, thereby avoiding expensive grid searches. We demonstrate the computational efficacy of TB-Transolver across four physically distinct domains, namely, plasticity, fluid dynamics, reaction-diffusion, and global weather forecasting; achieving training speedups of up to $29\times$ over conventional autoregressive and time-conditioned baselines. Finally, these computational gains are further amplified through distributed multi-node and multi-GPU parallelism, yielding aggregated speedups of up to $45.6\times$ at optimal bundle widths for systems exhibiting high spatiotemporal coherence compared to single-GPU implementations.

</details>


### [180] [Graph-Localized Coverage for Conformal Prediction on Inductive Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04066)
*Jinwen Xu,Qin Lu,Georgios B. Giannakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GLocalCP：一种面向归纳式节点分类与边预测的在线共形预测框架，结合边际校准与图局部校准，并采用不确定性自适应预测集，在保持目标覆盖率的同时显著降低图局部校准误差与预测集规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测(CP)仅提供边际覆盖保证，而边际覆盖无法刻画误差在图上的位置分布；不同图邻域查询的不确定性差异可能很大，即使总体覆盖率接近目标值，局部可靠性仍可能严重不足。因此需要一个同时兼顾边际覆盖与图局部覆盖的在线CP框架。

Method: 提出GLocalCP在线CP框架：(1) 设计基于MC dropout预测所得互信息的不确定性自适应非共形分数；(2) 在全局在线阈值基础上，引入基于核的局部修正项，将覆盖反馈在相似图查询间传播；(3) 理论上证明在无需可交换性与平稳性假设下达到路径式O(N^{-1/2})边际校准误差，并对精确核形式给出显式O(N^{-1/2})图局部化校准保证；(4) 采用不确定性缩放来调控预测集规模。

Result: 在7个节点分类与7个边预测基准上，相近的边际覆盖率掩盖了显著不同的局部可靠性；GLocalCP在维持目标覆盖率的同时持续降低图局部校准误差；不确定性缩放在几乎所有节点与边数据集上减小了预测集规模，且边际覆盖率基本不变。

Conclusion: 边际覆盖不足以刻画图上的局部不确定性，GLocalCP通过在线的全局—局部联合校准与不确定性自适应分数，在无交换性/平稳性假设下实现了有效的图局部覆盖保证与更紧的预测集，为图结构数据上的可靠不确定性量化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) provides marginal coverage guarantees, but marginal coverage does not determine where prediction errors occur on a graph. Queries from different graph neighborhoods can exhibit substantially different levels of uncertainty even when aggregate coverage remains near its target. We introduce GLocalCP, an online CP framework for inductive node classification and edge prediction that combines marginal and graph-local calibration with uncertainty-adaptive prediction sets. Specifically, GLocalCP puts forth a novel uncertainty-adaptive nonconformality score that relies on the mutual information obtained from Monte-Carlo (MC) dropout based prediction. Then, it augments a global online threshold with a kernel-based local correction that propagates coverage feedback among similar graph queries. On the theoretical horizon, we establish pathwise $O(N^{-1/2})$ marginal calibration error without exchangeability or stationarity and, for the exact-kernel formulation, an explicit $O(N^{-1/2})$ graph-localized calibration guarantee. Experiments on seven node-classification and seven edge-prediction benchmarks show that similar marginal coverage can conceal substantially different local reliability. Our proposed GLocalCP maintains coverage near the target while consistently reducing graph-local calibration error. Uncertainty scaling in GLocalCP further reduces prediction-set size across all evaluated node and edge datasets while leaving marginal coverage essentially unchanged.

</details>


### [181] [Finite-Sample Convergence in Networked Average Reward MARL: Decentralization Pitfalls and Entropy Remedies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04072)
*Yizhou Zhang,Yashaswini Murthy,Laixi Shi,Adam Vierman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究网络化多智能体平均奖励强化学习，证明去中心化仅靠局部信息无法收敛到全局最优联合策略，给出线性函数逼近去中心化actor-critic的有限样本近似驻点收敛保证，并说明固定状态熵正则可重塑目标景观获得更强全局收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体仅在k-hop邻域内做局部更新并协作最大化全网平均奖励，此时多智能体独立优化（multiagency）与部分可观测性构成根本障碍，需明确去中心化学习的能力边界。

Method: 先建立不可能性结果刻画全局最优不可达；随后提出带线性函数逼近的去中心化actor-critic算法，分析其有限样本收敛性；再引入固定状态熵正则化改变目标函数几何结构。

Result: 不可能性结果表明去中心化策略优化一般只能认证到驻点；所提算法可在有限样本下收敛到梯度范数较小的近似驻点；加入固定状态熵正则后获得更强的全局收敛保证。

Conclusion: 局部信息下的去中心化MARL存在本质收敛极限，驻点级保证是可达的基本目标；熵正则化可有效重塑优化景观，是突破该极限、获得全局收敛的有效手段。

Abstract: We study the problem of average reward Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) where agents interact within a network. Each agent in the network conducts local updates with information within its $k$-hop neighborhood and collaboratively maximizes the overall reward of the entire network. We first provide impossibility results in achieving convergence to the global optimal joint policies in our setting. These impossibility results highlight the challenges of decentralized learning with local information in multi-agent systems, namely the issues of multiagency, where each agent independently optimizes its policy, and partial observability, where agents have limited visibility into the global state. Given these challenges which indicate that decentralized policy optimization can generally be certified only up to stationarity, we provide finite-sample convergence guarantees for a decentralized actor-critic algorithm with linear function approximation that converges to an approximate stationary point with small gradient norm. To overcome such impossibility results, we further show that adding fixed-state entropy regularization reshapes the objective landscape which leads to even stronger global convergence guarantees for our proposed actor-critic algorithm.

</details>


### [182] [Computer-Use Agents Gain Little Benefit from a World Model When Conditioned on Tutorials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04073)
*Anh Nguyen,Tianyi Xu,Phat Tran,Harry Yu,Huazheng Wang,Stefan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过构建多模态教程数据集并提出RAG-PolicyJudge（RAG-PJ）框架，在对教程条件下分离评估世界模型的作用后发现：有了教程后，世界模型rollout带来的增益很小（即使使用完美视觉未来），增益主要来自教程理解与judge的候选动作选择能力；瓶颈在于judge对未来状态的推理与任务完成判定，而非下一状态预测保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期computer-use agents（CUAs）的提升通常来自三部分：检索到的任务相关教程、MLLM judge、以及基于世界模型的前瞻规划。但各组件对整体性能提升的单独贡献未被隔离评估，存在归因不清的空白。

Method: 1）构建OSWorld-Tutorial与ScienceBoard-Tutorial两个带精选多模态教程的CUA数据集，覆盖日常与科学领域；2）提出RAG-PolicyJudge（RAG-PJ）框架：以任务特定教程为条件的policy-judge结构，policy提出候选动作、judge从中选择；3）为隔离世界模型作用，在RAG-PJ上对比有/无世界模型的设置，包括零样本文本式下一状态描述，以及返回真实视觉观测的oracle。

Result: 在有教程的条件下，RAG-PJ从这些rollout中获益甚微，即使提供完美视觉未来也是如此。说明此前教程条件方法中的世界模型增益主要来自教程理解与judge选择候选动作的能力，而非预测rollout。错误分析显示瓶颈不是rollout保真度，而是judge对未来状态的推理与任务完成识别能力。

Conclusion: 世界模型的价值不止取决于下一状态预测质量，更取决于对预测rollout的推理能力；未来工作应同时改进世界模型的预测与judge在rollout上的推理。数据集与代码已开源（https://github.com/realjoenguyen/RAGPJ-official）。

Abstract: Recent work has improved computer-use agents (CUAs) by combining retrieved task-relevant tutorials and look-ahead planning using world models with MLLM judges. However, the contribution of each component -- tutorials, the judge, and the world model -- to overall improvement has not been evaluated in isolation. To fill this gap, we first construct OSWorld-Tutorial and ScienceBoard-Tutorial: two datasets for CUAs with curated multimodal tutorials, covering everyday and scientific domains. We then propose RAG-PolicyJudge (RAG-PJ), a policy-judge framework for CUAs conditioned on task-specific tutorials: the policy proposes candidate actions, and the judge selects among them. To isolate the effect of world models given tutorials, we compare RAG-PJ with and without common world models: using either zero-shot text-based descriptions of next states or an oracle that returns ground-truth visual observations. We find that RAG-PJ gains little benefit from these rollouts when tutorials are present, even with perfect visual futures. This suggests prior world-model gains in tutorial-conditioned methods come mainly from tutorial understanding and the judge's ability to select the proposed actions, rather than from predicted rollouts. Error analysis reveals that the bottleneck is not rollout fidelity, but the judge's ability to reason over future states and recognize task completion. This highlights the need to improve not just next-state prediction in world models, but also reasoning over their predicted rollouts. Datasets and code are available at https://github.com/realjoenguyen/RAGPJ-official.

</details>


### [183] [RAPID: Increasing Efficiency of Internal Distillation via Randomized Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04081)
*Ryan Swift,Konstantinos Psounis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAPID提出用基于Johnson-Lindenstrauss引理的随机投影替代可学习投影矩阵，将教师内部激活压缩到学生嵌入空间，并结合EMA与同方差不确定性加权的多目标损失，在保持性能相当的前提下将内部蒸馏训练效率提升至多21.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内部蒸馏需解决师生表示维度不兼容问题；现有方法引入可学习投影矩阵，但投影器参数仅在训练期有用、训练后即被丢弃，造成额外训练开销与低效。

Method: RAPID采用基于Johnson-Lindenstrauss引理的高效随机变换构造，把维度较大的教师表示投影到学生嵌入空间，并具结构保持的理论保证；同时设计面向内部蒸馏的多目标函数，融合指数移动平均（EMA）与同方差不确定性加权。

Result: 在面向推理的后训练实验中，RAPID在性能与现有方法相当的情况下，将训练效率最高提升21.1%。

Conclusion: 随机投影可有效消除投影学习的浪费，在结构保持保证下实现高效内部蒸馏，是一种兼顾效率与效果的方法。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation is a technique used to transfer the expertise of large teacher models into small, efficient student models. Recent advances have integrated the distillation of internal activations, more directly training students to learn similar representations as the teacher. One of the core challenges of internal distillation is the dimensional incompatibility between the student and teacher internal representations. Existing methods add learnable matrices to project the larger teacher activations into the smaller representation space of the student. This is a notable point of inefficiency, as learning additional parameters for the projectors increases training costs, only for them to be discarded immediately after training. We introduce RAPID (RAndomized Projection for Internal Distillation) a novel distillation methodology which eliminates the wasteful projection learning by leveraging randomized methods. RAPID utilizes an efficient transform construction based on the Johnson-Lindenstrauss lemma to compress the teacher representations into the student embedding space with strong theoretical guarantees of structural preservation. RAPID also employs a combination of exponential moving average (EMA) and homoscedastic uncertainty weighting in a novel multi-objective function designed for internal distillation. Our experiments in post-training for reasoning demonstrate that RAPID increases training efficiency by as much as 21.1% while maintaining performance comparable to existing methods.

</details>


### [184] [Follow the Features: SAE Feature-Guided Data-Efficient Instruction-Tuning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04085)
*Ryan Swift,Konstantinos Psounis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Follow the Features (FtF)方法，利用稀疏自编码器(SAE)特征统计指导指令微调数据子集选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选取小而有效的指令微调数据集需判断原始数据中哪些方面应被保留，缺乏可靠的样本重要性信号。

Method: 用SAE特征刻画训练数据：基于数据集级激活统计对特征排序，按样本对高排名特征的表达强度打分，并引入特征重采样以鼓励多样性；所选用的LLM-SAE对与待微调模型相互独立，可跨目标模型复用。

Result: 在100样本训练预算、固定序列长度256 token的两个模型-数据集设定下，FtF与GRAPE、S2L性能相当，相对排名随设定变化；在Qwen 3 8B + Alpaca上，其AlpacaEval胜率与Open LLM Leaderboard表现的调和平均比全量数据训练高1.59个百分点，但Leaderboard单项分数仍低于全量训练。

Conclusion: SAE特征统计可作为数据高效指令微调的有效信号。

Abstract: Selecting small, effective instruction-tuning datasets requires identifying which aspects of the original data to retain. We introduce Follow the Features (FtF), a data-selection method that uses sparse autoencoder (SAE) features to characterize training data and guide subset construction. FtF ranks features using dataset-level activation statistics, scores samples by their expression of highly ranked features, and incorporates feature resampling to encourage diversity. The LLM-SAE pair used for selection is independent of the model being fine-tuned, enabling reuse across target models. We evaluate FtF on two model-dataset settings at a training budget of 100 samples with a fixed sequence length of 256 tokens. FtF achieves performance competitive with GRAPE and S2L, with relative rankings varying across settings. On Qwen 3 8B trained on Alpaca, FtF exceeds full-dataset training by 1.59 percentage points in the harmonic mean of an AlpacaEval-based win rate and Open LLM Leaderboard performance, although full-dataset training retains a higher Leaderboard score. These results support SAE feature statistics as a useful signal for data-efficient instruction tuning.

</details>


### [185] [ScaleTraj: Scale-Decomposed Human Trajectory Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04097)
*Yulin Zeng,Yiheng Zhao,Jun Yan,Yuhong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有轨迹生成方法在个体级与群体级统计保真度间存在张力：自顶向下模型复现群体统计但个体轨迹不真实，自底向上生成器合成个体轨迹却在聚合后丢失群体位移规律（尤其截断幂律重尾）。作者将这一现象形式化为"群体级位移差距"（Population-Level Displacement Gap），通过系统评估证实其普遍性，提出尺度分解指标 SFP 诊断该差距源于跨空间尺度的出行分配误差，并据此设计尺度感知的自回归 Transformer——ScaleTraj，显著缩小该差距并保持保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体级轨迹对依赖用户移动行为的应用至关重要，但现有生成方法难以同时保证个体级真实性与群体级统计保真度：自顶向下模型能复现聚合统计，却难以保证个体轨迹合理；自底向上生成器虽能合成个体轨迹，聚合后却常无法保持关键群体级规律，尤其是由截断幂律刻画的重尾位移分布。作者据此提出并形式化了"群体级位移差距"问题。

Method: 1）形式化"群体级位移差距"，并对代表性模型驱动与数据驱动轨迹生成器做系统评估以考察其普遍性；2）提出尺度保真度剖面（Scale Fidelity Profile, SFP），一种按空间尺度分解的指标，量化不同尺度上出行分配的差异，用于诊断差距来源；3）提出 ScaleTraj：自回归 Transformer，将位置预测分解为"尺度选择头"（决定移动尺度）与"尺度感知单元选择头"（在给定尺度条件下预测目的地）。

Result: 系统评估表明，群体级位移差距在多种生成范式（模型驱动与数据驱动）中普遍存在；SFP 显示群体级位移失配与跨空间尺度的出行分配系统性误差同时出现。在两个数据集上的实验表明，相比模型驱动与数据驱动基线，ScaleTraj 在保持保真度的同时有效缩小了群体级位移差距。

Conclusion: 群体级位移差距是当前轨迹生成范式的共性问题，其根源可归因于跨空间尺度的出行分配偏差；通过将位置预测显式分解为尺度选择与尺度条件下的目的地选择，ScaleTraj 能在维持个体级与群体级保真度的前提下缓解该差距，代码已开源。

Abstract: Individual-level trajectories are essential for applications that rely on user-level mobility behavior. Existing mobility-generation approaches face a fundamental tension between individual-level and population-level statistical fidelity: top-down models reproduce aggregate mobility statistics but struggle to guarantee realistic individual trajectories, while bottom-up generators synthesize individual trajectories yet often fail to preserve key population-level mobility regularities after aggregation. In particular, many bottom-up generated populations fail to reproduce the displacement distribution, whose heavy tail is well summarized by a truncated power law. We formalize this phenomenon as the \textbf{Population-Level Displacement Gap}. To investigate its prevalence, we conduct a systematic evaluation of representative model-driven and data-driven trajectory generators. Our results provide evidence that the Population-Level Displacement Gap is prevalent across diverse generation paradigms. To diagnose this gap, we introduce the \textbf{Scale Fidelity Profile (SFP)}, a scale-decomposed metric that quantifies discrepancies in trip allocations across spatial scales. SFP indicates that population-level displacement mismatches co-occur with systematic errors in how trips are allocated across spatial scales. Motivated by this observation, we develop \textbf{ScaleTraj}, an autoregressive Transformer that decomposes location prediction into a Scale Selection Head that decides the movement scale and a Scale-Aware Cell Selection Head that predicts the destination conditioned on the selected scale. Experiments on two datasets show that ScaleTraj reduces the Population-Level Displacement Gap versus model-driven and data-driven baselines, while preserving fidelity. Our code is available at https://github.com/SCU-TrustworthyAI/ScaleTraj.

</details>


### [186] [When Strong Reasoners Join the Pool: Small Union Premiums and Costly Runaway Generations in LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04098)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在固定协议下测量推理模型加入候选池后的"事后oracle溢价"：两个推理模型均达96.1%准确率，而多模型取任一正确的溢价仅为0.215–0.588 pp，远低于伴随研究的3.5 pp。作者主张oracle差距必须连同池、协议、每格样本数与解码预算一并报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路由基准常用"事后oracle"评判：只要任一模型记录的答案正确即算解决，但随机解码下这一oracle把两种量混在一起——每查询固定选一个模型的"先知上界"与调用多个模型取任一正确的"溢价"。伴随研究在11个小开源模型、MATH-500上测得该溢价为3.5 pp；当前推理模型进入候选池后该溢价多大尚不清楚。

Method: 固定协议、预先承诺式生成，单张NVIDIA B300；MATH-500全部500题×每题30个带种子样本×3个模型（Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct、Nemotron Nano 9B v2、Nemotron Super 49B v1.5），采用厂商采样设置、65,536 token上限、固定版本Math-Verify评分器与仅追加的尝试台账，共45,000条已评分响应。关键指标：经验乘积耦合溢价（两/三模型池）、相对于"总调用Nano"的插件选择余量、重复重采样与跨模型调用的任意正确率比较。

Result: 两个推理模型均达96.1%准确率，70B基线为76.2%。经验乘积耦合溢价在所有两模型与三模型池中为0.215–0.588 pp；相对"总调用Nano"的插件选择余量为1.27 pp；对Nano重采样三次与三模型各调用一次的任意正确率几乎相同。仅31条响应触及token上限（其中25条尾部严格周期化），但这些截断响应占某模型输出长度平方代理量的最高60.2%；长生成使原计划的第四个模型Llama-3.1-8B过慢而未能完成。

Conclusion: 该溢价远低于伴随研究的3.5 pp，但模型、池规模与协议同时改变，故不可直接归因；应把oracle差距与其产生条件（模型池、协议、每格样本数、解码预算）一并报告，并指出截断与长生成对"长度平方"类成本代理和基准可行性的显著影响。

Abstract: Routing benchmarks often judge a router against a hindsight oracle that counts a query as solved whenever any model's recorded answer is correct, but under stochastic decoding that oracle mixes two different objects: the clairvoyant ceiling of committing to one model per query and the premium of calling several models and keeping any correct answer. A companion study measured this premium at 3.5 percentage points (pp) for 11 small open models on MATH-500; how large it is once current reasoning models enter the pool was unknown. We measure it with a fixed-protocol, pre-committed generation on a single NVIDIA B300: all 500 MATH-500 problems, 30 seeded samples per problem, Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct and NVIDIA's Nemotron Nano 9B v2 and Nemotron Super 49B v1.5 (a NAS derivative of the same 70B) under vendor sampling, a 65,536-token cap, a pinned Math-Verify scorer, and an append-only attempt ledger, for 45,000 scored responses. The two reasoning models both reach 96.1% accuracy against 76.2% for the 70B baseline. The empirical product-coupling premium is 0.215-0.588 pp across all two- and three-model pools; the empirical plug-in selection headroom over always calling Nano is 1.27 pp; and resampling Nano three times gives practically the same empirical any-correct rate as calling all three models once. Only 31 responses reached the cap, 25 of them with exactly periodic tails, yet the capped responses account for up to 60.2% of a model's output-length-squared proxy, and long generations made a fourth planned model, Llama-3.1-8B, too slow to finish. The premium is far below the companion's 3.5 pp, but models, pool size and protocol changed together; an oracle gap should therefore be reported with the pool, the protocol, the samples per cell and the decoding budget that produced it.

</details>


### [187] [ModelBlaster: Closing the Performance Gap through Learning-Augmented Agents Across Models, Kernels and Hardware Backends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04104)
*Shengjun Kris Dong,Dima Nikiforov,Sahil Modi,Sana Damani,Edward C Lin,Siva Kumar Sastry Hari,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ModelBlaster：基于记忆增强上下文强化学习（MAIC-RL）的LLM智能体框架，以可变知识库为策略，实现跨SIMD/SIMT异构加速器的kernel级与模型级代码优化，并支持经验复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨硬件架构优化加速器代码困难：ML编译器受限于预定义调度与微内核搜索空间；LLM引导优化虽可探索替代算法与算子分解，但现有智能体通常只针对单一编程接口、工具链或加速器家族，局限于kernel级优化而缺乏模型级变换，且难以跨硬件目标复用经验。

Method: 提出ModelBlaster框架，其可变策略是一个持久化知识库，在推理时由Actor-Critic文本梯度下降（AC-TGD）更新；知识库将性能状态映射到动作、结果与约束，实现跨kernel、模型与硬件目标的经验复用；模型级优化器利用kernel级知识指导整模型决策；统一优化循环与知识模式支持NVIDIA GPU上的CUDA、Qualcomm GPU上的OpenCL及RISC-V向量核上的RVV intrinsics，仅lowering与测量层需按目标定制。

Result: 相比PyTorch，在B200 GPU上于KernelBench Level 1/2/3分别取得1.85x、5.58x、5.18x几何平均加速，SOL-ExecBench Level 1/2上为1.69x；在Qualcomm Adreno GPU上相比厂商SDK达5.8x、相比Apache TVM达1.7x；在RISC-V向量核上于70个KernelBench问题相比ExecuTorch+XNNPACK达2.7x。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM智能体的策略外化为可在线更新的知识库，ModelBlaster在统一框架下实现了跨多样加速器的高效kernel与全模型优化，并验证了经验复用与模型级变换的显著收益。

Abstract: Optimizing accelerator code across hardware architectures is difficult. ML compilers search predefined spaces of schedules and micro-kernels, while LLM-guided optimization can explore alternative algorithms, operator decompositions, and hardware-specific techniques. However, existing LLM agents typically target a single programming interface, toolchain, or accelerator family, focus on kernel-level optimization without model-level transforms, and offer limited reuse of experience across hardware targets.
  We present ModelBlaster, a Memory-Augmented In-Context Reinforcement Learning (MAIC-RL) framework for optimizing data-parallel kernels and whole models across heterogeneous SIMD and SIMT accelerators. Its mutable policy is a persistent knowledge base updated at inference time by Actor-Critic Textual Gradient Descent (AC-TGD). The knowledge base maps performance states to actions, outcomes, and constraints, enabling experience reuse across kernels, models, and hardware targets. A model-level optimizer uses kernel-level knowledge to inform whole-model decisions. The same optimization loop and knowledge schema support CUDA on NVIDIA GPUs, OpenCL on Qualcomm GPUs, and RVV intrinsics on RISC-V vector cores, with only lowering and measurement layers specific to each target.
  Compared with PyTorch, ModelBlaster achieves geometric-mean speedups of 1.85x, 5.58x, and 5.18x on KernelBench Levels 1, 2, and 3, and 1.69x on SOL-ExecBench Levels 1 and 2 on a B200 GPU. On Qualcomm Adreno GPUs, it achieves 5.8x over the vendor SDK and 1.7x over Apache TVM; on RISC-V vector cores, it achieves 2.7x over ExecuTorch with XNNPACK across 70 KernelBench problems.

</details>


### [188] [Structure, Dimensionality, and Distribution Shape - What Makes Pretraining Data Transferable, and Why the Input Pipeline Decides Which](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04106)
*Sangyoon Bae,Jiook Cha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"迁移不变性原理"：语料属性是否影响迁移取决于模型输入变换而非数据模态，并构建TransferForge生成器按指定结构、维度、分布形状合成语料，验证合成预训练可替代真实预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成预训练语料（如Chronos的KernelSynth）只采样独立序列，无法控制语料的结构、维度与分布形状，因此"什么样的预训练语料可迁移"缺乏可控工具与原则性解释。

Method: 用结构、维度、各单元取值分布形状三属性刻画语料；提出迁移不变性原理，认为输入变换决定哪些属性起作用；构建TransferForge生成器，可在任意给定结构/维度/分布形状上放置合成总体，并与KernelSynth对比；在PatchTST（UCR，窗口标准化）与POYO Plus（钙荧光信号，无归一化）上做对照实验，仅翻转输入归一化。

Result: 标准化输入下分布形状失效、结构设定主导，合成预训练可匹配同规模真实语料并在扩大规模后超越；无归一化编码下形状成为支配约束，形状匹配的合成语料可在零真实神经活动下替代真实预训练；仅翻转归一化即令同一次形状扰动的效应改变5.3倍。

Conclusion: 决定迁移的是模型流水线而非数据模态；该原理给出训练前即可选定"合成什么"的配方，使预训练数据成为可为模型制造的产物，在真实数据稀缺、昂贵或私密的场景中价值最大。

Abstract: What makes a pretraining corpus transferable? We describe a corpus by three properties: its structure, its dimensionality, and the distribution shape of each unit's values. We propose the Transfer-Invariance Principle: the same property can be irrelevant to transfer under one input transform and decisive under another, so the model's input transform, not the data modality, decides which properties can matter. To test it we build TransferForge, a generator that places a synthetic population at any prescribed structure, dimensionality, and distribution shape. KernelSynth, the synthetic prior of Chronos, cannot, because it draws independent series. When the model standardizes each input window (PatchTST on UCR), the distribution shape is inert and the structural setting governs. Synthetic pretraining then matches a real corpus of the same size and exceeds it once scaled. When the model encodes calcium fluorescence signals without normalization (POYO Plus), the shape becomes a governing constraint, and a shape-matched synthetic corpus replaces real pretraining with zero real neural activity. Flipping only the input normalization, with the model and data fixed, changes the effect of a shape manipulation by a factor of 5.3, so the pipeline, not the modality, sets the switch. The principle yields a recipe that chooses what to synthesize before any training. Pretraining data then becomes something to manufacture for the model, most valuably where real data is scarce, expensive, or private.

</details>


### [189] [Separating Common and Unique Directions for Data-Free Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04108)
*Shiting Wang,Yingjie Zou,Ke li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出免数据的预合并方法CUSD：利用任务向量在共享方向基中呈"低色散公共主体+高色散任务主导尾部"的两段结构，将任务向量分解为公共与独有方向分量分别处理，从而提升各类算术合并算子的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算术式模型合并方法在参数空间中处理任务向量，并对可合并性不同的方向施加统一的逐元素规则，忽略了方向间差异，限制了合并效果。

Method: 先在一个共享方向基中分析任务向量的谱结构，发现一致的两段结构：跨任务共享、低色散的公共主体（bulk）与承载不成比例能量的高色散、任务主导尾部（tail）。基于此提出Common–Unique Subspace Decomposition (CUSD)：免数据地将任务向量分解为公共方向分量与独有方向分量，公共分量交由下游算术算子合并，独有残差单独保留。

Result: 在11个基座模型、4个模型家族、4种基于算术的合并算子上评测，CUSD在44个"模型—算子"组合中的42个上取得提升，相对对应基线最高提升22.5%。

Conclusion: 任务向量应按方向可合并性区别对待；将公共子空间与独有子空间分离的免数据预处理可作为通用插件增强现有算术合并方法，性能提升广泛且显著。

Abstract: Model merging combines multiple fine-tuned models without additional training, and arithmetic methods have emerged as a simple yet effective way to merge models without data. However, existing arithmetic methods treat task vectors in parameter space and apply a single element-wise rule to directions that can differ in mergeability. In a shared directional basis, we find a consistent two-regime structure: a low-dispersion common bulk shared across tasks and a high-dispersion task-dominated tail that carries disproportionate energy. These findings motivate Common--Unique Subspace Decomposition (CUSD), a data-free pre-merging method that separates task vectors into common and unique directional components, merges the common components with downstream arithmetic operator, and retains unique residuals separately. Experiments across $11$ base models, four model families, and four arithmetic-based merging operators show that CUSD improves 42 of 44 model--operator combinations and achieves up to 22.5% improvement over the corresponding baselines. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/stwang666/CUSD

</details>


### [190] [Decomposing Rank Reversals under Temporal Nonstationarity: Alignment, Aggressiveness, and Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04116)
*Deying Song,Xueying Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一个与模型无关的样本外R²分解框架，将时间非平稳下的模型排名反转归因于"错校准激进度"的可预测激增，并给出复杂度相界、岭回归最优记忆闭式解及向复杂架构的代理界推广，用14种架构在大规模金融面板上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间非平稳导致不同训练窗口（记忆长度）下的模型性能排名发生反转。已有工作分别研究自适应回看窗口、有效模型复杂度和经验预测评估，但缺乏统一框架解释驱动排名反转的结构性机制。

Method: 推导样本外R²的模型无关分解：目标对齐、错校准激进度、预测不稳定性三部分；在此框架内数学刻画复杂度相变边界；在局部漂移下建立岭回归的最优记忆闭式方程；通过代理界将保证推广到复杂模型架构；并对14种线性、树模型与神经网络架构进行大规模金融面板基准测试。

Result: 实证模型排名反转由错校准激进展的可预测激增所驱动；理论上的复杂度相界与最优记忆方程与分析结果一致，并在基准测试中得到验证。

Conclusion: 该框架为时间漂移下的泛化提供了严格的机制性诊断，将排名反转从经验现象提升为可由记忆敏感度异质性解释的结构性结果。

Abstract: Under temporal nonstationarity, model performance rankings reverse across training horizons due to heterogeneous sensitivities to memory. While existing literature addresses adaptive lookbacks, effective model complexity, and empirical forecast evaluation in isolation, no unified framework explains the structural mechanics driving these rank reversals. We bridge this gap by deriving a model-agnostic decomposition of out-of-sample $R^2$ into target alignment, miscalibrated aggressiveness, and predictive instability. Within this framework, we mathematically characterize complexity phase boundaries, establish closed-form optimal-memory equations for ridge regression under local drift, and extend these guarantees to complex model architectures via surrogate bounds. We validate these theoretical dynamics by benchmarking 14 linear, tree-based, and neural architectures across large-scale financial panels. In doing so, our framework demonstrates that empirical model rank reversals are driven by predictable surges in miscalibrated aggressiveness, providing a rigorous mechanistic diagnosis of generalization under temporal shift.

</details>


### [191] [LoopKV: Loop-Aware KV Cache Compression for Serving Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04137)
*Tianle Zhou,Yang Zhang,Tianyu Wu,Zhuohan Wang,William Nixon,Juncheng Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoopKV 为循环 Transformer 的每个 (loop, layer) 单元做一次性离线 profiling，按敏感度分配 4-bit 存储、2-bit 存储或复用末轮 KV，并在解码时按注意力权重选择性读取低比特位，由单个融合 kernel 在 vLLM 中执行该计划，实现 35% 的 KV 内存节省和 1.7–2.2× 的吞吐提升且精度不显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环 Transformer（如 Ouro）每 token 多次复用同一权重块，且每轮维护独立 KV cache，导致 cache 内存限制批大小、难以摊薄重复的权重读取。现有均匀比特量化和固定逐轮规则无法按各层在每一轮的敏感度分配精度。

Method: 测量发现量化敏感度在 (loop, layer) 单元间存在差异，且各单元损伤近似可加；KV 共享损伤亦随单元而异，后几轮更耐受共享。据此采用一次性离线 profile，为每个单元在 4-bit 存储、2-bit 存储、复用末轮 KV 三选一；规划器在预测损伤与存储预算约束下最小化读取量。解码时每轮先读 4-bit 值的高 2 位，仅在自身注意力权重需要 4-bit 精度处取低位；单一融合 kernel 在 vLLM 中执行该方案。

Result: 在单张 RTX PRO 6000 上服务 Ouro-1.4B、16K 最大长度：LoopKV-M 在相同融合 kernel 与 CUDA graph 下比均匀 4-bit（KIVI-4）少存 35% KV，在延迟 SLO 下 goodput 达 KIVI-4 的 1.7–2.2×；六个公开基准的平均值均未显著低于 BF16。

Conclusion: 按 (loop, layer) 单元细粒度分配 KV 精度与共享策略，并配合比特分层按需读取，可在循环 Transformer 上同时降低 KV 内存与提升服务吞吐，且保持与 BF16 相当的精度。

Abstract: Looped transformers apply a shared block several times per token to improve accuracy without adding parameters. For models such as Ouro that keep a separate key-value (KV) cache per loop, cache memory limits the batch size that amortizes repeated weight reads. Uniform-bit quantization and fixed rules per loop do not allocate precision according to each layer's sensitivity in each loop. Measurements on Ouro show that quantization sensitivity varies across (loop, layer) cells, and the sum of individual cell damages approximates the overall output damage. Sharing damage also varies by cell, with later loops generally tolerating sharing better. Skipping low-attention values to save reads produces errors that compound across loops. LoopKV uses a one-time offline profile to choose 4-bit storage, 2-bit storage or reuse of the final loop's KV for each cell. The planner minimizes reads under budgets for predicted damage and storage. During decoding, each loop reads the high 2 bits of each 4-bit value and fetches the low bits only where its own attention weights require 4-bit precision. A single fused kernel in vLLM executes the plan. Serving Ouro-1.4B on one RTX PRO 6000, the memory-optimized LoopKV-M plan stores 35% less KV than uniform 4-bit quantization (KIVI-4) on the same fused kernel and CUDA graph. It sustains 1.7-2.2x KIVI-4's goodput under a latency service-level objective with a 16K-token maximum length. None of its averages on six public benchmarks falls significantly below BF16's.

</details>


### [192] [RGDM: Robust Graph Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04146)
*Yancheng Wang,Ping Li,Dongfang Sun,Yingzhen Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出鲁棒图扩散模型RGDM，由鲁棒图自编码器（RGAE）与潜空间扩散模型（LDM）组成，可在对抗攻击后的图上生成高质量带标签的合成图结构，用于增强GNN鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有节点级扩散方法能在图内合成节点与边，但并非为从对抗扰动的图中学习鲁棒生成器而设计，难以在攻击数据上产生干净结构。

Method: RGDM = Robust Graph Autoencoder (RGAE) + Latent Diffusion Model (LDM)；RGAE中引入边选择机制（edge selection mechanism）与对潜特征的低秩正则化（low-rank regularization），在潜空间进行扩散以生成节点与边构成的带标签合成图结构，并与给定图融合。

Result: 在攻击图与合成结构合并得到的增强图上训练GNN，在半监督节点分类任务中对多种图对抗攻击的鲁棒性显著提升；即使训练图受过对抗攻击，仍能生成干净、高质量的合成图结构。

Conclusion: 结合边选择与低秩正则的潜空间图扩散可有效实现鲁棒图数据增强，代码已开源。

Abstract: Diffusion models represent a powerful class of generative models known for their solid theoretical foundations and remarkable performance across diverse tasks and domains. Existing node level diffusion methods synthesize nodes and edges within a graph, but are not designed to learn a robust generator from adversarially corrupted graphs. In this study, we introduce the Robust Graph Diffusion Model (RGDM), designed to generate labeled synthetic graph structures consisting of nodes and edges that integrate seamlessly into a given graph. The RGDM consists of a Robust Graph Autoencoder (RGAE) and a Latent Diffusion Model (LDM). Leveraging an edge selection mechanism and an innovative low-rank regularization on the latent feature, the RGDM produces clean and high-quality synthetic graph structures, even when trained on graphs subject to adversarial attacks. Comprehensive experimental evaluations reveal that Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) trained on the augmented graph, which is formed by merging the original attacked graph with the synthetic graph structures, exhibit significantly improved robustness against various graph adversarial attacks in the context of semi-supervised node classification. The code of the RGDM is available at https://github.com/Statistical-Deep-Learning/RGDM.

</details>


### [193] [On the Feature Evolution of Temporal Difference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04158)
*Shangzhe Li,Dechen Zhang,Difan Zou,Weitong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在有限宽度两层ReLU评论家上分析TD学习中预测特征如何习得与传播，证明同步更新隐藏层与输出头可获得严格的有限样本预测风险改进，并给出耦合神经网络更新的统一预测误差瞬态界及探索-提交算法的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TD学习用自举目标训练神经表示，但在神经网络更新不完整（有限步、有限宽度）的情况下，预测特征如何产生并向前传播仍缺乏理论理解；同时已有分析多用线性函数逼近或需解中间Bellman回归，难以刻画自举与神经细化同时发生的情形。

Method: 构造一类未知二次结构、d维观测的平滑随机horizon-H MDP，采用有限宽度两层ReLU评论家；分析信息性TD更新如何获取二次Bellman信号并产生有界预测读出；跟踪耦合的隐藏权重与输出头更新（每个目标副本一步SGD），不求解中间Bellman回归，推导统一预测误差瞬态；在总奖励有界的带符号动作MDP族上设计探索-提交算法。

Result: 隐藏权重与输出头同步更新相较匹配的头-only SGD取得严格的有限样本预测风险改进，总体二次响应大2(d+1)倍；不完美的已学习continuation即可为更早的零奖励阶段提供信息性监督；得到预测误差瞬态exp[-Ω(K/(Hd))]（至多相差动作无关平移，并含显式采样与有限宽度误差）；在T回合内以K=Õ(Hd)次全局更新实现Õ(d³H^{1/3}T^{2/3})遗憾。

Conclusion: 分析将自举特征获取、并发神经细化与有限预算策略性能统一起来：证明TD自举可逐步向前传播信息性监督，同步特征学习优于仅训练读出，并在有限神经更新预算下给出端到端的策略遗憾保证。

Abstract: Temporal-difference (TD) learning trains neural representations using bootstrapped targets generated by evolving value estimates, yet how predictive features emerge and propagate under incomplete neural updates remains poorly understood. We investigate this problem using finite-width two-layer ReLU critics on a constructed family of smooth stochastic horizon-$H$ MDPs with unknown quadratic structure and $d$-dimensional observations. We first show that an informative TD update acquires the quadratic Bellman signal and yields a representation admitting a bounded predictive readout. The simultaneous hidden-weight and head update achieves a strict finite-sample prediction-risk improvement over matched head-only SGD, with a $2(d+1)$-fold larger population quadratic response. We then establish that an imperfect learned continuation can already provide informative supervision to an earlier zero-reward stage. By tracking coupled neural updates with one SGD step per target copy, without requiring intermediate Bellman regressions to be solved, we obtain a uniform prediction-error transient $\exp[-Ω(K/(Hd))]$, up to action-independent shifts and explicit sampling and finite-width errors. On the constructed signed-action MDP family whose total reward is bounded by 1, we show that an exploration-then-commit algorithm can achieve $\widetilde{O}(d^3H^{1/3}T^{2/3})$ regret over $T$ episodes, using $K=\widetilde{O}(Hd)$ global updates. Our analysis connects bootstrapped feature acquisition, concurrent neural refinement, and finite-budget policy performance.

</details>


### [194] [Say the Same, Act Differently: Diagnosing and Steering Text-Action Divergence in Reasoning VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04166)
*Zihao Feng,Qingzhao Zhang,Chunyu Xia,Bo Yu,Z. Morley Mao,Ramesh Govindan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 视觉扰动可改变 VLA 模型的物理动作，同时使生成的自然语言推理理由逐字不变，说明文本理由无法证明动作安全，监控必须直接验证动作通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对 VLA 推理的攻击会污染推理文本本身、留下可见痕迹，因此需要考察是否存在更隐蔽的攻击：文本理由完全不变但动作被悄然篡改，从而检验"可读理由可用于安全监控"这一前提是否成立。

Method: 提出文本中性动作子空间（TNAS）诊断：在 Alpamayo（驾驶）与 InstructVLA（操作）共享的文本/动作接口上，比较局部动作最敏感方向与当前文本损失梯度的正交性（范数重叠仅 1.87% 与 5.28%）；在等范数隐状态扰动下对比沿该文本中性残差方向与沿文本梯度方向的干预效果；并进一步沿该方向对视觉扰动进行引导（全图扰动与前向相机局部补丁）。

Result: 等隐状态扰动范数下，TNAS 方向对动作的偏移比文本梯度扰动大 4.64–47.8 倍，而文本梯度扰动引起的文本漂移大 21.2–277 倍。Alpamayo 154 个障碍标注驾驶场景中，TNAS 引导攻击在理由逐字不变下于 52.6%（全图）和 35.1%（局部补丁）场景引发新碰撞，未引导攻击仅 4.55%；相比文本保持基线，精确理由保持率由 83.0% 升至 89.5%，教师强制文本漂移降低 68.4% 且碰撞率相当。InstructVLA 中文本漂移降低 40.5%，85.0% 样本响应完全保持，同时动作块偏移 1.73 单位。

Conclusion: 理由不变不能作为动作安全的认证，VLA 推理模型的监控机制必须直接验证其预测动作通道。

Abstract: Reasoning vision-language-action (VLA) models pair physical control with natural-language rationales that supervisors can read to monitor embodied safety. Prior attacks on VLA reasoning corrupt the rationale itself and leave visible evidence in the text. We show that visual perturbations can instead alter the physical action while keeping the generated rationale word-for-word identical. At the shared text/action interface of Alpamayo for driving and InstructVLA for manipulation, our text-neutral action-subspace (TNAS) diagnostic shows that the locally most action-sensitive direction is nearly orthogonal to the active text-loss gradients, with 1.87% and 5.28% norm overlap. At equal hidden-state perturbation norms across both models, interventions along this text-neutral residual shift actions 4.64x to 47.8x more than text-gradient perturbations, whereas text-gradient perturbations induce 21.2x to 277x more text drift. Steering visual perturbations along this direction reaches the same decoupling from the image. Across 154 obstacle-annotated driving scenes in Alpamayo, TNAS-guided attacks induce a new collision under a word-for-word identical rationale in 52.6% of scenes under full-image perturbations and 35.1% under a localized forward-camera patch, compared with 4.55% for unguided action attacks. Relative to a text-preserving baseline, TNAS guidance raises exact rationale preservation from 83.0% to 89.5% and cuts teacher-forced text drift by 68.4% at a comparable collision rate. In InstructVLA, TNAS guidance cuts text drift by 40.5% and preserves the exact response on 85.0% of examples alongside a 1.73-unit action-chunk shift. An unchanged rationale therefore cannot certify a safe action, so monitors for reasoning VLAs must verify the predicted action channel directly.

</details>


### [195] [What Does 4-Bit Microscaling Cost a Wearable Activity Recognizer?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04185)
*Megan Healy,Hossein Valavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在159,492参数的1D CNN篮球动作识别模型上实测/模拟了MXFP4与NVFP4两种4-bit块缩放格式，NVFP4精度损失显著更小，差异主要源于MXFP4的scale向下取整导致的块裁剪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴运动传感器需要在设备端做动作识别，低精度数值格式可降低模型存储与内存带宽开销。MXFP4（OCP, 2023）与NVFP4（NVIDIA, 2025）是两种新兴4-bit块缩放格式，但已有评测集中在通用深度学习基准与大语言模型，尚无针对小型可穿戴传感器模型的实际代价测量。

Method: 训练159,492参数的一维CNN，在Hang-Time HAR数据集上用腕部加速度计数据识别4类篮球动作；采用按受试者分组的五折交叉验证，在未见过的受试者上测试，所有格式比较均按折配对进行。对比FP16、INT8的TFLite实测模型与4-bit格式的仿真（权重量化、权重+激活量化），并做了2×3消融（两种块大小×三种scale规则：向下取整、向上取整、8-bit浮点scale）。

Result: 全精度macro-F1为69.26%。实测TFLite FP16与INT8损失≤0.16个百分点。仿真中仅量化权重：MXFP4损失1.45点，NVFP4损失0.72点；权重与激活均量化时，MXFP4损失5.14点，NVFP4仅0.75点，且NVFP4在全部五折均领先。该排序在两种替代仿真器设置下保持，领先幅度3.49–4.40点。消融表明scale选择是MXFP4损失的主因：参考MXFP4向下取整scale会选出过小的scale，导致大量块裁剪最大值；改为向上取整可分别恢复3.18点（32值块）与3.63点（16值块），再改用8-bit浮点scale（同NVFP4）额外增加0.84与1.20点。块大小影响较小：向下取整规则下16值块比32值块低0.44点（五折一致），其他两种规则下无一致效应。

Conclusion: 对于小型可穿戴传感器模型，NVFP4在4-bit权重+激活场景下明显优于MXFP4；性能差距可由scale舍入策略解释，向上取整或采用8-bit浮点scale能大幅缩小差距，而块大小的作用次之。说明微缩放格式的scale规则设计比块粒度更关键。

Abstract: Wearable sports sensors can recognize actions on the device itself, where lower-precision numbers reduce model storage and memory traffic. Two 4-bit block-scaled formats, MXFP4 from the Open Compute Project, introduced in 2023, and NVFP4 from NVIDIA, introduced in 2025, are drawing interest. Published evaluations cover general deep-learning benchmarks and large language models; to our knowledge, nobody has yet measured what they cost a small wearable-sensor model.
  We train a 159,492-parameter 1D CNN to recognize four basketball actions from wrist-accelerometer data in Hang-Time HAR and test it on players it has never seen, using five-fold cross-validation grouped by player. Every comparison between number formats is made fold by fold.
  In full precision the model reaches 69.26\% macro-F1. Measured TFLite FP16 and INT8 models lose at most 0.16 percentage points. In simulation, 4-bit weights cost 1.45 points with MXFP4 and 0.72 points with NVFP4. When activations are quantized to 4 bits as well, MXFP4 loses 5.14 points and NVFP4 only 0.75, and NVFP4 comes out ahead in all five held-out-player folds.
  The ranking survives two alternative simulator settings, with NVFP4 leading by 3.49 to 4.40 points. A $2 \times 3$ ablation with 4-bit weights and activations (two block sizes and three scale rules) points to scale selection as the main source of MXFP4's loss. The reference MXFP4 conversion we simulate rounds the scale down, which can pick scales that are too small, so many blocks clip their largest values. Rounding the scale up recovers 3.18 points with 32-value blocks and 3.63 points with 16-value blocks, and switching to an 8-bit floating-point scale, as NVFP4 does, adds another 0.84 and 1.20 points. Block size matters less. Under this round-down rule, 16-value blocks score 0.44 points below 32-value blocks in all five folds; under the other two rules there is no consistent effect.

</details>


### [196] [Where Did the Update Go? Certifying Declared Change Envelopes for Model Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04192)
*Muhammad Usama,Muhammad Saad Qadir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"声明变更包络"框架，将模型更新后的行为变化区分为"声明范围内"与"范围外泄漏"，用无标注流量上的随时有效检验对泄漏进行认证，并给出样本代价上下界与终身漂移的组合界；实证显示窄更新泄漏严重，仅蒸馏锚定可使其可认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监管者日益接受"事前声明的模型变更"，但证明"更新只在声明处改变行为"与"界定改变了多少"是两个不同的统计问题：有用的更新本就要改变部分决策，因此全局不一致度与回归界无法为其背书。需要一个能刻画"变化位置合规性"的形式化与可认证方法。

Method: 形式化"声明变更包络"：训练前开发者承诺一个输入区域，区域外任何决策改变计为泄漏。泄漏是两模型对比量，可仅用无标注流量通过随时有效（anytime-valid）检验认证，并给出样本复杂度的上界与下界；单次更新的证书可按序组合为对所有声明域之外的终身漂移界。在1.5B–72B模型上测评LoRA、全量微调、偏好优化、强化学习与知识编辑的泄漏。

Result: 窄更新广泛泄漏：领域与策略LoRA更新仅约7–36%的改动决策落在声明域内。256个训练变体中，泄漏与该更新在域外输入上相对现役模型的KL散度高度相关（Spearman 0.94）。常规补救措施均无法在不大幅牺牲预期改动的前提下使规则型更新可认证；而在部署格式的无标注提示上蒸馏现役模型决策（anchored LoRA、GRPO及小学习率全量微调）可从数百至数千样本认证5%泄漏，且证书在留出流量与书面答案上可复现。证书仅覆盖决策型输出；开放对话中仅重新分批就改变11–21%的响应，且无更新可认证。

Conclusion: "变化是否发生在声明处"需独立于"变化幅度"的认证框架，声明变更包络提供了可组合、无标注、随时有效的统计工具。实践上，窄范围更新天然高泄漏，唯一稳健可认证的路径是以现役模型决策为锚的蒸馏；开放生成场景下该保证尚不成立，因采样/分批等非语义扰动已足以产生大量决策翻转。

Abstract: Deployed language models are updated continually, and regulators increasingly accept changes that were declared in advance. Showing that an update changed behavior only where it was declared to is a different statistical problem from bounding how much it changed: a useful update is meant to change some decisions, so global disagreement and regression bounds cannot certify it. We formalize the problem with declared change envelopes. Before training, the developer commits to a region of inputs, and every decision that changes outside it counts as leakage. Leakage compares two models, so it can be certified from unlabeled traffic with anytime-valid tests whose sample cost we bound from above and below, and per-update certificates compose into a bound on lifetime drift outside all declared scopes. We measure leakage for LoRA and full fine-tuning, preference optimization, reinforcement learning, and knowledge editing on models from 1.5B to 72B parameters. Narrow updates leak widely: domain and policy LoRA updates keep only about 7-36% of the decisions they change inside the declared scope. Across 256 trained variants, leakage tracks the update's KL divergence to the incumbent on out-of-scope inputs (Spearman 0.94). None of the customary remedies we tested makes a rule-like update certifiable without giving up much of its intended change. Distilling the incumbent's decisions on unlabeled prompts in the deployment format does: anchored LoRA, GRPO, and (at a small learning rate) full fine-tuning updates certify 5% leakage from a few hundred to a few thousand unlabeled samples, and the certificates replicate on held-out traffic and on written answers. The certificates cover decision-like outputs only; on open-ended chat, re-batching alone changes 11-21% of responses and no update certifies. Code: https://github.com/Usama1002/declared-change-envelopes

</details>


### [197] [An Information Budget for Label-Noise Memorization, and What Statistical Complexity Tracks: A Study with Multilayer Perceptrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04199)
*Nancy Baygorrea C.,Otávio da Fonseca Martins Gomes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究多层感知机对标签噪声的"记忆"程度及其出现时机，提出用标准化记忆信息(具零总体空值)刻画记忆，并考察统计复杂度的作用。89次MNIST实验表明记忆信息随M上升、留出样本近零、接近Fano界；在40%以内噪声率下统计复杂度峰值先于记忆阶段，高噪声率下两者不可区分；作为停止准则仅在低噪声时有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答两个问题：网络记忆了多少标签噪声、何时开始记忆；并检验统计复杂度对预测描述是否有增量贡献。同时指出原始记忆分数M在噪声独立条件下缺乏可用的总体零假设与上界性质。

Method: 以多层感知机为对象，用标准恒等式将预测信息分解为信号项、记忆项与异质性项；给出Fano型界联系记忆项与训练准确率；在MNIST上做89次运行、检验10个预设假设(5支持/4不支持/1不可检验)，比较标准化记忆信息与原始M，并跟踪统计复杂度峰值相对记忆阶段的位置；扩展至280次运行，涵盖Fashion-MNIST、成对翻转噪声及四种训练变体。

Result: 标准化记忆信息在训练样本上随M升高，在带新噪声标签的留出样本上接近零，且接近Fano界；与原始M不同，它在独立条件下具有零总体空值，机会校正基本消除了差异。标签全被替换为错误类时测试准确率降至0.5%，但预测仍保留真实类信息。平均统计复杂度峰值在≤40%损坏率下早于记忆阶段；更高损坏率下峰值落入记忆阶段，与平均训练置信度达0.6的epoch难以分离。作为停止准则仅低噪声时与既有准则一致，检测损坏样本的能力弱于简单无标签分数。扩展实验在均匀噪声下复现，成对翻转噪声下复杂度在两epoch内达峰。

Conclusion: 记忆信息提供了比M更规范的可检验度量，统计复杂度的时序关系随噪声率与噪声类型而变化；作为实用停止准则或数据清洗信号效力有限。结论受限于单一架构(MLP)、两个小数据集与合成噪声。

Abstract: How much label noise has a network memorized, and when? We study a multilayer perceptron and what statistical complexity adds to a description of its predictions. Standard identities decompose predictive information into signal, memorization, and heterogeneity. The memorization term vanishes in the population under the stated noise-independence condition. A Fano-type bound relates it to training accuracies, but the memorized fraction M provides no upper bound. Across 89 MNIST runs with ten pre-specified hypotheses (five supported, four unsupported, one not testable), normalized memorization information rose with M on training samples, remained near zero on held-out samples with fresh noisy labels, and was close to its Fano bound. Unlike raw M, it has a zero population null under the independence condition and chance correction largely removed this difference. When every label was replaced by an incorrect class, test accuracy fell to 0.5%, yet predictions retained information about the true class. Mean statistical complexity peaked before memorization at corruption rates up to 40%. At higher rates, its peak fell within the memorization phase, near the epoch when mean training confidence reached 0.6 and the two could not be separated. As a stopping rule, it matched established rules only at low noise and detected corrupted samples less well than simple label-free scores. An exploratory extension of 280 runs covering Fashion-MNIST, pair-flip noise, and four training variants yielded similar results under uniform noise, but not pair-flip noise, where complexity peaked within two epochs. Conclusions are limited to this architecture, two small datasets, and synthetic noise.

</details>


### [198] [LENS: Reliability-Aware Stock Prediction via LLM-Extracted Priors and News-Driven Semantic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04217)
*Hao Zhou,Tiejun Ma,Luis Felipe Costa Sperb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LENS将关系可靠性作为一等信号，融合LLM提取的静态多关系公司图与新闻驱动的动态语义图，用于股票收益预测与排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN方法将每条抽取出的边视为可信的二值事实，忽略了不同关系来源之间可靠性的异质性；同时难以同时刻画持久的公司关系与时变的市场信号。

Method: 静态侧：从LLM抽取多关系公司图，并多次运行聚合、以置信度加权降低低一致性边的权重。动态侧：构建新闻驱动语义图，采用指数衰减的时间聚合应对覆盖不均。融合侧：引入上下文相关的门控融合模块，在每只股票、每一天的粒度上重新平衡两图。

Result: S&P 500上AR@5提升16.4%、SR@5提升22.6%；NASDAQ 100上分别提升15.5%与20.9%。消融实验分离了各组件的贡献，抽样关系的人工验证支持聚合结果具有依据。

Conclusion: 可靠性感知的LLM关系与新闻语义图携带互补信息，可为基于图的金融预测带来收益。

Abstract: Predicting stock returns requires modeling persistent company relations and time-varying market signals. Graph Neural Network (GNN) approaches combine static priors with dynamic relational graphs, but treat every extracted edge as a trustworthy binary fact, ignoring heterogeneous reliability across relational sources. We propose LENS (LLM-Extracted priors and News-driven Semantics), a static--dynamic graph framework for stock return prediction and ranking that models relational reliability as a first-class signal across the pipeline. On the static side, we extract a multi-relational company graph from a Large Language Model and aggregate it over multiple runs with confidence weighting to downweight low-consistency edges. On the dynamic side, we build a news-driven semantic graph with exponential-decay temporal aggregation to capture changing market context under uneven coverage. A context-dependent gated fusion module rebalances the two graphs at the per-stock, per-day level. On S&P 500, LENS improves AR@5 by 16.4% and SR@5 by 22.6% over the strongest baseline, and on NASDAQ 100 it achieves comparable improvements (15.5% and 20.9% respectively). Ablations isolate each component's contribution, and a human-validation study of sampled relations supports that the aggregation is grounded, providing evidence that reliability-aware LLM relations and news-based semantic graphs carry complementary information for graph-based financial prediction.

</details>


### [199] [LIRA: A More Interpretable Dynamic Loss-Informed Rank Allocation for Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04239)
*Sihao Wang,Bozheng Li,Xuemiao Xu,Haoxin Yang,Yujie Xu,Jie Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出LIRA，一种基于损失信息的动态秩LoRA方法，通过二阶近似估计各层LoRA因子更新引起的局部损失扰动幅度来打分并周期性重分配秩，使秩调整更具可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LoRA固定秩，忽视层与阶段的差异化适配需求；现有自适应秩方法多依赖启发式或一阶信号，秩重分配与局部损失行为之间仅为间接联系，导致每次秩调整的依据难以解释。

Method: LIRA以二阶近似估计近期LoRA因子移动对应的局部损失扰动幅度，为每层打分，逐项给出秩变化依据；并周期性应用基于位移的DELTA算法进行LoRA秩重分配，在匹配预算下产生离散有效秩，同时兼容标准(A,B)适配器。

Result: 在RoBERTa-base的GLUE数据集上、遵循La-LoRA/LoRA协议，LIRA优于该协议下的uniform-LoRA与自适应秩基线；Trajectory EMA以更低估计成本达到与Fixed-point Fisher相当的性能。

Conclusion: 以损失扰动为核心的秩分配信号可在保持标准适配器兼容性与预算匹配的同时提升秩重分配的可解释性与性能，轨迹EMA为高效替代估计方案。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has become a widely used parameter-efficient fine-tuning method for large pretrained models. However, standard LoRA typically assigns a fixed rank profile despite layer- and stage-dependent adaptation needs, while recent adaptive-rank variants often rely on heuristic or first-order signals that provide only an indirect link between rank reallocation and local loss behavior. This leaves the rationale behind each rank reallocation insufficiently interpretable. Motivated by this gap, we propose LIRA (Loss-Informed Rank Allocation), a dynamic-rank LoRA method designed to make online rank reallocation more interpretable. LIRA scores each layer by the estimated magnitude of the local loss perturbation associated with its recent LoRA-factor movements under a second-order approximation, making the basis for each rank change interpretable term by term. By periodically applying DELTA (Displacement-Evaluated Loss-Trajectory Allocation), a displacement-based algorithm for LoRA rank reallocation, LIRA produces discrete effective ranks under matched budgets while remaining compatible with standard (A,B) adapters. On RoBERTa-base GLUE under the La-LoRA/LoRA protocol, LIRA outperforms the uniform-LoRA and adaptive-rank baselines reported under this protocol; Trajectory EMA attains performance competitive with Fixed-point Fisher at lower estimation cost.

</details>


### [200] [OmniST: Topology-Invariant Spatiotemporal Forecasting with Guaranteed-Coverage Uncertainty at Unseen Sensor Locations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04256)
*Praveg Vashishtha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OmniST：一种结构与节点数无关的拓扑不变时空预测器，结合归纳式split-conformal校准，可在任意（含未见）传感器位置给出带有限样本覆盖率保证的预测区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准时空图卷积网络依赖固定尺寸邻接矩阵，模型权重与传感器数N耦合，在训练中未出现的传感器上无法输出，且缺乏不确定性保证。

Method: 坐标化tokenization、每步前向基于地理坐标的k近邻注意力、逐节点双向GRU时间处理，实现零节点数结构依赖；再与归纳式split-conformal校准组合，并采用滚动自适应保形估计应对时间漂移。

Result: Delhi数据30天PM2.5 MAE为31.0 μg/m³，去除40%站点仅退化≤1.1%；未见站点预测优于最近邻插值约12%、优于IGNNK约21%；在四个数据集三领域、30–50%节点缺失下覆盖率≥90%，325个真实传感器仍成立；静态保形后期覆盖率降至约0.50，滚动自适应恢复至整体0.89、后期0.96（区间更宽）。

Conclusion: 拓扑不变的架构设计可实现跨节点数的可迁移预测并保持覆盖率保证，自适应保形校准是修复时间漂移下覆盖率不足的有效手段，代价是区间宽度增大。

Abstract: Standard spatiotemporal graph convolutional networks need a fixed-size adjacency matrix, coupling model weights to a specific sensor count N; they cannot produce any output at a sensor absent from training. We present OmniST, a topology-invariant forecaster whose computation has zero structural dependency on the number of nodes, and which, composed with inductive split-conformal calibration, gives distribution-free, coverage-guaranteed prediction intervals at arbitrary (including never-seen) sensor locations, preserving that guarantee as sensors are added or removed. Topology invariance follows from coordinate-based tokenization, k-nearest-neighbor attention from geographic coordinates at each forward pass, and per-node temporal processing via a bidirectional GRU. On real Delhi air-quality data (37 stations, 2019-2023), OmniST attains a 30-day PM2.5 MAE of 31.0 ug/m3 and degrades at most 1.1 percent when 40 percent of stations are removed. Its inductive forecasts at unseen stations beat nearest-neighbor interpolation (about 12 percent) and a learned kriging GNN (IGNNK, about 21 percent), and, unlike either, carry a finite-sample coverage guarantee. The guarantee holds at 90 percent or above under 30-50 percent node dropout across four real datasets over three domains (Delhi and Beijing air quality, NOAA GHCN weather, Caltrans PeMSD7-M traffic), and still holds at 325 real sensors (full PeMS-BAY). We also fix a temporal-drift failure: static conformal under-covers over time (about 0.50 late), but a rolling adaptive-conformal estimator restores target coverage (0.89 overall, 0.96 late) at the cost of wider intervals.

</details>


### [201] [Deep Learning Compilers: Architecture, Optimization, and Empirical Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04273)
*Jinghao Wen,Ruixuan Wang,Xun Jiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对深度学习编译器进行系统梳理，划分为前端、中端、后端三阶段，重点分析中端在高层模型表示与硬件特定优化之间的桥梁作用；并结合代表性编译器与优化技术，在 NVIDIA RTX A6000 上用 PyTorch/TorchInductor 开展实证评估，量化图优化收益、编译开销与动态形状处理效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DL 模型日益复杂、硬件架构趋于异构，DL 编译器在计算效率优化与可扩展部署中作用关键；然而缺乏将架构性分析与定量实验证据相结合的系统研究，特别是编译收益与工作负载特征的关系、编译开销摊销成本及动态形状编译的收益-代价权衡尚不清晰。

Method: (1) 按前端—中端—后端三阶段组织 DL 编译器架构，突出中端优化；(2) 梳理代表性 DL 编译器及各编译阶段的近期优化技术；(3) 实证评估：基于 PyTorch/TorchInductor 在 NVIDIA RTX A6000 上测量图优化加速比、编译开销（摊销所需执行次数）与动态形状编译的累积形状切换开销及稳态性能。

Result: 编译器收益高度依赖工作负载特性：对利于算子融合的计算图最高获得 8.21× 加速，其他负载提升有限；编译开销需数千次执行方可摊销；动态形状编译使累积形状切换开销降低约 70%，但伴随一定的稳态性能下降。

Conclusion: 将系统化架构分析与定量实验证据相结合，可为现代 DL 编译器的设计、优化与实际部署提供参考：需针对工作负载特征评估编译收益，权衡编译开销摊销与动态形状的收益-代价。

Abstract: As deep learning (DL) models become increasingly complex and hardware architectures grow more heterogeneous, DL compilers play a critical role in optimizing computational efficiency and enabling scalable deployment. This paper presents a systematic analysis of DL compilers, covering their architectural evolution, compilation workflows, optimization techniques, and emerging research directions. We organize DL compiler architectures into frontend, middle-end, and backend stages, emphasizing the role of the middle-end in connecting high-level model representations with hardware-specific optimizations. We further examine representative DL compilers and recent optimization techniques across different compilation stages. Beyond architectural and technical analysis, we conduct an empirical evaluation of compiler behaviors to investigate the effectiveness of graph optimization, compilation overhead, and dynamic-shape handling. Experiments using PyTorch/TorchInductor on an NVIDIA RTX A6000 demonstrate that compiler benefits depend strongly on workload characteristics, with speedups of up to 8.21\times for fusion-friendly computation graphs, while other workloads exhibit limited improvements. Our results further reveal that compilation overhead can require thousands of executions to amortize, and dynamic-shape compilation reduces cumulative shape-transition costs by approximately 70\% at the expense of modest steady-state performance degradation. By combining systematic architectural analysis with quantitative experimental evidence, this work provides insights into the design, optimization, and practical deployment of modern DL compilers.

</details>


### [202] [Reciprocity Can Erase Direction:tructural Boundaries and Controlled Utility of Magnetic Graph Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04295)
*Dawei Han,Zicheng Wang,Yaoxing Ren,Yi Lv,Rongqian Wu,Fumin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究基于支撑集的磁拉普拉斯图网络在预测前可能抹除方向机制的问题，通过精确恒等式、可证明的配对构造与72次固定拟合实验，揭示top-k与稠密支撑导致的互易性伪影，并区分"方向被抹除"与"任务效用未建立"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁图网络通过相位编码方向，但学习到的图结构可能在预测前就破坏预期的方向机制；现有工作缺乏对这一失效形式的刻画，也难以区分"方向信息被擦除"与"方向对任务本无用处"。

Method: 针对支撑集型磁拉普拉斯给出精确恒等式，联系虚部能量、与零相位的距离和幅值加权互易性；分析top-k选择的坍缩条件（观测节点数满足m≤k+1时完全坍缩）与稠密支撑下互易弧比例下界；构造节点特征与对称幅值相同但标签相反的成对传播样例；在单向环、互易环、完全支撑上做72次固定神经拟合对照（支撑相位 vs 连续分数相位 vs 实值有向比较器），并在DeepDRiD开发集的保留患者上做描述性验证。

Result: 支撑相位在单向环上测试AUROC为1.0000，但在互易环和完全支撑上退化为0.5000（机会水平）；连续分数相位与实值有向比较器在所有情形下均为1.0000；所有模型均能求解无方向对照任务。在DeepDRiD开发集验证患者上，连续相位虽处于激活状态，但相对零相位的稳健性差异仅为+0.0036（描述性95%区间−0.0014至+0.0095）。

Conclusion: 该文提出了区分"方向被抹除"与"任务效用未确立"的结构性与实验性框架，给出磁相位保留有用信息的确切条件；作者强调这些是oracle方向机制测试，不构成学习到的临床方向证据，也不主张普遍或临床优势。

Abstract: Magnetic graph networks encode direction through phase, but a learned graph can erase the intended directional mechanism before prediction. We characterize this failure for support-based magnetic Laplacians. Exact identities relate imaginary energy and distance from zero phase to amplitude-weighted reciprocity. Top-$k$ selection forces complete collapse when the observed-node count satisfies $m\leq k+1$, and dense support imposes a lower bound on reciprocal-arc fraction. We then construct paired propagation examples with identical node features and symmetric amplitudes but opposite labels, giving a provable chance-level limit for direction-erasing representations. In 72 fixed neural fits, support phase achieved test AUROC 1.0000 on a unilateral ring but 0.5000 on reciprocal ring and complete supports; continuous score phase and a real directed comparator achieved 1.0000 throughout. All models solved the direction-free control. These are oracle-direction mechanism tests, not evidence of learned clinical direction.
