In the preserved public DeepDRiD development study, continuous phase was active but its robustness difference from zero phase was only $+0.0036$ (descriptive 95\% interval $-0.0014$ to $+0.0095$) on validation patients used for checkpoint selection. The contribution is a structural and experimental framework for distinguishing erased direction from unestablished task utility. It provides a precise circumstance in which magnetic phase preserves useful information, without asserting universal or clinical superiority.

</details>


### [203] [What Is the Influence of Task Order in Continual Learning? An Empirical Study and Order-Aware Evaluation for Motion Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04309)
*Zirui Li,Yunlong Lin,Haohan Yang,Xiaocong Zhao,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 持续学习算法的聚合分数依赖于任务顺序分布，但该分布很少被声明。作者将算法比较表述为声明式顺序分布上的期望，并在INTERACTION数据集四个道路布局任务的运动预测上穷举24种顺序、4种算法、3次训练重复（288次运行），发现任务顺序占误差与后向迁移变异的65%–91%，其中90%–99%来自起始任务身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习基准的聚合分数隐含了一个未加声明的任务顺序分布，混合了训练波动与顺序效应，导致算法排名不可解释；作者希望显式分离这两种变异来源。

Method: 将算法比较形式化为声明顺序分布上的期望；在INTERACTION数据集四个道路布局任务上穷举全部24种任务顺序，测试4种算法、3次训练重复共288次运行；分解顺序效应来源，分析起始任务身份、后向迁移的参考项权重、以及基于任务统计构造的转移方向描述子。

Result: 任务顺序解释当前任务误差与后向迁移变异的65%–91%，其中90%–99%源于起始任务：最小任务无法从冷启动学习。首阶段之后顺序效应与训练波动相当；多数顺序下领先算法与次优算法差距在一个重复标准差内。后向迁移主要通过权重最大给予首任务的参考项依赖顺序，故相对改进与最终误差会选出不同领先算法。转移方向描述子可追踪最小任务位置，按其加权不改变算法排名。

Conclusion: 呼吁基准声明其顺序分布，并在聚合分数之外报告顺序分辨、起始任务分辨、热启动与最终误差。

Abstract: Aggregate scores for continual learning algorithms rest on a distribution over task orders that is rarely stated. We formulate algorithm comparison as an expectation over a declared order distribution, which separates training variation from order variation, and apply it to motion forecasting with four road-layout tasks from the INTERACTION dataset, all 24 task orders, four algorithms, and three training replicates (288 runs). Task order accounts for 65\% to 91\% of the variation in current-task error and backward transfer, and 90\% to 99\% of this order effect comes from the identity of the starting task: the smallest task cannot be learned from a cold start. Beyond the first stage, the order effect is comparable to training variability. In most orders, the leading algorithm in current-task error and backward transfer is within one replicate standard deviation of the runner-up. Backward transfer depends on the order mainly through its reference term, which weights the first task most heavily, so relative improvement and final error identify different leading algorithms for displacement error. A transition-direction descriptor computed from task statistics tracks the position of the smallest task, and weighting orders by it leaves the algorithm ranking unchanged. These results call for benchmarks that declare their order distribution and report order-resolved, starting-task-resolved, warm-start, and final errors alongside aggregate scores. The code and data are available \href{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/task-order-cl-4f7b/}{here}.

</details>


### [204] [Coarse Semantic Blocks for Efficient Training-Free Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04313)
*Sosuke Kobayashi,Shoichiro Yamaguchi,Seiya Tokui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练的稀疏注意力方法：基于提示构建键索引，将键聚类为跨非连续位置的大语义块，查询按块质心打分选块后在块内对原始键值做注意力，并对选同块的查询分组处理，从而同时加速prefill与解码，且更大块/更大预算可兼顾更快与更准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文下稠密注意力的prefill与解码代价过高；现有稀疏注意力在查询各自选块时难以保持prefill效率，且常需训练。作者希望以减少的索引与分组开销，让每个查询在相同时间内覆盖更多token，从而实现“更大块反而更快更准”。

Method: 免训练稀疏注意力 + 提示特定键索引：(1) 将键聚类为大语义块，块内包含来自非连续位置的token；(2) 每个查询位置通过给各块质心打分来选择块；(3) 选中块后对其中原始键和值做精确注意力；(4) 选择相同块的查询被分组批处理，使prefill在每查询独立选块的前提下仍保持高效；(5) 大块使质心打分与分组处理代价低廉，从而在同等时间内允许更大token预算。评估Qwen3.5-4B与35B-A3B两个混合LLM，单张NVIDIA H200，含索引构建与使用成本的整体wall-clock对比稠密注意力。

Result: 512K token时prefill加速2.3–2.4x、解码1.7–2.0x；1M时prefill加速2.9–3.0x。35B-A3B在1M以内的五项RULER任务、LongBench-v2整体及256K以内的MRCR上接近稠密注意力。4B模型主要在multi-key retrieval上掉点，但在三个基准上均优于延迟相近（512K）的稠密Qwen3.5-2B。

Conclusion: 免训练、提示特定的块级键索引稀疏注意力可在长上下文（至1M）显著加速prefill与解码，并在大模型上保持接近稠密的精度；块粒度越大配合更大token预算，可同时提升速度与长上下文准确率。

Abstract: We introduce a training-free sparse attention method that builds a prompt-specific key index to accelerate both prefill and decoding. The method clusters the keys into large semantic blocks that group tokens from noncontiguous positions. Each query position selects blocks by scoring their centroids and attends to the original keys and values within them. Queries that select the same block are processed together, so prefill stays fast even though each query chooses its own blocks. Large blocks make centroid scoring and this grouped processing cheap, so each query can attend to more tokens in the same time. Larger blocks with a larger token budget can thus be both faster and more accurate on long-context tasks than smaller blocks with a smaller budget. We evaluate two hybrid LLMs, Qwen3.5-4B and 35B-A3B, on one NVIDIA H200. Including the cost of building and using the index, whole-model wall-clock speedups over dense attention are 2.3-2.4x for prefill and 1.7-2.0x for decoding at 512K tokens, and 2.9-3.0x for prefill at 1M. The 35B-A3B model stays close to dense attention on five RULER tasks through 1M, on LongBench-v2 overall, and on MRCR through 256K. The 4B model loses accuracy mainly on multi-key retrieval, yet outperforms dense Qwen3.5-2B, whose latency is similar at 512K, on all three benchmarks.

</details>


### [205] [Anchor: Augmented Cross-Layer Recurrent State for Long-Context Retrieval in Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04327)
*Vimal William,Yuanzhe Li,Danella Zhao,Huanrui Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Anchor方法：仅在少数"锚点层"放置TTT循环记忆，并让跨层共享的循环状态由多层通过各自的读映射读取，从而以10倍更小的循环状态显著提升长上下文检索与MMLU表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究SWA与TTT混合的线性化模型中，各层循环状态是否都对长上下文检索有贡献，以解决循环状态冗余、参数与延迟开销大的问题。

Method: 关键发现有二：检索能力在深度上稀疏分布、集中于少数锚点层；其余状态所存信息相似、仅经层特异的变换表达。据此设计Anchor：只在锚点层放置TTT记忆，并采用"写一次、多层读"的跨层循环状态，每层通过自身学习到的读映射实现层特异的拉伸与旋转；该方法可推广到其他混合注意力。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B与Mistral-7B上，相比LoLCATs，Anchor的召回率高2.3-3.1倍、MMLU准确率高6.7-8.9个点，而循环状态小10倍；保留教师模型MMLU准确率的91-95%；端到端延迟相对softmax注意力最高降低4.3倍。

Conclusion: 循环状态在深度上高度冗余且可共享，将记忆集中于锚点层并配合层特异读映射能在更小状态下取得更优检索与下游性能及更低延迟，具备对其他混合注意力的通用性。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient linearization turns a pretrained autoregressive Transformer into an efficient model by hybridizing sliding-window attention (SWA) with a constant-size recurrent memory. But does every layer's recurrent state contribute to long-context retrieval? Investigating an SWA and Test-Time Training (TTT) hybrid, we find that retrieval is sparse across depth, concentrated in a few anchor layers, and that the remaining states hold similar information expressed through layer-specific transformations. We exploit both observations in Anchor: TTT memory placed only at the anchor layers, and a cross-layer recurrent state written once and read by many layers, each through its own learned read map that realizes a layer-specific stretch and rotation, along with demonstrated generalization to other hybrid attentions. On Llama-3.1-8B and Mistral-7B, Anchor delivers 2.3-3.1x higher recall and 6.7-8.9 points higher MMLU accuracy than LoLCATs with a 10x smaller recurrent state, retains 91-95% of the teacher's MMLU accuracy, and cuts end-to-end latency by up to 4.3x relative to softmax attention.

</details>


### [206] [Proper Agnostic Learning of Affine Halfspaces under Log-Concave Marginals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04344)
*Zhijie Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文给出了在全维对数凹边缘分布下仿射半空间的常数因子不可知学习器，用独立标注样本输出单一半空间，误差不超过 C·OPT+ε。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不可知学习结果多依赖已知均值协方差或同质半空间设定，需在对数凹分布下实现无需已知矩的仿射半空间常数因子不可知学习。

Method: 核心是比较全局 disagreement 与平衡限制下条件 disagreement 的几何引理；用噪声标签稳健估计少数类"帽"的均值与协方差，近似最小化 zonoid-depth 对偶目标，并将超水平集扩展至边缘分位数。分析结合对数凹分布的四次 SOS 矩界，以及将平衡仿射学习归约为同质半空间学习的平移变换。

Result: 算法样本与实算术复杂度关于维度、1/ε 及 log(1/δ) 多项式，且样本量对该置信因子可线性；均值与协方差无需已知。

Conclusion: 几何引理抵消了条件化带来的噪声放大，使所学半空间可用于整个空间，从而获得对数凹分布下仿射半空间的常数因子不可知保证。

Abstract: We give a proper constant-factor agnostic learner for affine halfspaces under full-dimensional log-concave marginals. For arbitrary binary labels, the algorithm uses independent labeled samples and returns a single affine halfspace with error at most $C\mathrm{OPT}+\varepsilon$. Its sample and real-arithmetic complexity are polynomial in dimension, inverse accuracy, and the logarithm of the reciprocal failure probability. The mean and covariance of the marginal need not be known, and the sample complexity can be made linear in this logarithmic confidence factor. The main geometric result compares global disagreement with disagreement on a balanced restriction: for a minority halfspace of mass $p$, global disagreement is within universal constant factors of $p$ times conditional disagreement, provided the latter is sufficiently small. To construct the restriction from noisy labels, we estimate the minority cap's mean and covariance robustly, approximately minimize the classical zonoid-depth dual objective, and expand a superlevel set to a marginal quantile. The disagreement comparison cancels the noise amplification caused by conditioning and allows the learned halfspace to be used on the entire space. The analysis combines degree-four sum-of-squares moment bounds for log-concave laws with a translation-based reduction from balanced affine learning to homogeneous halfspace learning.

</details>


### [207] [Aligning Training and Rollout Policies for End-to-End FP4 RL on Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04376)
*Hei Yi Mak,Hoang Le,Mehran Taghian Jazi,Yunke Peng,Huan Lin,Zhengzhi Tang,Zhexu Liu,Yao Wang,Yaoyuan Wang,Anandharaju Durai Raju,Tanzila Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 MoE 大模型的端到端 FP4 强化学习后训练，现有面向稠密模型的恢复配方失效；本文将其归因于路由离散选择导致的训练-推理对齐对"对称性"的敏感性，并提出对称化恢复配方 MoeResQ，显著缩小与全精度的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FP4 RL 的低精度恢复方法在稠密模型上有效，但直接迁移到 MoE 模型时相对朴素 FP4 基线毫无提升，作者旨在查清这一失效原因。

Method: 通过深入分析定位根因为 MoE 特有的路由机制：router 做离散专家选择，使训练-推理策略对齐对恢复技术是否在训练侧与推理侧对称施加高度敏感。以训练/推理概率的 Pearson 相关系数与路由选择匹配率作为度量验证非对称配方下对齐的急剧退化，并据此提出 MoeResQ——强制训练侧与推理侧校正对称的量化恢复配方。

Result: 非对称配方（包括仅校正 rollout 的稠密模型做法）会导致训练-推理对齐指标显著恶化；MoeResQ 恢复了对齐，并大幅缩小与全精度的精度差距。

Conclusion: 对称恢复是 MoE 模型 FP4 强化学习的关键原则；恢复配方需在训练与推理两侧一致施加，才能避免路由决策引发对齐失配。

Abstract: In this work, we study end-to-end FP4 reinforcement learning (RL) post-training for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) large language models. Recent work on FP4 RL has introduced effective recovery recipes that restore accuracy under aggressive low-precision quantization for dense models, but we find that the same approach yields no improvement over the naive FP4 baseline in the MoE setting. We conduct an in-depth study to understand why. Our analysis identifies the root cause in the routing mechanism unique to MoE: because the router makes discrete expert-selection decisions, training-inference policy alignment becomes acutely sensitive to whether the same recovery technique is applied symmetrically on the training and inference sides. When an asymmetric recipe is used-including the dense-model approach, which corrects only the rollout-training-inference alignment deteriorates sharply, as measured by the Pearson correlation coefficient between training and inference probabilities, and router selection matching rates. Building on this finding, we propose MoeResQ, a quantization-recovery recipe for MoE RL post-training that enforces symmetry between the training- and inference-side corrections. MoeResQ restores training-inference alignment and substantially closes the accuracy gap to full precision, establishing symmetric recovery as the key principle in FP4 RL for MoE models.

</details>


### [208] [Sketchpad: certified exact arithmetic with a small vision-language model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04434)
*Mostafa 'Neo' Mohsenvand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Sketchpad 将算术计算外化为可渲染的"草稿纸"工作记忆，每步只读取三格视图并输出一位结果，使"任意长度输入上的精确性"化为有限可验证性质，并在 Lean 中完成证明与部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在长算术上仅靠采样估计精度，无法对未见输入提供保证；而工具调用虽精确却把计算移出网络，失去了模型本身的能力边界刻画。作者希望找到一种机制，使"对所有输入长度的精确性"成为网络可检验的有限性质。

Method: 构建 Sketchpad：以 Qwen3.5-0.8B + 低秩适配器在渲染的"纸面"上执行算术流程。每次前向只读取三格视图，输出单步（数字、进位/借位、下一步操作）；固定运行时负责移动视图且不写入任何结果数字。随后在 Lean 中形式化证明：模型在所有长度输入上精确当且仅当在运行时呈现的有限视图集合上精确；若单步也读取此前步骤则任何有限测试都不充分；该保证对有界数值扰动稳健，并可扩展到流程组合。适配器上加乘性门控，未启用纸面时精确恢复基模型。

Result: 全部 2,446 个视图在部署设备（浏览器中运行模型的笔记本 GPU）上通过；认证模型可做任意长度的有符号加减、长乘除、开方与最大公约数。浏览器中预注册 200 题全对；1000 位加和与 20 位乘积累计 220/220 正确，而十个对照模型中最强的 GPT-6.1 Sol 为 217。基于这些流程的符号运行时可在所有数字均由模型计算的前提下完成多项式求解、展开与因式分解。

Conclusion: 通过有界读取 + 外部工作记忆 + 固定运行时的设计，长程算术的精确性可被有限枚举验证并形式化证明，兼顾了模型内生能力与可证明保证，且能组合成更复杂的符号计算系统。

Abstract: Language models are accurate on most long-arithmetic problems, but their accuracy is estimated on samples and carries no guarantee for an unseen input; tool use is exact but moves the computation outside the network. We show that exactness at all input lengths becomes a finite, checkable property of a network when each of its steps reads only a bounded view of an external working memory. In Sketchpad, Qwen3.5-0.8B with a low-rank adapter executes arithmetic procedures on a rendered sheet. Each forward pass reads a three-cell view and emits one step: a digit, a carry or borrow, and the next operation. A fixed runtime moves the view and writes no digit of a result. We prove in Lean that the model is then exact on all inputs of any length if and only if it is exact on the finite set of views the runtime presents, that no finite test suffices when steps also read earlier steps, and that the guarantee tolerates bounded numerical perturbation and extends to compositions of procedures. All 2,446 views pass on the deployment device, a laptop GPU running the model in a web browser. The certified model performs signed addition and subtraction, long multiplication and division, square roots and greatest common divisors at arbitrary length. It solved 200 of 200 pre-registered problems in the browser, and 220 of 220 sums of up to 1,000 digits and products of up to 20 digits, of which GPT-6.1 Sol, the strongest of ten other models, solved 217. A symbolic runtime built on these procedures solves, expands and factors polynomials with every number computed by the model, and a multiplicative gate on the adapter recovers the base model exactly when the sheet is not in use.

</details>


### [209] [Near-Zero Token Embeddings Silently Stall Training in Gated Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04449)
*Xin Liu,Boyuan Sun,Yuxin Wang,Haoran Song,Mingjian Zhao,Mengyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模仓库调研发现，非权重共享的门控/混合模型中输入端嵌入行可能塌缩至近零范数，进而在门控序列模型中引发梯度放大 30+ 个数量级，导致 FP32 全局范数裁剪溢出、整批梯度被清零、相应嵌入行冻结；作者提出 GroupClip（检查点修复）与 GateFloor（预训练预防）两种方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放权重模型广泛使用非权重共享（untied）的输入嵌入，若某些稀有或模板 token 在预训练中从未被输入（零梯度），AdamW 的权重衰减会持续缩小这些行直至接近零。此前缺乏对这一"近零嵌入行"现象的系统性实证，也未认识到它在门控序列模型中会引发灾难性的梯度爆炸与训练更新丢失。

Method: 1) 调研 291 个开放权重仓库（280 个嵌入表），统计近零输入嵌入行（范数 < 1e-6）的分布；2) 分析机制：零输入下 SiLU 门/线性查询失效 → 历史状态读出被抑制 → 残差 RMS 低于 RMSNorm 下限的平方根 → Pre-RMSNorm 逐层放大反向梯度（至多 1e3/层）；3) 定位故障点：FP32 全局范数裁剪（PyTorch / HF Trainer / FSDP2）在梯度项超过 1.84e19 时上溢为 inf，导致该批梯度被清零；4) 提出 GroupClip：对输入嵌入解耦裁剪，使用 64 位或重缩放范数；5) 提出 GateFloor：将门控改为 SiLU(z)+δ，δ∈[0.01,0.1]，使零输入下门仍为正。

Result: 52.6%（20/38）的 untied 门控与混合检查点含有可达的近零输入嵌入行，而权重共享模型为 0%。在 Mamba、Gated DeltaNet、xLSTM 中，近零 token 可将输入嵌入梯度放大约 30 个数量级（最高 35），Pre-RMSNorm 每层可放大反向梯度达 1e3。在 Telugu 持续预训练 Nemotron-3-Nano-4B 中，批梯度清零导致约 20% 的更新步被丢弃且被触发行冻结。GroupClip 的重缩放实现可消除更新丢失，并在一步内修复被触发行，端到端开销可忽略；GateFloor 在 370M 参数规模实验中将近零梯度降低 28 个数量级，困惑度与基线相当。

Conclusion: 非权重共享输入嵌入 + 权重衰减 + 门控架构 + FP32 全局范数裁剪构成一条可复现的失效链，在已发布模型与预训练中均真实存在。GroupClip 用于发布检查点的低成本修复，GateFloor 用于预训练阶段从源头预防，二者分别针对"事后补救"与"事前防御"，且不损害训练效果。

Abstract: Among 291 surveyed open-weight language model repositories (280 distinct embedding tables), 52.6% of untied gated and hybrid checkpoints (20/38) contain reachable input embeddings with norms below $10^{-6}$, compared with 0% of weight-tied models. Such near-zero rows can arise during pretraining when unseen rare or template tokens receive zero input gradients while AdamW weight decay shrinks untied embeddings. In tested Transformer configurations, non-initial positions or a BOS token prevent this failure mode. In gated sequence models (Mamba, Gated DeltaNet, xLSTM), a near-zero token can amplify input-embedding gradients by more than 30 orders of magnitude (up to 35). At zero input, current-token readout factors (SiLU gates or linear queries) vanish, suppressing historical-state readout. Residual RMS values can then fall below the square root of the RMSNorm floor across consecutive layers, causing Pre-RMSNorm to amplify backward gradients by up to $10^3$ per layer. When a gradient entry exceeds $1.84 \times 10^{19}$, FP32 global-norm clipping in PyTorch, Hugging Face Trainer, and FSDP2 can overflow to infinity and zero the current batch's gradients. In Telugu continual pretraining of Nemotron-3-Nano-4B, this discards updates on approximately 20% of steps and leaves triggered rows frozen. We propose two remedies. For released checkpoints, GroupClip decouples input-embedding clipping using a 64-bit or rescaled norm; its rescaled implementation eliminates update loss and heals triggered rows in one step with negligible measured end-to-end overhead. For pretraining, GateFloor ($\mathrm{SiLU}(z) + δ$, $δ\in [0.01, 0.1]$) keeps the gate positive at zero input, reducing near-zero gradients by 28 orders of magnitude in experiments up to 370M parameters, with perplexity comparable to the baseline.

</details>


### [210] [SaMA: Morpho Adaptation via Asymmetric Expansion of Kronecker Product](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04462)
*An Nguyen,Anh Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种参数高效微调方法 SaMA，通过对 Kronecker 积的完美洗牌分解进行推广，突破了 LoRA 的有效秩与优化瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA 存在两个根本局限：一是不变流形在损失曲面上诱导出平坦方向，给自适应优化器造成优化瓶颈；二是其有效秩仅随参数预算线性增长。

Method: 提出 Scalable Morpho Adaptation (SaMA)，基于 Kronecker 积的原理性推广。通过放松完美洗牌分解中的共享块约束，使秩覆盖从低秩到满秩，且实际秩随参数预算呈二次增长；同时说明其不变流形收缩为对角群，结构上对优化器更友好。

Result: 在常识推理与算术推理基准上，SaMA 一致优于强 PEFT 基线，且参数效率更高。

Conclusion: SaMA 在保持参数高效的同时实现更高的有效秩与更优化的损失几何，是 LoRA 的有力替代方案。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has attracted significant attention in the parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) landscape. However, its design suffers from two fundamental limitations. First, its invariance manifold induces flat directions in the loss landscape, creating an optimization bottleneck for adaptive optimizers. Second, its effective rank scales only linearly with the parameter budget. In this paper, we propose Scalable Morpho Adaptation (SaMA), a PEFT method grounded in a principled generalization of the Kronecker product. By relaxing the shared-block constraint in the perfect-shuffle factorization, SaMA spans from low to full rank and achieves a practical rank that scales quadratically with the parameter budget, up to full-rank. Furthermore, we show that its invariance manifold collapses to a diagonal group, making it structurally more optimizer-friendly. Empirically, SaMA consistently outperforms strong PEFT baselines on commonsense and arithmetic reasoning benchmarks while being more parameter-efficient.

</details>


### [211] [Theoretical Analysis of Muon on Transformers for Next-Token Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04465)
*Shengwei Wu,Yixuan Yang,Gao Huang,Song Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上分析Muon优化器在Transformer上训练下一词预测（NTP）的动力学，将训练分为预处理阶段（学习输出-值分量）与主阶段（学习键-查询分量），分别给出精确优化轨迹与非渐近收敛速率，并给出未见上下文的泛化保证与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon在训练Transformer大语言模型上经验表现优异，但其优化动力学缺乏理论理解；作者希望解释其有效性来源。

Method: 对Muon训练NTP的整个过程做理论分析：预处理阶段刻画精确优化轨迹并证明每次更新后归一化谱间隔最优；主阶段在softmax注意力下推导训练损失与归一化间隔差的非渐近收敛速率；并分析所学权重结构以得到泛化界；辅以NTP任务实验验证。

Result: 预处理阶段：优化轨迹可精确刻画，更新后归一化谱间隔最优。主阶段：在softmax注意力下得到损失与归一化间隔差的非渐近收敛速率；学到的权重结构给出对未见上下文的泛化保证；实验支持理论结论。

Conclusion: Muon的更新规则能帮助模型高效学习哪些token对预测重要，这可能解释其在大语言模型训练中的强表现。

Abstract: Muon has recently shown strong empirical performance in training Transformer-based large language models (LLMs), yet its optimization dynamics remain poorly understood from a theoretical perspective. In this paper, we analyze the training dynamics of Muon for next-token prediction (NTP) in Transformers. The training procedure consists of two phases: the preprocessing phase learns the output-value component, while the main phase learns the key-query component. For the preprocessing phase, we characterize the optimization trajectory exactly and show that the normalized spectral margin is optimal after each update. For the main phase, we establish nonasymptotic convergence rates for the training loss and the normalized margin gap under softmax attention. These results suggest that Muon's update rule can help models efficiently learn which tokens matter for prediction, offering a possible explanation for its strong performance in large language model training. The learned weight structure also yields a generalization guarantee for unseen contexts. Furthermore, we validate our theoretical insights with experiments on next-token prediction tasks.

</details>


### [212] [SKILL2STEER: One-Pass Hypernetwork for Mapping Natural-Language Skills to Low-Rank Post-Block Activation Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04497)
*Ran Li,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有"将自然语言技能编译进模型参数"的方法都假设产物携带技能内容；作者用三项控制实验证明其实际只是一个领域通用的行为先验，随后提出SKILL2STEER：一次性读取技能文档并映射为各解码块输出的低秩激活偏移，每技能仅2.36M参数（比权重空间适配器少9.25倍），在SearchQA七数据集基准上达37.9 EM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编译技能可把技能文本从提示中移除，留下可分发的轻量产物。但已有方法仅在"注入位置"上不同，却共同假设产物编码了技能内容，该假设未被检验。

Method: 对检索智能体做三项控制：(1)错误技能文档 vs 正确技能文档；(2)单一平均化bundle vs 逐技能路由（双侧等价检验）；(3)同范数随机方向对照。据此提出SKILL2STEER——单次前向的超网络，读取技能文档一次，输出加在每个解码块输出端的低秩激活偏移；基座权重冻结，生成器仅在编译时运行，可按检索步调度并以无路由的单一bundle部署。

Result: 错误技能与正确技能表现相当；单一平均bundle在双侧等价检验下与逐技能路由相匹配；同范数随机方向退化为无注入基线，说明训练得到的偏移方向是关键承载。SearchQA上37.9 EM，比冻结模型高9.8点，比参数多9.25倍的权重空间方法高2.3点。

Conclusion: 编译技能携带的是领域通用行为先验而非技能内容，故逐技能路由的必要性被削弱；激活层低秩偏移是更参数高效的注入面，支持无路由单bundle部署。

Abstract: Compiling a natural-language agent skill into model parameters removes the skill text from the prompt and leaves behind a small artifact that can be distributed and reused. Existing methods differ in where they inject the compiled quantity, and all of them assume that the artifact carries the content of the skill. We test that assumption. Through three controls on a retrieval agent, we find that a compiled skill shift carries a domain-general behavioural prior, not the content of the skill. A wrong skill document performs on par with the correct one, a single averaged bundle matches per-skill routing under a two-sided equivalence test, and a same-norm random direction collapses to the no-injection baseline, which shows that the trained direction stays load-bearing. We then introduce SKILL2STEER, a one-pass hypernetwork that reads a skill document once and maps it to low-rank activation shifts added at the output of every decoder block. Each skill compiles to 2.36M parameters, 9.25 times fewer than a weight-space adapter, while the base weights stay frozen and the generator runs only at compile time. The shift can be scheduled across retrieval steps and deployed as one routing-free bundle. On SearchQA, a seven-dataset benchmark, SKILL2STEER reaches 37.9 exact match, 9.8 points above the frozen model and 2.3 points above a weight-space method that ships 9.25 times more parameters per skill.

</details>


### [213] [Sample-Grained Approximate Unlearning with Provable Per-Sample Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04501)
*Yaxin Xiao,Yulin Jin,Li Tang,Haoyang LI,Huadi Zheng,Qingqing Ye,Haibo Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SALA（Sample-grained Likelihoods ALignment）插件式优化框架，将近似机器遗忘从数据集级聚合指标推进到单样本粒度，并给出理论保证与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近似遗忘方法仅用数据集级聚合指标评估（如整体准确率、平均损失等），这些聚合量会掩盖个体样本层面与精确重训练之间的偏差，导致单样本遗忘效果被高估或忽视。

Method: （1）SALA：一个插件式优化框架，使任意近似遗忘方法在单样本层面运作，将每个样本引导至其正确的目标分布（成员/非成员目标分布）；（2）提出sample-grained distribution gap这一度量，量化近似遗忘与精确重训练在每样本输出分布上的差异；（3）给出低成本的成员与非成员目标分布估计方法，使框架可落地。

Result: 理论上证明SALA收缩该分布差距，并给出与重训练之间逐样本差异的有界性保证；在分类与生成任务的实验中，与现有近似遗忘基线结合可将sample-grained distribution gap最多降低86.67%，同时提升对遗忘超参数的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 单样本粒度的评估与优化是近似机器遗忘中不可忽视的维度；SALA作为通用插件可在不重新训练的前提下显著缩小与精确重训练的逐样本差距，并改善方法对超参数的稳定性。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to erase the influence of a designated forget set from a trained model while preserving strong utility on the retain set. Approximate unlearning methods efficiently achieve this goal without full retraining, but their effectiveness is primarily assessed using dataset-level aggregate metrics. These aggregate metrics can mask deviations from retraining at the level of individual samples. In this work, we focus on per-sample unlearning effectiveness. We introduce SALA (Sample-grained Likelihoods ALignment), a plug-in optimization framework that enables approximate unlearning methods to operate at the level of individual samples by steering each example toward its correct target distribution. To quantitatively assess this behavior, we further propose the sample-grained distribution gap, a metric that captures per-sample discrepancies between approximate unlearning and exact retraining output distributions. We provide theoretical guarantees showing that SALA contracts this gap and yields a bounded per-sample discrepancy to retraining. To make this framework practical, we develop a low-cost estimation method for the member and non-member target distributions. Extensive experiments on classification and generation tasks demonstrate that SALA reduces the sample-grained distribution gap by up to $86.67\%$ when paired with existing approximate unlearning baselines, while also improving robustness to unlearning hyperparameters.

</details>


### [214] [Variational Learning with Lie Group Exponential Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04513)
*Eren Mehmet Kıral,Thomas Möllenhoff,Koichi Tojo,Mohammad Emtiyaz Khan,Kenichi Bannai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将Lie群变分学习框架推广到一般Lie群，通过与调和指数族建立联系，证明Lie群算法的线性近似是贝叶斯学习规则（BLR）的推广，从而给出从群表示系统构造高效变分学习算法的通用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络变分贝叶斯学习虽有多方面潜在优势，但实现难点在于变分分布参数的约束以及低方差梯度估计器的需求。已有的乘性/仿射Lie群方法能自然克服这些困难，但适用范围有限，且与标准自然梯度变分学习（如BLR）关系不明。

Method: 引入调和指数族（harmonic exponential family）——与Lie群表示相关联的一类Lie群指数族，作为核心工具；将Lie群框架从乘性/仿射群推广到任意一般Lie群；并对Lie群算法作线性近似，与BLR等自然梯度变分学习方法建立显式对应关系。

Result: 证明了Lie群算法的线性近似是对贝叶斯学习规则（BLR）的推广，即在BLR之外给出一族更一般的算法；并给出由群表示系统构造可高效实现变分学习算法的系统化流程。

Conclusion: Lie群与调和指数族的结合为变分学习提供了统一而通用的理论框架，既涵盖并推广了自然梯度/贝叶斯学习规则类方法，又提供了可高效实现算法的构造性途径。

Abstract: Variational Bayesian learning for deep neural networks offers many potential benefits. The difficulty in implementation arises from constraints on the parameters of the variational distributions, as well as the need for low-variance gradient estimators. Our recent work proposes Lie group methods for multiplicative or affine groups that naturally overcome such difficulties. Lie groups are essentially parameter spaces with a binary operator such as addition or multiplication, which allows for natural updates within the parameter space. In this article, we streamline and extend our Lie group framework to any general Lie group, and give an explicit relation to standard natural-gradient variational learning methods such as the Bayesian learning rule (BLR). In particular, we prove that linear approximations of the Lie group algorithm give generalizations of the BLR. The key ingredient is the harmonic exponential family, a general class of Lie group exponential families associated to representations of Lie groups. Our work provides a systematic method to construct from such representations efficiently implementable algorithms for variational learning.

</details>


### [215] [The Price of List Replicability: Tight Bounds for Bias Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04532)
*Michael Chen,Atieh Bastan,A. Pavan,N. V. Vinodchandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了估计d枚硬币偏置的list-replicable算法的样本复杂度，首次给出非平凡下界，并给出与之匹配（至多对数因子）的改进算法，从而确定列表大小为d+1时最优样本复杂度为Õ(d²/ε²)，一般k∈[d+1,2^d]时为Õ(d²/(ε²log k))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作给出了(d+1,ε,δ)-list replicable算法，样本复杂度为Õ(d³/ε²)，但缺乏非平凡的样本复杂度下界，无法判断该复杂度是否最优，也未知维度依赖的本质阶数。

Method: 建立该问题的首个非平凡样本复杂度下界；同时设计改进的list-replicable估计算法，其上界与所证下界在polylog因子内相匹配；并将结果推广到任意列表规模k∈[d+1,2^d]。

Result: 证明了k=d+1时样本复杂度的最优值为Õ(d²/ε²)，表明对维度的二次依赖本质成立（至多对数因子）；对每个k∈[d+1,2^d]，总样本复杂度的上下界匹配为Õ(d²/(ε²log k))。

Conclusion: 本文闭合了硬币偏置估计中list-replicable算法的样本复杂度问题：将已有Õ(d³/ε²)的上界改进至Õ(d²/ε²)并证明其最优性（至多polylog因子），并给出关于列表规模k的完整刻画，确定维度二次依赖的内在性。

Abstract: We study the sample complexity of list replicable algorithms for estimating the biases of $d$ coins. An algorithm is $(k,\varepsilon,δ)$-list replicable if for every bias vector $\mathbf{p}\in[0,1]^d$, there is a set of outputs $L_{\mathbf{p}}\subseteq \mathbb{R}^d$ of at most $k$ elements such that every $\mathbf{y}\in L_{\mathbf{p}}$ satisfies $\lVert\mathbf{y}-\mathbf{p}\rVert_\infty\leq\varepsilon$, and the algorithm output is in $L_{\mathbf{p}}$ with probability at least $1-δ$. Prior work gave a $(d+1,\varepsilon,δ)$-list replicable algorithm with sample complexity $\widetilde{O}\left(\frac{d^3}{\varepsilon^2}\right)$, but no nontrivial sample complexity lower bound was known.
  We establish the first non-trivial sample complexity lower bound for this problem and give an improved algorithm that matches it up to polylogarithmic factors. Together, these results determine the optimal sample complexity for list size $d+1$ to be $\widetildeΘ\left(\frac{d^2}{\varepsilon^2}\right)$, showing that the quadratic dependence on dimension is inherent up to polylogarithmic factors. More generally, for every $k\in[d+1,2^d]$, we establish matching upper and lower bounds of $\widetildeΘ\left(\frac{d^2}{\varepsilon^2\log k}\right)$ on the total sample complexity.

</details>


### [216] [CANDO: Conservative Action-Noise Directional Optimization for Residual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04543)
*Zeyu Gao,Chengkai Hou,Chaoyue Li,Zhuangzhe Wu,Jiaming Liu,Yao Mu,Peng Jia,Li Zhang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出噪声空间残差RL方法CNDO，用软保守约束替代固定潜空间可行域；并扩展为CANDO，加入动作空间残差，在机器人控制任务上取得76.1 NAUC与91.4%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练生成策略提供了强行为先验，但在线强化学习微调困难：需在不破坏离线演示所学结构的前提下改进冻结的生成策略。现有噪声空间残差方法用固定潜空间可行域约束适应，可能排除有价值的噪声编辑；动作空间残差方法样本效率更高，但仍有提升空间。

Method: CNDO：对冻结生成策略，在高斯潜样本周围学习状态与噪声条件化的方向（noise-space 残差），去除预定义潜空间可行域，改用软保守约束（保守价值估计 + 门控执行）调控噪声空间适应。CANDO：在CNDO基础上叠加动作空间残差，将结构化潜空间改进与局部动作修正结合。

Result: CNDO缩小了噪声空间与动作空间残差学习之间的差距；CANDO进一步优于单一空间优化，达到76.1 NAUC与91.4%最终成功率，较最佳单空间结果提升6.6 NAUC与4.1%成功率。

Conclusion: 以软保守约束替代硬性潜空间可行域可有效释放噪声空间残差的学习潜力；潜空间与动作空间残差的互补组合是高效微调冻结生成策略的有效途径。

Abstract: Pretrained generative policies provide strong behavior priors for robot control, but adapting them efficiently with online reinforcement learning remains difficult. A central challenge is how to improve a frozen generative policy without destroying the structure learned from offline demonstrations. Noise-space residual methods address this by modifying the policy's input noise, but existing noise-space methods typically regulate adaptation through fixed latent feasible regions, which may exclude valuable noise edits. In contrast, action-space residual methods are more sample-efficient, but their efficiency can still be further improved. We propose Conservative Noise Directional Optimization (CNDO), a noise-space residual RL method that improves a frozen generative policy by learning state- and noise-conditioned directions around Gaussian latent samples. CNDO removes predefined latent feasible regions and instead regulates noise-space adaptation through the soft conservative constraint, which consists of conservative value estimation and gated execution. We further introduce Conservative Action-Noise Directional Optimization (CANDO), which adds an action-space residual, combining structured latent-space improvement with local action correction. Experiments show that, once CNDO narrows the gap between noise-space and action-space residual learning, CANDO further improves over single-space optimization, achieving 76.1 NAUC and a 91.4% final success rate, surpassing the best single-space results by 6.6 NAUC and 4.1% success rate. More demos and info are available on https://cando-rl.github.io/.

</details>


### [217] [Curriculum unlearning in humans and neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04565)
*Jirko Rubruck,Alice Zhang,Andrew Saxe,Christopher Summerfield*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用典型行为任务与神经网络建模，提出并验证"由简到繁"课程为何有效：学习者的简单性偏置导致欠拟合的简单规则与表征压缩，课程通过帮助"反学习"简单规则、恢复任务所需表征维度而提升学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类学习常从简单规则过渡到例外，但缺乏对这一课程效应（curriculum effect）的计算解释。作者希望给出机制性理论。

Method: 采用经典行为任务，结合人类被试实验与神经网络建模，考察学习中的简单性偏置（simplicity bias）、表征压缩（representation compression）及课程安排的影响；并检验理论的两个预测：预暴露于简单任务者、初始表征更压缩者从课程中获益更多。

Result: 人类与神经网络均表现出简单性偏置，倾向学习对数据欠拟合的简单规则；神经网络中该偏置伴随表征压缩。由简到繁课程帮助两类学习者克服压缩，形成维度合适的表征与策略，且理论预测得到确认。

Conclusion: 课程的作用在于帮助学习者"反学习"（unlearning）简单规则，从而摆脱表征压缩、获得与任务匹配的维度；这为课程学习提供了统一的计算解释。

Abstract: During learning, humans often benefit from curricula that teach simple rules before exceptions. However, we lack a computational explanation for this phenomenon. Here, using a canonical behavioural task and modelling with neural networks, we develop and test a theory for why simple-to-complex curricula succeed. We show that during learning, both humans and neural networks show simplicity biases, a tendency to learn simple rules that are underfit to the data. In neural networks this bias is accompanied by compression of neural representations. Simple-to-complex curricula help both types of learners overcome this compression, developing representations (and policy) with appropriate dimensionality for the task. We test and confirm a further prediction from this theory: learners that have been pre-exposed to simple tasks and with more compressed initial representations benefit more from curricula. These findings suggest that curricula help with ``unlearning'' of simple rules.

</details>


### [218] [Ground-Wing: A Parametric 3D Wing-in-Ground-Effect Dataset for Benchmarking Neural Surrogate Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04571)
*Andrea Cenzato,Lukas Karch,Andre Luckow,Yoran Pichon,Francesco Pasqua,Abouzar Ghasemi,Rishikesh Ranade,Ira Shokar,Julia Kowalski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 构建了一个面向赛车地面效应3D机翼的CFD数据集（770个RANS仿真、194种构型），并以此基准评测六种神经代理模型对表面压力与壁面剪切应力的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有翼型/机翼数据集缺乏地面效应这一独特流动物理场景，缺乏用于验证神经代理CFD模型的高难度基准。

Method: 构建高度参数化3D机翼地面效应CFD数据集：770次RANS仿真覆盖194种构型、27种翼型、4种离地距离，变化掠角、弦长、锥度、攻角与翼尖扭转；评测六种范式不同的网络架构（DoMINO、X-MeshGraphNet、AB-UPT、GeoTransolver、GAOT、AeroJEPA），预测表面压力系数与壁面剪切应力并积分得到阻力/升力系数。

Result: 提供可复现的仿真数据与多架构代理模型基准结果，涵盖表面场预测及由其恢复的整体气动系数。

Conclusion: 该数据集因包含其他机翼/翼型数据集所缺失的地面效应独特性，可作为具挑战性的代理CFD模型基准。

Abstract: This paper presents a CFD dataset of a highly parametrized 3D wing operating in ground effect, designed for benchmarking neural surrogate models for aerodynamic prediction, using a wing shape and flow conditions typically encountered in motorsport applications. The dataset comprises 770 RANS simulations of 194 unique wing configurations, sampled across 27 airfoil profile variants and 4 distances from the ground, spanning variations in wing sweep, chord, taper, angle of attack, and tip twist. Six neural network architectures representing distinct paradigms --- convolutional (DoMINO), graph-based (X-MeshGraphNet), transformer-based (AB-UPT, GeoTransolver, GAOT), and JEPA-based (AeroJEPA) --- are evaluated on their ability to predict surface pressure coefficient and wall shear stress distributions, from which integral drag and lift coefficients are recovered. The dataset is intended to be used as a challenging benchmark for surrogate CFD models, due to the uniqueness of the ground effect being absent from other wing and airfoil datasets.

</details>


### [219] [SCANO: Restoring Boundary-Condition Completeness in Sampled Meshes with a Sliced-Cross-Attention Neural Operator for 3D Thermal Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04603)
*Tianyu Li,Zhiwei Cao,Qingang Zhang,Ruihang Wang,Binyang Song,Yonggang Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究百万单元网格上神经算子的采样一致性问题：通过将解表示为边界数据对类型相关测度的积分，识别子采样对边界条件的三类扭曲，提出四个可检验的一致性条件及两个与骨干无关的模块（BQA、AGS），组合为SCANO，在受控与公开基准及百万网格数据中心热仿真数据集上显著降低整网格与区域误差并实现超百倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 百万单元网格训练神经算子会超GPU显存，故常采用子采样训练、全网格/区域推理；但子采样会扭曲边界信息导致精度下降。作者追问：何种情况下采样子条件下的边界条件与全网格训练保持一致，以及如何聚合与注入边界数据。

Method: 将解表示为边界数据对类型相关测度的积分，据此分析子采样在三个方面扭曲边界条件：边界覆盖不完整或样本相关的全局状态、按计数而非面积的面加权、边界输入被压缩为低维向量。由此导出四个可在训练前检验的可度量一致性条件。用两个骨干无关模块满足之：Boundary Quadrature Attention（BQA）将全部边界面按类型与面积加权聚合为切片token，供每个查询点在每一层读取；Anchored Global State（AGS）由与采样点无关的锚点估计全局状态。二者加入可扩展的消息传递与切片注意力骨干构成SCANO。

Result: 在受控与公开基准及百万单元网格的DCTS数据集上评估：两个基准上BQA提升每个骨干，整网格误差最多降低15倍；三个数据集上AGS使区域预测与全网格预测对齐，公开基准上多数骨干区域误差至少降低十倍；两模块结合使最佳原始骨干的整网格误差近乎减半。SCANO可在1秒内预测房间尺度场，比全屋锚点自注意力快100倍以上。

Conclusion: 子采样边界条件的失真可被理论刻画为测度积分层面的三类扭曲，并转化为可检验的一致性条件；BQA与AGS以骨干无关方式满足这些条件，带来大幅精度提升与显著推理加速，表明面向采样一致性的边界聚合与全局状态锚定是百万网格神经算子的有效设计原则。

Abstract: Training neural operators on million-cell meshes can exceed GPU memory limits. Models are therefore trained on subsamples and deployed on whole meshes or selected regions. However, subsampling can distort boundary information, degrading accuracy. We ask when boundary conditioning under sampling stays consistent with whole-mesh training, and how to aggregate and inject boundary data. Representing the solution as an integral of boundary data against type-dependent measures, we show that subsampling distorts this conditioning in three ways: incomplete boundary coverage or a sample-dependent global state, face weighting by count rather than area, and compression of boundary inputs into a low-dimensional vector. This analysis yields four measurable consistency conditions checkable before training. We satisfy them with two backbone-agnostic modules. Boundary Quadrature Attention (BQA) aggregates all boundary faces, typed and area-weighted, into slice tokens that every query point reads at every layer. Anchored Global State (AGS) estimates the global state from anchors independent of the sampled points. Adding both to a scalable message-passing and slice-attention backbone yields SCANO. We evaluate the approach on controlled and public benchmarks and a data-centre thermal simulation dataset (DCTS) containing million-cell meshes. On both benchmarks, BQA improves every backbone, reducing whole-mesh error up to 15-fold. On all three datasets, AGS aligns regional with whole-mesh predictions, cutting regional error at least tenfold for most backbones on the public benchmark. Together, the modules nearly halve the best original backbone's whole-mesh error on both benchmarks. SCANO predicts a room-scale field in under one second, over 100 times faster than whole-room anchor self-attention.

</details>


### [220] [CAPSkip: Cancellation-Aware Prefix Skipping for Selective Backpropagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04611)
*Heejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAPSkip proposes a cancellation-aware prefix-skipping rule for selective back-propagation: among correctly classified examples, it skips the longest prefix (ordered by increasing last-layer gradient norm) whose vector sum stays within a relative budget b of the batch's last-layer gradient, yielding ~1.74x speedup over dense training on DeiT-S/CIFAR-100 with slightly higher accuracy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing per-step selective back-propagation rules rank examples by scalar loss or gradient magnitude. Such scalar scores ignore cancellation between per-example gradients: the gradient removed by a set can be far smaller than the sum of individual magnitudes suggests, so scalar ranking over-skips or under-skips relative to the actual distortion it introduces.

Method: CAPSkip (Cancellation-Aware Prefix Skipping) restricts skipping to examples the model already classifies correctly, orders them by increasing last-layer gradient norm, and scans all prefixes to select the longest one whose vector sum remains within a relative budget b of the batch's last-layer gradient. A factorised Gram matrix makes the full-prefix scan cheap and keeps it on-device; training combines activation reuse with selected-row backward.

Result: In exact arithmetic the rule bounds the change of the raw final-layer gradient, using rank-one contributions already available from the forward pass; the bound does not extend to deeper layers, optimizer updates, or the training trajectory. Cumulative distortion is not monotone in prefix length, motivating the exhaustive prefix scan. Empirically, CAPSkip fine-tunes DeiT-S on CIFAR-100 for 103 epochs in 0.576x the time of 103-epoch dense training, and is +0.25 pp [+0.08, +0.42] above an approximately time-matched 59-epoch dense run (n=3).

Conclusion: Replacing scalar magnitude ranking with a vector-valued, cancellation-aware criterion enables aggressive yet controlled gradient skipping at the final layer, converting saved backward computation into more effective epochs under a fixed time budget, though the guarantee is local to the last layer.

Abstract: Per-step selective back-propagation forwards every example of a mini-batch but back-propagates only some of them. Most rules rank examples by loss or gradient magnitude, but such scalar scores ignore cancellation between per-example gradients: the gradient removed by a set can be much smaller than its individual magnitudes suggest. We introduce CAPSkip (Cancellation-Aware Prefix Skipping). Among the examples the model already classifies correctly, CAPSkip skips the longest prefix, in increasing order of last-layer gradient norm, whose vector sum stays within a relative budget $b$ of the batch's last-layer gradient. In exact arithmetic this bounds the change of the raw final-layer gradient, using rank-one contributions already available from the forward pass; the bound does not extend to deeper layers, optimizer updates, or the training trajectory. Because the cumulative distortion is not monotone in the prefix length, CAPSkip scans all prefixes; a factorised Gram matrix makes the scan cheap and keeps it on the device. With activation reuse and selected-row backward, CAPSkip fine-tunes DeiT-S on CIFAR-100 for 103 epochs in 0.576x the time of 103-epoch dense training, and is +0.25 pp [+0.08, +0.42] above an approximately time-matched 59-epoch dense run (n=3).

</details>


### [221] [Policy Network Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04614)
*Jiate Liu,Deheng Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DPPG，用"Transformer自编码器+任务条件扩散模型"生成强化学习策略网络参数，单一统一模型可跨经典控制、Atari、MuJoCo生成高质量策略，并在未见环境中展现泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索扩散模型能否生成RL策略网络的参数，以及生成策略是否具备泛化能力；此前参数生成方法生成的网络规模受限，作者希望突破该限制并实现跨域统一生成。

Method: DPPG由两部分组成：(1) 基于Transformer的自编码器，通过对网络参数进行patchify（分块化）实现高压缩率；(2) 任务条件扩散模型，以不同环境为条件生成对应的策略参数。

Result: 单一统一的DPPG模型能在经典控制、Atari游戏、MuJoCo等多个任务域上合成高质量策略；相比先前方法可生成参数量更大的网络；在未见环境中生成的参数能实现更快收敛并达到有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 扩散模型可用于RL策略参数生成并具备跨任务泛化潜力，展示了基于扩散的参数生成在未来的应用前景。

Abstract: Diffusion models have shown remarkable progress in AI-generated content, including images and videos. In this paper, we explore whether diffusion models can generate the parameters of policy networks in reinforcement learning (RL), and whether the generated policies exhibit generalization capabilities. To achieve this, we propose Diffusion-based Conditional Policy Network Parameter Generation (DPPG), which consists of a Transformer-based autoencoder and a task-conditioned diffusion model. The autoencoder achieves a high compression rate by patchifying network parameters, while the task-conditioned diffusion model generates corresponding parameters conditioned on different environments. Our experiments demonstrate that, with a single unified DPPG model, we can synthesize high-quality policies across a diverse set of tasks from different environment domains, including Classic Control, Atari games, and MuJoCo. Additionally, our method can generate networks with a larger number of parameters compared to previous approaches. Furthermore, we find that in previously unseen environments, DPPG can generate parameters that achieve faster convergence and reach competitive performance, showcasing the potential future applications of parameter generation via diffusion.

</details>


### [222] [Self-Privileged Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04632)
*Yangrong Hu,Xueyu Zhou,Yue Wu,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析 token 级加权 SFT 的优化目标，指出动态加权（如 DFT）会导致分布过度尖锐化，静态加权则拟合数据分布与冻结先验的乘积。据此提出 SPFT，用基于特权信息的初始模型冻结副本作先验，并通过贝叶斯分解实现按信息量重加权，在数学推理与代码生成上优于 SFT 及基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通过 token 级权重改进 SFT 的方法多经验性地从模型输出分布导权重，缺乏对其优化目标与所诱导目标分布的理论理解，也未厘清动态/静态加权各自的稳定性与局限。

Method: 理论上刻画动态与静态加权 SFT 所诱导的目标分布；提出 SPFT：以初始模型在特权信息（如数学题标准答案）条件下的冻结副本作为先验，为 token 级权重提供引导；再用贝叶斯分解证明 SPFT 相当于按各目标 token 对真实结果的可预测性（信息量）重加权标准静态先验。

Result: 分析表明动态加权（DFT）诱导高度尖锐化的分布，静态加权拟合"数据分布×冻结先验"的正比目标，训练目标更稳定但依赖先验质量；SPFT 通过按信息量重加权，对更具预测性的 token 赋予更大权重。在数学推理与代码生成任务的主要设置上，SPFT 优于标准 SFT 及其他基线。

Conclusion: 静态加权因目标稳定而更可取，其效果关键取决于先验质量；以特权信息条件化的初始模型作先验并结合贝叶斯信息量重加权（SPFT），是提升 SFT 的有效且可解释的途径。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is a key component of language model post-training, enabling effective task adaptation and serving as an important foundation for subsequent reinforcement learning. Recent methods seek to improve SFT by introducing token-level weights into the training objective, where these weights are typically derived from a model's output distribution at each token position. To better understand how such weighting schemes affect SFT, we analyze their optimization objectives and theoretically characterize the target distributions they induce. Our analysis shows that dynamic weighting methods, such as Dynamic Fine-Tuning (DFT), induce a heavily sharpened distribution, whereas static weighting methods fit a target distribution proportional to the product of the data distribution and a frozen prior. These findings suggest that static weighting provides a more stable training target, while its effectiveness critically depends on the quality of the prior distribution. Building on this observation, we propose Self-Privileged Fine-Tuning (SPFT), which uses a frozen copy of the initial model conditioned on privileged information (such as ground truth for mathematical questions) as a prior to provide guidance for token-level weighting during fine-tuning. Through a Bayesian decomposition, we further show that SPFT reweights the standard static prior according to how informative each target token is for predicting the ground-truth outcome, thereby assigning larger weights to more predictive tokens. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation show that SPFT improves over standard SFT and other baselines across the main settings.

</details>


### [223] [Does LinUCB Need Valid Confidence Sets?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04641)
*Ahmed Ben Yahmed,Marc Abeille,David Janz,Clément Calauzènes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将LinUCB中标准置信宽度β替换为任意固定α>0，在动作集M-smooth、m-强凸时仍保持次线性regret；α-LinUCB表明有效置信集并非LinUCB成功的必要条件，其机制是动作集曲率驱使算法探索各方向。作者引入对数截断谱势给出设计矩阵最小特征值下界，得到O((M/m)d√(T log T))的regret界，较经典LinUCB改进√(log T)因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典LinUCB依赖精心构造的置信宽度β（如β∝√(d log T)）以保证乐观性（optimism）与有效置信集，这一要求在理论上被视为算法成功的关键前提。作者质疑该前提是否必要，希望探究LinUCB在失去有效置信集时是否仍能保证次线性regret，并揭示其背后的探索机制。

Method: 提出α-LinUCB：用任意固定α>0替代原置信宽度β，完全放弃乐观性保证。在M-smooth、m-强凸动作集下进行高概率regret分析；核心工具是引入对数截断谱势（logarithmically capped spectral potential），对已充分探索方向的贡献进行截断，区别于标准log-determinant势，从而推出设计矩阵最小特征值的下界，并证明曲率驱动算法沿所有方向探索。

Result: 证明α-LinUCB在M-smooth、m-strongly convex动作集上对任意固定α>0均具有次线性regret的高概率界；当取标准宽度α=β≍√(d log T)时，T轮、d维regret为O((M/m)d√(T log T))（忽略迭代对数因子），相比经典LinUCB将时间跨度依赖改进√(log T)倍。

Conclusion: 有效置信集并非LinUCB成功的必要条件；动作集曲率本身即可诱导充分探索，替代乐观性机制。对数截断谱势为分析此类探索提供新工具，并获得优于经典LinUCB的regret界。

Abstract: We show that replacing LinUCB's usual confidence width $β$ by any fixed $α>0$ preserves sublinear regret when the action set is $M$-smooth and $m$-strongly convex. Our high-probability regret bound for this generalisation, $α$-LinUCB, establishes that valid confidence sets are not necessary for the success of LinUCB. We identify the mechanism behind this result: action-set curvature drives $α$-LinUCB to explore every direction, even when optimism is not guaranteed. To analyse this mechanism, we introduce a logarithmically capped spectral potential that, unlike the standard log-determinant potential, caps contributions from well-explored directions. This yields lower bounds on the smallest eigenvalue of the design matrix. At the standard confidence width $α=β\asymp\sqrt{d\log T}$, our $T$-round regret bound in $d$ dimensions is $O\bigl(\frac{M}{m}d\sqrt{T\log T}\bigr)$, up to iterated-logarithmic factors, improving the horizon dependence of the classical LinUCB guarantee by a factor of $\sqrt{\log T}$.

</details>


### [224] [Exploiting Biases in Attention for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04657)
*Charles Herr,Gabriele Spadaro,Enzo Tartaglione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文分析视觉Transformer中注意力矩阵列的稀疏性，提出在query-key点积中加入输入相关偏置的注意力重表述，使每个token获得表征其全局影响力的标量，可作为剪枝注意力图的代理，从而显著降低计算量与推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉Transformer的注意力具有稀疏性，作者观察到注意力矩阵各列对最终计算的相关性不相等，且该现象随网络深度加剧，表明token对注意力过程存在与查询无关的绝对重要性，而标准注意力公式未显式表达这一点。

Method: 分析注意力矩阵列的重要性分布随深度的变化；据此重述注意力公式，在query-key点积上增加输入相关偏置项，为每个token生成标量值，表示其在注意力过程中对其他token的预期影响；利用该标量作为剪枝注意力图的代理，按重要性筛选token以减少注意力计算。

Result: 在多种规模的Transformer模型上，该方法在FLOPs与推理延迟两方面大幅降低注意力计算成本，同时保留大部分原始分类精度。

Conclusion: 注意力中存在与查询无关的token绝对重要性，显式建模这一偏置可实现高效注意力剪枝，在精度与效率间取得良好折衷；代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Attention is known to be sparse in vision transformers. In particular, we analyze the unequal relevance of attention-matrix columns to the final computation and show that this phenomenon becomes increasingly pronounced with network depth. This can be interpreted as saying that tokens have an absolute importance for the attention process, independently of queries. Inspired by this, we propose a reformulation of the attention formula that makes this phenomenon explicit by adding an input-dependent bias to the query-key dot product. We show that this new term gives each token a scalar value indicating its anticipated influence on other tokens during the attention process, and can thus serve as a proxy for pruning attention maps. Across multiple transformer model sizes, this allows us to substantially reduce the computational cost of attention, in terms of both FLOPs and inference latency, while retaining most of the original classification accuracy. We publicly released the code and models at https://github.com/EIDOSLAB/AttentionWithBias.

</details>


### [225] [Leakage-free evaluation of residual graph convolutional networks for network intrusion detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04660)
*Zahid Iqbal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在去除数据泄漏与重复流等乐观来源的严格协议下，残差图卷积网络相对MLP及其他图网络无显著精度增益，树集成在精度上更强，图模型的优势仅体现在特征噪声鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络入侵检测中图神经网络的高性能报告多来自存在乐观偏差的实验协议：测试流参与了训练图构建、重复流跨越训练/测试划分、仅报告单一划分结果，导致结论不可靠。

Method: 采用标签感知的精确重复去除（NF-UNSW-NB15-v2去除69%流）；仅用训练流构建最近邻图，验证/测试流在预测时挂接至最近训练流；两层残差GCN与8个模型（含同图最近邻投票、梯度提升树、随机森林、MLP）在3个不同随机划分种子上对比，并进行消融、留一族剔除与特征噪声测试。

Result: CICIDS2017二分类F1为98.11%（SD 0.18），NF-UNSW-NB15-v2为90.57%（0.38），与无图MLP及其他图网络难以区分；树集成在CICIDS2017高1.48–1.57个F1点，两数据集PR-AUC均领先；CICIDS2017多分类宏F1为93.08% vs 64.51%（差异主要源于残差网络将良性流误判为小攻击类）；所有消融（含直推式构图）变化均未超种子波动；高斯噪声SD 0.5下残差网络F1为96.16%，XGBoost降至0.67%。

Conclusion: 在泄漏受控协议下，流图上的消息传递相对感知机无显著精度增益，树集成在两数据集上均更强；图模型的真正优势在于对特征噪声的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Graph neural networks are increasingly proposed for network intrusion detection, yet many reported results come from protocols in which test flows shape the graph used for training, duplicated flows sit on both sides of the split, or a single split is reported. This study measures what a residual graph convolutional network gains over simpler models once these sources of optimism are removed. CICIDS2017 and NF-UNSW-NB15-v2 were cleaned with label-aware removal of exact duplicates, removing 69% of NF-UNSW-NB15-v2 flows. Flows were linked in a nearest-neighbour graph built from training flows only, and validation and test flows were attached to their nearest training flows at prediction time. A two-layer residual network was compared with eight models under the same settings, including a nearest-neighbour vote over the same graph, gradient-boosted trees and a random forest, over three seeds that each draw a new split, with ablations, leave-one-family-out and feature-noise tests. On CICIDS2017 the residual network reached a binary F1 of 98.11% (SD 0.18) and on NF-UNSW-NB15-v2 90.57% (0.38), indistinguishable from a multilayer perceptron without the graph and from other graph networks. Tree ensembles were 1.48 to 1.57 F1 points higher on CICIDS2017 and led on the area under the precision-recall curve on both datasets; their CICIDS2017 multiclass macro-F1 reached 93.08% against 64.51%, a gap mainly from benign flows the residual network assigned to small attack classes. No ablation, including transductive graph construction, changed F1 beyond seed variation. Under Gaussian feature noise (SD 0.5), the residual network kept an F1 of 96.16%, while XGBoost fell to 0.67%. Under a leakage-controlled protocol, message passing over flow graphs adds no measurable accuracy over a perceptron, and tree ensembles remain stronger on both; the advantage of graph models lies in robustness to feature noise.

</details>


### [226] [Best-of-Depth: Learning Exploratory Dynamics in Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04663)
*Shota Takashiro,Takeshi Kojima,Masanori Koyama,Kohei Hayashi,Yusuke Iwasawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Best-of-Depth (BoD) 训练目标，将循环 Transformer 的展开轨迹视为候选集合，通过在最小化各深度的预测损失来提升中间深度的解覆盖率，在 N-Queens 上覆盖率达 91.03%（最终深度训练仅 45.75%），并迁移到代码生成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环/looped Transformer 通过重复的隐状态更新扩展推理期计算，但传统的最终深度训练只优化最后一步输出，并不直接鼓励中间深度产生有用的备选解，导致轨迹中的中间状态被浪费，解覆盖率低。

Method: BoD 将循环轨迹视为一个候选集合，对多个深度同时施加预测损失（对所有有效目标在不同深度上取最小损失），无需改动架构、也无需深度特定的标签；不同深度可匹配不同的有效目标。作者给出精确匹配（exact-match）理论分析，将目标函数与解覆盖率联系起来。

Result: 在 N-Queens completion 与图着色任务上，BoD 相比最终深度训练和中间监督目标显著提升覆盖率（N-Queens：91.03% vs 45.75%）；实验还显示模型能发现超出训练深度的解，并在熟悉输入上恢复被教师池剔除的解；对预训练循环语言模型用 BoD 微调后，在固定深度分别生成程序时，pooled 代码生成成功率高于最终深度训练。

Conclusion: 结果支持将循环计算沿轨迹训练为"解发现"过程，即让中间深度也承担有效候选的生成职责，从而更充分地利用推理期计算。

Abstract: Looped Transformers extend inference-time computation through repeated latent-state updates, but final-depth training does not directly encourage useful intermediate alternatives. We introduce Best-of-Depth (BoD), which trains a recurrent trajectory as a candidate set by minimizing prediction loss across depths. Different valid targets can be matched at different depths without architectural changes or depth-specific labels. An exact-match analysis connects this objective to solution coverage. On N-Queens completion and graph coloring, BoD improves coverage over final-depth and intermediate-supervision objectives. N-Queens coverage reaches 91.03%, versus 45.75% for final-depth training. Further experiments show discovery beyond the training depth and recovery of solutions withheld from teacher pools on familiar inputs. Fine-tuning a pretrained looped language model with BoD yields higher pooled code-generation success than final-depth training when programs are generated separately at evaluated fixed depths. These results support training recurrent computation for solution discovery along its trajectory.

</details>


### [227] [Feature-Based and Deep Learning Methods for Astronomical Light Curve Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04664)
*V. Baymakanov,Kh. Khamitov,A. Raimova,A. Isaeva,R. Taran,M. Zedan,M. Kornilov,M. Demianenko,K. Malanchev,M. Hushchyn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在ZTF StarEmbed数据集上系统比较了基于特征的传统机器学习与端到端深度学习用于变星分类，发现经典模型稳定优于直接处理原始光变曲线的深度网络，主要受限于标注数据规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习方法在天文时域数据分析中具有直接从观测学习表征的潜力，但其相较于基于物理特征的传统方法是否真的更有优势尚缺乏定量、可复现的基准对比。

Method: 基于ZTF StarEmbed数据集（约4万天体、7个类别、类别不平衡）构建统一评测：一类模型使用物理意义明确的人工特征进行训练，另一类为直接以原始多变量、不规则采样光变曲线为输入的端到端深度网络，并在相同设置下进行比较与归因分析。

Result: 基于特征的经典模型在各评价中一致优于深度网络；性能差距的重要来源是标注数据量有限，同时光变曲线的多变量、不规则采样与形态多样性进一步增加了深度模型的建模难度，现有模型在给定训练规模下难以泛化。

Conclusion: 该工作为后续研究建立了定量基线，指出需通过扩大标注数据集规模与在大规模无标注巡天数据上开展自监督预训练来释放深度学习在天文时间序列分析中的潜力，并需进一步的方法创新。

Abstract: We compare feature-based machine learning and end-to-end deep learning for variable star classification using the Zwicky Transient Facility (ZTF) StarEmbed dataset, which contains about 40,000 objects across seven imbalanced classes. Classical models trained on physically motivated features consistently outperform deep networks trained directly on raw light curves. We find that limited labeled data contributes to the performance gap, while the multivariate, irregularly sampled, and morphologically diverse nature of light curves poses additional challenges for deep learning models. While deep learning holds promise for learning representations directly from observations, our results indicate that current models fail to generalize with the available training set size. These findings motivate further investigation of the benefits of scaling labeled datasets and self-supervised pretraining on large unlabeled surveys. This work establishes a quantitative baseline for future research and highlights the need for further innovations to unlock the potential of deep learning in astronomical time series analysis.

</details>


### [228] [FedCSGA: Client Selection Based on Genetic Algorithm for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04667)
*Ghani Haider,Majid Kundroo,Leo Zhang,Jinchul Choi,Taehong Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于遗传算法的联邦学习客户端选择方法FedCSGA，将客户端子集编码为染色体，用融合资源感知与历史参与权重的非线性适应度函数联合刻画统计异构与系统异构，在多个数据集上取得最高7.0%的精度提升并加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习每轮需选择客户端子集以降低通信开销、缓解掉队者（straggler）并提升训练效率。边缘环境中统计异构与系统异构同时影响收敛效率与模型性能，但现有客户端选择方法通常将二者独立处理，忽视了它们在多轮通信中的耦合与动态影响。

Method: FedCSGA：以遗传算法进行客户端选择。将客户端子集编码为染色体；设计非线性适应度函数，联合集成资源感知指标（resource-aware）与历史感知参与权重（history-aware participation weighting），从而捕捉子集层面的交互效应、缓解持续的选取偏差，并在统计效用与系统效率之间取得平衡。

Result: 在FMNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100与Tiny-ImageNet上的大量实验表明，FedCSGA在多种异构设置下持续优于SOTA基线，精度提升最高达7.0%，且以更少通信轮数实现更快收敛。

Conclusion: 结果验证了联合建模统计与系统因素的有效性，能够在异构联邦环境中实现鲁棒且高效的学习。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative training of machine learning models across distributed clients while preserving data privacy. In practical deployments, FL relies on selecting a subset of clients in each communication round to reduce communication cost, mitigate stragglers, and improve training efficiency under heterogeneous data and system conditions. In heterogeneous edge computing environments, both statistical heterogeneity and system heterogeneity significantly affect convergence efficiency and model performance. However, existing client selection methods often address these factors independently, overlooking their coupled and dynamic impact across communication rounds. To address this limitation, we propose FedCSGA, a Client Selection method for Federated learning based on a Genetic Algorithm. FedCSGA encodes client subsets as chromosomes and evaluates these subsets through a non-linear fitness function that jointly integrates resource-aware metrics and history-aware participation weighting. This design captures subset-level interactions, mitigates persistent selection bias, and balances statistical utility with system efficiency. Extensive experiments on FMNIST, CIFAR-10, CIFAR- 100, and Tiny-ImageNet demonstrate that FedCSGA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across diverse heterogeneity settings, achieving accuracy improvements of up to 7.0% and faster convergence with fewer communication rounds. These results validate the effectiveness of jointly modeling statistical and system factors to achieve robust and efficient learning in heterogeneous federated environments.

</details>


### [229] [Sheaf-MPNN: A Library for Sheaf Neural Networks as Message Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04670)
*Alessio Borgi,Mario Severino,Luke Braithwaite,Steve Azzolin,Rémi Bourgerie,Olga Zaghen,Emanuele Mule,Riccardo Ali,Leo Minh Kustermann,Edoardo Coppola,Antonio Purificato,Stefano Fiorini,Christopher Irwin,Filippo Spiga,Fabrizio Silvestri,Michael Bronstein,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Sheaf-MPNN，一个 PyTorch Geometric 库，将 sheaf 扩散重构为局部关联（incidence-wise）消息传递，避免显式构造全局 sheaf Laplacian，统一实现 15 类 SNN 并配套基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 GNN 隐含假设相邻节点共享同一特征空间，导致过平滑及异配图（heterophilic graph）上表现不佳；SNN 通过为节点/边赋予独立向量空间和限制映射缓解该问题，但现有实现常将 sheaf Laplacian 显式装配为全局稀疏块矩阵，带来内存与计算开销，限制可扩展性。

Method: 在统一消息传递框架下，将 sheaf 扩散重写为局部 incidence-wise 的传输与聚合，无需构造全局 Laplacian；支持 15 类 SNN，覆盖同配/异配图、有向/无向设定，以及对角、一般、正交、低秩限制映射与基于注意力的预测器；并给出统一基准套件，涵盖节点级/图层级任务、异构图学习与计算效率评测；同时建立消息传递形式与原形式之间的理论等价性，并识别保持其传播动态所需的架构机制。

Result: 实现支持上述多种 SNN 变体与图设定的统一框架；理论证明消息传递形式与原始形式等价，并明确保持传播动态所需的关键组件；基准套件支撑跨架构系统、可复现比较（含效率评估）。

Conclusion: 该统一视角为在标准图学习框架内实现、比较与扩展 sheaf 架构提供了有原则的基础，代码开源。

Abstract: Standard Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) implicitly assume that neighboring nodes share the same feature space, which contributes to oversmoothing and poor performance on heterophilic graphs. Sheaf Neural Networks (SNNs) remove this assumption by giving each node and edge its own vector space and learning restriction maps that transform features as they move between neighbors. These maps define a sheaf Laplacian that drives diffusion over the graph, but existing implementations often assemble it explicitly as a global sparse block matrix, which adds memory and computational overhead and can hinder scalability. In this paper, we introduce Sheaf-MPNN, a PyTorch Geometric library that reformulates sheaf diffusion as local incidence-wise transport and aggregation, eliminating the need to construct the global Laplacian. Sheaf-MPNN implements fifteen families of SNNs under a unified message-passing framework, each supporting both homogeneous and heterogeneous graphs, and directed and undirected settings, with diagonal, general, orthogonal, and low-rank restriction maps and attention-based predictors. We further provide a unified benchmarking suite covering node- and graph-level tasks, heterogeneous graph learning, and computational efficiency, enabling systematic and reproducible comparisons across architectures. We establish theoretical equivalence between these message-passing formulations and their original counterparts and identify the architectural mechanisms needed to preserve their propagation dynamics. This unified perspective provides a principled foundation for implementing, comparing, and extending sheaf-based architectures within standard graph learning frameworks. Source code is available at https://github.com/alessioborgi/sheaf-mpnn.

</details>


### [230] [Uncertainty-Aware Prototype Learning for Open-Set Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04689)
*Jinwu Hu,Langyu Bian,Jian Sun,Dongjin Yang,Chao Shuai,Yufeng Wang,Kai Zhou,Bin Xiao,Mingkui Tan,Hongmin Cai,Wei Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出UAPA，一种即插即用的开放集测试时自适应方法：用不确定性驱动的ID/OOD分离、动态原型精炼与对比原型熵自适应，可靠区分csID/csOOD样本，提升已知类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTA方法多基于闭集假设，缺乏可靠区分协变量偏移的分布内(csID)与分布外(csOOD)样本的机制；csOOD样本被误纳入置信度增强自适应，产生有害梯度，损害已知类性能。

Method: 1) Uncertainty-Driven ID/OOD Separation：结合分类器-原型得分与经小批量归一化的证据推导不确定性得分来区分csID与csOOD；2) Dynamic Prototype Refinement：利用缓存的csID特征周期性精炼类特定的目标原型，渐进跟踪估计的类方向；3) Contrastive Prototype Entropy Adaptation：对csID做熵最小化、对csOOD做熵最大化，并在原型层面做对比学习，使csID特征对齐对应目标原型、降低csOOD特征与已知类原型的最大相似度。整体为插件式模块。

Result: 在CIFAR系列基准与Tiny-ImageNet-C上优于最强基线，平均OSCR分别提升2.08与1.28个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过不确定性感知的原型学习显式分离csID/csOOD并施加差异化熵与原型对比约束，可在开放集测试流中实现可靠的测试时自适应，且具即插即用特性。

Abstract: Open-set test-time adaptation (TTA) aims to adapt source-trained models to unlabeled test streams where covariate-shifted in-distribution (csID) samples and covariate-shifted out-of-distribution (csOOD) samples may coexist. Existing TTA methods are typically designed under a closed-set assumption and therefore lack explicit mechanisms for reliably distinguishing csID from csOOD samples. Consequently, csOOD samples may be mistakenly included in confidence-enhancing adaptation, producing harmful gradients that degrade known-class performance. To address this, we propose Uncertainty-Aware Prototype Learning for Open-Set Test-Time Adaptation (UAPA), a plug-and-play method for reliable open-set adaptation. Specifically, we first introduce Uncertainty-Driven ID/OOD Separation, which combines a classifier-prototype score with a mini-batch-normalized evidence-derived uncertainty score to distinguish csID from csOOD samples. We then propose a Dynamic Prototype Refinement strategy that periodically refines class-specific target prototypes using buffered csID features to progressively follow the estimated class directions. Lastly, we introduce Contrastive Prototype Entropy Adaptation, which applies entropy minimization to csID samples and entropy maximization to csOOD samples, while prototype-level contrastive learning aligns csID features with their corresponding target prototypes and reduces the maximum similarity between csOOD features and known-class prototypes. Empirically, UAPA outperforms the strongest baselines on the CIFAR benchmarks and Tiny-ImageNet-C, improving average OSCR by 2.08 and 1.28 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [231] [Surrogate Models for Stochastic Al-Cu Phase-Field Solidification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04695)
*Simon Savukoski,Tatu Pinomaa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出保方差去噪扩散桥模型（VP-DDBM），在给定当前相场、浓度与温度场条件下自回归预测Al-Cu凝固二维演化，长期误差低于确定性U-Net与SI-ConvNeXt基线，并保持枝晶细节，速度约为相场求解器的73倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场模型虽能解析快速凝固的微观组织演化，但计算成本高，难以支持长轨迹、随机系综以及跨工艺条件研究。

Method: 构建保方差去噪扩散桥模型（VP-DDBM），以当前相场、浓度和给定温度场为条件预测下一时刻状态，并以自回归方式推进长时序演化；与确定性U-Net、SI-ConvNeXt基线对比。

Result: 在两个留出热梯度条件下，VP-DDBM在区域平均相、区域平均Cu浓度及前沿尖端位置上的长期误差均低于基线；长时滚动中保持精细枝晶结构，而U-Net逐步平滑形貌；预测速度约为相场求解器的73倍。

Conclusion: VP-DDBM可作为相场求解的快速代理模型，具备工艺条件快速筛选与基于系综的微观组织研究的潜力。

Abstract: Phase-field models resolve microstructural evolution during rapid alloy solidification, but their computational cost limits long trajectories, stochastic ensembles, and studies across process conditions. We present a variance-preserving denoising diffusion bridge model (VP-DDBM) for state-conditioned prediction of two-dimensional Al-Cu solidification. Given the current phase, concentration, and prescribed temperature fields, the model predicts the subsequent state and is applied autoregressively. Across two held-out thermal gradients, VP-DDBM produces lower long-horizon errors than deterministic U-Net and SI-ConvNeXt baselines in domain-mean phase, domain-mean Cu concentration, and leading-tip position. It also retains fine dendritic structure over long rollouts, whereas the deterministic U-Net progressively smooths the morphology. VP-DDBM predicts the phase and concentration evolution approximately $73\times$ faster than the phase-field solver, showing potential for faster process-condition screening and ensemble-based microstructure studies.

</details>


### [232] [TAPES: Task-Aligned Joint Predictive Pretraining for Transaction Event Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04747)
*Ruobing Zuo,Weiyu Han,Huilin He,Haoyu Jiang,Ze Zhao,Jie Sheng,Xiao Tan,Shuai Chen,Tianyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TAPES，一种任务对齐的交易序列联合预测预训练框架：从字段-算子组合中构造候选目标集，通过短周期联合预训练的下游效用选择目标集，再以共享离散隐变量参数化联合似然重初始化并预训练编码器，最终在下游监督适配中优于代表性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一交易记录可派生语义不同的预测目标（数值、变化量、与历史事件的关系），这些目标对历史表示提出不同约束；但目标本身的可预测性并不能证明目标集的下游效用，且逐目标预测损失未显式建模目标间的条件共现关系。

Method: 1）从可容许的字段-算子组合构造有限候选目标集；2）在匹配预算下通过短周期联合预训练试验，依据所学表示的下游效用选择目标集；3）重初始化编码器，在选定目标上以共享离散隐变量参数化的联合似然进行预训练；4）进行下游监督适配。

Result: 在真实交易风险场景及多个覆盖多样下游任务的公开金融基准上，TAPES 优于代表性基线；消融实验验证了预测目标选择与联合建模两部分的贡献。

Conclusion: 任务对齐的目标集选择与基于共享离散隐变量的联合建模共同提升了交易序列预训练表示的下游效用，为交易序列预训练提供了一种以效用驱动、显式建模目标共现的建模范式。

Abstract: Transaction event sequences support diverse prediction tasks in online commerce and digital financial services. The same transaction records can yield prediction targets with different semantics, including values, changes, and relations to past events. These targets impose different constraints on history representations, but their predictability alone does not establish the downstream utility of a target set. Moreover, target-wise prediction losses do not explicitly model conditional co-occurrence among targets. To address these challenges, we propose TAPES, a task-aligned joint predictive pretraining framework for transaction sequences. TAPES constructs a finite collection of candidate target sets from admissible field-operator combinations. It selects a target set according to the downstream utility of representations learned through short joint pretraining trials under matched budgets. It then reinitializes the encoder and pretrains it on the selected targets through a joint likelihood parameterized by a shared discrete latent variable, before downstream supervised adaptation. Experiments in a real-world transaction-risk setting and on multiple public financial benchmarks spanning diverse downstream tasks demonstrate that TAPES outperforms representative baselines. Ablation studies further validate the contributions of predictive target selection and joint modeling.

</details>


### [233] [What Does It Mean to Remove an Edge? Intervention-Aware Fidelity for Graph Neural Network Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04751)
*Rocco Mattia Di Mauro,Marco Grassia,Giuseppe Mangioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 删除保真度并非边排序的固有属性，而取决于干预语义：结构删除与消息抑制在非加性聚合下可产生不同排序保真性，评估协议须显式声明干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN解释常以"删除top-ranked边后模型输出变化"衡量保真度，但在消息传递框架中"删边"并非唯一干预：结构删除会重算度归一化与注意力等量，消息抑制则保留结构上下文而仅屏蔽边消息。二者语义不同，使删除保真度的含义依赖于干预所针对的计算环节，现有评估未区分此点。

Method: 形式化区分结构删除与消息抑制两类干预，推导二者精确等价的充分条件；构造干预特定的边归因，并在两种干预下分别评估各自排序，检验相对保真度是否随评估干预改变。实验覆盖9个数据集与深度2–5的GIN、GCN、GATv2模型面板，并设9个GIN对照。

Result: 理论表明：对系数与边集无关的加性消息传递二者等价；当聚合依赖零消息的存在、或删边改变幸存消息所乘系数时二者发散。9个GIN对照全部满足理论预测的等价性；在目标logit上，11个可报告的GCN/GATv2单元中有10个其相对保真度随评估干预变化，其中9个单元各自在本构干预下取得更大的删除面积。

Conclusion: 删除保真度不是边排序单独决定的属性。评估协议应显式报告干预方式、输出泛函、目标与扰动单元，并在科学问题不能唯一确定干预时检验替代干预语义。

Abstract: Graph neural network explanations are often evaluated via deletion fidelity, i.e., by deleting their top-ranked edges and measuring the resulting change in model output. Yet, in a message-passing GNN, "deleting an edge" does not specify a unique intervention. Structural deletion removes the edge from the graph and recomputes quantities such as degree normalization and attention, whereas message suppression leaves this structural context intact while blocking the message carried by the edge. The meaning of deletion fidelity therefore depends on what the intervention targets in the model computation. We formalize this distinction and derive sufficient conditions for exact equivalence. The two interventions coincide for additive message passing with edge-set-independent coefficients, but can diverge when aggregation depends on the presence of zero messages or when removing an edge changes the coefficients applied to surviving messages. We then construct intervention-specific edge attributions and evaluate each resulting ranking under both interventions, testing whether their relative fidelity changes with the evaluation intervention. Across nine datasets and a panel of GIN, GCN, and GATv2 models at depths 2--5, all nine GIN controls satisfy the equivalence predicted by the theory. On the target logit, the relative fidelity of the two rankings depends on the evaluation intervention in 10 of 11 reportable GCN and GATv2 cells, and in nine of them each ranking has the larger deletion area under the intervention it was built with. Deletion fidelity is thus not a property of an edge ranking alone. Evaluation protocols should explicitly report the intervention, output functional, target, and perturbation unit, and test alternative intervention semantics when the scientific question does not uniquely determine one.

</details>


### [234] [Non-Interfering Asymmetric AdamW Updates for Constrained Exploration of Equifinality in Hybrid Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04756)
*Ekaterina Gikalo,Zavud Baghirov,Christian Reimers,Markus Reichstein,Martin Jung,Marco Körner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究混合模型中参数与观测聚合通量之间的"等效性"(equifinality)问题，以蒸散发(ET)的总通量可观测、蒸腾/蒸发拆分不可观测为场景，提出NANO优化方法，在保证主损失拟合不退化前提下，刻意产生对不可观测内部划分的多样化解集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合模型用神经网络提供过程模型未解析参数，但拟合目标仅约束聚合通量与储量，内部划分不可观测，导致等效性：在聚合观测上无法区分的配置，对内部机制的解释却可能差异巨大。仅靠随机种子构建的集成只能覆盖数据所允许划分的很窄区间，因此需要主动、受控地产生这种分歧。

Method: 以蒸散发的蒸腾/蒸发拆分为对象，对成员施加关于蒸腾的辅助排斥(repulsion)训练，约束主观测拟合不退化。指出在AdamW下，应用步长是预条件动量方向，原始梯度的正交不蕴含实际更新步正交，故常规正交化非干扰失效。提出NANO(Non-interfering Asymmetric Network Optimization)：其对主损失的一阶影响与仅用主目标完全相同，辅助步被有界约束，并将排斥集中于主梯度留下的空闲坐标。

Result: 随机种子集成仅覆盖数据允许的划分的窄带；通过受控排斥训练可有意地产生更宽的划分分布，同时主观测拟合不退化。NANO实现严格非干扰(一阶意义上)、辅助步有界、排斥集中于空闲坐标。

Conclusion: 等效性是混合模型拟合的固有性质，应被视为可被显式利用的自由度而非缺陷；通过NANO这类非干扰有界优化，可在不牺牲观测拟合的前提下可控地刻画不可观测内部机制的不确定性范围。

Abstract: Hybrid models let neural networks supply the unresolved parameters of a process-based simulator, and are fitted to observations that constrain aggregate fluxes and storages but not the internal partitioning that produces them. Such a fit is equifinal: configurations indistinguishable in the observed aggregates can disagree substantially about the unobserved internals, and therefore about the mechanism they attribute to the same data. We study evapotranspiration, whose total flux is observed while its split into transpiration and evaporation is not. A model ensemble built from random seeds alone spans only a narrow band of the partitionings the data admit. We produce the spread deliberately, training members under auxiliary repulsion on transpiration, subject to the fit to observations not degrading. Non-interference is usually arranged by making the auxiliary gradient orthogonal to the main one, but under AdamW the applied step is a preconditioned momentum direction, so orthogonality between raw gradients no longer implies it. We introduce Non-interfering Asymmetric Network Optimization (NANO), an update whose first-order effect on the main loss is exactly that of the main objective alone, whose auxiliary step is bounded, and which concentrates repulsion in the coordinates the main gradient leaves idle.

</details>


### [235] [Koopman Generative Operators for Efficient Probabilistic Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04760)
*Raz Marshanski,Liran Nochumsohn,Mayank Jauhari,Boris N. Oreshkin,Omri Azencot*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KGO将概率时序预测建模为"结构化不确定性的演化"，用Koopman潜空间的闭式矩阵指数实现单步生成，兼顾精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以同时满足结构化时序动态建模、表达性不确定性刻画与高效推理：潜动力学模型依赖受限的生成机制，扩散/流匹配等现代生成方法虽灵活但需迭代采样、计算代价高。

Method: 提出Koopman Generative Operator (KGO)，含三部分：(1) Koopman Patch Embedding (KoPE) 实现时间一致的潜轨迹外推；(2) Koopman Flow Matching (KoFM) 借助Koopman潜空间中的闭式矩阵指数实现快速单步生成，规避迭代采样；(3) Adaptive Uncertainty Gate (AUG) 按变量与预测步长自适应调整不确定性，对应可学习的偶然不确定性分解，无需人工调参。

Result: 在ProbTS基准上，CRPS指标17项中13项、NMAE指标17项中16项达到SOTA；消除迭代采样后，推理时间相比迭代式生成模型至少降低25倍。

Conclusion: KGO作为有原则的实用框架，改善了概率时序预测的精度-效率权衡，在评测基准上兼具领先精度与快速单步生成能力。

Abstract: Probabilistic time-series forecasting requires models that simultaneously capture structured temporal dynamics, expressive uncertainty, and efficient inference, yet existing approaches fall short of this goal: latent dynamical models impose structure but rely on restrictive generation mechanisms, while modern generative methods such as diffusion and flow matching achieve flexibility at the cost of iterative and computationally expensive sampling. We introduce the Koopman Generative Operator (KGO), a probabilistic forecasting method that conceptualizes prediction as the evolution of structured uncertainty. KGO integrates three core components: (1) Koopman Patch Embedding (KoPE) for temporally consistent latent trajectory extrapolation; (2) Koopman Flow Matching (KoFM), which enables fast, single-step generation through a closed-form matrix exponential in a Koopman latent space, bypassing iterative sampling; and (3) an Adaptive Uncertainty Gate (AUG), which provides probabilistic forecasts by adapting uncertainty per-variable and per-horizon, corresponding to a learned decomposition of aleatoric uncertainty without manual tuning. By unifying these components, KGO achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on 13/17 ProbTS benchmarks in CRPS and 16/17 in NMAE. Further, by eliminating iterative sampling, KGO delivers at least a 25$\times$ reduction in inference time compared to iterative generative models. These results position KGO as a practical and principled framework that advances the accuracy-efficiency trade-off in probabilistic time-series forecasting, combining leading accuracy on the evaluated benchmarks with fast, single-step generation.

</details>


### [236] [Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning with Submodular Trajectory Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04774)
*Yasaman Zolfimoselo,Nikolas Geroliminis,Maryam Kamgarpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有解耦动力学与联合轨迹上单调子模奖励的多智能体协同强化学习，提出"pre-window curvature" $c_m$ 度量早期状态对当前边际增益的削弱程度，并据此设计顺序贪心算法，在固定 $m$ 下获得 $\frac{1-c_m}{2-c_m}$ 近似保证，计算复杂度关于智能体数与时间跨度多项式级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合轨迹上的子模奖励不可加，最优策略可能依赖所有智能体的历史，精确优化在时间跨度与智能体数上呈指数复杂度。作者希望在保留理论保证的前提下规避这一指数爆炸，将策略依赖截断为最近的有限历史窗口。

Method: 引入 pre-window curvature $c_m$，量化 $m$ 步之前的访问状态对当前边际增益的削减；用该曲率控制"将每个智能体策略限制为仅依赖最近 $m+1$ 个状态"所引入的误差；利用解耦动力学，提出顺序优化各局部策略的贪心算法。

Result: 对固定 $m$，算法达到 $\frac{1-c_m}{2-c_m}$ 的近似保证（不计估计误差），且计算时间关于智能体数与时间跨度多项式。对时间可加奖励可退化为已知的 $1/2$ 近似；对单智能体情形，近似因子不小于已知的 total-curvature 下界。作者将多智能体交通监控建模为该框架并进行了实验评估。

Conclusion: 通过历史窗口截断与 pre-window curvature 的桥接，可在子模奖励的协同多智能体强化学习中同时获得可计算性与近似保证，并统一/推广已有的可加奖励与单智能体曲率结论；交通监控实例验证了框架的适用性。

Abstract: We study cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning with decoupled dynamics and a monotone submodular reward function over joint trajectories. Due to the non-additive reward, an optimal policy may depend on the history of all agents. This dependence makes exact optimization exponential in the horizon and the number of agents. To overcome this challenge while retaining theoretical guarantees, we introduce pre-window curvature $c_m$, which quantifies how states visited more than $m$ steps earlier reduce current marginal gains. Using this curvature to control the error from restricting the policy of each agent to depend only on its last $m+1$ states, we propose a greedy algorithm that exploits the decoupled dynamics to optimize local policies sequentially. For fixed $m$, the algorithm achieves a $\frac{1-c_m}{2-c_m}$ approximation guarantee up to estimation error, and its computation time is polynomial in the number of agents and the horizon. We recover the known $1/2$-factor approximation for time-additive rewards and obtain an approximation factor no smaller than the known total-curvature bound for a single agent. We formulate multi-agent traffic monitoring as an example of this framework and evaluate our algorithm on it.

</details>


### [237] [Density-Aware Reward Scaling: A Reinforcement Learning Framework for Reward-Proportional Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04792)
*Ria Shekhawat,Abhijeet Sinha,Yingtao Zhu,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出密度感知奖励缩放（DARS），通过按同组 rollout 结果的局部密度反比缩放奖励，在保持标准策略梯度训练的前提下促进奖励比例采样，缓解多解场景下的模式坍缩并提升多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大化期望回报的强化学习目标易将概率质量集中于少数高奖励结果，当存在多个不同且同样合意的解时会限制多样性（单模式坍缩）。
