Method: DARS 是一种轻量奖励处理机制：在每个 on-policy rollout 组内估计结果的局部密度，并用该密度的倒数对奖励进行缩放，从而在连续结果空间中实现奖励比例采样；无需学习密度模型、辅助目标、critic，也不改动底层策略梯度目标。

Result: 在多模态导航可控任务中，DARS-GRPO 避免了 GRPO 的单模式坍缩，并较好恢复目标模式分配；在文本到图像提示适配上，跨数据集、策略 LM 与奖励设置均一致提升图像与提示多样性，同时保持良好语义对齐。

Conclusion: 密度感知奖励缩放是一种轻量方法，可在现有强化学习流程内提升对多样高奖励结果的覆盖度。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning objectives that maximize expected return can concentrate probability mass on a small subset of high-reward outcomes, limiting diversity when multiple distinct solutions are desirable. We introduce Density-Aware Reward Scaling (DARS), a simple reward-processing mechanism that promotes reward-proportional sampling in continuous outcome spaces while retaining standard policy-gradient training. DARS estimates the local density of outcomes within each on-policy rollout group and inversely scales rewards by this density. The method requires no learned density model, auxiliary objective, critic, or modification to the underlying policy-gradient objective. In a controlled multimodal navigation task, DARS-GRPO avoids the single-mode collapse exhibited by GRPO and closely recovers the target mode allocation. Applied to text-to-image prompt adaptation, DARS-GRPO consistently improves image and prompt diversity across datasets, Policy LMs, and reward settings while maintaining good semantic alignment. These results show that density-aware reward scaling provides a lightweight way to improve coverage of diverse high-reward outcomes within existing reinforcement-learning pipelines.

</details>


### [238] [Training Specialist LLMs on Your Phone: Forward-Only Optimization via Block-Spectral Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04797)
*Liyan Tan,Yequan Zhao,Ziyue Liu,Yupeng Su,Ruijie Zhang,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MobileSpec 提出一种仅用前向传播、基于可验证奖励（RLVR 范式）在个人设备上训练轻量化专家的系统；其核心算法 FuRA-S 只训练预训练权重列块的奇异值（块谱坐标），从而产生与 RLVR 权重更新相似的随机扰动，实现高效前向式微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧 LLM 可保证数据本地化，但内存受限削弱通用能力；而多数用户特定任务并不需要前沿模型的广度，故将共享端侧模型适配为轻量专家是务实方案。然而反向传播内存开销过大，且用户通常缺乏标注数据。

Method: MobileSpec 仅用前向传播训练专家，从自动校验的任务结果中学习（RLVR）。由于前向式更新基于随机扰动，只有当扰动近似 RLVR 的权重变化（基本保持预训练权重奇异值、旋转其奇异向量）时才高效。核心算法 FuRA-S 仅训练预训练权重列块的块谱坐标（即列块奇异值）来生成此类扰动：独立重缩放各块会改变其对该权重矩阵的相对贡献，从而旋转奇异向量（理论与实验均证实）。该更新可改写为对层输入的变换，因此无需修改权重即可适配 INT4 模型。

Result: 仅 1–5 MB、更新 0.015–0.05% 参数的专家，将手机使用智能体的成功率从 6–23% 提升至 89–100%；可在 iPhone 16 Pro 上过夜训练个性化 8B 模型。在 RLVR 基准上，FuRA-S 与全参数进化策略（ES）相当，并优于 LoRA 适配器上的 ES 与 INT4 模型上的量化 ES。

Conclusion: 通过块谱坐标的低维前向式更新，MobileSpec 在严苛内存约束下实现了端侧、无标注、可验证奖励驱动的高效个性化专家训练，兼顾存储效率与性能，并原生支持量化模型。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) on personal devices keep user data local, but limited memory restricts their general capability. Since most user-specific tasks do not require the breadth of frontier models, a practical solution is to adapt a shared on-device model into lightweight specialists. However, backpropagation is prohibitive in memory, and users rarely provide labeled data. We present MobileSpec, a system that trains specialists with forward passes alone and learns from automatically checked task outcomes, as in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). Because forward-only updates are built from random perturbations, training is efficient only when these perturbations resemble the weight changes of RLVR, which largely preserve the singular values of pretrained weights and rotate their singular vectors. The core algorithm of MobileSpec, FuRA-S, produces such perturbations while training only the block-spectral coordinates of pretrained weights, namely the singular values of their column blocks. As we show theoretically and empirically, rescaling these blocks independently shifts their relative contributions to each weight matrix and thereby rotates its singular vectors. Since this update can be rewritten as a transformation of the layer input, FuRA-S also adapts INT4 models without modifying their weights. With specialists of only 1-5 MB that update 0.015-0.05% of the parameters, MobileSpec raises the success rate of a phone-use agent from 6-23% up to 89-100% and trains a personalized 8B model on an iPhone 16 Pro overnight. On RLVR benchmarks, FuRA-S is competitive with full-parameter evolution strategies (ES) and outperforms ES over LoRA adapters and quantized ES on INT4 models.

</details>


### [239] [On the Complexity of Finite-Sum Nonconvex-Strongly-Concave Minimax Optimization with Exact Proximal Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04806)
*Yuxing Peng,Zhiqing Tang,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究有限和、非凸-强凹极小极大问题的复杂度下界，证明即使允许精确的分量近端步（返回正则化分量问题的全局鞍点）与不受限的自适应策略，多项式复杂度也无法改善；并给出达到该下界（至 log²(2+κ) 因子）的仅用梯度的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限和 NC-SC 极小极大问题广泛出现于对抗训练与分布鲁棒学习。已有下界仅覆盖线性张成算法、zero-respecting 算法或受限的近端协议，未回答"精确分量近端步"（可暴露梯度无法揭示的方向）是否能突破多项式复杂度这一开放问题。

Method: 构造最坏情形硬实例（n 个分量、联合均方光滑常数 L、条件数 κ，初始原始间隙 Δ、联合梯度范数 ≤√(2LΔ)），在此类实例上分析任意随机自适应算法的分量查询复杂度；硬实例设计为：每个分量允许相同步长的近端步，且某分量的近端响应仅能揭示隐藏方向，直至该方向被分配给另一分量为止。上界侧给出仅使用分量梯度的算法。

Result: 当 ε²/(LΔ) 小于某通用常数时，任意随机自适应算法（可访问精确分量值、梯度与近端响应）求 ε-稳定点最坏情形需 Ω(n + min{√n·κ, n^{3/4}√κ}·LΔ/ε²) 次分量查询；仅用梯度的算法在含初始化在内、至多 log²(2+κ) 因子内达到该速率。

Conclusion: 不受限自适应性与精确近端访问均不能改善多项式复杂度；该下界与已有 zero-respecting 一阶方法结果（Zhou, arXiv:2610.04944）一致，确立了该问题在该设定下的紧致复杂度刻画（至对数因子）。

Abstract: Finite-sum nonconvex-strongly-concave minimax problems arise in adversarial training and distributionally robust learning. Their known lower bounds cover only linear-span algorithms, zero-respecting algorithms, or a restricted proximal protocol. They leave open the power of exact component proximal steps, whose responses are global saddle points of regularized component problems and can expose directions that gradients do not reveal. We show that neither unrestricted adaptivity nor exact proximal access improves the polynomial complexity. We consider $n$ components with joint mean-squared smoothness $L$ and condition number $κ$, started from a point with primal gap at most $Δ$ and joint gradient norm at most $\sqrt{2LΔ}$. For $\varepsilon^2/(LΔ)$ below a universal constant, every randomized adaptive algorithm with exact component values, gradients, and proximal responses needs $Ω(n+\min\{\sqrt n\,κ,n^{3/4}\sqrtκ\}LΔ/\varepsilon^2)$ component queries in the worst case to find an $\varepsilon$-stationary point of the value function. A method using only component gradients attains this rate up to a factor $\log^2(2+κ)$, including initialization. Zhou (arXiv:2610.04944) concurrently proves the same rate for zero-respecting first-order methods. In our hard instances, every component admits proximal steps with the same step sizes, and a response for a component reveals hidden directions only up to the first one assigned to another component.

</details>


### [240] [Sleep-Disordered Breathing Detection with Quantum Recurrent Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04818)
*Alejandro Mayorga-Redondo,José Daniel Viqueira,Diego Alvarez-Estevez,Eduardo Mosqueira-Rey,Andrés Gómez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究将变分量子循环神经网络用于睡眠呼吸障碍事件的二元时序分类，并与经典RNN对比，发现量子模型以极少参数取得相当性能，在小数据集上更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子循环神经网络在真实生理时序数据（呼吸暂停 vs 正常呼吸）分类中的可行性与相对经典RNN的优劣势，评估其在当前量子硬件与算法限制下的潜力。

Method: 提出变分经典—量子混合架构（variational classical–quantum），与经典循环神经网络模型进行对比；在多个复杂度不同的真实数据集上开展实验。

Result: 经典与量子模型整体性能相当；量子模型在最小数据集上表现更优，在最复杂数据集上略弱；但量子方法在极少的参数量与低量子比特条件下仍具竞争力，并展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 尽管当前受硬件与算法限制，变分量子循环模型是时序分类的一种有前景的替代方案，随量子计算技术成熟具有进一步发展潜力。

Abstract: This work investigates the application of Quantum Recurrent Neural Networks to the binary classification of sleep-disordered breathing events in time-series data, aiming to distinguish between apneic and normal breathing episodes. A variational classical--quantum architecture is proposed and evaluated against classical Recurrent Neural Network models. Experiments were conducted on multiple real-world datasets with varying levels of complexity to assess the strengths and limitations of the quantum approach. In the comparative analysis, both classical and quantum models showed comparable performance. The quantum model achieved superior performance on the smallest dataset, while yielding slightly weaker results in the most complex setting. However, the quantum approach exhibited notable strengths, such as competitive performance despite a drastically reduced parameter count and low-qubit regime, as well as good generalization capabilities. These results suggest that, despite current hardware and algorithmic limitations, variational quantum recurrent models represent a promising alternative for time-series classification as Quantum Computing technologies continue to mature.

</details>


### [241] [Anomaly Detection on Resource-Constrained Devices with Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04835)
*Marco Angioli,Mathias Persson,Hiroto Tamura,Hadi Banaee,Amy Loutfi,Gouhei Tanaka,Alberto Giaretta,Denis Kleyko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究储层状态马氏距离（MDRS）这一无监督时序异常检测方法在资源受限边缘设备上的适用性，提出循环移位储层连接（MDRS-Circ）与仅用最终储层状态计算整段序列异常分数两项优化，并在MLPerf Tiny基准与NUCLEO-F401RE微控制器上评估性能、延迟、能耗与内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘端异常检测需在保持竞争性性能的同时最小化计算开销与内存占用。作者希望验证MDRS这类无监督时序异常检测方法是否适合部署于资源受限设备，并通过优化进一步降低其计算需求。

Method: 提出两项优化：一是以循环移位结构（MDRS-Circ）替代随机储层连接（MDRS-Rand）；二是证明整段时间序列的异常分数可仅由最终储层状态计算得到。此外，利用储层的时间记忆特性实现更高效的输入预处理以进一步降低计算开销。在MLPerf Tiny异常检测基准上与参考自编码器及其他异常检测方法对比，并在NUCLEO-F401RE单片机上部署推理内核，测量延迟、估算能耗并评估内存占用。

Result: 在评估的方法中，仅MDRS与自编码器达到基准性能目标，而MDRS所需运算更少、模型更小。经预处理优化后，MDRS达到ROC AUC 0.869、模型大小24 KB、单次推理估算能耗24 uJ、延迟0.32 ms，相较采用标准预处理的自编码器实现75.9倍加速。

Conclusion: MDRS（尤其是MDRS-Circ结合高效预处理）是一种适用于资源受限边缘设备的高效无监督时序异常检测方案，在满足基准性能目标的同时显著降低运算量、模型体积、能耗与延迟。

Abstract: Anomaly detection at the edge requires the deployment of models that achieve competitive performance, while minimizing computational cost and memory footprint. In this article, we investigate the suitability of the Mahalanobis Distance of Reservoir States (MDRS), an unsupervised time-series anomaly detection method, for resource-constrained devices and propose two optimizations to reduce its computational requirements. Specifically, we replace random reservoir connectivity (MDRS-Rand) with a circular shift (MDRS-Circ) and show that the anomaly score for an entire time series can be computed from the final reservoir state alone. Furthermore, we show that exploiting the reservoir's temporal memory enables more efficient input preprocessing, further reducing computational cost. We evaluate the MDRS variants on the MLPerf Tiny anomaly detection benchmark against the reference autoencoder and other anomaly detection methods, and deploy their inference kernels on a NUCLEO-F401RE microcontroller to assess latency, estimate energy consumption, and measure memory footprint. Among the evaluated methods, only MDRS and the autoencoder meet the benchmark's performance target, with MDRS requiring fewer operations and smaller models. With optimized preprocessing, MDRS achieves an ROC AUC of 0.869, a model size of 24 KB, an estimated energy consumption of 24 uJ per inference, and a latency of 0.32 ms, corresponding to a 75.9x speedup over the autoencoder with standard preprocessing.

</details>


### [242] [Found by Search, Broken by a Shortcut: Transfer and Tampering of Searched Intrinsic Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04838)
*Chase J. Metoyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究用预注册的三阶段搜索（CMA-ES 与等预算随机搜索，共1,560次PPO训练，另340次用于最终评估与篡改测试）在四种通用事件（新视野、拾取、放回、切换）构成的先天驱动力空间中寻找内在奖励，检验其能否迁移到未见任务并抵抗搜索时缺失的漏洞利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究搜索先天驱动力所得的内在奖励是否具备迁移性（到未见任务）与鲁棒性（面对搜索时未出现的可利用漏洞），并以此检验“任务无关内在奖励”主张的强度。

Method: 奖励由四种通用事件构成，每个事件带权重、释放量与补充速率；采用预注册三阶段协议，协议在结果产生前已锁定于公开、带时间戳的提交中；用CMA-ES与等预算随机搜索进行选择，共1,560次PPO训练；最终评估与篡改测试使用340次运行；在KeyCorridorS3R2（留出任务）、UnlockPickup及DoorKey-8x8上进行注册比较与描述性比较。

Result: 在留出任务KeyCorridorS3R2上，CMA-ES所选奖励E在3项注册比较中均优于仅任务PPO、episodic novelty与随机搜索所选奖励R，且均达到注册的效应量标准；但前作饱食度奖励（该任务上手工接入钥匙与锁门事件）20/20种子成功，而E仅4/20（描述性）。UnlockPickup上E均值面积领先该奖励0.276（描述性）。训练任务上E与R无注册差异。搜索时缺失的免费reset动作使E在DoorKey-8x8上崩溃（均值面积0.829→0.136，注册），低于仅任务PPO（描述性）。

Conclusion: 搜索所得驱动力在各测试任务上均有帮助，但任务特定事件奖励在留出任务上表现更佳，且选择过程未能赋予E对搜索中缺失漏洞的抵抗力——即内在奖励的迁移性有限、鲁棒性不成立。

Abstract: Can a search over innate drives find intrinsic rewards that help a reinforcement-learning agent learn, transfer to a task it never saw, and resist an exploit that was absent during the search? Each candidate reward combines four generic events (a new view, a pickup, a drop and a toggle), each with a weight, a relief and a refill rate. We ran three pre-registered stages; the search protocol was locked in a public, timestamped commit before any search result existed. CMA-ES and an equal-budget random search used 1,560 PPO training runs, and the final evaluation and tamper test used 340. On the held-out task, KeyCorridorS3R2, the CMA-ES-selected reward (E) beat task-only PPO, episodic novelty and the random-search-selected reward (R) in all 3 registered comparisons, all of them meaningful by the registered size rule. Among these generic rewards E transferred best; the earlier paper's satiation reward, which on this task is given hand-wired key and locked-door events, did better (20 of 20 seeds solved against 4 of 20; descriptive). On UnlockPickup, where that reward uses generic events, E's mean area exceeded it by 0.276 (descriptive). On the training tasks there was no registered difference between E and R: both E-over-R tests missed. A free reset action, absent during the search, collapsed E's learning on DoorKey-8x8 (mean area 0.829 without it, 0.136 with it; registered) to below task-only PPO (descriptive). The searched drives helped on every task tested, but a reward given task-specific events did better on the held-out task, and selection gave E no protection against an exploit absent from the search.

</details>


### [243] [DLER: Dual-Logit Experience Replay for Online Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04885)
*Huiwei Lin,Shanshan Feng,Kong Aik Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Dual-Logit Experience Replay (DLER)，将点积logit分解为角度因子与范数因子，用双logit同时驱动模型优化与记忆管理，缓解在线持续学习中的预测偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线持续学习面临灾难性遗忘，基于回放的方法因新旧数据不平衡导致预测偏向当前类；现有偏置纠正方法在在线场景下仍不充分。

Method: 将点积logit分解为角度因子与范数因子：角度因子善于捕获新知识但易受预测偏置影响，范数因子有助于保持历史知识。据此，DLER用余弦logit学习当前样本，回放旧样本时引入点积logit以缓解偏置；并加入辅助监督对比目标，促进类内角度紧凑，将有限回放样本的纠偏推广到同类其他样本；同时设计记忆更新策略，优先保留"点积预测对正确类概率高于余弦预测"的样本，使样本选择与双logit训练一致。

Result: 在三个数据集上的大量实验验证了DLER的有效性，尤其在有限记忆预算下表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过logit分解揭示角度/范数因子的不同作用，DLER在优化与记忆管理两个层面统一利用双logit，有效缓解在线持续学习中的预测偏置，并在有限存储条件下具备优势。

Abstract: Online continual learning aims to continuously train neural networks on a non-stationary data stream in a single pass. Rehearsal-based methods alleviate catastrophic forgetting by replaying stored samples, but the imbalance between current and previous data often leads to predictions biased toward current classes. Many approaches have been proposed to overcome the forgetting problem by correcting the bias; however, they still need to be improved in an online fashion. By decomposing the dot-product logits into an angle factor and a norm factor, our analysis reveals that the angle factor efficiently captures novel knowledge but is particularly susceptible to prediction bias, whereas the norm factor helps preserve historical knowledge. Based on these observations, we propose Dual-Logit Experience Replay (DLER), which exploits dual-logits for both model optimization and memory management. Specifically, DLER learns current samples using cosine logits and incorporates additional dot-product logits when replaying previous samples to mitigate prediction bias. To strengthen dual-logit learning under limited replay memory, an auxiliary supervised contrastive objective promotes angular compactness within each class, extending corrections learned from limited replay samples to other samples of the same class. Meanwhile, we introduce a memory update strategy that preferentially retains samples for which dot-product prediction assigns a higher probability to the correct class than cosine prediction, aligning sample selection with dual-logit training. Extensive experiments on three datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of DLER, particularly under limited memory budgets.

</details>


### [244] [Fault-tolerant shallow recurrent decoders for sparse sensing with missing sensor measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04893)
*Matteo Romilio Pasqual,Matteo Tomasetto,J. Nathan Kutz,Andrea Manzoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向浅层循环解码器（SHRED）的容错稀疏感知策略，包括四种基于Transformer的掩码机制与多步解码训练目标，实现稀疏、异步、缺失传感器下的高维状态估计，并在保持计算效率的同时优于插补与容错循环架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实传感系统存在故障、间歇断连、异步采样与噪声，导致测量流不规则、不完整、异构；而现有实时状态重建的感知策略通常假设传感器同步且持续工作，限制了实际部署的适用性。

Method: 在SHRED框架下引入容错稀疏感知：(1) 朴素插补策略；(2) 容错循环时间编码器；(3) 四种基于Transformer的掩码机制，通过二元数据可用性掩码使SHRED显式感知传感器可用性；(4) 多步解码训练目标。

Result: 在合成基准及水动力与海洋建模的三个真实问题上：(i) Transformer掩码机制优于标准插补与容错循环架构；(ii) 多步解码目标在异构缺失模式下提升重建保真度与鲁棒性；(iii) SHRED计算效率得以保持，无额外推理时开销。

Conclusion: 基于掩码的容错感知设计可有效应对稀疏、异步与缺失测量，且不牺牲SHRED的在线效率，适用于实际运营环境中的高维状态重建。

Abstract: Real-world sensing systems are inherently imperfect: sensors may fail, intermittently disconnect, operate with asynchronous sampling rates, and be subject to noise, leading to measurement streams that are irregular, incomplete, and heterogeneous. Nevertheless, sensing strategies for real-time state reconstruction typically assume synchronous and continuously active sensors, a constraint that substantially limits their applicability in operational environments. In this work, we introduce fault-tolerant sparse sensing strategies in the context of shallow recurrent decoders (SHRED) to enable high-dimensional state estimation from sparse, asynchronous, and missing sensor measurements. Specifically, we consider naive imputation strategies, fault-tolerant recurrent temporal encoders, and we introduce four different transformer-based masking mechanisms to make SHRED explicitly aware of sensor availability through the binary data-availability mask. Moreover, we further introduce a multi-step decoding training objective for SHRED, resulting in smoother latent trajectories and more robust predictions. Throughout a synthetic benchmark and three real-world problems in hydrodynamic and ocean modeling, we show that (i) transformer-based masking mechanisms outperform standard imputation techniques and fault-tolerant recurrent architectures, (ii) the multi-step decoding training objective enhances reconstruction fidelity and robustness in the presence of heterogeneous missing-data patterns, while (iii) the computational efficiency of SHRED is preserved, with no additional inference-time overhead.

</details>


### [245] [Attending to Memory Writes Reduces Seed Variability in Recurrent Length Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04902)
*Sungwoo Goo,Hwi-yeol Yun,Sangkeun Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究 bounded delta attention（对未校正更新提案做因果注意力，将有界校正纳入循环更新），在 TraceRelay 中用加法相位递推 + 并行扫描前计算注意力实现；在 1–40 训练、80 长度测试的前缀监督 modulo-5 任务上，上/下 delta attention 主要带来种子间方差降低与较缓的 extrapolation 退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环记忆需要学习有用的更新，但为辅助学习而加入的注意力可能损害长度泛化。作者希望考察"有界更新级注意力"能否作为循环学习的支架，在保持均值的同时降低种子间波动并缓解长度外推退化。

Method: 提出 bounded delta attention：在未校正的更新提案上做因果注意力，其有界校正进入循环更新。实现于 TraceRelay：加法相位递推结合在并行扫描之前计算的注意力。实验设定为 prefix-supervised modulo-5，训练长度 1–40，测试长度 80，七种子对比，包括上/下 delta attention、上/下 state attention、禁用 delta 校正、以及输入梯度阻断消融。

Result: (1) 上 delta attention 达 70.81±8.26%（七种子），对比局部注意力循环基线 69.21±22.42%，均值相近但 SD 降低 63.2%；上 state attention 也达 74.75±6.24%，说明降方差非更新级注意力独有；禁用上 delta 校正仍保持 70.62±8.60%。(2) 阻断 delta-attention 输入梯度后精度变为 65.25±12.40%，SD 增加 50.2%；在七个种子中 95% 验证阈值平均更早达到，而在同样达到的六个种子中 99% 阈值被延迟。(3) 下 delta attention 的外推退化远小于下 state attention（62.60% vs 19.40%，基线 71.06%）；移除其校正降至 22.56%，且在输入梯度阻断下优势仍存在。

Conclusion: 有界更新级注意力可作为循环学习支架：降低种子间变异，并在 state attention 失效的位置表现出更缓的退化；但其降方差效应并非更新级注意力独有，且对输入梯度通路敏感。

Abstract: Recurrent memory requires learning useful updates, but attention added to assist learning can also compromise length generalization. We study bounded delta attention: causal attention over uncorrected update proposals whose bounded correction enters the recurrent update. Its implementation in TraceRelay combines additive phase recurrence with attention computed before a parallel scan. On prefix-supervised modulo-5, trained at lengths 1--40, three findings characterize its behavior at length 80. First, upper delta attention attains $70.81\pm8.26$% accuracy across seven seeds, versus $69.21\pm22.42$% for the local-attention recurrent baseline (mean $\pm$ sample SD): a 63.2% reduction in SD with a similar mean. Upper state attention also reduces variation, reaching $74.75\pm6.24$%, so this benefit is not unique to update-level attention. Disabling the upper delta correction retains $70.62\pm8.60$%. Second, blocking gradients at the delta-attention inputs changes accuracy to $65.25\pm12.40$% in the matched seven-seed comparison, increasing SD by 50.2%. It also delays a 99% validation threshold on average among the same six seeds that reach it; the 95% threshold is reached earlier on average with input-gradient blocking across all seven seeds. Third, lower delta attention exhibits substantially less extrapolation degradation than lower state attention: 62.60% versus 19.40%, against a 71.06% baseline. Removing its correction reduces accuracy to 22.56%; its advantage over lower state attention also persists under input-gradient blocking. These observations motivate bounded update-level attention as a recurrent learning scaffold, combining reduced seed variation with less severe degradation in a placement where state attention fails.

</details>


### [246] [Cool Graphs: Active Property Search Towards Quantum Nano-Refrigerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04907)
*Sofia Blyufer,Amir Kleiner,Uri Peskin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将石墨变分自编码器(GVAE)与不确定性驱动主动学习(AL)耦合的搜索框架，在指数级庞大的多体参数空间中高效筛选能吸热（即具致冷能力）的电子结构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米器件可从热环境中吸能而充当量子制冷机，但候选构型空间随器件尺寸指数增长，每个构型都需昂贵量子化学计算，属“大海捞针”式搜索难题；同时高性能计算的环境足迹也亟需低功耗/制冷方案。

Method: 以二维“工作材料”电子结为对象，将位点能量、耦合矩阵、温度等物理参数映射到平滑潜空间；用GVAE区分制冷与耗散参数区，并采用基于蒙特卡洛dropout的主动学习循环，围绕决策边界迭代挑选模型最不确定的候选进行模拟；共运行32轮。

Result: 分类判别度在留出构型上达0.920±0.004，在未见外推拓扑上达0.895±0.010；32轮AL中吸热体系发现率在所有测试拓扑上持续超过随机基线（随机基线下仅1.9–8.4%构型具目标性质）；潜空间沿与微观模型参数相关的可解释轴组织构型，暗示存在低维“制冷坐标”。

Conclusion: 该GVAE+AL框架为组合爆炸的设计空间提供了数据高效导航路径，并为纳米电子信息处理器件的高效制冷及降低高性能计算环境足迹开辟新方向。

Abstract: Nanodevices that absorb energy from their thermal environment can act as quantum refrigerators. Finding suitable configurations, however, is a needle-in-a-haystack problem: the many-body state space scales exponentially with the size of the device, where each candidate configuration requires an expensive quantum-chemical computation. We introduce a search framework that couples a Graph Variational Autoencoder (GVAE) with an uncertainty-driven Active Learning (AL) loop to efficiently identify energy-absorbing configurations in electronic junctions with a 2D "working material" to be optimized. The GVAE maps physical parameters - site energies, coupling matrices, temperature - into a smooth latent space that distinguishes cooling from dissipative parameter regimes, achieving a classification discriminability of $0.920 \pm 0.004$ on held-out configurations and $0.895 \pm 0.010$ on unseen extrapolation topologies. An AL pipeline based on Monte Carlo dropout then targets the decision boundary, iteratively selecting the candidates where the model is least certain for simulation. Over 32 AL cycles, the uncertainty-driven strategy discovers energy absorbing systems at rates consistently exceeding the random baseline across all tested topologies (where only 1.9-8.4% of configurations exhibit the desired property), providing a data-efficient pathway for navigating a combinatorically large design space. The learned latent space organizes configurations along physically interpretable axes, that correlate with underlying microscopic model parameters. This hints at the existence of "cooling coordinates" - low-dimensional, physically interpretable quantities separating cooling from non-cooling regimes. Our framework, thus, paves a new way towards effective cooling of nanoscale electronic information processing devices, to reduce the environmental footprint of high performance computing.

</details>


### [247] [Modulating Merging Strengths via Joint Loss Estimation for LoRA-based Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04931)
*Kun Gu,De Cheng,Zhipeng Xu,Lingfeng He,Di Xu,Nannan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出M2LE，通过联合损失估计实现LoRA合并强度的逐元素调制，在多个基准上优于现有PEFT持续学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LoRA的持续学习方法在合并新任务更新时通常使用单一共享标量系数缩放整个更新，但不同更新元素对旧任务与新任务的影响方式不同，全局系数无法刻画这种差异。

Method: M2LE包含两部分：(1) Hessian信息引导的自适应合并(HIM)，不再搜索全局合并系数，而是在旧任务与新任务的联合目标下直接求解目标合并更新，其解析解自然表现为对当前任务LoRA更新的逐元素调制；(2) 任务模拟扰动(TSP)，通过模拟不同任务间的干扰来提升所学更新在后续合并中的稳定性。

Result: 在多个基准上的实验表明，M2LE持续优于现有的基于PEFT的持续学习方法。

Conclusion: 以逐元素调制替代全局标量缩放、并结合任务干扰模拟，是提升LoRA式持续学习合并质量的有效途径。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) aims to adapt models to new tasks while preserving previously learned knowledge. Recently, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), a representative Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) method, has gained increasing attention in CL due to its efficiency and scalability. Several LoRA-based merging methods maintain a single inference model by scaling the newly learned LoRA update with one coefficient and merging it into the previous weights after each task. However, they usually control the whole update with a shared scalar factor, although different update elements may affect previous and current tasks in different ways. To address this issue, we propose Merging Strength Modulation via Joint Loss Estimation (M2LE). It consists of Hessian Information-guided Adaptive Merging (HIM) and Task Simulation Perturbation for Merging (TSP). Instead of searching for a global merge coefficient, HIM directly solves for the target merged update under a joint objective over previous and current tasks. Solving this objective gives an analytic solution that naturally takes the form of element-wise modulation over the current task LoRA update. To improve the stability of the learned update under subsequent merging, we introduce TSP which simulates different tasks interference. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that M2LE consistently outperforms existing PEFT-based continual learning methods. Code is available at https://github.com/bbdeeer/M2LE_NeurIPS2026.

</details>


### [248] [Deep Neural Operator Learning for stochastic heat equations and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04955)
*Hengrong Du,Qi Feng,Weinan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为空间维数 \(d\ge2\) 的半线性随机热方程建立了解算子（solution operator）的通用逼近定理，将深度神经算子逼近结果推广至含分布值高斯驱动的 SPDE，并给出显式的驱动测量数与 trunk 函数数量界以及各有限随机矩下的逼近误差；该框架进一步应用于随机 Navier–Stokes 方程，得到高概率、局部时间内的逼近结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经算子逼近理论已在线性/确定性 PDE 求解算子方面取得进展，但含分布值高斯驱动的半线性 SPDE 的求解算子逼近尚缺乏理论刻画。核心困难在于驱动项是分布值随机对象、无法直接作为网络输入，需将其化为有限维可观测量的同时控制解误差。作者旨在填补这一空白，并给出可量化的测量复杂度与实际应用（随机 N–S 方程）。

Method: 1) 用光滑 Fourier 截断将驱动项约化为有限多个观测量；2) 借助 Besov 估计与 Schauder 估计控制截断导致的解误差；3) 对约化后的有限维解映射，采用 ReLU branch 网络 + 基于网格的 trunk 函数构成的算子网络进行逼近；4) 给出驱动测量数与 trunk 函数数量的显式界，并在每个有限随机矩意义下建立逼近误差估计；5) 对线性方程证明该约化等价于第一 Wiener 混沌的截断；6) 应用于随机 Navier–Stokes 方程，对初值与驱动均作有限测量并给出显式 sensing rate，得到高概率局部时间逼近定理。

Result: 建立了 \(d\ge2\) 半线性随机热方程解算子的通用逼近定理；获得在每个有限随机矩下的逼近误差界，并给出驱动测量数与 trunk 函数数量的显式复杂度界；证明线性情形下 Fourier 截断约化与第一 Wiener 混沌截断一致；导出随机 Navier–Stokes 方程的高概率局部时间逼近定理，含初值与驱动的有限测量及显式 sensing rate。

Conclusion: 神经算子逼近可推广到分布值高斯驱动下的半线性 SPDE，且复杂度可显式量化；Fourier 截断约化与 Wiener 混沌截断在线性情形自然衔接，表明该框架的合理性；所得结果可用于随机 Navier–Stokes 方程的实际逼近与传感方案设计。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish a universal approximation theorem for solution operators of semilinear stochastic heat equations in spatial dimension \(d\ge2\), extending deep neural operator approximation results to SPDEs with distribution-valued Gaussian forcing. A smooth Fourier cutoff reduces the forcing to finitely many measurements, while Besov and Schauder estimates control the resulting solution error. The finite-dimensional solution map is then approximated by ReLU branch networks together with grid-based trunk functions, yielding explicit bounds on the numbers of forcing measurements and trunk functions and approximation in every finite stochastic moment. For linear equations, the reduction coincides with truncation of the first Wiener chaos. Lastly, as an application, we prove a high-probability local-in-time approximation theorem for stochastic Navier--Stokes equations, with finite measurements of both the initial condition and forcing and an explicit sensing rate.

</details>


### [249] [ZEMA: confidence-calibrated signed descent with only one state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04987)
*Ali Dadsetan,Domenic Rosati,Frank Rudzicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ZEMA优化器：用批内逐样本梯度的z-score度量符号支持度，转为符号概率后平滑，仅需单状态（Adam的一半），每步时间增加4.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作将Adam刻画为"受梯度不确定性调制的符号下降"：更新方向是全批梯度的符号，幅度由批数据对该符号的支持强度缩放。本文直接沿用这一视角，尝试显式地度量并利用这种"符号支持度"来设计更简洁的优化器。

Method: ZEMA（Z-score Evidence Momentum Adaptation）：对每个坐标，利用当前批内逐样本梯度计算z-score，衡量该坐标上符号证据的强弱；将z-score转换为符号概率；先形成概率再做单一EMA平滑（即平滑对象为概率，而非平滑后再取符号）。因概率在平滑之前形成，每参数只需维护一个状态（Adam需两个）。所需逐样本方差可由批的一小部分估计，降低开销。

Result: 在微调与预训练实验中，ZEMA与Adam性能相当；降低验证损失的波动；在部分设定下更稳定地恢复全数据集梯度的符号。每步时间开销约为4.6（相对增加）。

Conclusion: ZEMA以单状态、基于z-score的符号置信度自适应，实现与Adam相当的优化效果并改善稳定性。作者展望两个方向：将置信度自适应推广到符号几何之外（如Muon），以及应用于差分隐私训练。

Abstract: Recent work characterizes the Adam optimizer as sign descent modulated by gradient uncertainty: the update is the sign of the batch gradient, scaled by how strongly the batch supports that sign. We apply this view directly and introduce ZEMA (Z-score Evidence Momentum Adaptation), which measures that support with a per-coordinate $z$-score computed from the per-example gradients in the current batch, converts it into a sign probability, and smooths the resulting update with a single exponential moving average. Because the probability is formed before smoothing rather than after, ZEMA keeps one state per parameter instead of Adam's two, at a $4.6$ increase in per-step time; the per-example variance it requires can also be estimated from a small fraction of the batch. In fine-tuning and pretraining experiments, ZEMA is competitive with Adam, reduces the variability of the validation loss, and in some settings recovers the sign of the full-dataset gradient more consistently. We close with two directions this opens: extending confidence adaptation beyond the sign geometry, to methods such as Muon, and to differentially private training.

</details>


### [250] [Exact Selective Inference for Spectral Clustering via Piecewise-Constant Graph Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04995)
*Naotoshi Nakamura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文解决谱聚类标签"双重使用"导致的p值反保守问题：证明在无权ε-或k近邻图上，沿截断χ选择性检验所用的一维扰动方向，图结构仅在有限个断点处改变，从而选择集是区间的有限并，可构造有限样本有效的选择性p值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类标签被先用发现分组、再用于检验组间差异，这种数据复用使普通两样本p值反保守。选择性推断通过条件化于聚类事件来修正，但谱聚类事件涉及亲和图、特征分解与取整三步，通常被认为难以刻画。作者指出真正的障碍在亲和图构造而非特征分解。

Method: 基于无权ε-或k近邻图进行谱聚类；沿截断χ选择性检验的一维扰动方向，图变化只发生在数据依赖的二次距离比较的有限个实根处。断点之间，图、拉普拉斯谱、嵌入、取整及报告的簇对均为有限图状态的确定性函数。在实算术与已知尺度的球面高斯模型下，选择集为区间的精确有限并，从而给出被选谱簇对的有限样本选择性p值；同一论证还给出逐特征的截断正态检验。并扩展至已知特征协方差、特征间隙选K及辅助坐标图。

Result: 验证了零假设与部分零假设下的校准性、实际操作功效、数值敏感性及模型适用范围诊断，并给出基于工作模型的PBMC算法示例。RBF等光滑核亲和图不满足有限状态保证。

Conclusion: 谱聚类的选择性推断是可行的，关键在于亲和图的分段常值结构；该方法提供有限样本有效的选择性p值，但适用范围限于离散图状态可有限刻画的情形。

Abstract: Cluster labels are often used twice: first to discover groups, and then to test whether the discovered groups differ. This double use of the data makes ordinary two-sample $p$-values anti-conservative. Selective inference fixes this by conditioning on the clustering event, but for spectral clustering the event appears difficult because the algorithm combines an affinity map, an eigendecomposition, and a rounding step.
  We show that, for spectral clustering based on unweighted $\varepsilon$- or $k$-nearest-neighbor graphs, the obstruction is the affinity construction rather than the eigendecomposition. Along the one-dimensional perturbation used by the truncated-$χ$ selective test, graph changes occur only at finitely many real roots of data-dependent quadratic distance comparisons. Between these breakpoints, the graph, Laplacian spectrum, embedding, rounding, and reported cluster pair are deterministic functions of a finite graph state. Consequently, in real arithmetic and under a known-scale spherical Gaussian model, the selection set is an exact finite union of intervals, yielding finite-sample selective $p$-values for selected spectral-cluster pairs. The same argument gives feature-wise truncated-normal tests. We also record direct extensions to known feature covariance, eigengap-selected $K$, and auxiliary-coordinate graphs. We validate null and partial-null calibration, operational power, numerical sensitivity, and model-scope diagnostics, and give a working-model PBMC algorithmic illustration. Smooth kernels such as RBF affinities fall outside the finite-state guarantee.

</details>


### [251] [Unifying and Beyond the Theory of Pervasive Capability Degradation in LLM Post-training SFT from Feature Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05003)
*Hang Chen,Jiaying Zhu,Wenya Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从"叠加下的特征干扰"出发建立PCD的自下而上几何理论，指出one-hot交叉熵无法单独解决PCD，并提出直接约束激活margin漂移的AEGIS损失，在3个模型家族、14个SFT任务上同时提升目标任务与通用能力，计算开销仅约标准SFT的1.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流解释将PCD归因于宏观分布偏移，缺乏微观可操作性，导致现有缓解方法缺少完整理论基础。作者希望给出微观几何机制并据此设计有理论保证的损失函数。

Method: 1）建立基于叠加下特征干扰的几何理论：窄域SFT数据破坏预训练形成的近乎正交特征结构，干扰导致激活跨越输出Laguerre-Voronoi几何中的决策超平面，从而翻转通用任务预测；该理论将现有方法统一为六大范式并指出各自有效性与理论局限。2）证明one-hot监督下的交叉熵本身无法消除PCD。3）提出AEGIS损失，沿所有竞争token方向直接约束激活margin的漂移，使干扰抑制与任务适配解耦。

Result: 在3个模型家族、14个SFT任务上，AEGIS在目标任务和通用能力两方面同时优于六大范式的代表性方法，计算成本约为标准SFT的1.3倍；并具备通用性、可扩展性与机制可解释性。

Conclusion: PCD可由特征干扰的几何机制解释，且可通过显式约束激活margin漂移在损失层面解耦干扰抑制与任务适配；AEGIS为完全解决PCD提供了新视角与可行路径。

Abstract: Pervasive Capability Degradation (PCD) refers to the phenomenon in which supervised fine-tuning (SFT), while adapting a large language model (LLM) to a target task, often erodes the general capabilities acquired during pre-training. Prevailing explanations attribute PCD to macroscopic distribution shift and therefore lack microscopic manipulability, leaving existing mitigation methods without a complete theoretical foundation. In this paper, we first develop a bottom-up geometric theory of PCD grounded in feature interference under superposition: narrow-domain SFT data disrupt the almost-orthogonal feature structure formed during pre-training, and the resulting interference drives activations across decision hyperplanes in the output Laguerre-Voronoi geometry, flipping predictions on general tasks. This theory unifies existing methods into six paradigms and reveals both the source of their efficacy and their theoretical limits. We then prove that cross-entropy under one-hot supervision cannot resolve PCD on its own, and accordingly propose the AEGIS loss, which directly constrains the drift of activation margins along all competing token directions, thereby decoupling interference suppression from task adaptation. Across 3 model families and 14 SFT tasks, AEGIS outperforms representative methods of every paradigm on target tasks and pervasive capabilities simultaneously, at only about 1.3 times the computational cost of standard SFT. Its generality, scalability, and mechanistic interpretability offer a new perspective on fully resolving PCD.

</details>


### [252] [Rethinking GA2Ms: performance and out-of-distribution generalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05012)
*George Dimitriadis,Damian Machlanski,Paul Clarke,Spyridon Samothrakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出多分辨率高阶复合广义加性模型 CoGA2M，在 TALENT 大规模表格基准上取得与 SOTA 相当的竞争力，并在分布偏移设定下优于 LightGBM，揭示了加性结构在 OOD 场景中的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究存在两个空白：其一，缺乏样条型 GAM 在大规模表格学习（实际应用的主导模态）上的系统性基准评测；其二，GAM 在分布偏移下的鲁棒性尚未被探索，而结构化归纳偏置理论上应在此场景带来优势。

Method: 提出多分辨率、高阶的复合广义加性模型 CoGA2M（Composite Generalised Additive Model），并在 TALENT 基准套件上进行评测；同时扩展 TALENT，构建具有训练-测试分布差异的 OOD 划分，将 CoGA2M 与 TALENT 上表现始终领先的 LightGBM 进行对比。

Result: CoGA2M 在所有基线中排名前三分之一（回归 29 个、分类 32 个基线的对比中），性能与 SOTA 表格方法相当；在分布偏移下，CoGA2M 优于 LightGBM，表现出明显的结构性优势。

Conclusion: 研究将样条型 GAM 定位为表格预测的有力竞争者，并揭示了加性结构在 OOD 场景中此前未被认识到的益处。

Abstract: Generalised Additive Models (GAMs) are a cornerstone of interpretable machine learning, offering transparent predictions through structured additive decompositions. Despite advances from classical GAMs to modern neural and spline-based variants, two notable gaps remain. First, comprehensive benchmarking of spline-based GAMs on large-scale tabular learning tasks, the dominant modality in applied settings, is lacking. Second, their robustness under distribution shift, a setting where structured inductive biases should confer advantages, remains unexplored. We propose a multi-resolution, higher-order Composite Generalised Additive Model (CoGA2M) and evaluate it on the TALENT benchmark suite. CoGA2M ranks in the top third across all baselines (29 for regression, 32 for classification), establishing competitive performance with state-of-the-art tabular methods. We further extend TALENT with out-of-distribution (OOD) splits exhibiting train-test distributional shift and benchmark CoGA2M against LightGBM, a consistently top-performing model on TALENT. Under distribution shift, CoGA2M outperforms LightGBM, demonstrating a clear structural advantage. Our results position spline-based GAMs as strong contenders for tabular prediction and reveal a previously unrecognised benefit of additive structure in the OOD regime.

</details>


### [253] [Finite Spectral Depth Controls Gaussian Entrance Rank and Avoidance of the First Gradient-Flow Saddle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05022)
*Yanlin Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究对归一化矩阵梯度施加有限步 Newton-Schulz 迭代（即 Muon 型更新）的连续时间动力学，证明 Newton-Schulz 步数可改变训练早期选定的秩，并可使轨迹绕过梯度流首先到达的首个非零驻点；在附加"残差-惯性"条件下证明了充分大有限深度下绕行成立并给出显式阈值，还构造了阈值为 4 的例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵分解为研究神经网络训练动力学提供了简洁模型。既有工作多在小初始化下分析梯度流如何逃离原点邻域并趋于首个非零驻点，但缺乏对归一化梯度型（Muon/Newton-Schulz）更新如何影响这一早期秩选择与收敛目标的认识，本文旨在填补该空白。

Method: 将 Muon 型更新建模为连续时间动力学：对归一化矩阵梯度施加有限步 Newton-Schulz 迭代作为近似正交化算子，分析小初始化下轨迹的秩选择与驻点接近行为；引入"残差-惯性"条件，用以证明对充分大的有限 Newton-Schulz 深度可绕过梯度流的首个非零驻点，并推导显式充分阈值；辅以构造性例子验证阈值为 4。

Result: Newton-Schulz 步数会改变训练早期所选定的秩；轨迹可绕过梯度流所趋近的首个非零驻点；在残差-惯性条件下，对所有充分大的有限深度均可发生绕行，并获得显式充分阈值；构造出绕行阈值为 4 的实例，说明小深度即可能产生质的差异。

Conclusion: 归一化梯度加 Newton-Schulz 近似正交化的离散深度是一个影响训练动力学的实质超参数，而非仅为计算近似手段；它可改变早期秩选择与收敛目标，说明有限深度 Muon 的行为在定性上不同于梯度流。

Abstract: Matrix factorization provides a simple model for studying the training dynamics of neural networks. Recent studies of gradient flow under small initialization have analyzed how trajectories escape from a neighborhood of the origin and approach their first nonzero stationary point. In this paper, we study continuous-time dynamics that applies a finite number of Newton-Schulz iterations to the normalized matrix gradient (Muon). We show that the number of Newton-Schulz steps can change the rank selected during the early stages of training and allow the resulting trajectory to bypass the first nonzero stationary point approached by gradient flow. Under an additional residual-inertia condition, we prove that bypass occurs for all sufficiently large finite Newton-Schulz depths and provide an explicit sufficient threshold. We also construct an example in which the bypass threshold is four.

</details>


### [254] [DRAP: Dynamics-Risk Adaptive Plasticity for Continual Learning in Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05029)
*Oswaldo Ludwig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无回放的持续学习方法DRAP，用局部轨迹放大风险评估张量重要性，并按归一化累加重要性缩放各张量的AdamW学习率，在OpenVLA子网络上实现LIBERO顺序任务的高保留率与强新任务学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI中的持续学习不同于传统场景：每一步的小策略误差会沿轨迹累积，使智能体偏离训练分布、放大后续预测误差，最终导致闭环任务失败。因此参数重要性的度量应体现"动态敏感点上的扰动放大"这一风险，而非仅基于损失或梯度幅值。

Method: DRAP为无回放方法：基于样本级动作梯度估计历史重要性，并以局部轨迹放大风险加权；具体用岭正则化局部线性模型拟合专家状态-动作转移，取其状态转移矩阵最大奇异值作为策略无关的、受Lyapunov思想启发的一步扰动放大估计；每个任务后将风险加权平方梯度累加进张量级重要性记忆；后续任务中用归一化累积重要性缩放各张量的有效AdamW学习率，降低对动态敏感点历史行为有贡献组件的可塑性。实例化于KappaTune选定的OpenVLA固定子网络。

Result: 在LIBERO Spatial、Object、Goal三个套件的无回放顺序适配上，相较标准AdamW微调与EWC，显著提升已学行为的保持能力，同时保持较强的新套件获取能力。

Conclusion: 将参数重要性与闭环动力学风险耦合、以张量级学习率调制替代回放或高成本正则，可有效缓解策略持续学习中的误差累积与遗忘，为物理AI中的无回放持续适配提供可行方案。

Abstract: Continual learning in physical AI differs from conventional continual learning because small per-step policy errors can accumulate over a trajectory. Each deviation may move the agent outside the training distribution, increase subsequent prediction errors, and ultimately cause closed-loop task failure. This work introduces Dynamics-Risk Adaptive Plasticity (DRAP), a replay-free method that estimates the historical importance of adaptable model tensors from sample-level action gradients weighted by local trajectory-amplification risk. A ridge-regularized local linear model is fitted to expert state-action transitions, and the largest singular value of its state-transition matrix provides a policy-independent, Lyapunov-inspired estimate of one-step perturbation amplification. After each task, risk-weighted squared gradients are accumulated in a tensor-level importance memory. During subsequent tasks, DRAP uses normalized cumulative importance to scale the effective AdamW learning rate of each tensor, immediately reducing the plasticity of components that supported previous behavior at dynamically sensitive trajectory points. We instantiate DRAP on a fixed KappaTune-selected subnetwork of OpenVLA and evaluate replay-free sequential adaptation across the LIBERO Spatial, Object, and Goal suites. Compared with standard AdamW fine-tuning and EWC, the results show substantially improved retention of previously learned behaviors while preserving strong new-suite acquisition.

</details>


### [255] [Federated Transfer Learning for Medical Image Classification under Mixed Skew](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05035)
*Alexandre G. Leclercq,Sébastien Bougleux,Aurélien Corroyer-Dulmont,Romain Hérault*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PAFP框架：在预训练骨干上做个性化联邦学习，通过客户端专属特征对齐模块缓解多中心医学数据的混合异构偏斜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像深度学习受数据稀缺与隐私法规制约，联邦学习(FL)虽保留数据本地化，但分布式训练易受强统计异构性影响；多中心医学数据存在特征、标签、概念与数量偏移的混合偏斜。个性化FL可缓解异构性，但多数方法假定从零训练，而医学特征维度高、数据有限，这一假设不现实。

Method: PAFP基于预训练骨干网络；在微调块之前插入客户端专属的Feature Alignment模块，将隐藏特征分布重新对齐到服务器聚合的目标分布；服务器聚合对齐目标，实现个性化与全局共享的折中。

Result: 在FedISIC基准上，PAFP取得最高的未加权平衡准确率，同时将客户端间性能方差相对最强基线降低约六倍，兼顾精度与跨客户端公平性。

Conclusion: 在预训练骨干上以轻量对齐模块实现个性化FL，可同时提升准确率与跨中心公平性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Deep learning in medical imaging is constrained by data scarcity and by privacy regulations that make centralizing patient data across institutions impractical. Federated Learning (FL) addresses these constraints by keeping data local, but its distributed nature exposes training to strong statistical heterogeneity. Multicentric medical data is particularly affected, exhibiting a mixed skew that combines feature, label, concept, and quantity shifts across centers. Personalized FL mitigates heterogeneity by tailoring a model to each client, yet most methods assume training from scratch, which is unrealistic given the high dimensionality of medical features relative to the limited data available. We propose the Personalized Aligner Federated Pretrained (PAFP) framework, a personalized FL approach built on a pre-trained backbone. PAFP inserts client-specific Feature Alignment modules before the fine-tuned blocks to realign hidden feature distributions toward a server-aggregated target. On the FedISIC benchmark, PAFP attains the highest unweighted balanced accuracy while reducing inter-client performance variance roughly six-fold relative to the strongest baseline, yielding accuracy and cross-client equity jointly. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Alexandre-Leclercq/PAFP.

</details>


### [256] [Positional Encoding is All You Need for Scalable Equivariance Constraint Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05041)
*Hagay Michaeli,Haggai Maron,Daniel Soudry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于位置编码（PE）的高效约束松弛方法：注入带可学习尺度的位置编码以打破原有对称性，并在训练中逐步退火至零，从而在训练结束后恢复严格等变模型。相比引入非等变稠密线性层的方法，计算与内存开销仅边际增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严格等变性虽能提升样本效率与泛化，但当数据仅近似满足假设对称性或等变约束导致损失地形病态时，优化变得困难。已有通过在训练中引入非等变稠密线性层来松弛约束、测试时再移除的做法虽有效，却带来显著计算开销，难以扩展到可规模化的架构。

Method: 用位置编码实现约束松弛：向模型中注入带可学习尺度的位置编码，打破模型原有的对称性；训练过程中将该位置信号逐步退火（anneal）至零，使模型在训练结束时恢复为原始等变结构。

Result: 在平移等变与旋转等变模型上均取得显著性能提升，且仅带来边际的计算与内存开销。

Conclusion: 基于位置编码的约束松弛是一种高效、可扩展的等变网络优化辅助手段，在保持推理时严格等变性的前提下改善训练效果，计算代价远低于稠密线性层松弛方案。

Abstract: Equivariant neural networks incorporate known symmetries of the data, such as permutations, rotations, or translations, directly into their architecture, often improving sample efficiency and generalization. However, strict equivariance can make optimization difficult, for example, when the data only approximately satisfy the assumed symmetry, or when the equivariance constraints induce a challenging loss landscape. Recent studies have shown that relaxing equivariance during training by introducing non-equivariant dense linear layers can ease optimization in such cases and improve the performance of the resulting strictly equivariant model, after removing the relaxation layers at test time. While effective, this approach incurs a significant computational overhead, limiting its applicability in scalable architectures. In this work, we propose a more efficient constraint-relaxation method based on positional encoding (PE). Our framework injects a positional encoding with learnable scale that breaks the model's original symmetry. This positional signal is gradually annealed to zero, recovering the original equivariant model by the end of training. Empirically, we demonstrate significant improvements for translational and rotational equivariant models with only marginal compute and memory overhead.

</details>


### [257] [Effects of Parton Pair Correlations on Direct-Photon Yields and Elliptic Flow in Heavy-Ion Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05042)
*Xingjian Lu,Shuzhe Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨发射部分子之间的连通关联如何同时影响重离子碰撞中的直接光子产额与椭圆流 $v_2$，提出部分子关联可作为一种同时解释两观测量的一致机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接光子几乎无再散射地逃逸，携带完整发射历史信息，但现有理论难以同时描述测得的直接光子产额与可观的椭圆流 $v_2$，这是长期存在的挑战。

Method: 利用局域 $2\to2$ 光子产生率依赖于入射部分子联合分布这一事实，引入入射部分子间的连通关联（不改变介质粒子单粒子分布），并将其在 Au+Au 与 Pb+Pb 碰撞的演化介质上做折叠计算。

Result: 关联使光子产额提升至接近 PHENIX 与 ALICE 拟合数据的水平，并在中等 $p_T$ 区间主要提升 $v_2$；拟合的温度依赖可解释该区间净流增强的大部分。

Conclusion: 部分子间关联通过改变热区与冷区的相对贡献，可同时提升直接光子产额与中等 $p_T$ 的 $v_2$，为解释"光子产额-$v_2$ 之谜"提供新思路。

Abstract: Direct photons escape heavy-ion collisions with little rescattering, carrying information from the full emission history. Yet describing the measured direct-photon yield and the sizable elliptic flow $v_2$ together remains a long-standing challenge. Here we explore how correlations between emitting partons may affect both observables. The local $2\to2$ photon rate depends on the joint distribution of incoming partons, so a connected correlation between them can change photon emission without changing the one-particle distribution of medium particles. Once folded over the evolving medium in Au+Au and Pb+Pb collisions, the correlation raises the photon yield toward the fitted PHENIX and ALICE data and increases $v_2$ mainly at intermediate $p_T$. The fitted temperature dependence accounts for most of the net flow increase in this region, suggesting that the correlation changes the relative contributions between hotter and cooler regions.

</details>


### [258] [Dual-VaDE: Efficient and Robust Prototype Learning via Variational Dual-Domain Mixture Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05099)
*Wugeng Zheng,Song Wang,Jian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Dual-VaDE是一个无监督生成框架，通过引入空间-傅里叶双域高斯混合先验，从无标签医学图像中学习可解释的解剖原型，并推导闭式变分下界、以交替EM端到端训练，在OASIS-3脑MRI上显著提升原型质量与聚类分离度且推理开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有变分方法仅依赖单一空间隐变量，导致全局几何与局部纹理纠缠、无法刻画跨域依赖；同时原型学习常依赖启发式两阶段聚类，训练不稳定、对种子敏感。

Method: 提出Dual-VaDE生成式框架：采用双域高斯混合先验，显式耦合空间解剖表示与保相位傅里叶频谱表示；推导精确闭式变分下界，将该联合先验融合进解码器；用交替EM形式端到端优化整个网络，避免两阶段聚类。

Result: 在OASIS-3脑MRI上，生成的解剖原型更清晰、更合理；聚类分离度较仅空间基线最高提升94%；随机种子间方差明显降低；推理开销可忽略（每张图像0.28 ms）。

Conclusion: 双域先验与闭式EM式联合优化能有效解耦并融合空间—频谱信息，在提升可解释原型质量、聚类性能与训练稳定性的同时保持极低推理成本。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Dual-VaDE, an unsupervised generative framework for learning interpretable anatomical prototypes from unlabeled medical images. In contrast to previous variational methods that rely on a single spatial latent code, which often entangles global geometry with local textures and fails to capture their cross-domain dependencies, Dual-VaDE introduces a dual-domain Gaussian mixture prior that explicitly couples spatial anatomy with phase-preserving Fourier spectral representations. We derive an exact, closed-form variational lower bound that fuses this joint prior into the decoder and optimizes the entire network end-to-end via an alternating EM formulation, avoiding heuristic two-stage clustering while ensuring superior training stability and robustness. Evaluated on OASIS-3 brain MRI, Dual-VaDE delivers substantially sharper and more plausible prototypes, achieves superior cluster separation with improvements of up to 94% over spatial-only baselines, exhibits markedly reduced variance across random seeds, and incurs negligible inference overhead (0.28 ms per image).

</details>


### [259] [Molecular Prior Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05103)
*Pawel Gomoluch,Anthony Bradley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于表格基础模型（PFN）的分子-靶标亲和力预测新方法：将亲和力建模转化为"单次前向传播内由带标签支持集预测查询分子标签"的上下文学习任务，在数千个现有亲和力数据集上训练，在小支持集下匹配FS-Mol回归任务SOTA且推理更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物-靶标亲和力预测是药物发现的核心问题，但湿实验成本高导致数据集规模很小（数十到数百个分子）。不同靶标与化学系列之间泛化困难，现有亲和力数据集通常无法迁移到新项目，因此亟需能在极小数据条件下快速适配新靶标的建模方式。

Method: 借鉴先验数据拟合网络（PFN）等表格基础模型的思想，用transformer对"支持集（带标签分子）+查询分子"整体建模，在一次前向传播中直接输出查询分子的亲和力预测，无需任务特定训练。模型在数千个现有亲和力数据集上预训练，学习跨靶标、跨化学系列、跨数据集规模的分子活性通用模式。并设计消融对比：将架构简化为元学习特征空间中的核回归，以检验预测性能是否仅来自简单分子相似性。

Result: 在最小支持集设置下，模型在FS-Mol基准回归变体上达到SOTA水平，且由于无需任务级训练而推理速度快于现有方法；与"元学习特征空间核回归"简化变体的对比表明，transformer的预测性能只能部分由简单分子相似性解释。

Conclusion: 上下文学习式（单次前向传播）的亲和力建模是利用大量异构小数据集实现跨靶标泛化的有效途径，其增益不能仅归因于相似性度量，暗示模型学到了超越简单相似性的迁移表征。

Abstract: Predicting affinity between small molecules and their protein targets remains one of the central problems in drug discovery. Due to the substantial cost of wet-lab experiments, machine learning models of drug-target affinity are usually trained on relatively small datasets, ranging from tens to hundreds of molecules. Given the inherent difficulty of generalization between different targets and chemical series, existing datasets of protein-ligand affinities are typically not used for new projects. At the same time, tabular foundation models, including Prior-data Fitted Networks, have recently shown how transformers can be used to solve machine learning problems in a single forward pass of the network, after being trained over a large corpus of, typically, synthetic datasets. Inspired by that line of work, we propose a novel affinity modelling approach, in which the labels of the query molecules are predicted from the labelled support molecules in a single forward pass of a transformer model. The model is trained on thousands of existing affinity datasets, enabling it to capture the general patterns of molecular activity across different targets, chemical series and dataset sizes. For the smallest support sets, our model matches the state of the art on the regression variant of the FS-Mol benchmark, while being faster than existing methods, thanks to the lack of task-specific training. A comparison against a reduced variant of our architecture, which amounts to kernel regression in a meta-learned feature space, shows that the predictive performance of the transformer model is only partially explained by simple molecular similarity.

</details>


### [260] [Zero-Gated Cross-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01116)
*Basavaraj R M,Manav Verma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出零初始化门控交叉注意力桥接模块IGCA，在冻结骨干模型上注入非文本模态时保持初始化处的严格恒等，实现渐进多模态条件化，并在三个冻结骨干上验证了显著的低损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非文本模态注入冻结语言模型常引发早期优化不稳定，导致难以区分架构收益与瞬态训练动态，因此需要一个在初始化时对预训练模型行为完全无扰动的注入机制。

Method: 设计零初始化的交叉注意力桥接（IGCA），通过tanh门控实现渐进式多模态条件化；集成于Falcon-40B、open_llama_30b、Bloom-7b1的六个解码器层，训练可训练适配器参数约404亿。

Result: 初始化时IGCA为严格代数no-op（最大输出偏差0.00e+00）；Falcon-40B上59,500步训练损失由3.9403降至0.0007；留出评估中最佳gate-on检查点损失2.2201，对比gate-off基线4.3469，降低48.9%（2.1268 nats），平均留出损失由4.3423降至2.2498，平均门幅|tanh(α)|=0.0713。

Conclusion: IGCA以零初始化保证初始行为无损，消除早期优化不稳定，使多模态条件化收益可与瞬态训练动态分离，并显著降低留出损失。

Abstract: Injecting non-text modalities into frozen language models often introduces early optimization instability, making it difficult to separate architectural gains from transient training dynamics. We introduce Inductively Gated Cross-Attention (IGCA), a zero-initialized cross-attention bridge that preserves pretrained model behavior at initialization while enabling gradual multimodal conditioning. Integrated across six decoder layers of frozen Falcon-40B, open_llama_30b, and Bloom-7b1 backbones (with 40,404,961,288 trainable adapter parameters used during training), IGCA acts as an exact algebraic no-op at initialization with a maximum output discrepancy of 0.00e+00. Over 59,500 training steps on Falcon-40B, the training loss decreases from 3.9403 to 0.0007 (with final ten step losses of [0.0001, 0.0003, 0.0005, 0.0002, 0.0005, 0.0011, 0.0005, 0.0006, 0.0005, 0.0007]). On held-out evaluation, the best gate-on checkpoint achieves a loss of 2.2201 compared to 4.3469 for the gate-off baseline -- a 48.9% reduction (2.1268 nats) -- while average held-out loss improves from 4.3423 to 2.2498 (1.9075 nats) with a mean gate magnitude of $\vert{}\tanh(α)\vert{} = 0.0713$.

</details>


### [261] [Beyond Multi-Index Models: Learning Intersections of Degree-2 PTFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05114)
*Ilias Diakonikolas,Daniel M. Kane,Mingchen Ma,Lisheng Ren,Trung Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文给出在 Gaussian 分布下 PAC 学习 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 上 $k$ 个二次多项式阈值函数交集的算法，运行与采样复杂度为 $d^{O(k)}2^{O_k(\log^3(1/\varepsilon))}$，首次实现与精度参数无关的维度指数（即便 $k=2$），并给出 SQ 下界证明 $d^{\Omega(k)}$ 依赖不可避免。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项式回归方法需要 $d^{O(k^2/\varepsilon^2)}$ 时间，维度指数随 $1/\varepsilon^2$ 增长；作者希望消除对精度参数的维度指数依赖，并在理论上刻画该问题维度复杂度的本质下界。

Method: 将输入提升到多项式特征空间，把目标转化为 $k$ 个半空间的交集，从而用前两阶带标签矩做子空间恢复。关键工具包括：(1) Gaussian 比较定理，说明对足够正则的定义二次型，恢复所需带标签矩近似保持；(2) 新结构定理，证明任何具有显著重采样影响的方向都可由 $O(k)$ 阶矩检测，据此用自适应限制得到 $d^{O(k)}2^{\mathrm{poly}_k(1/\varepsilon)}$ 时间学习器；(3) 两阶段降维：先识别包含全部大影响方向的子空间，再借助广义正则性与 Gaussian 比较，通过新的基于导数的矩算子恢复剩余相关二次特征，最后结合鲁棒 hinge-loss 最小化得到拟多项式时间算法。

Result: PAC 学习误差 $\varepsilon$ 的运行与采样复杂度为 $d^{O(k)}2^{O_k(\log^3(1/\varepsilon))}$，优于多项式回归的 $d^{O(k^2/\varepsilon^2)}$；维度指数与 $\varepsilon$ 无关，对 $k=2$ 亦属首次。SQ 下界表明 $d^{\Omega(k)}$ 的复杂度依赖不可规避。

Conclusion: 论文建立了二次 PTF 交集在 Gaussian 分布下学习的新复杂度上界与匹配的 SQ 下界，核心贡献是免自适应的两阶段降维与导数型矩算子，将精度依赖从指数降至拟多项式的对数三次方。

Abstract: We give an algorithm for PAC learning intersections of $k$ degree-2 polynomial threshold functions over $R^d$ under a Gaussian distribution. Our algorithm achieves classification error $ε$ using $d^{O(k)}2^{O_k(\log^3(1/ε))}$ time and samples, improving over the $d^{O(k^2/ε^2)}$ running time of the polynomial regression approach. To our knowledge, our result is the first to obtain a dimension exponent independent of the accuracy parameter, even for $k=2$. We complement our algorithmic result with an SQ lower bound showing that the complexity dependence of $d^{Ω(k)}$ is unavoidable.
  Lifting the input to polynomial features turns the target into an intersection of $k$ halfspaces. This suggests using the first two labeled moments for subspace recovery. In the lifted space, however, these moments can reveal nothing. Our starting point is a Gaussian comparison theorem showing that, for sufficiently regular defining quadratics, the labeled moments needed for subspace recovery are approximately preserved. To handle arbitrary defining quadratics, we establish a new structural result showing that every direction with significant resampling influence can be detected through degree-$O(k)$ moments. These results yield an initial $d^{O(k)}2^{\mathrm{poly}_k(1/ε)}$-time learner using adaptive restrictions.
  To improve the exponential dependence on $1/ε$, we develop a two-stage dimension-reduction approach that avoids the costly adaptive partitioning. After identifying a subspace containing all directions with large influence, we use generalized regularity and Gaussian comparison to recover the remaining relevant quadratic features through a new derivative-based moment operator. The recovered features support an intersection close to the target. Combining them with robust hinge-loss minimization gives our quasipolynomial-time learner.

</details>


### [262] [Towards an Optimal Control Foundation of Early Exits - Turnpikes and ResNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05120)
*Jens Püttschneider,Simon Heilig,Asja Fischer,Timm Faulwasser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文用最优控制理论分析输出层与中间层联合优化的ResNet，证明其展现"转辙线(turnpike)"现象，由此解释早退网络内部表示的相似性及"过思考(overthinking)"现象，并从理论上说明基于softmax熵/最大概率的早退准则为何有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为兼顾效率与精度，已有工作将隐藏层中间输出同时用作训练时的数据依赖正则与推理时的早退出口，但缺乏对"输出层损失与中间层损失联合优化"这一训练范式的理论刻画，也缺乏对早退准则（熵、最大概率）成功原因的解释。

Method: 将ResNet的前向传播与联合训练建模为连续时间最优控制问题，对输出层及中间层损失之和的联合优化进行解析；证明联合训练下出现转辙线现象，建立层数需求的上界与内部表示相似性的性质，并将转辙线与过思考现象及softmax概率型早退准则（熵、最大概率）建立理论联系；随后通过多组实验验证转辙线存在性及其对早退性能的影响。

Result: 理论上证明：达到给定损失水平所需的层数存在与总深度无关的上界；不同深度早退网络的内部表示具有相似性；并为基于熵与最大概率的早退判据的有效性提供理论依据。实验上验证了转辙线的存在，以及不同深度ResNet早退性能的相似性。

Conclusion: 联合优化训练使ResNet呈现转辙线特性，从而解释了早退机制的可行性与内部表示相似性，并为过思考现象和softmax型早退准则提供了理论支撑；该分析为设计高效早退网络与训练策略提供了理论指导。

Abstract: The feed-forward propagation through neural networks is crucial for training and inference. To trade off efficiency and accuracy, it has been proposed to consider intermediate outputs of hidden states as data-dependent regularization during training and, ultimately, as early-exits during inference. In this work, we analyze the joint optimization of loss functions computed for the output layer and intermediate layers using optimal control theory. We prove that ResNets trained in this joint fashion exhibit the so-called turnpike phenomenon, this implies that the number of layers required to reach a prescribed loss level is bounded from above independently of the overall network depth. This induces a similarity property across the internal representations of early-exiting networks of varying depths. We connect the turnpike to the overthinking phenomenon, and provide theoretical insights into the success of early-exit criteria derived from softmax probabilities, specifically entropy and max-probability. In a range of experiments, we demonstrate the existence and effect of turnpikes and the resulting similarity in the early-exit performance for ResNets of varying depths.

</details>


### [263] [Do Ray-Traced Cities Make a Better Channel Prior for an Unseen Site? A Pre-Registered Study in Simulation, and a Test on Measured Channels that Reverses It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05128)
*Krish Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文检验"跨城市池化的射线追踪信道先验"能否优于3GPP CDL先验服务于未测站点，并考察学习型估计器的作用。仿真中池化先验距站点训练估计器约0.6 dB，但实测（DICHASUS等）结果反转：池化射线追踪先验比CDL差3.2–4.1 dB，学习模型增益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新部署基站的估计器通常以3GPP随机信道模型（CDL）初始化，但该类模型不描述任何具体街道。作者要回答两个问题：(1) 由多城市射线追踪汇总（pooled）得到的信道先验，对未见站点是否优于3GPP CDL；(2) 学习型估计器能否带来实质差异。研究动机在于验证"复杂先验+学习模型必然更优"这一普遍假设。

Method: 基于Sionna RT在14个城市场景生成信道，采用协方差先验的LMMSE估计，并做留一城市（leave-one-city-out）评估；每个主实验前预登记pass/fail判定阈值（pre-registered）。对比基线为3GPP CDL。学习模型用于估计与预测（含CNN预测器）。随后在DICHASUS室外实测数据（1.272 GHz，同一建筑上三个2x8阵列）以及两个额外实测环境（工厂大厅、小型实验室）做验证；并以可微射线追踪标定的走廊模型作为先验进行测试。

Result: 仿真中池化先验与站点训练估计器（无站点实测）相差约0.6 dB，相当于CDL在50次测量后达到的水平；学习模型增益很小，估计上最多0.65 dB，信道预测中简单经典方法在2 ms提前下比CNN预测器好1.8–12 dB。实测（DICHASUS）结果反转：池化射线追踪先验比CDL差3.2–4.1 dB，说明仿真信道"过于干净"；即使按50次站点测量调校散射水平，先验仍落后CDL约2 dB。后续预登记的两个实测环境中，通用CDL先验距站点训练估计器2.1–4.7 dB（工厂大厅不逊于街道，小型实验室最差）。走廊标定模型能复现时延扩展，但在未测区域作为先验于全阵列上比CDL差4.5 dB。

Conclusion: 跨城市池化的射线追踪先验并未在实测中优于通用3GPP CDL，学习型估计器的收益也有限，说明通用先验在实际信道条件下更稳健；仿真结论因信道过"干净"而不具外推性。需注意所有实测结果均来自单一sounder，结论的泛化性受限。

Abstract: Channel estimators at a newly deployed base station usually start from 3GPP stochastic channel models, which describe no particular street. We ask whether a channel prior pooled from many ray-traced cities serves an unseen site better than the 3GPP CDL models, and whether learned estimators make the difference. With Sionna RT on 14 city scenes, LMMSE estimation from covariance priors, leave-one-city-out evaluation, and pass/fail bars written down before each main experiment, we find in simulation that the pooled prior sits about 0.6 dB from a site-trained estimator with no site measurements, about what CDL reaches after 50 measurements. Learned models added little: at most 0.65 dB in estimation, and in channel prediction a simple classical method beat CNN predictors by 1.8-12 dB from 2 ms ahead. On measured channels from the DICHASUS outdoor dataset (1.272 GHz, three 2x8 arrays on one building) the result reversed: the pooled ray-traced prior was 3.2-4.1 dB worse than CDL. The simulated channels were too clean; with users at the real distances, even a scattering level tuned from 50 site measurements left the prior about 2 dB behind CDL. A pre-registered follow-on on two more measured environments found the generic CDL prior 2.1-4.7 dB from a site-trained one: no further in a factory hall than on the street, furthest in a small lab room. Finally, a hallway model calibrated to measurements by differentiable ray tracing reproduced delay spread, but as a prior in an unmeasured part of the hallway it was 4.5 dB worse than CDL on the full array. All measured results come from one sounder.

</details>


### [264] [PAC Learning with Multiple Correct Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05133)
*Steve Hanneke,Farnam Mansouri,Alireza F. Pour,Amirreza Shaeiri,Kun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多标签PAC学习在三种监督形式（完整标签集、bandit反馈、示范单标签）下的可学习性差异，核心发现是示范反馈远难于完整集甚至bandit反馈，需用假设交的维度刻画其可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实学习场景中示例可能对应多个正确标签，且监督信号强度不同（完整集/仅判定正确/示范一个正确标签）。已有工作未系统比较这些反馈模式的统计与组合复杂度差异。

Method: 引入广义Natarajan维刻画有限标签空间下完整集与bandit反馈的学习性（realizable与agnostic）；证明示范可学习当且仅当任意ℓ+1个假设的交可由完整集学习；对可数标签空间提出packed维（ℓ+1个互不相交、无公共标签的假设组）刻画完整集学习性；并分析去掉标签数上界ℓ后的情形。

Result: 示范反馈严格难于完整集与bandit反馈：即使在3个标签下，可由单个完整集样本或单个bandit答案学习的类也可能不可由示范学习；条件满足时示范样本复杂度为Õ(ε^{-ℓ})且指数ℓ一般不可改进。可数标签下packed维刻画完整集学习；示范的agnostic学习无法由刻画realizable学习的交类维度决定。无ℓ上界时，不存在由有限子类决定的维度可刻画学习性。

Conclusion: 监督信号的形式对多标签PAC学习具有本质影响，示范反馈在组合条件与样本复杂度上均显著劣于完整集和bandit反馈；ℓ上界是维度刻画存在性的必要条件。

Abstract: We study PAC learning when each example comes with a set of at most $\ell$ correct labels and a prediction is correct if it belongs to this set. Training may reveal the full set, only whether a proposed label is correct (bandit feedback), or one correct label chosen by a demonstrator (emonstrations).
  Our main finding is that demonstrations can be much harder than full sets, and even than bandit feedback, both in which classes can be learned and in how many examples are needed. For finite label spaces, a generalized Natarajan dimension characterizes learnability from full sets and from bandit feedback, in both the realizable and the agnostic setting. From demonstrations, however, a class is realizably learnable if and only if the intersections of at most $\ell+1$ of its hypotheses are learnable from full sets. Already with three labels, this condition can fail for a class learnable from a single full-set example or even a single bandit answer. When the condition holds, we show that $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-\ell})$ demonstrations suffice, and the exponent $\ell$ cannot be improved in general, even for classes learnable from $\widetilde O(1/\varepsilon)$ full sets or bandit answers.
  For countable label spaces and $\ell \geq 2$, our packed dimension, which asks for many disjoint groups of at most $\ell+1$ hypotheses with no common label, characterizes full-set learnability, and demonstrations still reduce to intersections. Agnostic learning from demonstrations, already for finite label spaces, is not determined by the dimensions of the intersection class that characterize realizable learning.
  Finally, we show that the uniform bound $\ell$ on the number of correct labels is essential. Without this bound, it is impossible to characterize learnability by any dimension determined by finite subclasses.

</details>


### [265] [Beyond Foundation Models: PFN Priors Improve Tabular Hyperparameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05141)
*Artem Chistyakov,Renat Sergazinov,Artem Babenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PriorTune 利用已发布的 PFN 合成先验在离线阶段对传统表格模型的超参数配置池进行元调优，在线阶段仅拟合胜率最高的子集配置，从而在不增加额外成本的前提下提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成先验此前仅用于预训练表格基础模型；作者质疑同一先验能否用于改进从零开始训练的传统表格模型。

Method: 离线阶段：对给定模型，在由已发布 PFN 先验采样的数据集上拟合一组配置池，并以对随机基线的胜率为准则贪心选择子集。在线阶段：新数据集只拟合被选中的配置，因此增益不带来额外成本。

Result: 实证发现配置数与合成数据集数量的扩展对最终子集性能具有等效的缩放效应，并给出理论解释；在完整 BeyondArena 基准上，配备 TabPrep 特征的 LightGBM 结合该配置排名高于 TabPFN-3 与 RealMLP，且推理效率高得多。

Conclusion: 现有 PFN 先验可作为面向非基础表格模型超参数选择的可扩展元训练数据来源。

Abstract: Synthetic priors have so far served one purpose: pretraining tabular foundation models. We ask whether the same priors can improve conventional tabular models, which are trained from scratch on each dataset. We propose PriorTune, a method that meta-tunes hyperparameter configurations on synthetic tasks alone. Offline, for a given model, we fit a pool of configurations on datasets sampled from a published PFN prior and greedily select the subset with the highest winrate over the random baseline. Online, a new dataset fits only the selected configurations, so the gain comes at no extra cost. Empirically, we observe that scaling both the number of configurations and synthetic datasets has an equal scaling effect on the performance of the final subset of configurations, which we also explain theoretically. On the full BeyondArena benchmark, LightGBM with TabPrep features with our configurations ranks above TabPFN-3 and RealMLP, while being much more efficient at inference. Overall, our results establish existing PFN priors as a scalable source of meta-training data for hyperparameter selection for non-foundational tabular models.

</details>


### [266] [Macrocanonical Generator Networks: Data-Efficient Neural Surrogates for Amortized Physics Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05157)
*Niall Jeffrey,Benjamin D. Wandelt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"宏正则生成网络"(macrocanonical generator networks)，通过匹配目标过程的多尺度统计量，学习快速、数据高效的神经代理模型，用于平稳物理过程的摊销模拟；理论证明其训练等价于样本空间中的预条件梯度下降，并保持对称性与局部熵，缓解模式坍缩；在宇宙学与流体模拟中仅用极少样本即可生成逼真结果，速度比参考模拟器快10^6倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学模拟计算代价高昂，限制了其在基于模拟的推断、不确定性量化和决策中的应用。已有的微正则最大熵合成(microcanonical maximum-entropy synthesis)方法通过对每个样本做内循环优化来匹配特征统计量，代价高、无法摊销；因此需要一种既能保持真实样本间涨落、又能一次性训练后快速采样的数据高效神经代理框架。

Method: 1) 训练神经生成器在期望意义上满足与微正则方法相同的特征统计量约束，从而保留物理真实的样本间涨落；2) 理论证明梯度下降训练在样本空间诱导出预条件梯度下降动力学，继承Bruna与Mallat的微正则梯度下降的对称性保持性质，将逐样本内循环优化摊销为单次训练、每样本一次前向传播；3) 采用近恒等初始化的残差生成器参数化并配合小学习率，在优化早期局部保持熵、缓解模式坍缩。

Result: 在宇宙学和流体模拟实验中，宏正则生成器能从极小的训练集(某些情况下仅单样本)恢复逼真样本；每样本生成速度比参考模拟器快超过10^6倍，比微正则梯度下降快超过10^3倍；训练好的生成器可复用于下游预训练(如基于模拟的推断)。

Conclusion: 宏正则生成网络为平稳物理过程提供了数据高效、高速的摊销模拟途径，并可复用于多保真度科学机器学习工作流(如仿真推断预训练)，是一种面向科学计算的实用神经代理框架。

Abstract: Many scientific simulations are computationally expensive, limiting their use in simulation-based inference, uncertainty quantification, and decision making. We introduce macrocanonical generator networks, a framework for learning fast, data-efficient neural surrogates for amortized simulation of stationary physical processes by matching multiscale statistics of a target process. Inspired by microcanonical maximum-entropy synthesis, which matches feature statistics per sample by inner-loop optimization, our approach instead trains a neural generator to satisfy the same constraints in expectation, preserving physically realistic sample-to-sample fluctuations. We prove that gradient-descent training induces preconditioned gradient-descent dynamics in sample space that inherit the symmetry-preservation properties of the microcanonical gradient descent of Bruna and Mallat, amortizing its per-sample inner-loop optimization into a single training run after which every sample is generated in one forward pass. We further show that residual generator parameterizations initialized near the identity, trained with a small learning rate, preserve entropy locally during early optimization and mitigate mode collapse. Experiments on cosmology and fluid simulations show that macrocanonical generators recover realistic samples from extremely small training sets, in some cases a single training example, while generating new realizations more than $10^6$ times faster per sample than the reference simulator and more than $10^3$ times faster than microcanonical gradient descent. The trained generators can be reused for downstream pretraining, including simulation-based inference, providing a data-efficient route to multi-fidelity scientific machine learning workflows.

</details>


### [267] [Basin2Vec: Learning Basin-Centric Embeddings for Scalable Hydrological Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05164)
*Muhammad Talha,Xiaofeng Liu,Yuan Yang,Pang-Ning Tan,A. Pouyan Nejadhashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自监督流域表征学习框架Basin2Vec，无需观测径流即可从水文气象与地貌数据中学习紧凑流域表征，在时间泛化与零样本空间迁移上优于人工属性和通用地球观测嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨时间可靠径流预测及无资料流域预测需要能刻画水文相关流域特征的表征。现有方法要么依赖固定的人工构造属性，要么使用通用的地球观测嵌入，均未针对流域结构进行定制，因而迁移能力受限。

Method: Basin2Vec为自监督框架，在不使用实测径流的前提下，从水文气象与地貌（physiographic）数据中学习紧凑的流域表征；表征学习围绕流域尺度（basin scale）以及空间与时间上的流域间关系进行组织。

Result: 在多种预报架构下，Basin2Vec在时间泛化与零样本空间迁移方面一致优于预定义属性和通用地理空间嵌入；时间维度上平均相对提升最高达8.3%，在未见流域上的中位预测性能达最强基线的3.5倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明以流域为中心（basin-centric）的预训练对可迁移水文预测有效；代码已开源。

Abstract: Reliable streamflow prediction across time and in ungauged basins requires representations that capture hydrologically relevant basin characteristics. Existing methods rely on either fixed handcrafted attributes or general-purpose Earth observation embeddings not tailored to basin structure. We introduce \textbf{Basin2Vec}, a self-supervised framework that learns compact basin representations from hydroclimatic and physiographic data without observed streamflow. Basin2Vec organizes representation learning around basin scale and relationships across space and time. Across multiple forecasting architectures, it consistently outperforms predefined attributes and general-purpose geospatial embeddings in temporal generalization and zero-shot spatial transfer. Basin2Vec provides average relative gains of up to 8.3% temporally and achieves 3.5x the median predictive performance of the strongest baselines on unseen basins. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of basin-centric pretraining for transferable hydrologic prediction. Code is available at https://github.com/Muhammad-Talha-MT/basin2vec

</details>


### [268] [Provably Learning Mixtures of Linear Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05184)
*Adrian Candocia,Aamod Varma,Mark Davenport*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文给出门控线性专家 MoE 在梯度 EM 联合训练下，首个全局收敛保证：在对数级过参数化与门中心分离条件下，从随机初始化门、最小二乘初始化专家出发，多项式迭代内收敛到任意精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 已被广泛部署，但基于梯度的联合训练缺乏收敛理论：目标非凸，即使简单混合模型也会让一阶方法陷入虚假局部最优。作者希望为门与专家的联合训练建立可证的全局收敛性。

Method: 研究一个典型门控混合模型：线性专家 + 由输入空间高斯簇诱导的门，对似然做梯度 EM。相对真实专家数 m 采用对数级过参数化，门随机初始化、专家用最小二乘初始化；通过把损失解耦为逐簇子问题，为耦合动力学建立梯度支配（gradient-dominance）不等式。

Result: 在门中心分离条件下，梯度 EM 从上述初始化出发，在多项式次迭代内收敛到任意给定精度，给出首个此类模型的全局收敛保证。实验验证了预测的 m log m 相变；并发现实际中不需要最小二乘热启动——随机初始化专家表现几乎一样好；混合权重不加约束会使路由集中到真实专家数，而负载均衡惩罚会使路由分散，拟合模型可剪枝到原宽度的很小比例而不损失精度。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为门控 MoE 的联合梯度训练提供全局收敛理论，并揭示过参数化阈值、初始化要求与混合权重正则化对路由集中度及可剪枝性的实际影响。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are widely deployed in modern machine learning. However, few convergence guarantees exist for gradient-based training of MoEs: the objective is nonconvex, and even simple mixtures trap first-order methods in spurious local optima. We analyze a canonical gated mixture featuring linear experts and a gate induced by Gaussian clusters in the input space, and give, to our knowledge, the first global convergence guarantee for jointly training the gate and the experts of such a model by gradient EM on its likelihood. Given a logarithmic overparameterization relative to the true number of experts $m$, gradient EM converges from randomly initialized gates and least-squares-initialized experts to any prescribed accuracy in polynomially many iterations, under a separation condition on the gate centers. Our analysis establishes a gradient-dominance inequality for the coupled dynamics by decoupling the loss into per-cluster subproblems. Empirical results validate the predicted $m\log m$ phase transition and suggest that the least-squares warm start required by our analysis is not needed in practice: randomly initialized experts perform nearly as well. We also find that leaving the mixing weights unconstrained concentrates routing onto the true number of experts, whereas load-balancing penalties spread it, so that the fitted model can be pruned to a fraction of its width without loss of accuracy.

</details>


### [269] [Stepsize modulation for cross entropy from local quadratic optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05191)
*Bruno Bauwens,Viacheslav Krivodub,Bingyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两种利用额外前向传播估计小批量损失导数来扰动学习率调度的方法，在CIFAR上训练ResNet可更快降低损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习率调度（schedule）是预先给定的，未根据训练过程中的实际损失曲率自适应调整；作者希望用廉价的计算估计合适的步长扰动。

Method: 在交叉熵损失SGD中做额外一次前向传播估计小批量损失的各阶导数：方法一将softmax值线性化，最小化平方误差 ½||y−q−ηq'||_F² 求η；方法二在Hessian外近似下对损失做二次Taylor展开，最小化 L(0)+ηL'(0)+½η²L''(0)，其中L'、L''由logits导数（结合损失Hessian外近似）计算。

Result: 两种方法给出的η值非常接近；将估计的η用于轻微增减现有调度的学习率，在CIFAR10/CIFAR100训练ResNet时损失下降更快、单调性更好；CIFAR10测试精度略有提升，CIFAR100测试精度持平。

Conclusion: 基于一次额外前向传播的导数估计可有效扰动学习率调度，加速收敛并改善训练单调性，但对测试精度的收益有限且依数据集而异。

Abstract: Two methods are presented that perturb a given learning rate schedule in stochastic gradient descent with cross entropy loss.
  Both methods use an extra forward pass to estimate various derivatives of the minibatch loss.
  The first method selects a value $η$ by linearising the softmax values and minimizing the squared error $\tfrac 1 2 ||y - q - ηq'||_F^2$, where $q$ is the matrix representing the softmax values of the minibatch and $y$ is the matrix with one-hot encodings of the labels. The second method considers a quadratic Taylor approximation of the minibatch loss under the outer approximation of the Hessian. Thus, it minimizes $L(0) + ηL'(0) + \tfrac 1 2 η^2 L''(0)$. Here, the derivatives $L'$ and $L''$ are computed from the derivatives of the logits (using the outer approximation of the Hessian of the loss).
  We observe that both methods give very similar values of $η$.
  Finally, these estimated $η$ are used to slightly increase or decrease the learning rate of an existing schedule. The method is evaluated on Cifar10 and Cifar100 datasets for training Resnets, where they decrease the loss faster than the schedule that is perturbed and increase monotonicity. For Cifar10 it also leads to a small improvement of test accuracy, but in Cifar100 test accuracy remains the same.

</details>


### [270] [Ensembles of Random Partition-Based Models under Covariate Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05195)
*Mary Versa Clemens-Sewall,Eliza O'Reilly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将协变量偏移严重程度的理论从单模型推广到集成模型，提出新的偏移严重程度差异度量，并证明足够大的集成规模可在协变量偏移下达到极小极大最优风险收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验证据显示，无论在有无下游自适应的情况下，集成模型对协变量偏移的鲁棒性普遍优于单模型，但缺乏理论解释。作者希望为这一经验趋势提供理论依据，并刻画集成规模与偏移严重程度之间的关系。

Method: 将单模型的协变量偏移严重程度理论扩展到集成模型；引入新的协变量偏移严重程度差异度量（discrepancy measures），用于控制基于划分的核估计器（partition-based kernel estimators）的风险；在此基础上分析集成规模对风险的影响。

Result: 在集成规模足够大时，基于划分的核估计器在协变量偏移下可达到极小极大最优的风险收敛速率；结果表明协变量偏移越严重，所需的集成规模越大。

Conclusion: 集成模型对协变量偏移的鲁棒性具有理论支撑；集成规模应随偏移严重程度增加而增大，为模型设计者选择集成规模提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Distribution shifts, including covariate shifts, are ubiquitous in real-world applications of machine learning. Empirical evidence suggests that, across a variety of models with and without downstream adaptation, ensembles tend to be more robust to covariate shift than single models. We extend the theory of covariate shift severity from single models to ensembles, providing theoretical justification for the empirical trends. We introduce new discrepancy measures of covariate shift severity to control the risk of partition-based kernel estimators. We then show that with sufficient ensemble size, these models can achieve minimax-optimal risk convergence rates under covariate shift. Our results suggest that modelers can expect to need larger ensemble sizes for more severe covariate shifts.

</details>


### [271] [Empirical Bayes Flow Matching for Continuous Cryo-EM Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05202)
*Daniel Aibinder,Azmi Haider,Dan Rosenbaum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Empirical Bayes Flow Matching (EB-FM)，在无干净数据、仅有间接含噪观测（如 cryo-EM 二维投影）条件下，用蒙特卡洛随机近似 EM 框架直接从观测中学习流匹配生成先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维潜变量（如三维原子结构）无法直接观测，只能由复杂非线性前向算子产生的间接噪声观测获得；cryo-EM 中连续构象异质性需要刻画原子坐标空间上的连续分布，而现有方法依赖干净数据预训练且难以适配非线性前向模型。

Method: EB-FM 以 Monte Carlo 随机近似 EM 学习 flow matching 先验 p_θ(x)：E 步通过引导式流生成从 p_θ(x|y) 近似后验采样，并用随机近似维持持久潜变量估计；M 步以这些稳定化潜变量为目标更新流模型。该方法无需干净数据预训练，天然支持非线性前向算子。

Result: 在 two-moons 与 MNIST 控制的逆问题上验证；在已知姿态的模拟 cryo-EM 数据上，EB-FM 能在原子坐标空间直接恢复蛋白质构象的连续分布。

Conclusion: EB-FM 将随机近似 EM 与流匹配生成先验结合，为间接观测下的高维连续异质性问题提供了无需干净数据的可行方案，并在模拟 cryo-EM 上展示了恢复原子坐标级连续构象分布的能力。

Abstract: We address the problem of learning generative priors over high-dimensional latent variables from indirect, noisy observations, with a focus on continuous heterogeneity in cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM). In this setting, 3D atomic structures $x \in \mathbb{R}^{3N_a}$ are never directly observed and must be inferred from 2D projection images $y$ generated by a complex, non-linear forward operator. We propose Empirical Bayes Flow Matching (EB-FM), a method that learns a flow matching prior $p_θ(x)$ directly from observations using a Monte Carlo stochastic approximation Expectation-Maximization framework. EB-FM alternates between an E-step that performs approximate posterior sampling from $p_θ(x \mid y)$ via guided flow-based generation while maintaining persistent latent estimates through stochastic approximation, and an M-step that updates the flow model using these stabilized latent variables as training targets. This approach eliminates the need for pretraining on clean data and naturally accommodates non-linear forward models. We validate our method on controlled inverse problems using two-moons and MNIST data, and demonstrate its effectiveness on simulated cryo-EM data with known poses, showing that EB-FM can recover continuous distributions of protein conformations directly in atomic-coordinate space.

</details>


### [272] [Multi-Objective Generative Optimization via Expected Hypervolume Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05207)
*Juan Guevara,Riccardo De Santi,Andreas Krause,Raul Astudillo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 HVFT，用期望超体积的首变分构造自适应奖励，将目标分解为 KL 近端标量奖励子问题，在离散扩散与连续流匹配模型上实现 SOTA 的期望超体积与权衡覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现需要在相互竞争的性质间取得合理权衡（如结合亲和力与可合成性）。预训练生成模型能提出候选设计，但引导其产生高质量且互补的权衡仍困难：推理时搜索单样本成本高，微调依赖固定的逐候选奖励，无法刻画候选之间的互补性。
