Method: HVFT 以样本的期望超体积为目标，并约束与预训练分布的偏离。由于期望超体积不能写成固定逐候选奖励的期望，无法用标准方法直接优化；作者观察到其一阶变分等于候选的期望边际超体积贡献，从而由模型样本得到无偏估计的自适应奖励。再将该目标分解为一系列 KL 近端标量奖励子问题。

Result: 理论上证明了所得分布过程的凹性、相对光滑性与收敛性保证。实验上，在治疗性肽与分子设计任务（覆盖离散扩散与连续流匹配模型）中，HVFT 取得 SOTA 的期望超体积与权衡覆盖。

Conclusion: 单次微调预训练生成器即可实现高质量、覆盖良好的多目标权衡，自适应奖励是处理集合级（非可分解）目标的有效途径。

Abstract: Scientific discovery requires plausible designs spanning useful trade-offs among competing properties: a molecule with strong binding affinity, for example, may be difficult to synthesize. Pretrained generative models, e.g., flow and diffusion generators, provide a foundation for proposing such designs, but steering them toward high-quality, complementary trade-offs remains challenging. Inference-time search incurs high per-sample costs, while fine-tuning typically relies on fixed per-candidate rewards that do not capture how designs complement one another. We introduce Hypervolume Fine-Tuning (HVFT), which adapts a single pretrained generator to maximize the expected hypervolume of its samples while controlling departure from the pretrained distribution. Expected hypervolume jointly rewards candidate quality and collective trade-off coverage. Nonetheless, we show that it cannot be expressed as the expectation of a fixed per-candidate reward and therefore cannot be optimized directly with standard techniques. A key observation of this work is that its first variation corresponds to the expected marginal hypervolume contribution of a candidate, which yields a tractable adaptive reward via an unbiased estimator based on model samples. HVFT uses this reward to decompose the objective into a sequence of KL-proximal scalar-reward subproblems. We establish concavity, relative smoothness, and convergence guarantees for the resulting distributional procedure. Across therapeutic-peptide and molecular design tasks spanning discrete diffusion and continuous flow-matching models, HVFT achieves state-of-the-art expected hypervolume and trade-off coverage.

</details>


### [273] [Privacy for Free in Efficient Agnostic Halfspace Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05208)
*Giorgio Racca,Amartya Sanyal,Vikrant Singhal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次给出计算高效且差分隐私的不可知半空间学习算法，覆盖三种设定，隐私带来的样本复杂度开销与非隐私情形相当，即"免费获得隐私"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不可知学习半空间是学习理论核心问题，但现有算法缺乏在保证差分隐私的同时保持计算效率与样本复杂度最优的方案；本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对三种设定设计计算高效的差分隐私算法：(1)高斯与对数凹边缘分布下的非恰当学习；(2)高斯边缘分布下的恰当学习；(3)高斯边缘分布下的半不可知学习。

Result: 三种设定下隐私导致的样本复杂度增量均与各自非隐私样本复杂度相匹配，实现隐私零额外代价。

Conclusion: 差分隐私可在不牺牲计算效率与样本复杂度的前提下实现不可知半空间学习，表明隐私与效用在该问题族上可兼得。

Abstract: We provide the first computationally efficient, differentially private algorithms for agnostically learning halfspaces. In particular, we study three different settings of agnostic learning: improper learning under Gaussian and log-concave marginals; proper learning under Gaussian marginals; and semi-agnostic learning under Gaussian marginals. In all three aforementioned settings, the added cost in the sample complexity due to privacy matches the respective non-private sample complexity, implying that we get privacy for free.

</details>


### [274] [Fusing Earth observation embeddings and spaceborne terrain with meteorological forcing for flood peak depth prediction from high-water marks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05220)
*Wen Zhou,Shaowen Wang,Upmanu Lall,Venkatesh Merwade,Dingqi Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究用5689条USGS洪痕调查数据训练跨模态融合网络，可在洪峰前6小时以MAE 0.39 m预测洪水峰值水深，2.4倍优于基于DEM的估算，但跨事件泛化仍存挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常态干燥陆地上的洪峰水深决定灾害损失与生命风险，但观测稀疏（仅事后洪痕调查）；由卫星水体范围反推的水深滞后且继承DEM垂直误差；深度学习洪水模型多以水动力模拟而非实测水深训练。作者因此探究：洪峰水深能否从野外观测中学习，并在洪峰到来前依赖星载地形、地理空间基础模型嵌入与前期气象强迫进行预测。

Method: 汇集2011–2025年24个美国州及波多黎各39场事件的5689条USGS洪痕调查；为每点从邻近传感器确定洪峰时刻，配对同址无降雨窗口，并关联Copernicus GLO-30地形、AlphaEarth嵌入与经前置时间掩膜的144小时逐时强迫；采用跨模态融合网络进行监督学习。

Result: 洪峰前6小时预测MAE为0.39 m；同点位"实测水位−DEM"的差值误差为0.93 m，为模型误差的2.4倍，且其依赖实测水位而模型不依赖，说明实测水深监督可隐式校正DEM误差。归因分析中强迫贡献77%的误差下降，地形与嵌入统计等价，合计贡献23%。在11场留出事件上模型仍能区分淹没与无雨条件，但无法解析点位水深（R2=0.03）。

Conclusion: 野外调查水深可作为洪水水深预测的监督信号，并校正基于范围估计无法消除的地形误差；但向未见过风暴的迁移仍是待解问题，现有技巧仅限于所采样的事件与区域。

Abstract: Flood peak depth on normally dry land governs damage and risk to life, but is observed only sparsely, by post-event high-water mark (HWM) surveys, and depth derived from satellite flood extent is produced after the event and inherits the vertical error of the digital elevation model (DEM) it is computed from. Deep-learning flood models have in turn been trained on hydrodynamic simulations rather than on observed depths. We ask whether flood peak depth can be learned from field observations and predicted before the peak from spaceborne terrain, geospatial foundation-model embeddings and antecedent meteorological forcing. We assembled 5689 USGS HWM surveys from 39 events (2011-2025) in 24 U.S. states and Puerto Rico, assigned each a peak time from nearby sensors, paired each with a rain-free window at the same site, and linked them to Copernicus GLO-30 terrain, AlphaEarth embeddings and 144 h of hourly forcing masked to the lead time. A cross-modal fusion network trained on these observations predicts depth at the surveyed marks with a mean absolute error of 0.39 m 6 h ahead of the peak. Supervision by surveyed depth corrects the DEM error implicitly: differencing the measured peak water level with the same DEM gives 0.93 m at the same marks, 2.4 times larger, although it uses the measured water level and the model does not. Forcing accounts for 77% of the attributed reduction in depth error; terrain and the embeddings are statistically equivalent to each other and contribute the remaining 23%. On eleven held-out events the model still separates flooded from rain-free conditions but no longer resolves depth at the marks (R2 = 0.03), so the skill reported here is confined to the events and regions sampled. Field-surveyed depths can thus supervise flood depth prediction and correct terrain error that extent-based estimation cannot, while transfer to unseen storms remains the open problem.

</details>


### [275] [Fairness Metrics based on the AUC in Clinical Predictive AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05222)
*João Matos,Ben Van Calster,Richard D. Riley,Paula Dhiman,Gary S. Collins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文系统剖析了8种基于AUC的公平性度量，指出AUC的不可折叠性使亚组与总体AUC不可直接比较，并通过EuroSCORE II模拟展示各度量行为，最终建议以AUC矩阵为主、谨慎选择公平性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测模型（二分类结局）的公平性评估常以亚组间AUC（c统计量）比较为核心，但AUC是不可折叠（non-collapsible）指标，亚组值与总体值并不像通常假设的那样可直接比较。这使现有基于AUC的公平性度量在使用与解释上存在根本性问题，需要系统梳理。

Method: 考察文献中8种AUC类公平性度量：组内AUC一致性（within-group AUC parity）、跨组AUC（xAUC）一致性、gap-to-overall AUC、equity-scaled AUC、基于最小值的度量、xAUC¹、xAUC⁰、以及xAUC¹或xAUC⁰一致性。对每种度量刻画其估计目标（estimand），将其与总体AUC分解为组内与跨组成分的关系相联系，并进行批判性评价；再基于EuroSCORE II（院内死亡预测模型）开展模拟研究，展示各度量的实际行为。

Result: AUC的不可折叠性导致总体AUC与亚组AUC之间、以及不同亚组的xAUC之间不可直接比较；gap-to-overall AUC、equity-scaled AUC与基于最小值的度量实质上比较了不可比的量，缺乏可操作定义；xAUC¹与xAUC⁰虽可将组内与跨组AUC汇总为单一值，但解释复杂且可能与校准性冲突；组内AUC一致性与xAUC一致性相对更具可解释性。

Conclusion: 建议报告AUC矩阵（含总体AUC及所有组内、跨组AUC成分）并同时给出人群特征。若需公平性度量，推荐报告组内AUC一致性，xAUC一致性可作补充信息但需谨慎（可能与校准冲突）。xAUC¹/xAUC⁰类度量有价值但解释不直观、应慎用。gap-to-总体AUC、equity-scaled AUC与基于最小值的度量不适用于该目的，一般不予推荐。

Abstract: Fairness evaluation of clinical prediction models for binary outcomes often involves fairness metrics that compare the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC, or c-statistic) across subgroups. However, the AUC has been shown to be a non-collapsible metric, meaning that its subgroup specific and overall values are not directly comparable as often implicitly assumed. This raises important questions about how existing AUC-based fairness metrics should be used and interpreted. We examine eight AUC-based fairness metrics proposed in the literature, including: within-group AUC parity; cross-group AUC (xAUC) parity; gap-to-overall AUC; equity-scaled AUC; minimum-based metrics; $\mathrm{xAUC}^1$; $\mathrm{xAUC}^0$; and $\mathrm{xAUC}^1$ or $\mathrm{xAUC}^0$ parity. We characterise their estimands, relate them to the decomposition of overall AUC (into within- and cross-group components), and critically appraise them. We then illustrate their behaviour using simulations based on the EuroSCORE II, a clinical prediction model for in-hospital mortality. We recommend reporting an AUC matrix, containing the overall AUC and all within- and cross-group AUC components, alongside population characteristics. When fairness metrics are needed, we recommend reporting the within-group AUC parity, with cross-group AUC parity providing supplementary information but requiring caution due to its potential conflict with calibration. Metrics involving $\mathrm{xAUC}^1$ and $\mathrm{xAUC}^0$ have merit, summarising within- and cross-group AUCs into single values, but their interpretation is non-trivial, and they should be used with caution. Gap-to-overall AUC, equity-scaled AUC, and minimum-based metrics compare quantities that should not be compared and it is unclear how to operationalise them, making them not fit for purpose and generally not recommended.

</details>


### [276] [Post-Inference Model Chaining: A Problem Statement and a Model-based Bootstrap for Training End-to-End Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05225)
*Lukas Madenach,Timo Bailer,Tobias Eilert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究端到端数字孪生中"逐阶段拟合、推理后串联"的链式预测性质，揭示常用蒙特卡洛前向模拟（MCSim）会导致区间与覆盖率膨胀、均值偏差依赖参数，并提出基于模型的bootstrap予以修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中数字孪生常通过分阶段建模再串联实现，而将完整预测抽样沿链条前向传播的蒙特卡洛方案被广泛使用，但其频率派预测性质（区间覆盖、偏差）缺乏系统检验，存在被误用的风险。

Method: 设定各阶段观测含独立正态误差、潜在阶段均值为确定性的情形；用解析两阶段例子推导渐近性质；对4阶段与8阶段线性—二次单变量链（7/15/30次训练运行）做模拟；用无误差潜在训练预测子作诊断以区分"变量误差"与MCSim本身的影响；提出模型bootstrap：初始链前向拟合均值，bootstrap链在固定中心的bootstrap数据上重拟合并前向均值预测，目标阶段未来误差进入studentized预测根。

Result: 解析与模拟均显示MCSim存在持续的渐近区间与覆盖率膨胀，二次传播下均值偏差依赖待估参数；诊断表明失真主因不是变量误差，而是MCSim方法本身（甚至出现覆盖率低估）。所提bootstrap显著降低相对均值偏差，覆盖率更接近名义90%；样本量30时8阶段bootstrap平均宽度比为1.16–1.42（相对已知参数正态参考），而观测到观测的MCSim为4.11–4.97；补充材料中二元6阶段过程结果类似。

Conclusion: 在测试设定下，基于模型的bootstrap能改善端点预测的偏差与覆盖率，支持将其作为链式数字孪生端点预测的更稳健方案；结论限于所考察的线性—二次结构，需进一步推广验证。

Abstract: End-to-end digital twins can be constructed by fitting separate stage models and connecting them after inference. We investigate the Frequentist prediction properties of a commonly used Monte Carlo simulation scheme that forwards complete predictive draws through such a chain when measured stage outputs contain independent normal errors, but the latent stage means are deterministic. Analytical two-stage examples show persistent asymptotic interval and coverage inflation and, with quadratic propagation, mean bias dependent on the parameters to be estimated. Simulations of four- and eight-stage linear-quadratic univariate chains with 7, 15 or 30 training runs demonstrate pronounced input-dependent distortions. A diagnostic using error-free latent training predictors shows that the effect is mainly not attributable to errors in variables, but to the MCSim approach even showing underestimation of coverage. We propose a model-based bootstrap: an initial chain forwards fitted means, bootstrap chains are refitted on fixed-center bootstrap data while forwarding their mean prediction, and a target-stage future error enters a studentized prediction root. Across the investigated scenarios, relative mean bias is substantially reduced, and coverage is closer to the nominal 90%. At sample size 30, the eight-stage bootstrap has mean width ratios of 1.16-1.42 relative to the known-parameter normal reference, compared with 4.11-4.97 for observed-to-observed MCSim. These results support improved endpoint prediction in the tested setting. Further investigations of the bootstrap scheme in the supplement for a bivariate 6-stage process show similar satisfactory results.

</details>


### [277] [Shrinkage prior for implicit generative models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05241)
*Shreya Sinha-Roy,Christian P. Robert,Ritabrata Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于正则化推断、收缩先验与广义贝叶斯的样本高效训练机制，用于隐式生成模型的稀疏表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式生成模型能建模复杂随机对象，但在大参数维度 p、小样本 n 场景下训练样本效率低；稀疏诱导先验在 p≫n 线性回归中有效，却因似然不可解难以用于隐式生成模型。

Method: 采用基于严格恰当评分规则的评分规则后验，纳入广义贝叶斯框架以绕开不可解似然；再设计 Gibbs 采样内的分块 SGMCMC 机制，实现高维参数空间的高效探索并识别神经隐式生成模型的稀疏表示。

Result: 在 p≫n 线性回归及神经生成模型应用中验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过广义贝叶斯评分规则后验与分块 SGMCMC-Gibbs 采样结合收缩先验，可在不依赖显式似然的情况下实现隐式生成模型的样本高效训练与稀疏化。

Abstract: Implicit generative models based on deep neural networks have revolutionized many scientific fields, from weather research to language generation. They successfully model complex random objects arising in real-world problems. In this work, we propose a sample-efficient training mechanism for these models. Our approach leverages regularized inference with shrinkage priors and generalized Bayes. In the large $p$ (parameter dimension) and small $n$ (sample size) setting, sparsity-inducing priors have been successfully used for linear regression when $p \gg n$. However, their application to implicit generative models remains challenging due to the intractability of the likelihood function. To address this, we adapt a scoring rule posterior based on a strictly proper scoring rule, following the generalized Bayes framework. We then propose a block SGMCMC within Gibbs sampling mechanism. This enables efficient exploration of high-dimensional parameter spaces by identifying a sparse representation of neural implicit generative models. We demonstrate the performance of our proposed method through experiments on $p \gg n$ linear regression and an application of neural generative models.

</details>


### [278] [Logarithmic Regret for Alternating Mirror Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05243)
*Tianlong Nan,Christian Kroer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文正面解决了交替在线镜像下降（AltOMD）能否达到近常数遗憾的开放问题：在紧凑凸策略集的双人零和双线性博弈中，双方外部遗憾之和可达到 $O(\log T)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AltOMD 是零和博弈中简单且数值/理论上优于同步 OMD 的学习规则，已有工作借助修正能量函数分析仅得到 $O(T^{1/3})$ 与 $O(T^{1/5})$ 的遗憾界；此前 AltOMD 是否能达到近常数遗憾始终是未解问题，本文旨在回答该问题。

Method: 在正则化子的温和正则性假设下，对任意具有紧凑凸策略集的双人零和双线性博弈分析交替更新过程，导出双方外部遗憾和的 $O(\log T)$ 界；并验证负熵正则化子满足该假设，从而覆盖交替乘性权重更新（altMWU）。

Result: 证明了 AltOMD 的双方外部遗憾之和为 $O(\log T)$，显著优于此前的 $O(T^{1/3})$、$O(T^{1/5})$；对负熵正则化子假设成立，获得 altMWU 的对数遗憾；并由此导出平均策略的 $O(\log T / T)$ 对偶间隙界。

Conclusion: AltOMD 确实可实现近常数（对数级）遗憾，回答了原有开放问题；对数遗憾进一步给出平均化策略的 $O(\log T/T)$ 对偶间隙收敛率，说明交替更新在零和博弈中具有更优的收敛性质。

Abstract: Alternating online mirror descent (AltOMD) is a simple learning rule for zero-sum games, which improves upon simultaneous OMD numerically and theoretically. Previous work obtained $O(T^{1/3})$ and $O(T^{1/5})$ regret bounds for AltOMD, by leveraging modified-energy-function-based analyses. However, settling whether AltOMD achieves near-constant regret has remained an open question. In this paper we resolve the question of whether AltOMD achieves near-constant regret in the affirmative. In particular, we show an $O(log(T))$ regret bound on the sum of the players' external regrets for any two-player zero-sum bilinear game with compact convex strategy sets, under mild regularity assumptions on the regularizers. We verify that our assumptions hold for the negative entropy regularizer, thereby obtaining logarithmic regret for the alternating multiplicative weights updates algorithm. The regret bound also yields an $O(log(T)/T)$ duality gap bound for the averaged strategies.

</details>


### [279] [Going Deeper With Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05255)
*Afshin Khadangi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DeepMem，一种因果多尺度字节级语言模型架构，将语义压缩与字节精确保真分离，在压缩瓶颈处推理，从而以极少计算量获得更强性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字节级语言模型可规避分词器依赖并实现全覆盖，但在全分辨率下逐字节处理代价高昂，需要一种既有全局覆盖又能降低计算成本的方案。

Method: 采用可学习的可逆子带变换构建短序列层级；通过无损载体与显式细节边带保留字节身份；在编码器上使用快速权重记忆块，在压缩瓶颈处进行迭代共享状态计算；由基于注意力的超分辨率解码器恢复字节分辨率表示。

Result: 在FineWeb-Edu和PG19上优于参数匹配的字节级Transformer；在FLORES评测语言上取得最佳宏平均表现；提升PIQA准确率，并在代码建模、多项选择推理和字节级损坏鲁棒性上保持竞争力；瓶颈细化所需计FLOPs约为等效全分辨率推理的1/7600，同时保持可比的评测吞吐。

Conclusion: 多尺度“压缩—保真”分离设计可在字节级建模中同时提升性能与计算效率，验证了在压缩瓶颈处进行语义推理、以边带保字节精确性的可行性与优势。

Abstract: Byte level language models avoid tokenizer dependence and provide universal coverage, but processing every byte at full resolution is expensive. We introduce DeepMem, a causal multiscale architecture that separates semantic compression from exact byte preservation. A learned invertible subband transform constructs a hierarchy of shorter sequences, while a lossless carrier and explicit detail sidebands preserve byte identity. Fast weight memory blocks operate along the encoder, with iterative shared state computation at the compressed bottleneck, while an attention based super resolution decoder restores byte resolution representations. DeepMem achieves stronger language modeling performance than a matched byte level Transformer on FineWeb-Edu and PG19, and obtains the best macro average performance across the evaluated FLORES languages. It also improves PIQA accuracy while remaining competitive on code modeling, multiple choice reasoning, and byte level corruption robustness. Bottleneck refinement requires approximately $7{,}600\times$ fewer counted FLOPs than equivalent full resolution reasoning, while maintaining competitive evaluation throughput.

</details>


### [280] [NovoCRF: Globally Normalized De Novo Peptide Sequencing on a Prefix-Mass Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05260)
*Rui Qiao,Lei Xin,Baozhen Shan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NovoCRF 是以前缀质量（prefix mass）为状态、路径严格落在前体质量 ±0.06 Da 内的条件随机场，首个在所有质量可行肽上做精确归一化训练的新肽测序方法，310k 参数、17 GPU-hours 训练，7 个数据集上平均肽召回 42.4%（Casanovo v5 39.1%），通量 1,746 spectra/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 de novo 肽测序从手工/生成式打分上的动态规划转向端到端大 Transformer，但缺乏在全部质量可行肽上的精确归一化（exact normalization），学习目标与解码空间不一致。

Method: 构建条件随机场，状态为前缀质量，使路径恰为前体质量 ±0.06 Da 内的肽；小网络基于物理推导的离子匹配特征为每条边打分，并在所有质量可行肽上全局归一化训练；软件分三模块：MS1 前体质量精确恢复、CRF 精确 k-best 解码、两个 BiGRU reranker 引入序列上下文与内部碎片证据。

Result: 7 个 2026 年发表的 DDA 数据集（126,262 张标注谱）上，平均肽召回 42.4% vs Casanovo v5 五束 39.1%，氨基酸召回 64.0% vs 53.1%；18 线程 CPU 上 1,746 spectra/s，为 Casanovo GPU 通量的 55 倍；在至多三个肽键无 b/y 离子的标签上领先，Casanovo 仅在其外占优；对未训练修饰的精确质量可搜索，狮骨样本中检出 32.1% 羟脯氨酸肽。

Conclusion: 精确归一化的质量约束 CRF 以极小参数量与极低算力超越大 Transformer，且在稀疏碎片证据与未见修饰场景具备优势，体现结构化建模与物理先验的竞争力。

Abstract: De novo peptide sequencing has moved from dynamic programming over hand-designed or generative scores to large transformers that learn the task end to end. We present NovoCRF, a conditional random field (CRF) whose states are prefix masses, so that its paths are exactly the peptides within +/-0.06 Da of the precursor mass. A small network scores each edge from physics-derived ion-match features and is trained with its scores globally normalized over all mass-feasible peptides. To our knowledge, NovoCRF is the first de novo method trained with exact normalization over all mass-feasible peptides. Our software has three modules, each with exactly one job: a precursor mass finder recovers the accurate precursor mass from MS1, the CRF finds the peptides that fit the precursor mass by exact k-best decoding, and two BiGRU rerankers add sequence context and internal-fragment evidence. The model has 310k parameters and trains in about 17 GPU-hours. On seven held-out data-dependent acquisition datasets published in 2026 (126,262 labeled spectra), NovoCRF's mean peptide recall is 42.4%, against 39.1% for Casanovo v5 with five beams, and its amino-acid recall 64.0% against 53.1%, at 1,746 spectra/s on 18 CPU threads, 55x Casanovo's throughput on a GPU. Split by fragment evidence, NovoCRF leads on labels with up to three peptide bonds without a b or y ion, and Casanovo only beyond. NovoCRF can also search modifications it was never trained on at their exact mass: it finds 32.1% of the hydroxyproline peptides of a lion bone sample.

</details>


### [281] [Stabilizing On-Policy Targeted Guidance in Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05266)
*Kevin Frans,Alexander M Rush,Sergey Levine,Pieter Abbeel,Ashvin Nair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"targeted guidance"框架：利用逐轮（per-turn）文本反馈，通过on-policy自蒸馏目标为策略提供方向性信号，并与逐准则RL奖励结合，在agentic多轮软件工程环境中提升难优化行为的训练效果且不损害基线RL性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 朴素on-policy RL受限于基座模型能力：探索覆盖不足会导致某些奖励无法被学习；而现代后训练中的奖励常来自丰富文本源（如带评分准则的judge模型）。作者希望利用这些结构化文本信息，为难以优化的奖励提供更丰富的方向性信号，同时在训练全程保持基线RL性能不退化。

Method: 提出targeted guidance框架：从per-turn文本反馈中学习，采用on-policy自蒸馏目标；关键设计为将其与逐准则（per-criteria）RL奖励相结合，并将反馈仅限制在策略明确失败的轮次。作者还分析了维持熵稳定、减少训练振荡的设计选择。

Result: 在agentic多轮软件工程环境的多种设置下，"targeted self-distillation + per-criteria RL奖励 + 仅在失败轮次给予反馈"的组合取得成功，既能改进难优化行为，又不降低基线RL表现，并实现更稳定的熵与更少的振荡。

Conclusion: 结构化文本反馈可作为有效的方向性蒸馏信号补充on-policy RL；关键在于反馈的精准投放（限于失败轮次）与与逐准则奖励的耦合，这为提升难优化行为的训练提供了一条稳定可行的路径。

Abstract: Naive on-policy RL is often bottlenecked by the capabilities of the base model --- insufficient coverage can result in an inability to learn from certain rewards. At the same time, rewards in modern post-training systems often come from rich textual sources, e.g. judge models with a rubric of criteria. Can we utilize such structures to provide a rich directional signal that enables improving on otherwise difficult to optimize rewards? In this work, we examine a framework we refer to as targeted guidance that learns from per-turn textual feedback via an on-policy self-distillation objective. Our focus is not only on directly improving hard-to-optimize behaviors, but on how to do so throughout the training process without degrading baseline RL performance. Over a range of settings across an agentic multi-turn software engineering environment, we find success by combining targeted self-distillation with a per-criteria RL reward, and by limiting feedback to turns where the policy specifically fails. We further examine the design choices that lead to stable entropy and reduce oscillatory behavior throughout training.

</details>


### [282] [iGC: Antibody Optimization via Reinforcement Learning over a Learned Somatic Hypermutation Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05270)
*Thao Nguyen,Robert G. Alberstein,Ewa Nowara,Homa MohammadiPeyhani,Holly R. Steach,Sarah A. Robinson,Yuval Shechter,Donald W. Lee,Jason A. Vander Heiden,Franziska Seeger,Andrew Leaver-Fay,Richard Bonneau,Joshua Yao-Yu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出iGC——一个两阶段强化学习框架，将体细胞超突变（SHM）先验与抗原特异性亲和力优化结合，通过奖励-税收元梯度目标防止奖励利用，实现生物学合理的抗体亲和力成熟模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗体亲和力成熟依赖SHM突变与抗原特异性选择，但现有突变模型不直接优化结合亲和力，而亲和力优化方法又常忽略真实的突变轨迹，二者难以兼得。

Method: 两阶段RL：阶段1从B细胞谱系树父子对中学习SHM突变先验，并在目标亲和力成熟数据集上自适应；阶段2以该先验初始化RL策略，在冻结先验的KL正则下优化抗原结合。为防止奖励利用（重复高奖励替换、结构容忍的框架区突变），引入Reward-Tax元梯度目标：RewardNet平滑亲和力景观，TaxNet惩罚奖励过度利用，共同构成学习到的元奖励。

Result: 在突变预测、SHM一致性、亲和力优化和突变多样性上评估：突变先验在突变位点预测上超过最强基线两倍以上；RL优化后iGC保持SHM序列结构，并在各候选筛选阈值下取得有竞争力的奖励-多样性乘积，展现良好的探索-利用平衡。

Conclusion: iGC将生物学合理的突变生成与抗原特异性亲和力选择相融合，为迭代式抗体亲和力成熟提供了in silico框架。

Abstract: Antibody affinity maturation is a natural optimization process in which B cells acquire mutations through somatic hypermutation (SHM), undergo selection for improved antigen binding, and iteratively evolve toward higher affinity. Computationally modeling this process requires capturing both biologically plausible mutations and antigen-specific selection. However, existing mutation models do not directly optimize binding, while affinity optimization often neglects realistic mutation trajectories.
  We introduce iGC (in silico Germinal Centers), a two-stage reinforcement learning (RL) framework for antigen-specific antibody optimization. Stage 1 learns an SHM mutation prior from parent-child pairs in B-cell lineage trees and adapts it to a target affinity-maturation dataset. Stage 2 initializes an RL policy from this prior and optimizes antigen binding with Kullback-Leibler (KL) regularization toward the frozen prior. To prevent reward exploitation, including repetitive high-reward substitutions and structurally tolerated framework mutations, we introduce a Reward-Tax Meta-Gradient objective. RewardNet smooths the affinity landscape, while TaxNet penalizes reward overexploitation, jointly defining a learned meta-reward for policy optimization.
  We evaluate iGC on mutation prediction, SHM consistency, affinity optimization, and mutation diversity. The mutation prior more than doubles the strongest baseline in mutation-site prediction. Following RL optimization, iGC maintains SHM sequence structure. It also achieves a strong exploration-exploitation balance, yielding competitive reward-diversity products across candidate-selection cutoffs. These results demonstrate that iGC integrates biologically plausible mutation generation with antigen-specific affinity selection, providing an in silico framework for iterative antibody affinity maturation.

</details>


### [283] [Universal Hardness From Masking Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05296)
*Parikshit Gopalan,Manoj Prabhakaran,Pranay Tankala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了硬核引理（hardcore lemma）在广泛学习论框架下的推广：将"硬核测度"重述为"输入依赖的掩蔽信道"，用凸对偶选取相应保真度，从而对非布尔函数、非0-1损失给出通用的硬度认证框架，并给出多类推广、通用掩蔽信道及其与omnipredictor、伪熵的对偶关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典硬核引理（Impagliazzo, 1995）只针对布尔函数与0-1损失，其表述依赖于重加权输入分布的"硬核测度"这一较窄概念。作者希望将其推广到非布尔（多类）函数、更广泛的硬度概念与凸损失，并揭示"学习与硬度是同一硬币两面"这一原则的一般化形式。

Method: 核心观察：硬核测度（对输入分布重加权）等价于一个输入依赖的掩蔽信道（masking channel），后者向函数输出注入随机性直至其看起来完全伪随机；测度的"密度"对应信道的"保真度（fidelity）"。据此，通过凸对偶选择合适的保真度概念，作者建立认证硬度的通用框架：用类C以凸损失学习f的难度，被掩蔽信道可达到的最大保真度紧密刻画，即信道为使f与随机噪声C-不可区分所需加入的最少噪声量。此外构造了可同时适用于多种自然硬度概念的通用掩蔽信道，并利用机器学习的MaxEnt算法给出伪熵的紧刻画。

Result: 得到若干多类（multiclass）硬核引理推广，并揭示在更大字母表下推广时出现的意外微妙之处；构造出对多种硬度概念同时适用的通用掩蔽信道，并指出其与omnipredictors（Gopalan et al., 2022）互为对偶；观察到掩蔽信道与基于伪熵的硬核引理推广之间的对称性，并给出伪熵基于MaxEnt算法的紧刻画。

Conclusion: 通过凸对偶，论文给出"学习与硬度是同一硬币两面"这一原则的简洁而一般的表述：掩蔽信道的最大保真度与用类C最小化凸损失学习f的难度紧密对应，统一并推广了硬核引理、omnipredictor与伪熵等概念。

Abstract: We prove a broad learning-theoretic generalization of the celebrated hardcore lemma concerning the average-case complexity of computing a Boolean function (Impagliazzo, 1995). Our starting point is the observation that the classic notion of a hardcore measure, which reweights a function's input distribution, is equivalent to an input-dependent masking channel that injects randomness into the function's output distribution until it appears fully pseudorandom. The analogue of density of the measure is the fidelity of the channel. From this viewpoint, we develop a general framework for certifying hardness that allows for non-Boolean functions $f$ and a wide range of notions of hardness besides the classic 0-1 loss, by choosing the right notion of fidelity via convex duality. We show the difficulty of learning $f$ using a class $\mathcal{C}$ to minimize a convex loss is tightly characterized by the maximum fidelity possible for a masking channel, which measures the least amount of noise the channel must add to hide the structure of $f$ and make it $\mathcal{C}$-indistinguishable from random noise. Using our framework, we derive a number of multiclass generalizations of the hardcore lemma, and reveal some unexpected subtleties in going to larger alphabets. We construct a universal masking channel that applies to many natural notions of hardness simultaneously. Such channels are dual to omnipredictors, which optimize multiple losses at once (Gopalan, Kalai, Reingold, Sharan, and Wieder, 2022). We observe a symmetry between masking channels and generalizations of the hardcore lemma using pseudoentropy. We present a tight characterization of pseudoentropy using the MaxEnt algorithm from machine learning. A long line of research has shown that learning and hardness are two sides of the same coin; our framework gives a crisp and general formulation of this principle via convex duality.

</details>


### [284] [LoopVQ: Sub 1-bit KV Cache Quantization for Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05304)
*Minsoo Cheong,Sungjoo Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LoopVQ，针对循环Transformer中沿循环轴高度冗余的KV cache进行跨循环步联合通道向量量化，在0.5 bit/元素下优于现有KV量化方法，并实现显著解码加速与吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer通过重复复用同一层降低参数内存，但每个循环步仍各自存储K/V，使KV cache随循环步数线性增长，成为主要内存瓶颈。作者观察到循环模型的KV cache沿循环轴冗余度高，且各循环步对量化的敏感度差异显著，据此设计专用压缩方案。

Method: LoopVQ对多个循环步的通道进行联合编码（跨循环轴共享码本/联合向量量化），并对码本进行微调以对齐全精度模型输出；该方法与标准LLM的KV量化方法正交，可与TaSQ组合使用。

Result: 在0.5 bit/元素下，于Ouro与Huginn模型上，在通用任务、长思维链推理和长上下文检索上优于现有KV cache向量量化方法；与TaSQ结合后在0.25 bit/元素仍保留大部分BF16精度。在SGLang中实现后，32K上下文解码延迟近乎减半，1.4B与2.6B Ouro模型峰值服务吞吐分别最高提升4.3×与6.1×。

Conclusion: 利用循环轴的KV冗余与分步敏感度差异进行联合向量量化，可有效缓解循环Transformer的KV cache内存瓶颈，在极低比特率下保持精度并获得实际部署级的延迟与吞吐收益。

Abstract: Looped transformers are a parameter-efficient alternative to standard transformers for language modeling. By reapplying the same layers across multiple loop steps, recent looped language models match the performance of non-looped models several times their size. While this architecture effectively reduces parameter memory, each loop step still stores its own keys and values, so the key-value (KV) cache grows linearly with the number of loop steps and becomes the main memory bottleneck. We observe that the KV cache of looped models is highly redundant along the loop axis and that the loop steps differ substantially in their sensitivity to quantization. Based on these observations, we propose $\textbf{LoopVQ}$, a KV cache vector quantization method for looped transformers that codes channels of multiple loop steps jointly, and fine-tunes the codebooks to match the output of the full-precision model. At 0.5 bits per element, LoopVQ outperforms existing KV cache vector quantization methods for standard LLMs on general, long chain-of-thought reasoning, and long-context retrieval tasks with Ouro and Huginn models. Its design is orthogonal to these methods, and combined with TaSQ, it retains much of the BF16 accuracy even at 0.25 bits per element. We also implement LoopVQ in SGLang, where it nearly halves the decode latency at a 32K-token context and raises the peak serving throughput by up to $4.3\times$ and $6.1\times$ on the 1.4B and 2.6B Ouro models compared to the BF16 baseline.

</details>


### [285] [$Φ$asmaNet: A Staged Spectral Token-Refinement Architecture for Fast Gravitational-Wave Signal-to-Noise Ratio Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05309)
*Ioannis Tsioulis,Anastasios Tefas,Nikolaos Stergioulas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ΦasmaNet：一种紧凑的神经表格回归架构，以分阶段光谱细化为显式先验，快速预测引力波信号的优化SNR，在10^6样本下100−MAPE≈98.7%–99.4%，单GPU约1.2×10^5次/秒，端到端处理10^6注入目录约8.3秒，比传统波形内积计算快约两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波族群研究中的选择效应量化需要海量昂贵的SNR计算，传统波形生成与内积计算成本过高；同时需要一个可复现的基准与可跨领域借鉴的表格回归架构。

Method: 提出ΦasmaNet：可学习的输入token经有序的注意力式Φ-Stage逐步精化，每阶段辅以Φ-Refractor子空间混合模块，并通过门控自顶向下Φ-Feedback回访；输入为进动、含高阶模式的BBH信号（IMRPhenomXPHM）的11维参数，输出固定Advanced LIGO设计灵敏度下单探测器的相位平均最优SNR；参数约732k。

Result: 在10^5–10^6样本匹配训练下优于MLP、1D-ResNet与Transformer编码器基线；10^6样本时测试集100−MAPE=98.73%，经保序单调（PCHIP）校准后99.29%，检测相关区ρ>8达99.39%（R²=0.9998），跨独立数据集实现稳定；单GPU约1.2×10^5次SNR评估/秒，10^6注入目录端到端约8.3秒，较16核CPU传统方法快约两个数量级；利用ρ∝1/d_L支持精确距离重标度。

Conclusion: 该架构为引力波族群选择效应研究提供高效代理模型与可复现基准，公开表格数据集上的结果亦显示其超越引力波领域的竞争力；当前限于固定单探测器配置，但构造上可扩展至探测器网络与时变灵敏度。

Abstract: Quantifying selection effects in gravitational-wave population studies requires millions of costly signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) calculations. We present $Φ$asmaNet, a compact neural architecture for structured tabular regression, with staged spectral refinement as an explicit architectural prior. Learned input tokens are refined through ordered attention-based $Φ$-Stages, each augmented by a $Φ$-Refractor subspace-mixing module, then revisited through gated top-down $Φ$-Feedback. Applied to the 11-dimensional parameters of precessing, higher-mode binary black hole signals (IMRPhenomXPHM), it predicts phase-averaged optimal SNR for a single detector at fixed Advanced LIGO design sensitivity. With approximately 732k parameters, it outperforms MLP, 1D-ResNet and Transformer-encoder baselines under matched training across $10^5$--$10^6$ samples. At $10^6$ samples, it achieves $100-\mathrm{MAPE}=98.73\%$ on the held-out test set, $99.29\%$ after rank-preserving monotonic (PCHIP) calibration, and $99.39\%$ ($R^2=0.9998$) in the detection-relevant regime $ρ>8$, with stable accuracy across independent dataset realizations. On a single GPU, it sustains approximately $1.2\times10^5$ SNR evaluations per second and processes a $10^6$-injection catalogue end-to-end in approximately 8.3 s, about two orders of magnitude faster than conventional waveform-and-inner-product calculations on a 16-core CPU. Exact distance rescaling through $ρ\propto1/d_L$ supports repeated catalogue-wide detectability evaluations. The fully documented pipeline is intended as a reproducible benchmark, while results on public tabular datasets indicate competitiveness beyond gravitational-wave applications. Although demonstrated for a fixed single-detector configuration, the construction supports extensions to detector networks and epoch-specific sensitivities. (Abridged)

</details>


### [286] [CATF: A Manager-Worker Framework for Context-Aware Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05325)
*Yueqiao Chen,Dongyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CATF——基于Manager-Worker架构的上下文感知时间序列预测框架，通过Transformer-based Manager对输入做硬路由，使Worker无需人工划分数据即可专业化，从而在多个数据集上超越单模型基线与零样本基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文感知预测中，单一模型难以覆盖上下文的多种模式（regimes）；而集成学习与MoE方法依赖人工数据划分或软路由，导致推理成本上升且可解释性差。

Method: CATF采用Manager-Worker架构：Transformer-based Manager根据上下文为每个输入硬路由至单个Worker，训练与推理阶段均使用硬路由；Worker在无人工数据划分下自然专业化，且任意单模型预测器均可作为Worker。

Result: 在5个富上下文数据集、4种预测步长与2种归一化方案下，CATF在200组对比中有162组优于其独立对应模型；最佳CATF增强模型在20个设置中19个超越最佳独立基线，并在20个设置中15个优于零样本Time-MoE基础模型。

Conclusion: 硬路由的Manager-Worker设计可在不增加人工划分与显著推理成本的前提下实现模型专业化，提升预测性能并保持可解释性；自行车共享数据案例显示Manager按日历信息路由、各Worker对应不同周模式。

Abstract: We study context-aware time-series forecasting, in which a single target variable is predicted from its own history and a set of contextual variables, such as weather and calendar information. As the context becomes more diverse, a single model struggles to capture all of its regimes, while ensemble learning and Mixture-of-Experts approaches rely on manual data partitioning or soft routing, which increases the inference cost and hinders interpretability. We propose CATF, a Context-Aware Time-Series Forecasting framework built on a Manager-Worker architecture: a Transformer-based Manager routes each input to a single Worker based on the context, using hard routing during both training and inference. The Workers thus specialize without manual data partitioning, and any single-model forecaster can serve as a Worker. On five context-rich datasets, four horizons, and two normalization schemes, CATF improves over its standalone counterpart in 162 of 200 comparisons, the best CATF-enhanced model outperforms the best standalone baseline in 19 of 20 settings, and it outperforms the zero-shot Time-MoE foundation model in 15 of 20 settings. A case study on bike-sharing data shows that the Manager routes inputs by the calendar and that each Worker specializes in a different weekly pattern.

</details>


### [287] [Agnostic Learning by Imitating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05346)
*Sílvia Casacuberta,Parikshit Gopalan,Varun Kanade,Omer Reingold,Konstantinos Stavropoulos,Pranay Tankala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在二分类数据分布下，“判别式（agnostic）学习”与“生成模型的多精度（indistinguishability/multiaccuracy）”在计算复杂度上等价，二者可互相高效归约，并进一步表明多精度预言机是极强的原语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判别式学习与生成式建模是计算学习理论中两个基础任务，此前只知一个方向的归约（由复杂性理论中的正则性引理/boosting给出从多精度到agnostic学习的高效归约），反方向未知。作者希望厘清二者的复杂度关系，并探究多精度预言机的实际能力边界。

Method: 核心是给出从判别（agnostic learning）任务到不可区分（multiaccuracy）任务的高效预言机归约；关键技术是将该离线问题与在线学习中的无遗憾（no-regret）学习相联系，利用follow-the-leader类算法自适应地修改标签分布并聚合多精度预言机的预测；并将该思路推广以直接构造其他更强的学习目标。

Result: 证明两类任务在复杂度上等价（双向高效归约）；表明自适应查询下的多精度预言机可达成agnostic学习及多种看似更苛刻的学习目标；与先前“对单个多精度预测器做后处理连弱学习都不够”的结果形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 判别式学习与生成式不可区分性在复杂度上等价；多精度预言机配合自适应查询是极为强大的原语；关键区别在于自适应查询能力，而非后处理单个预测器。

Abstract: Given a class of machine learning models and sample access to a data distribution with binary labels, which computational task is harder: training a discriminative model that distinguishes positive and negative labels as well as the best member of the class, or training a generative model whose outputs are indistinguishable from real data by all members of the class? We prove that these two fundamental tasks in computational learning theory are not just closely related, but actually equivalent to each other in complexity. One direction of this equivalence was previously known. Indeed, the powerful regularity lemma in complexity theory gives an efficient oracle reduction from the indistinguishability task (a.k.a. multiaccuracy) to the distinguishing task (a.k.a. agnostic learning) in the style of boosting algorithms. Our main contribution is an efficient oracle reduction in the other direction. Taking this idea further, we show that a multiaccuracy oracle is a remarkably powerful primitive, achieving not just agnostic learning but also a wide range of seemingly more demanding learning objectives via similarly efficient and direct constructions. Our results crucially use the power of making adaptive queries to the oracle, and stand in stark contrast to prior work, which showed that post-processing a single multiaccurate predictor is insufficient for even weak forms of learning. On a technical level, we draw a connection between our (offline) problem and a version of no-regret learning in the online setting, which allows us to use follow-the-leader type algorithms to modify the label distribution and aggregate the predictions of the multiaccuracy oracle.

</details>


### [288] [Aligning Language Models with General Preferences: An Empirical Study of Direct Nash Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05373)
*Austin Tsai,Saurav Nanda,Ganapathy Parthasarathy,Bhuvnesh Kumar,Parivesh Choudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文独立复现了Direct Nash Optimization (DNO)的实用变体DNO-Prct，在Llama 3.1 8B Instruct与Mistral 7B Instruct v0.1上验证其对齐效果，并系统分析了各组件贡献、超参敏感性与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNO以近似两方偏好博弈的纳什均衡来对齐语言模型；其实际变体DNO-Prct流程较复杂，缺乏独立复现与组件归因分析，且其是否依赖专有模型作标注者、各环节（黄金回复、数据回看、大间隔筛选、采样数、间隔阈值）的实际贡献尚不明确。

Method: 独立复现DNO-Prct：迭代进行在线策略采样，由标注者（GPT-4o或同族更大开源模型）对样本与教师黄金回复打分，进行大间隔偏好对筛选，再做DPO式对比更新；共3轮迭代。在AlpacaEval 2.0上以GPT-4o为裁判评估，并进行消融：移除黄金回复、移除数据回看、仅接受教师偏好对，以及扫描每提示在线采样数（1–7）与间隔阈值（1–5）。

Result: Llama的LC胜率由31.7%升至45.3%，Mistral由6.8%升至18.4%；调优后8B模型在所有报告的胜率上超过Llama 3.1 70B Instruct。以同族开源模型作标注者时，分别保留62%（Llama）和82%（Mistral）的LC增益，说明评分环节不依赖专有模型。消融显示移除黄金回复、移除数据回看或仅接受教师偏好对的变体比完整流程低6–10个LC点；而采样数1–7变动最多影响2.1点，间隔阈值1–5最多影响2.4点。

Conclusion: DNO-Prct可独立复现并带来显著偏好对齐增益，其核心贡献来自黄金回复、数据回看与偏好对筛选机制，而对采样数与间隔阈值不敏感。需注意所有结果为单次运行，且GPT-4o同时担任教师与评测裁判，存在对报告增益的潜在乐观偏差，文中亦讨论了实践算法与其所近似理论的偏离及流程成本。

Abstract: Direct Nash Optimization (DNO) aligns a language model to a general preference function by approximating the Nash equilibrium of a two-player preference game. Its practical variant, DNO-Prct, iterates on-policy sampling, LLM-based rating of the samples together with a teacher's gold response, large-margin preference-pair selection and a DPO-style contrastive update. We re-implement DNO-Prct independently and study it on two open-weight instruct models, Llama 3.1 8B Instruct and Mistral 7B Instruct v0.1, with GPT-4o or a larger open-weight model of the same family as the annotator. On AlpacaEval 2.0 with GPT-4o as judge, three iterations raise the length-controlled (LC) win rate against GPT-4o from 31.7% to 45.3% for Llama and from 6.8% to 18.4% for Mistral, and the tuned 8B model scores above Llama 3.1 70B Instruct on every reported win rate. With same-family open-weight annotators, the runs retain 62% (Llama) and 82% (Mistral) of the LC gain achieved with GPT-4o as annotator, so the rating step does not require a proprietary model. In a separate ablation configuration, variants that remove the gold responses, remove the data look-back, or accept only teacher-preferred pairs score 6 to 10 LC points below the full procedure, whereas varying the number of on-policy samples per prompt (1 to 7) moves the LC win rate by at most 2.1 points, and a margin sweep (1 to 5) run in its own pipeline by at most 2.4 points. We also report the cost of the pipeline and where the practical algorithm departs from the theory it approximates. All results are single runs, and GPT-4o is both the teacher and the evaluation judge; we discuss what this implies for the reported gains.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [289] [A Compact Formula-Aware Spreadsheet Abstraction for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03705)
*Tie Cheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种紧凑的公式感知电子表格抽象方法，用位置、类型、R1C1公式模式和边界值表示大型同质区域，先于详细内容提交给LLM，以提升处理准确性与效率并降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被广泛用于辅助电子表格建模、公式生成和数据分析，但电子表格的结构与通常提供给LLM的顺序文本表示之间存在不匹配，限制了其处理真实电子表格的能力。

Method: 设计一种紧凑的公式感知电子表格抽象，通过位置、类型、R1C1公式模式和选定边界值来表示大型同质范围；用高效算法在文本序列化之前检测并结构化这些范围，并将其作为先导信息提供给LLM。

Result: 在50个电子表格查询案例上评估：仅凭抽象即可回答部分查询；当需要额外内容时，抽象减少了必须提交给LLM的数据量，降低token消耗，并提升处理的可靠性与准确性。

Conclusion: 将电子表格静态分析与AI结合可提升AI辅助电子表格任务的可靠性、准确性和效率，并讨论了进一步的技术方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to assist spreadsheet users with tasks such as spreadsheet model construction, formula generation, and data analysis, yet their interaction with real-world spreadsheets remains limited by the mismatch between the structure of spreadsheets and the sequential textual representations typically provided to LLMs. We propose a compact formula-aware spreadsheet abstraction that represents large homogeneous ranges through their positions, types, R1C1 formula patterns, and selected boundary values. An efficient algorithm detects and structures these ranges before a textual serialization is supplied to an LLM. The abstraction is provided to the LLM before detailed spreadsheet content is submitted, improving the accuracy and efficiency of spreadsheet processing while reducing token consumption. We evaluate the method on 50 spreadsheet-query cases. The evaluation shows that the abstraction alone was sufficient to answer some queries. When additional spreadsheet content is required, the abstraction reduces the amount of data that must be submitted to the LLM, thereby reducing token consumption. The abstraction layer also improves the reliability and accuracy of spreadsheet processing. We conclude by discussing further techniques for combining spreadsheet static analysis with artificial intelligence to improve the reliability, accuracy, and efficiency of AI assisted spreadsheet tasks.

</details>


### [290] [Testing Processor Contracts in Speculative Decoding Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04042)
*Tianyu Ding,Yaocheng Tong,Muzhi Yang,Lei Yang,Fei Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文形式化"处理器-前缀契约"并提出BADC（分支感知差分一致性测试），实证确认投机采样加速中logits处理器读取未验证草稿或状态未回滚这两类失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机采样的标准分布不变性保证依赖一个隐含前提：在每个验证位置上，logits处理器（施加约束或修改token分数）接收到正确的前缀与状态。这一"处理器-前缀契约"未被形式化，其违反是否导致实际解码分布偏移缺乏系统检验。

Method: 提出BADC（Branch-Aware Differential Conformance Testing）：在微型随机模型上进行枚举式参考比对；检查处理器输入与状态；对输出事件概率施加同时置信界。开展预注册、源码引导的实验，覆盖若干Transformer与llama-cpp-python版本，并设置定向选择vs随机选择、置换检验等对照。

Result: 确认两类成因：处理器读取未验证草稿、状态推进后未回滚。在一训练检查点的试点提示上，普通解码与prompt-lookup解码对预定义首token事件概率之差的60.26个百分点下界，名义同时95%覆盖（同臂i.i.d.采样下）。定向选择相对随机选择无成因恢复优势；置换检验对每个受测族系的检出频率不低于BADC。

Conclusion: 研究将处理器接口失效与可观测的解码分布差异建立联系，但未确立诊断优越性或实际负载中的问题普遍性。

Abstract: Speculative sampling can accelerate decoding without changing the target distribution. Its standard guarantee assumes that logits processors, which enforce constraints or modify token scores, receive the appropriate token prefix and state at each verification position. We formalize this processor-prefix contract and present BADC (branch-aware differential conformance testing). BADC combines enumerated references on tiny random models, checks of processor inputs and state, and simultaneous confidence bounds on output-event probabilities. Preregistered, source-informed experiments on selected Transformers and llama-cpp-python releases confirm two causes: processors read unverified drafts or advance state without rollback. For one pilot-selected prompt on a trained checkpoint, fresh samples give a 60.26-percentage-point lower bound on the difference in the probability of a predefined first-token event between ordinary and prompt-lookup decoding, with nominal simultaneous 95% coverage under within-arm i.i.d. sampling. Controlled comparisons establish no cause-recovery advantage of directed selection over random selection, while permutation testing detects each tested family at least as often as BADC. These findings connect processor-interface failures to observable decoding differences without establishing diagnostic superiority or workload prevalence.

</details>


### [291] [Are We Just Applying LLMs? What SE Research Studies, Underexplores, and Could Do Next in the LLM Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04206)
*Zeinadsadat Saghi,Sadra Sabouri,Souti Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对2018–2026年三大SE会议4790篇论文的普查与244名研究者问卷显示，AI已占据ICSE/FSE/ASE过半研究论文（贡献93.1%的论文增长），但其影响主要是围绕AI能力对既有议程的不均衡重组，而非问题边界的开拓或对范式的批判性反思。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在软件工程研究中已高度普及，但"普及"本身无法说明AI究竟是扩展了发表版图、挤占了非AI工作，还是重塑了知识议程。作者希望通过实证普查刻画这一转变的性质与结构性后果。

Method: 双数据源实证研究：(1) 对ICSE、FSE、ASE三会2018–2026年共4790篇研究论文做文献普查与分类编码；(2) 结合ICSE 2026 FoSE调查中244名SE研究者的自由文本回答，考察社区对未来的认知。

Result: AI并非简单替代非AI研究，而是不均衡地重组领域：AI贡献93.1%的论文增长，但部分会议与问题领域的扩张掩盖了其他方向的收缩；LLM浪潮更多改变工具而非问题边界——92.6%的LLM时代AI论文重访既有SE问题，新问题多源于LLM被引入SE过程本身；参与方式偏下游——95.7%为应用AI或评估/支持其使用，仅4.3%质询该范式或提出替代方案。问卷中AI驱动变化是第二高频的领域担忧（20.2%），但被要求指出社区应做的一项改变时仅7.0%提及AI。

Conclusion: 该领域围绕AI重组既有议程的速度，快于其质疑这一重组本身的速度。作者建议SE社区：跨技术浪潮累积式构建知识、强化评估与保障、发展SE原生（SE-native）理论基础、研究AI重塑世界中的软件工程。

Abstract: Is AI taking over software engineering research? By 2025, AI appeared in more than half of the research-track papers at ICSE, FSE, and ASE. Yet ubiquity alone cannot show whether AI expanded the publication portfolio, displaced other work, or redirected the intellectual agenda. We investigate this shift through a census of 4,790 research papers (three SE conferences; 2018--2026) and free-form responses from 244 SE researchers discussing the future of the SE research community (ICSE 2026 FoSE survey). We found that while AI is taking over, it is not simply replacing non-AI research; it is unevenly reorganizing SE research around its capabilities. AI accounted for 93.1% of publication growth, but expansion in some venues and problem areas concealed contraction in others. The LLM wave also changed the field's tools more than its problem boundaries: 92.6% of LLM-wave AI papers revisit established SE problems, while most new problems arise from the introduction of LLMs in the SE process themselves. Engagement with AI remains predominantly downstream: 95.7% of AI papers apply AI or evaluate and support its use, while only 4.3% interrogate the paradigm or develop alternatives. The FoSE survey reveals a similar imbalance between recognition and response. AI-driven change was the second-most frequent concern about the field's future 20.2%, yet only 7.0% of responses mentioned AI when researchers were asked to identify one change the community should make. Together, our findings reveal a field reorganizing an inherited agenda around AI faster than it is questioning that reorganization. We discuss how SE can respond by building cumulatively across technological waves, strengthening evaluation and assurance, developing SE-native foundations, and studying software engineering in an AI-transformed world.

</details>


### [292] [Do Coding Agents Follow AGENTS.md? The Effect of Policy Wording on Instruction Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04367)
*Rikuto Tsuchida,Youmei Fan,Kazumasa Shimari,Raula Gaikovina Kula,Kenichi Matsumoto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 编码智能体并不总是遵守 Agent Context File（ACF）中的策略。本文将同一条策略以四种表述风格插入 86 个基准任务的 ACF 中，覆盖四种 harness-模型组合，发现条件式表述的遵循率低于规定式与解释式，而这两者中更优者随组合而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACF 已成为向编码智能体传递行为约束的主要载体，但智能体对其中策略的实际遵循情况缺乏系统量化，尤其是策略的措辞风格是否会显著影响遵循率尚不清楚。

Method: 受控对照实验：选取 86 个基准任务，在四种 harness-模型组合下，将同一条策略以四种表述风格（含条件式、规定式、解释式等）插入 ACF，比较不同风格下的策略遵循率。

Result: 条件式（Conditional）表述的遵循率显著低于规定式（Prescriptive）与解释式（Explanatory）；且规定式与解释式之间谁更优并不固定，随 harness-模型组合而变化。

Conclusion: ACF 中策略的措辞风格是影响智能体遵循行为的实质变量，条件式表述应避免用于强约束场景；同时由于风格优势存在组合依赖性，实践者需针对具体 harness-模型组合进行验证而非套用通用写作规范。

Abstract: Coding agents do not always follow the policies in an Agent Context File (ACF). We inserted one policy, in four styles, into the ACFs of 86 benchmark tasks across four harness-model combinations. Conditional phrasing was followed less often than Prescriptive and Explanatory. The better of those two varied by combination.

</details>


### [293] [Disposable Implementations: What Is Software Engineering For?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04383)
*Christoph Treude*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出"再生成"(regeneration)概念：当编码智能体能依据需求、测试、架构约束与运维记录重建实现时，实现可变为"一次性"(disposable)，但持续性与知识保留问题未解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统实现以长期维护为主、替换为例外；智能体或使"重建替代维护"成为常规选项，由此需要评估其可行性、经济性与风险。

Method: 对从业者文献进行探索性综述(exploratory review of practitioner literature)，梳理已公开的智能体替换实现的案例。

Result: 公开案例显示智能体确能替换现有实现，但不足以证明重复再生成是安全或经济的；现有代码中可能含有未记录的防护措施与隐含假设，仅靠制品与测试难以保证生产行为连续。

Conclusion: 再生成究竟降低还是转移维护成本尚不明确；关键开放问题是：若实现成为一次性产物，软件工程应当保留什么(需求、测试、架构约束、运维记录的完备性成为核心资产)。

Abstract: Existing implementations are usually maintained for most of their life and replaced only as an exception. Coding agents may change that. If agents can produce new implementations from requirements, tests, architectural constraints, and operational records that survive replacement, changing a system may no longer require modifying its existing codebase. We call this process regeneration. An implementation is disposable when regeneration becomes a routine alternative to maintenance. Regenerating and verifying a clean implementation from current requirements and tests may be cheaper than having agents navigate (and potentially compound) existing technical debt, and could eliminate much of that debt. Yet existing code may contain knowledge absent from the preserved artifacts, including safeguards introduced after failures and assumptions that were never documented. A regenerated implementation may pass the available tests and still lose behavior that matters in production. This leaves open how to establish continuity across replacements and whether regeneration reduces maintenance effort or shifts it to the artifacts and processes that support it. We examine these questions through an exploratory review of practitioner literature. Published examples show that agents can replace existing implementations, but do not establish that repeated regeneration is safe or economical. If implementations become disposable, what should software engineering preserve?

</details>


### [294] [How Agent Skills Evolve: A Large-Scale Empirical Study of Skills on GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04456)
*Jinjun Huang,Jialun Cao,Junxiao Han,Zhonghao Jiang,Shing-Chi Cheung,Zhongxin Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首个大规模实证研究：采集84,200个agent技能与750,898次GitHub提交，构建技能组件、变更模式与演化动机分类体系并自动标注，揭示可靠性不足、演化集中于项目耦合技能、以增量丰富/精炼为主、冗余与安全风险等五大启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的agent日益依赖外部技能复用任务知识而无需重训模型，但现有研究多从静态视角考察技能，缺乏对真实仓库中技能演化的探索，限制了对技能变更模式与动机的理解。

Method: 大规模实证研究：从GitHub采集84,200个技能与750,898次提交；人工构建技能组件、变更模式、演化动机三套分类体系；构建自动化标注流水线实现大规模变更标注；据此开展技能构成与创建后演化频率的定量分析，以及被修改组件、变更模式与动机的定性评估。

Result: 得到五点发现：(i) 技能构成与变更中对可靠性的关注不足，需引入含验证与恢复机制的执行契约式技能设计；(ii) 演化变更集中于项目耦合型技能，需自动化即时技能更新机制；(iii) 变更模式以增量丰富与精炼为主，需模式引导的技能增强器以诊断并改进不完整组件；(iv) 冗余是实际维护问题，需上下文高效的技能表示；(v) 安全动机变更的风险降低有限，需客观验证功能与安全的技能回归测试方法。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了agent技能演化研究的空白，系统刻画了技能构成、变更模式与动机，并提出可靠性、自动化更新、模式引导增强、上下文效率与回归测试五方面启示；所构建数据集为后续自动化技能技术研究提供基础。

Abstract: Large language model-based agents increasingly rely on external skills to reuse task-specific knowledge without retraining the model. As skills become common for extending agent capabilities, understanding creation and evolution is critical for reliable agent systems. However, existing studies mainly examine agent skills from a static perspective, leaving their evolution in real-world repositories largely unexplored. This limits our understanding of the patterns and motivations behind skill changes. To fill this gap, we present a large-scale empirical study of agent skills, capturing 84,200 skills and 750,898 commits from GitHub. We manually construct taxonomies of skill components, change patterns, and evolution motivations, and build an automated annotation pipeline to label changes at scale. Based on the taxonomy, we conduct quantitative analyses of skill composition and post-creation evolution frequency, together with qualitative assessments of modified components, change patterns, and motivations. Our findings reveal five implications: (i) reliability receives less attention in skill composition and change, motivating execution-contract skill design that incorporates verification and recovery mechanisms; (ii) evolution changes concentrate in project-coupled skills, calling for automated just-in-time skill updating mechanisms; (iii) change patterns are dominated by incremental enrichment and refinement, motivating pattern-guided skill enhancers that diagnose and improve incomplete skill components; (iv) redundancy is a practical maintenance issue, highlighting the need for context-efficient skill representation; (v) security-motivated changes show limited risk reduction, suggesting skill regression testing methods that objectively verify skill functionality and security. Beyond these insights, our dataset provides a basis for future studies on automated skill techniques.

</details>


### [295] [FreeForm Harness: A Mobile-Native Agent Harness for Open and Composable Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04579)
*Bo Zhang,Pengbo Zhang,Jiaxin Jiao,Yue Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出移动端原生智能体运行框架 FFH，以冻结底座+统一插件契约实现开放可组合扩展，并支持终端用户经"委托创作"由Web编码智能体生成并本地准入扩展，在Android原型上验证了架构边界、组合空间匹配与设备本地信任决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动智能体将能力捆绑为单体流水线，缺乏开放的再组合点，用户与第三方无法发布、安装、组合新行为，导致个性化日常请求得不到满足；手机虽是Web主要入口，但去中心化 authorship 下的开放发布与组合止步于智能体harness层。

Method: 设计移动原生智能体harness（FFH）：冻结底座与"持久性感知"的必需层应对位置、主动触发、稀缺通道等移动约束；统一插件契约使独立作者扩展可在冻结socket集上组合、可检查、可替换；通过分层"委托创作"路径支持终端用户创作——无匹配扩展时由Web编码智能体生成候选产物，在设备端按同一契约校验并准入。构建覆盖设备、市场与云端的Android原型。

Result: 运行时强制架构边界；真实日常请求能被声明式组合空间覆盖而无需扩展该空间；用户意图可通过检索或委托创作抵达设备端执行，最终信任决策保持设备本地。

Conclusion: FFH将移动端智能体Web从"一切皆插件"推进至"人人皆开发者"，为开放、可组合的移动智能体扩展提供了可行基座。

Abstract: Mobile phones support many everyday agent tasks, but current mobile agents bundle capabilities into monolithic pipelines with no open recombination point. Users and third parties cannot publish, install, and compose new behaviors, leaving personalized daily-life requests underserved. We argue that the missing piece is a substrate: although phones are the Web's dominant access point, open publication and composition under decentralized authorship stop at the agent harness. We present FreeForm Harness (FFH), a mobile-native agent harness for open, composable extensions. A frozen base and a permanence-aware required layer handle mobile constraints, including location, proactive triggering, and scarce channels. A uniform plugin contract makes independently authored extensions composable, checkable, and replaceable over a frozen socket set. FFH also supports end-user authorship through a tiered commissioned-authoring path. When no existing extension matches a user's intent, a Web coding agent generates a candidate artifact, which is validated and admitted on device under the same contract as other extensions. An Android prototype spans device, market, and cloud. Evaluation shows that architectural boundaries are enforced at runtime; realistic daily-life requests fit the declared composition space without widening it; and user intent reaches on-device execution through retrieval or commissioned authoring, with final trust decisions remaining device-local. FFH advances the agentic Web from "everything is a plugin" toward "everyone is a developer" on mobile.

</details>


### [296] [RESTberus: Trace-Aware Benchmarking of Automated REST API Testing Tools Across Microservice Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04769)
*John Toniutti,Massimiliano Baldo,Marino Miculan,Vincenzo Riccio*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RESTberus is a Kubernetes-based black-box REST API 测试基准平台，通过结合分布式追踪（distributed traces）区分目标服务自身失败与下游依赖引发的失败，在微服务系统上实现更可靠的工具评测与失败去重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 REST API 测试基准只关注孤立服务，忽略了因与下游依赖交互而产生的失败；在微服务系统中，仅凭响应体与入口操作无法可靠区分失败，导致工具间比较失真。

Method: 构建基于 Kubernetes 的基准平台，在已部署的微服务系统上评测黑盒 REST API 测试工具；将 API 层证据与分布式追踪互补，以实现 trace-aware failure deduplication（追踪感知的失败去重）。在六种工具、两个微服务基准上评估，并与既有基线（如 RESTgym）对比。

Result: trace-aware 去重策略达到 87.1% 准确率，较先前工作基线高出 10–13 个百分点；实证研究表明所有工具都会暴露下游失败，平均占估计唯一失败的 11.7%，为孤立服务基准所无法呈现；在单服务基准上，RESTberus 与 RESTgym 结果统计等价。

Conclusion: 集成级（integration-level）评测既能暴露下游依赖失败、提升工具比较的可靠性，又能保持与现有工具的兼容性及与既有评估结果的可比性。

Abstract: Existing REST API testing benchmarks focus on isolated services, overlooking failures arising from interactions with downstream dependencies. In microservice systems, response bodies and entry-point operations alone cannot reliably distinguish failures, compromising tool comparisons. We present RESTberus, a Kubernetes-based benchmarking platform for black-box REST API testing tools on deployed microservice systems. By complementing API-level evidence with distributed traces, RESTberus can distinguish failures manifesting in the target service from those manifesting in downstream dependencies. Across six tools on two microservice benchmarks, our trace-aware deduplication strategy achieves 87.1% accuracy, outperforming baselines from prior work by 10-13 percentage points. Our empirical study shows that all tools expose downstream failures, accounting for 11.7% of estimated unique failures on average, which isolated-service benchmarks cannot expose. On single-service benchmarks, RESTberus produces statistically equivalent results to RESTgym, demonstrating that integration-level assessments can preserve compatibility with existing tools and comparability with prior evaluations.

</details>


### [297] [Who Is Doing the Thinking? Characterising Human Agency in First-Year Students' AI Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04783)
*Ali Shakiba,Thomas Chaffey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"人类能动性深度分类法"(HADT)，一个初步的六层级分类框架，用于评判学生在提示词中做出的工程贡献程度（独立于AI输出），并在一门允许使用AI的一年级软件工程课程的962条学生AI日志提示词上进行了应用分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生使用生成式AI完成作业时学习过程变得不可见；Bloom分类法等既有框架只能评判AI产出内容的认知层次，而非学生自身的贡献，因此缺乏衡量学生真实学习投入的工具。

Method: 提出HADT六层级分类法，融合三个维度：情境带宽、工程抽象度、学生能动性。将其应用于145名学生课程中105名学生的962条结构化AI日志提示词，追踪10周内能动性变化，并采用带学生聚类的统计方法分析层级分布与生命周期阶段的关系。

Result: 84.2%的提示词包含超出单纯委托的学生贡献；能动性随开发生命周期变化：架构发现类提示词在需求阶段占48%，第9周后降至5–12%；分析现有系统的提示词在构建与验证阶段上升（比值比2.88，且按学生聚类）；层级方差三分之二来自学生个体内部，说明能动性更多取决于工作阶段而非学生特质；截止周的委托行为上升在聚类后不显著；明确约束AI输出的提示词始终稀少。

Conclusion: 学生能动性主要由任务阶段驱动而非个体差异；揭示约束设定稀缺是新手知识瓶颈而非教学缺失；讨论了对课程与评估设计的启示，并提出HADT可作为反思学生如何与AI互动的形成性视角。

Abstract: When students use generative AI to produce their work, the learning process becomes invisible. Established frameworks such as Bloom's taxonomy then classify the cognitive level of what the AI produced, not what the student contributed. We propose the Human Agency Depth Taxonomy (HADT), a preliminary six-tier taxonomy that classifies the engineering contribution a student makes in a prompt, independent of the AI's output. HADT combines three dimensions: contextual bandwidth, engineering abstraction and student agency. We applied HADT to 962 prompts from 105 students' structured AI Journals in an AI-permissive, first-year software engineering course of 145 students, and examined how agency changed over ten journalled weeks. Most prompts (84.2%) carried some student contribution beyond delegation. Agency tracked the development lifecycle. Architecture-discovery prompts dominated the requirements phase (48% of turns) and fell to 5-12% from week 9, while prompts analysing existing systems rose during build and verification (odds ratio 2.88, with turns clustered by student). Two-thirds of the variance in tier lay within students, so agency depended more on the phase of work than on the student. A turn-level rise in delegation in deadline weeks did not hold once turns were clustered by student. Explicitly specifying constraints on the AI's output was rare throughout, which we interpret as a knowledge bottleneck for novices rather than a gap in instruction. We discuss implications for course and assessment design, and for using HADT as a formative lens on how students engage with AI.

</details>


### [298] [Reproducibility in open-source RISC-V HW flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.04883)
*Peter Kourzanov,Anmol Anmol*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出让RISC-V与可复现构建社区合作，采用Guix解决开源硬件的依赖管理与可复现性问题，并以CVA6为例验证该工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源硬件快速增长，需防止碎片化、促进协作与复用；同时希望将创新集中于微架构、IP及SoC集成等最需要之处。开源硬件存在依赖管理与可复现性难题。

Method: 选用Guix这一严格的软件制品可复现方案，将其应用于开源硬件的依赖管理与可复现性问题，以CVA6作为运行示例进行实践验证。

Result: 得到完全可复现的打包测试、仿真、模拟及周期精确模型集合，表明该工作流可行。

Conclusion: 该方案展示了有前景的工作流，未来有望扩展以支撑更大的RISC-V社区，提供可复用的软硬件组件以服务下一代平台。

Abstract: Open-source hardware is booming. To prevent fragmentation, encourage collaboration and reuse we propose the RISC-V to join forces with Reproducible Builds communities and concentrate innovation potential where its needed most: creation of new micro-architectures, IPs and their integration into new SoCs and applications. To facilitate this goal, we chose Guix, a rigorous solution for reproducible software artefacts. We apply it to dependency management & reproducibility problems in open-source hardware and show the validity of the approach, taking CVA6 as a running example. The end result - a fully reproducible collection of packaged tests, emulation, simulation and cycle-accurate models - shows a promising workflow that could (in future) scale to support larger RISC-V community with reusable software & hardware components for next-generation platforms.

</details>


### [299] [One Strategy Does Not Fit All: Rethinking Data Labeling for Just-in-Time Vulnerability Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05013)
*Quan Tran,Duong Nguyen,Thanh Le-Cong,Triet Huynh Minh Le,M. Ali Babar,Hoang Dieu Vu,Quyet-Thang Huynh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了漏洞修复/引入提交标注策略如何影响JIT漏洞预测的分类、优先级排序与定位三类下游任务，在OpenSSL与Linux内核上构建28个数据集、评测6个代表性模型，发现标注效果强任务依赖：人工VFC标注+漏洞特定VIC标注在分类与排序上最优（显著优于15种组合中的11种），精度优先VFC+LLM辅助VIC标注在定位上最优（显著优于7种），不存在普适最优标注策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JIT-VP需在提交时识别安全风险，但其可靠性受制于准确标签难以构建；现有VFC（漏洞修复提交）与VIC（漏洞引入提交）识别策略取舍不同，导致数据集差异显著，使模型性能与数据集构建因素难以解耦；且实际质量保障不仅需分类，还需高风险提交排序与漏洞根因定位。

Method: 在OpenSSL与Linux内核上，组合4种VFC标注策略与4种VIC标注策略构建28个数据集，选取6个代表性JIT-VP模型，分别评估分类、优先级排序与定位三类任务的性能表现。

Result: 标注有效性呈强任务依赖性：人工VFC标注结合漏洞特定VIC标注在分类与排序任务上最可靠，显著优于15种备选组合中的11种；精度优先的VFC标注结合LLM辅助VIC标注在定位任务上最优，显著优于15种中的7种。

Conclusion: 不存在普遍最优的标注策略，应面向具体下游任务进行数据集构建；该结论为JIT-VP数据集设计与评估的规范性提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Just-In-Time Vulnerability Prediction (JIT-VP) identifies security risks in code changes at commit time, enabling early feedback before vulnerabilities enter software systems. However, its practical reliability is challenged by the difficulty of constructing accurate labels. Multiple strategies with different trade-offs have been proposed to identify vulnerability-fixing commits (VFCs) and vulnerability-introducing commits (VICs) for dataset construction, leading to substantially different datasets. This makes it difficult to separate model performance from dataset construction. Moreover, real-world quality assurance requires not only classification but also prioritizing high-risk commits and localizing vulnerable causes. This paper presents an extensive empirical study of how labeling strategies affect these downstream tasks. We constructed 28 datasets using four VFC and four VIC labeling strategies across OpenSSL and Linux kernel, then evaluated six representative JIT-VP models for classification, prioritization, and localization. Our results show that labeling effectiveness is strongly task-dependent. Manual VFC labeling combined with vulnerability-specific VIC labeling provides the most reliable performance for classification and prioritization, significantly outperforming 11 of 15 alternatives. Meanwhile, precision-first VFC labeling combined with LLM-assisted VIC labeling performs best for localization, significantly outperforming 7 of 15 alternatives. These findings demonstrate that no single labeling strategy is universally optimal and highlight the importance of task-oriented dataset construction.

</details>


### [300] [Equal Access, Unequal Gains? GitHub Copilot and Productivity Among Developers with Disabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05056)
*Emeralda Sesari,Daria Ana-Maria Mogoş,Federica Sarro,Ayushi Rastogi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究基于199名自报残障的GitHub开发者公开数据，比较GitHub Copilot免费开放前后的活动变化，未发现提交次数或净修改行数的变化在不同残障群体间存在差异，Copilot的普及与可观测开发活动的整体增减无关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具在软件开发中日益普及并被报告可提升完成速度与主观生产力，但其收益是否在不同开发者群体间一致尚不明确；残障开发者本就受可访问性障碍影响，而跨多种残障类型的证据仍然有限。

Method: 使用公开的个人资料与仓库数据，构建覆盖多个残障类别的199名开发者数据集，采用前后对比设计，比较GitHub Copilot免费开放前后的开发活动（提交次数、净修改行数及其活动水平分布）。

Result: 多数残障群体呈现"提交次数更少但修改代码更多"的描述性模式；部分群体的开发者分布向低活动与高活动两端移动，中等活动水平人数减少；但统计上未发现提交次数或净修改行数的变化在残障群体间存在差异。

Conclusion: GitHub Copilot的更广泛可用性不能与可观测开发活动的普遍增加或减少相关联，而变化模式因开发者个体及残障群体而异。

Abstract: Generative AI tools are increasingly used in software development and have been associated with benefits such as faster task completion and improved perceived productivity. However, whether these benefits extend across different developer populations remains unclear. This is especially relevant for developers with disabilities, whose work can already be impacted by accessibility barriers. Yet, evidence remains limited across a broader range of disability groups. This study investigates whether the wider availability of GitHub Copilot is associated with changes in productivity among GitHub users who self-identify as having a disability. Using publicly available profile and repository data, we curated a dataset of 199 developers across multiple disability groups and compared their activity before and after GitHub Copilot became freely available. We found that most disability groups showed a descriptive pattern of fewer commits but more code modified. In several disability groups, the distribution of developers across commit activity levels shifted toward both lower and higher level, leaving fewer developers at moderate level. However, we found no evidence that changes in commits or net lines modified differed across disability groups. These findings suggest that the wider availability of GitHub Copilot cannot be associated with a broad increase or decrease in observable development activity, while patterns of change varied across developers and disability groups.

</details>


### [301] [A Gated Spec-Driven Workflow: An Audit-Trail Study of Coding-Agent Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05094)
*Rodrigo Figueiredo Abdo,Pedro Carvalho Brom*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了 Claude Code 中一种基于规格的开发（SDD）工作流，其核心特征是几乎所有控制机制都是由同一模型执行的指令。通过对 7 个项目、9 个功能（含 Fortran 求解器、Python 程序、书籍乃至本文本身）的日志与转录分析，发现 5 个功能全部任务完成，581 个计划任务中完成 288 个；执行者仅产生 1 处未声明改动且被检出；主要薄弱点在于编排者自查自判，247 次块检查中记录 11 次未声明改动，但仅 4 次停止工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDD 主张编码代理应依据规格、计划与任务列表而非聊天记录工作。本文旨在实证考察此类工作流在真实使用中的有效性与可靠性，特别是当控制机制本身由被约束的同一模型执行时，各环节（人工审批、审查子代理、执行者、编排者）的表现如何。

Method: 在 Claude Code 中构建分阶段 SDD 工作流：用户逐阶段审批；审查子代理按固定的阶段专属缺陷清单校验产物；执行者子代理仅可修改任务列出的文件；每块任务完成后由编排者进行文件清单检查以检测未声明改动。对 7 个项目、9 个功能的日志与转录进行定性定量分析，项目类型涵盖 Fortran 求解器、Python 程序、书籍等，均由作者本人运行。

Result: 9 个功能中 5 个完成全部计划任务（含 Fortran 求解器功能）；581 个计划任务中 288 个按块完成。执行者基本遵守声明文件范围，交付目录仅出现 1 处未声明改动且被检查捕获，但其报告中记录了其他偏离协议的情况。编排者在 247 次块检查中有 11 次记录到未声明改动（多源于作者当轮请求与环境因素），却仅在 4 次中停止工作。

Conclusion: SDD 工作流在部分场景下能有效推进任务并约束执行者的文件改动范围，但核心脆弱性在于编排者"自跑自判"的检查机制——其检测结果存在漏判与执行不力，未能可靠触发停止。基于指令的控制在同模型自检下可靠性有限。

Abstract: Spec-driven development (SDD) asks a coding agent to work from specifications, plans, and task lists rather than from the chat transcript. We study one such workflow for Claude Code, in which almost every control is an instruction carried out by the same model that it constrains. The user must approve each stage before the next begins. A reviewer subagent checks each artefact against a fixed, stage-specific defect list, and an executor subagent may only touch the files listed in its task. After each block of tasks, the orchestrator runs a file-inventory check to detect undeclared changes. We analyse the logs and transcripts of nine features in seven projects, all run by the authors, ranging from a Fortran solver and Python programs to books and this paper. Of these, five finished with every planned task done, including the Fortran solver feature, and 288 of the 581 planned tasks were completed block by block. The executor kept to its declared files: it left only one undeclared change in the deliverable folder, and the check caught it, although its reports record other departures from the protocol. The weak point was the orchestrator, which runs and judges its own checks. It recorded undeclared changes in 11 of 247 block checks, mostly from the authors' in-round requests and the environment, and stopped work in only four.

</details>


### [302] [An Investigation on Numerical Bugs in GPU Programs Towards Automated Bug Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05121)
*Ravishka Rathnasuriya,Nidhi Majoju,Zihe Song,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对GitHub上397个真实GPU数值缺陷样本进行实证研究，总结其根因、症状、触发输入、测试预言与修复策略，并实现检测工具GPU-NBDetect，在4个库、186个数学函数中检出226个缺陷，其中60个获开发者确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU计算广泛应用于科学计算与深度学习，但GPU程序易受数值缺陷影响，导致结果错误或崩溃；此类缺陷依赖特定输入值/类型、缺乏可靠错误检查机制与预言，加之GPU独特编程约定，使其难以检测、调试与修复，故需系统刻画GPU数值缺陷特征以指导解决方案设计。

Method: 从GitHub收集并分析397个真实GPU数值缺陷样本，归纳常见根因、症状、触发输入模式、测试预言及修复策略；在此基础上设计原型检测工具GPU-NBDetect，覆盖六类缺陷类别，并在四个数学库的186个数学函数上开展检测与开发者确认。

Result: GPU-NBDetect在四个库的186个数学函数中共检出226个缺陷，其中60个已被开发者确认为真实缺陷。

Conclusion: 研究为GPU数值缺陷的检测与预防技术奠定实证基础，并为构建更有效的调试与自动修复工具提供洞见。

Abstract: General-purpose graphics processing unit (GPU) computing has emerged as a leading parallel computing paradigm, offering significant performance gains in various domains such as scientific computing and deep learning. However, GPU programs are susceptible to numerical bugs, which can lead to incorrect results or crashes. These bugs are difficult to detect, debug, and fix due to their dependence on specific input values or types and the absence of reliable error-checking mechanisms and oracles. Additionally, the unique programming conventions of GPUs complicate identifying the root causes of bugs, while fixing them requires domain-specific knowledge of GPU computing and numerical libraries. Therefore, understanding the characteristics of GPU numerical bugs (GPU-NBs) is crucial for developing effective solutions. In this paper, we conduct a comprehensive study of GPU-NBs by analyzing 397 real-world bug samples from GitHub. We identify common root causes, symptoms, input patterns, test oracles that trigger these bugs and the strategies used to fix them. We also present GPU-NBDetect, a preliminary tool designed to detect numerical bugs across six distinct bug categories. GPU-NBDetect detected a total of 226 bugs across 186 mathematical functions in four libraries, with 60 of the bugs confirmed by developers. Our findings lay the groundwork for developing detection and prevention techniques for GPU-NBs and offer insights for building more effective debugging and auto-repair tool.

</details>


### [303] [DSL-Bench: Evaluating and Explaining LLM Code Generation Across Domain-Specific Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05168)
*Zihan Zhou,Mingxi Ye,Yan Gao,Guipeng Wei,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出DSL-Bench，一个跨领域特定语言（DSL）的可执行代码生成基准与分析框架，覆盖18个应用领域，揭示LLM在DSL代码生成中语法可接受性与语义正确性间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在软件工程流程中应用日增，但代码生成基准多聚焦通用语言；DSL具有独特语法、语义与验证要求，单一DSL基准结果难以横向比较，缺乏统一可执行的跨DSL评测框架。

Method: 从13,045篇DSL相关论文出发，经LLM辅助开放式卡片分类归纳18个代表性应用领域；每领域选取一个可评测DSL并配备语法检查器与任务级语义预言机；在18个DSL上评测7个LLM；进行方差分解与DSL层面因子相关性分析；评测提示工程并对比5种改进方法；构建能力画像与DSL-Profiler摘要。

Result: 平均语法与语义通过率分别为31.37%和11.64%，语法接受与语义正确存在显著落差；语义通过率方差中76.99%归因于DSL差异、7.86%归因于模型差异；四个DSL层面因子中知识需求与语义正确性负相关最强（r=-0.508）；提示工程收益不均衡，RAG-Refinement为最强定向方法，较匹配基线平均提升语义正确性16.29个百分点。

Conclusion: 跨DSL代码生成存在工具链特异的语义瓶颈，评测需超越语法接受层面；所提能力画像与DSL-Profiler可辅助解读数据稀缺的DSL。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in software-engineering workflows, but code-generation benchmarks largely focus on general-purpose languages. Domain-specific languages (DSLs) have distinct syntax, semantics, and validation requirements, making results from individual DSL benchmarks difficult to compare. We present DSL-Bench, an executable benchmark and analysis framework for cross-DSL code generation. Starting from 13,045 DSL-related papers, LLM-assisted open card sorting identifies 18 representative application domains. For each domain, we select an evaluable DSL and provide a syntax checker and task-level semantic oracle. Evaluating 7 LLMs on 18 DSLs, we observe mean syntax and semantic pass rates of 31.37% and 11.64%, respectively, revealing a substantial gap between syntax acceptance and semantic correctness. A variance decomposition attributes 76.99% of semantic pass-rate variation to differences among DSLs and 7.86% to differences among models. Among four DSL-level factors, Knowledge Demand has the strongest negative association with semantic correctness (r = -0.508). We evaluate prompt engineering across all 18 DSLs and study five improvement methods on five targeted DSLs. Prompt engineering yields uneven gains; RAG-Refinement, the strongest targeted method, improves semantic correctness by 16.29 percentage points on average over matched baselines. We further derive capability profiles and DSL-Profiler summaries for interpreting data-scarce DSLs. These results highlight toolchain-specific semantic bottlenecks and the need to evaluate DSL code generation beyond syntax acceptance.

</details>


### [304] [Extending the MM-AR Metamodeling Platform with Process Mining Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05176)
*Geraldine Hürzeler,Hans-Georg Fill,Daniel Borcard*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个概念验证：通过基于PM4Py的独立Python服务，为XR元建模平台MM-AR扩展流程发现能力，无需修改平台内核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流程挖掘结果通常以二维可视化呈现，与企业模型创建与维护的环境相分离；XR元建模平台（如MM-AR）可定义建模语言并在2D/3D中显示模型，但缺乏对事件数据和挖掘算法的访问能力。

Method: 构建独立Python服务封装开源PM4Py库，与XR元建模平台解耦集成；采用三种常见发现算法，从CSV或XES事件日志生成Petri网与BPMN模型。

Result: 使用四个公开事件日志的技术评估显示，全部16种配置下所生成模型实例的元素数量与PM4Py原生结果一致；集成每次运行增加约8至53秒开销，且开销随发现模型规模而非事件日志规模增长。

Conclusion: 该原型虽尚未利用第三维度编码流程信息，但为在XR环境中开展此类研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Process mining derives process models from event data recorded by information systems, but its results are usually presented as two-dimensional visualizations that are separate from the environments in which enterprise models are created and maintained. Metamodeling platforms for extended reality (XR), such as MM-AR, allow users to define modeling languages and to display models in 2D and 3D, but they do not provide access to event data or mining algorithms so far. In this paper we therefore present a first proof-of-concept for showing how an XR-based metamodeling platform can be extended with process discovery capabilities without changes to the platform core through a separate Python service based on the open source PM4Py process mining library. We demonstrate the approach using three common discovery algorithms for obtaining Petri nets and BPMN models based on CSV or XES event logs. A technical evaluation using four public event logs shows that the element counts of the created model instances match those of the PM4Py models in all 16 configurations. The integration adds between about 8 and 53 seconds per run, and this overhead grows with the size of the discovered model rather than the size of the event log. Although the prototype does not yet use the third dimension to encode process information, it established the foundations for studying such approaches.

</details>


### [305] [Noisy Issues, Noisy Results: Mislabeling in Information Retrieval-based Bug Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05314)
*Moumita Asad,Rafed Muhammad Yasir,Yirui He,Ziyao He,Sam Malek*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 大规模实证研究揭示IRBL评测数据中平均34.82%的issue标签错误，去除误标后六类技术性能显著下降（MRR降4.68%–21.74%），但不改变技术间相对排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IRBL评测依赖缺陷跟踪系统的标签，而开源项目中标签常出错，威胁评测效度；已有研究多只考察传统技术在小型数据集上的表现，缺乏对现代方法及"噪声集与清洗集部分重叠"问题的系统分析。

Method: 分析5个基准数据集、逾55,000条issue报告以量化误标普遍性；基于Ye et al.基准及考虑数据集重叠的统计方法，评估6种IRBL技术（传统、深度学习、大语言模型三类），对比去除误标前后的性能。

Result: 平均34.82%的issue被误标；去除误标后所有六种技术测得性能显著下降，MRR下降4.68%–21.74%；技术相对排名不变，但性能差距幅度受影响。

Conclusion: 误标问题在IRBL基准中普遍且影响显著，可靠的评测数据是保证IRBL技术比较有效、有意义的前提，呼吁后续研究重视数据清洗与标签质量。

Abstract: Information Retrieval-based Bug Localization (IRBL) leverages bug reports to identify faulty source files. Evaluations of IRBL approaches primarily rely on labeled issues from issue tracking systems. However, prior research has shown that issue labels are frequently incorrect in open-source projects, threatening the validity of IRBL evaluations. Existing studies have primarily examined traditional IRBL techniques on small datasets, overlooking modern approaches and the partial overlap between noisy and cleaned datasets. In this paper, we conduct a large-scale empirical study to investigate the prevalence and impact of mislabeled issues in IRBL benchmarks. We analyze five benchmark datasets comprising over 55,000 issue reports and find that, on average, 34.82% of issues are mislabeled. We further evaluate six IRBL techniques, spanning traditional, deep learning-based, and large language model-based approaches using the widely adopted Ye et al. benchmark and statistical methods that account for dataset overlap. Our results show that removing mislabeled issues from evaluation datasets significantly reduces measured performance across all six techniques, with Mean Reciprocal Rank decreasing by 4.68%-21.74%. Although mislabeling does not change the relative ranking of techniques, it affects the magnitude of their performance differences. These findings highlight the importance of reliable evaluation data for ensuring valid and meaningful comparisons of IRBL techniques.

</details>
